    XVXijs(XWX+tpsu[rb] +  (ptrdiff_t) nxwx * tpsu[cb],rb,cb,r,c,X,k,ks,m,p,*nx,*n,ts, dt,*nt,e,a,ma,
	   off,work + tid * q,worki + tid * (ptrdiff_t) qi,nxwx);

  }

  FREE(work);
  work = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) 2*nxwx,sizeof(double));

  for (r=0;r < *nt;r++) for (c=r;c< *nt;c++) {

    if (dt[r]>1&&qc[r]!=0) {

      for (j=0;j<pt[c];j++) {
        x0 = XWX + tpsu[r] + (tpsu[c]+j) * (ptrdiff_t) nxwx;
	x1 = XWX + tps[r] + (tpsu[c]+j) * (ptrdiff_t) nxwx;
	Ztb(x1,x0,v+voff[r],qc+r,&one,pt+r,work);
      }
      pa = pt[r]-1;
    } else {
      pa = pt[r];
      if (tpsu[r]!=tps[r]) {
        for (j=0;j<pt[c];j++) {
	  x0 = XWX + tpsu[r] + (tpsu[c]+j) * (ptrdiff_t) nxwx;
	  x1 = XWX + tps[r] + (tpsu[c]+j) * (ptrdiff_t) nxwx;
	  for (p1 = x0 + pt[r];x0<p1;x0++,x1++) *x1 = *x0;
	}
      }
    }
    if (dt[c]>1&&qc[c]!=0) {

      for (j=0;j<pa;j++) {
        x0 = XWX + tps[r] + j + tpsu[c] * (ptrdiff_t) nxwx;
	x1 = XWX + tps[r] + j + tps[c] * (ptrdiff_t) nxwx;
	Ztb(x1,x0,v+voff[c],qc+c,&nxwx,pt+c,work);
      }
    } else if (tpsu[c]!=tps[c]) {
      for (j=0;j<pa;j++) {
	x0 = XWX + tps[r] + j + tpsu[c] * (ptrdiff_t) nxwx;
	x1 = XWX + tps[r] + j + tps[c] * (ptrdiff_t) nxwx;
	for (p1 = x0 + nxwx * pt[c];x0<p1;x0 += nxwx,x1 += nxwx) *x1 = *x0;
      }
    }
  }

  if (ptot<nxwx) row_squash(XWX,ptot,nxwx,ptot);
  up2lo(XWX,ptot);

  FREE(pt);FREE(pd);FREE(off);FREE(voff);FREE(tps);FREE(tpsu);FREE(work);
  FREE(B);FREE(R);FREE(C);FREE(sb);FREE(Cost);FREE(cost);FREE(b);FREE(worki);
}

SEXP CXVXd0(SEXP XWXr, SEXP Xr, SEXP er, SEXP kr, SEXP ksr, SEXP mr, SEXP pr, SEXP tsr, SEXP dtr,
	    SEXP vr,SEXP qcr, SEXP nthreadsr, SEXP ar, SEXP mar) {

  double *XWX,*X,*v,*mad=NULL,*e;
  int *k,*ks,*m,*p,*ts,*dt,*qc,*nthreads,nx,nt,*a,*mai=NULL,nma,i;
  ptrdiff_t n,*ma;
  n = (ptrdiff_t) nrows(kr); XWX = REAL(XWXr);
  X = REAL(Xr);
  k = INTEGER(kr); ks = INTEGER(ksr);
  m = INTEGER(mr); nx = length(mr);
  p = INTEGER(pr); e = REAL(er);
  a= INTEGER(ar);nma = length(mar);
  ma = (ptrdiff_t *)CALLOC((size_t)nma,sizeof(ptrdiff_t));
  if (isReal(mar)) {
    mad=REAL(mar);
    for (i=0;i<nma;i++) ma[i] = (ptrdiff_t) mad[i];
  } else {
    mai=INTEGER(mar);
    for (i=0;i<nma;i++) ma[i] = (ptrdiff_t) mai[i];
  }

  ts = INTEGER(tsr); dt = INTEGER(dtr); nt = length(tsr);
  v = REAL(vr);qc = INTEGER(qcr);
  nthreads = INTEGER(nthreadsr);
  XVXd0(XWX,X,e,k,ks,m,p,&n,&nx,ts,dt,&nt,v,qc,nthreads,a,ma);
  FREE(ma);
  return(R_NilValue);
}

SEXP CXWXd1(SEXP XWXr, SEXP Xr, SEXP wr, SEXP kr, SEXP ksr, SEXP mr, SEXP pr, SEXP tsr, SEXP dtr,
	    SEXP vr,SEXP qcr, SEXP nthreadsr, SEXP ar_stopr, SEXP ar_weightsr,
	    SEXP rsr,SEXP csr) {

  double *XWX,*X,*v,*w,*ar_weights;
  int *k,*ks,*m,*p,*ts,*dt,*qc,*nthreads,*cs,*rs,nx,nt,ncs,nrs,*ar_stop;
  ptrdiff_t n;
  n = (ptrdiff_t)nrows(kr); XWX = REAL(XWXr);
  X = REAL(Xr);w = REAL(wr);
  k = INTEGER(kr); ks = INTEGER(ksr);
  m = INTEGER(mr); nx = length(mr);
  p = INTEGER(pr);
  ar_stop = INTEGER(ar_stopr);
  ar_weights = REAL(ar_weightsr);
  ts = INTEGER(tsr); dt = INTEGER(dtr); nt = length(tsr);
  v = REAL(vr);qc = INTEGER(qcr);
  nthreads = INTEGER(nthreadsr);
  cs = INTEGER(csr); rs = INTEGER(rsr);
  nrs = length(rsr); ncs = length(csr);
  if (*cs<0) ncs = 0;
  if (*rs<0) nrs = 0;
  XWXd1(XWX,X,w,k,ks,m,p,&n,&nx,ts,dt,&nt,v,qc,nthreads,ar_stop,ar_weights,
	rs,cs,&nrs,&ncs);
  return(R_NilValue);
}

void XWXd1(double *XWX,double *X,double *w,int *k,int *ks, int *m,int *p, ptrdiff_t *n, int *nx, int *ts,
	   int *dt, int *nt,double *v,int *qc,int *nthreads,int *ar_stop,double *ar_weights,
	   int *rs, int *cs, int *nrs, int *ncs) {

  int *pt, *pd,ri,ci,si,maxp=0,nxwx=0,tri,r,c,rb,cb,rt,ct,pa,*tpsr,*tpsur,*tpsc,*tpsuc,ptot,
    *b,*B,*C,*R,*sb,N,kk,kb,tid=0,*worki,symmetric=1,one=1;
  ptrdiff_t *off,*voff,mmp,q,i,j,qi=0;
  double *work,*ws=NULL,*Cost,*cost,*x0,*x1,*p0,*p1,x;
  unsigned long long ht[256];
  SM **sm,*SMstack;
  #ifndef OPENMP_ON
  *nthreads = 1;
  #endif
  if (*nthreads<1) *nthreads = 1;
  SMinihash(ht);

  if (*nrs <= 0) {
    if (*ncs > 0) {
      *nrs = *ncs;rs=cs;
    } else {
      for (i=0;i<*nt;i++) rs[i] = cs[i] = i;
    }
  } else {
    if (*ncs>0) symmetric = 0; else {
      *ncs = *nrs; cs = rs;
    }
  }
  pt = (int *) CALLOC((size_t)*nt,sizeof(int));
  pd = (int *) CALLOC((size_t)*nt,sizeof(int));
  off = (ptrdiff_t *) CALLOC((size_t)*nx+1,sizeof(ptrdiff_t));
  voff = (ptrdiff_t *) CALLOC((size_t)*nt+1,sizeof(ptrdiff_t));
  tpsr = (int *) CALLOC((size_t)*nt+1,sizeof(int));
  tpsur = (int *) CALLOC((size_t)*nt+1,sizeof(int));
  tpsc = (int *) CALLOC((size_t)*nt+1,sizeof(int));
  tpsuc = (int *) CALLOC((size_t)*nt+1,sizeof(int));
  for (q=i=0;i< *nt; i++) {
    for (j=0;j<dt[i];j++,q++) {
      if (j==dt[i]-1) pd[i] = p[q];
      off[q+1] = off[q] + p[q] * (ptrdiff_t) m[q];
      if (j==0) pt[i] = p[q]; else pt[i] *= p[q];
    }
    if (qc[i]==0) voff[i+1] = voff[i]; else if (qc[i]>0) voff[i+1] = voff[i] + pt[i]; else {
      ri = (int) round(v[voff[i]]);
      voff[i+1] = voff[i] + ri + 2;
    }
    if (maxp<pt[i]) maxp=pt[i];
  }
  for (kk=i=j=0;j<*nrs;j++) {
    r = rs[j];
    tpsr[r] = kk;tpsur[r] = i;
    if (qc[r]==0) kk += pt[r];
    else if (qc[r]>0) kk += pt[r] - 1;
    else {
      ri = (int) round(v[voff[r]]);
      kk += pt[r] - (int) round(v[voff[r]+ri+1]);
    }
    i += pt[r];
  }
  ptot = kk;
  nxwx = i;
  for (kk=i=j=0;j<*ncs;j++) {
    c = cs[j];
    tpsc[c] = kk;tpsuc[c] = i;
    if (qc[c]==0) kk += pt[c];
    else if (qc[c]>0) kk += pt[c] - 1;
    else {
      ri = (int) round(v[voff[c]]);
      kk += pt[c] - (int) round(v[voff[c]+ri+1]);
    }
    i +=  pt[c];
  }

  qi =  6 * *n;
  worki = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)qi * *nthreads,sizeof(int));
  mmp = maxp;mmp = mmp*mmp;

  if (*ar_stop>=0) {

    ws = w;
    w = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(double));
    for (p0 = w,p1 = w + *n;p0<p1;p0++,ws++) *p0 = sqrt(*ws);
    ws = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) 2 * *n -2,sizeof(double));
    for (i=0;i<*n-1;i++) ws[i+*n-1] = ws[i] = ar_weights[2*i+1] * ar_weights[2*i+2] * w[i+1] * w[i];
    for (i=0;i<*n-1;i++) w[i] *= (ar_weights[2*i+1]*ar_weights[2*i+1]+ar_weights[2*i]*ar_weights[2*i])*w[i];
    i = *n-1;w[i] *= w[i]*ar_weights[2*i]*ar_weights[2*i];

    tri = 1;
  } else tri = 0;

  sm = (SM **)CALLOC((size_t) *n * *nthreads,sizeof(SM *));
  SMstack = (SM *)CALLOC((size_t) 3 * *n * *nthreads,sizeof(SM));
  if (symmetric) N = ((*nrs + 1) * *nrs)/2; else N = *nrs * *ncs;
  C = (int *) CALLOC((size_t)N,sizeof(int)); R = (int *) CALLOC((size_t)N,sizeof(int));
  sb = (int *) CALLOC((size_t)N+1,sizeof(int));
  Cost = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) N,sizeof(double));
  sb[0] = 0;
  for (kk=ri=0;ri < *nrs;ri++) {
    if (symmetric) ci=ri; else ci = 0;
    for (;ci< *ncs;ci++,kk++) {
      r = rs[ri];c=cs[ci];
      R[kk]=r;C[kk]=c;
      si = ks[ts[r] + *nx] - ks[ts[r]];
      if (symmetric && r==c && si==1 && !tri) {
        i = pt[r]/pd[r];
        i = i*(i+1)/2;
      } else {
        i = (pt[r]/pd[r])*(pt[c]/pd[c]);
      }
      sb[kk+1] = sb[kk] + i;

      if (m[r]*m[c] < *n) {
         Cost[kk] = m[r]*m[c]*(double) pd[c];
         x = m[r]*m[c]*(double) pd[r]; if (x<Cost[kk]) Cost[kk] = x;
      } else {
        Cost[kk] = *n * pd[c];;
        x = *n * pd[r]; if (x < Cost[kk]) Cost[kk] = x;
      }
    }
  }
  b = (int *) CALLOC((size_t)sb[N],sizeof(int));
  B = (int *) CALLOC((size_t)sb[N],sizeof(int));
  cost = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)sb[N],sizeof(double));
  for (kb=0,i=0;i<sb[N];i++) {
    b[i]=i;while (i>=sb[kb+1]) kb++;
    rb = R[kb];cb=C[kb];
    cost[i] = Cost[kb];
    B[i] = kb;
  }
  revsort(cost,b,sb[N]);
  q = XWXspace(N,sb,b,B,R,C,k,ks,m,p,pt,pd,*nx,*n,ts,dt,*nt,tri);
  work = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)q * *nthreads,sizeof(double));

  kb=0;
  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  #pragma omp parallel for private(j,kb,kk,r,c,rb,cb,rt,ct,tid,i) num_threads(*nthreads) schedule(dynamic)
  #endif
  for (j=0;j<sb[N];j++) {
    kk = b[j];kb=B[kk];
    rb = R[kb];cb=C[kb];
    i = kk - sb[kb];
    rt = pt[rb]/pd[rb]; ct = pt[cb]/pd[cb];

    if (symmetric && sb[kb+1]-sb[kb]<rt*ct) {
      r=0; while (i >= rt - r) { i -= rt - r;r++;}
      c = i + r;
    } else {
      r = i / ct;
      c = i % ct;
    }

    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    tid = omp_get_thread_num();
    #endif
    XWXijs(XWX+tpsur[rb] +  (ptrdiff_t) nxwx * tpsuc[cb],rb,cb,r,c,X,k,ks,m,p,*nx,*n,ts, dt,*nt,w,ws,
	   tri,off,work + tid * q,worki + tid * (ptrdiff_t) qi,nxwx,ht,
	   sm + tid * *n,SMstack + 3 * tid *  *n );

  }

  FREE(work);
  work = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) 2*nxwx,sizeof(double));

  for (ri=0;ri < *nrs;ri++) {
    if (symmetric) ci=ri; else ci = 0;
    for (;ci< *ncs;ci++) {

      r = rs[ri];c = cs[ci];
      if (dt[r]>1&&qc[r]!=0) {

        for (j=0;j<pt[c];j++) {
          x0 = XWX + tpsur[r] + (tpsuc[c]+j) * (ptrdiff_t) nxwx;
	  x1 = XWX + tpsr[r] + (tpsuc[c]+j) * (ptrdiff_t) nxwx;
	  Ztb(x1,x0,v+voff[r],qc+r,&one,pt+r,work);
        }
        pa = pt[r]-1;
      } else {
        pa = pt[r];
        if (tpsur[r]!=tpsr[r]) {
          for (j=0;j<pt[c];j++) {
	    x0 = XWX + tpsur[r] + (tpsuc[c]+j) * (ptrdiff_t) nxwx;
	    x1 = XWX + tpsr[r] + (tpsuc[c]+j) * (ptrdiff_t) nxwx;
	    for (p1 = x0 + pt[r];x0<p1;x0++,x1++) *x1 = *x0;
	  }
        }
      }
      if (dt[c]>1&&qc[c]!=0) {

        for (j=0;j<pa;j++) {
          x0 = XWX + tpsr[r] + j + tpsuc[c] * (ptrdiff_t) nxwx;
	  x1 = XWX + tpsr[r] + j + tpsc[c] * (ptrdiff_t) nxwx;
	  Ztb(x1,x0,v+voff[c],qc+c,&nxwx,pt+c,work);
        }
      } else if (tpsuc[c]!=tpsc[c]) {
        for (j=0;j<pa;j++) {
	  x0 = XWX + tpsr[r] + j + tpsuc[c] * (ptrdiff_t) nxwx;
	  x1 = XWX + tpsr[r] + j + tpsc[c] * (ptrdiff_t) nxwx;
	  for (p1 = x0 + nxwx * pt[c];x0<p1;x0 += nxwx,x1 += nxwx) *x1 = *x0;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if (ptot<nxwx) row_squash(XWX,ptot,nxwx,ptot);
  if (symmetric) up2lo(XWX,ptot);

  FREE(pt);FREE(pd);FREE(off);FREE(voff);FREE(tpsr);FREE(tpsur);FREE(tpsc);FREE(tpsuc);
  FREE(work); if (tri) {FREE(ws);FREE(w);}
  FREE(B);FREE(R);FREE(C);FREE(sb);FREE(Cost);FREE(cost);FREE(b);FREE(sm);FREE(SMstack);FREE(worki);
}

void fill_lt(double *A,int k) {

  int i;
  double *Al,*Au,*Au0,*Al0,*Au1;

  for (Al0=Au0=A,i=0;i<k;i++,Au0+=k,Al0++) for (Au=Au0,Au1=Au+i,Al=Al0;Au<Au1;Au++,Al+=k) *Al = *Au;
}

SEXP CNCV(SEXP NCVr, SEXP NCV1r, SEXP NCV2r, SEXP Gr, SEXP rsdr, SEXP betar, SEXP beta1r, SEXP wr,SEXP spr,
	    SEXP Xr,SEXP kr, SEXP ksr, SEXP mr, SEXP pr, SEXP tsr, SEXP dtr,
	    SEXP vr,SEXP qcr, SEXP nthreadsr, SEXP nei, SEXP Sr) {

  double *NCV,*NCV1,*NCV2,*w,*X,*v,*beta,*G,*rsd,*sp,**S,*db,*mar,*mdr;
  int *a,*d,*k,*ks,*m,nx,*p,*ts,*dt,nt,*qc,*nthreads,nprot=0,nS,ns,nsp,*sr,*soff,i,j,q,pg,nn,ck;
  SEXP K,M,I,MI,Sl,Si;
  ptrdiff_t n,*ma,*md,*p1,*p2;
  n = (ptrdiff_t)nrows(kr);
  ck = (int) ncols(kr);
  pg = (int) nrows(Gr);
  G = REAL(Gr); X = REAL(Xr);w = REAL(wr);
  beta = REAL(betar);rsd= REAL(rsdr);db = REAL(beta1r);
  k = INTEGER(kr); ks = INTEGER(ksr);
  m = INTEGER(mr); nx = length(mr);
  p = INTEGER(pr);sp = REAL(spr);nsp=length(spr);
  ts = INTEGER(tsr); dt = INTEGER(dtr); nt = length(tsr);
  v = REAL(vr);qc = INTEGER(qcr);
  nthreads = INTEGER(nthreadsr);
  NCV = REAL(NCVr);NCV1 = REAL(NCV1r);NCV2 = REAL(NCV2r);

  K = getListEl(nei,"a");
  K = PROTECT(coerceVector(K,INTSXP));nprot++;
  a = INTEGER(K);
  M = getListEl(nei,"ma");
  M = PROTECT(coerceVector(M,REALSXP));nprot++;
  mar = REAL(M);nn = length(M);
  ma = (ptrdiff_t *)CALLOC(nn,sizeof(ptrdiff_t));
  for (p1=ma,p2=p1+nn;p1<p2;p1++,mar++) *p1 = (ptrdiff_t) *mar;
  I = getListEl(nei,"d");
  I = PROTECT(coerceVector(I,INTSXP));nprot++;
  d = INTEGER(I);
  MI = getListEl(nei,"md");
  MI = PROTECT(coerceVector(MI,REALSXP));nprot++;
  mdr = REAL(MI);
  md = (ptrdiff_t *)CALLOC(nn,sizeof(ptrdiff_t));
  for (p1=md,p2=p1+nn;p1<p2;p1++,mdr++) *p1 = (ptrdiff_t) *mdr;

  nS = length(Sr);
  S = (double **)CALLOC(nsp,sizeof(double *));
  soff = (int *)CALLOC(nsp,sizeof(int));
  sr = (int*) CALLOC(nsp,sizeof(int));
  for (ns=i=0;i<nS;i++) {
    Sl = VECTOR_ELT(Sr, i);
    soff[ns] = asInteger(getListEl(Sl,"start")) - 1;
    sr[ns] = asInteger(getListEl(Sl,"stop")) - soff[ns];
    Si = getListEl(Sl,"S0");
    q = length(Si);
    for (j=0;j<q;j++,ns++) {
      K = VECTOR_ELT(Si, j);
      K = PROTECT(coerceVector(K,REALSXP));nprot++;
      S[ns] = REAL(K);
      if (j>0) { soff[ns] = soff[ns-1]; sr[ns] = sr[ns-1];}
    }

  }

  ncvd(NCV,NCV1,NCV2,beta,db,G,rsd,w,&pg,&nn,a,ma,d,md,X,k,&ck,ks,m,p,&n,
       S,ns,sr,soff,sp,&nx,ts,dt,&nt,v,qc,nthreads);
  FREE(S);FREE(soff);FREE(sr);FREE(ma);FREE(md);
  UNPROTECT(nprot);return(R_NilValue);
}

void tad(double *M,int p) {

  int i;
  double *Md,*Ms,*M1;
  for (i=0;i<p;i++) {
    Md = M+i; Ms = M + i*p; M1 = Ms + i;
    for (; Ms<=M1; Md+= p, Ms++) { *Md += *Ms;*Ms = *Md; }
  }
}

static int ucomp(const void *a, const void *b) {

  int k;
  k = *(*(int **)a) - *(*(int **)b);
  if (k==0) k = *(*(int **)a + 1) - *(*(int **)b + 1);
  return(k);
}

void upair(int *k1,int *k2, int *ind,ptrdiff_t *n) {

  int **a,*ap,*a0,i,j;
  a = (int **)CALLOC(*n,sizeof(int *));
  a0 = ap = (int *)CALLOC(*n * 3,sizeof(int));
  for (i=0;i<*n;i++,ap += 3) {
    a[i] = ap;ap[0] = k1[i];
    ap[1] = k2[i];ap[2] = i;
  }
  qsort(a,*n,sizeof(int *),ucomp);

  for (i=0;i<*n;i++,ap += 3) {
    ap = a[i];
    k1[i] = *ap;k2[i] = ap[1];ind[i] = ap[2];
  }
  for (i=0;i<*n;i++) a0[i] = ind[i];

  j = 0;
  ind[*a0] = j;
  for (i=1;i<*n;i++) {
    if (k1[i]!=k1[i-1]||k2[i]!=k2[i-1]) {
      j++; k1[j] = k1[i]; k2[j] = k2[i];
    }
    ind[a0[i]] = j;
  }
  *n = j+1;
  FREE(a0);FREE(a);
}

void thread_lim(int i0,int i1,int *thlim,int nth) {

  int di,ri,i;
  ri = (i1-i0+1);
  di = ri/nth;
  while (di*nth<ri) di++;
  for (i=0;i<=nth;i++) {
    thlim[i] = di*i + i0;
    if (thlim[i]>i1) thlim[i]=i1+1;
  }
}

void ncvd(double *NCV,double *NCV1,double *NCV2,double *beta,double *db, double *G,double *rsd,
	  double *w,int *pg,int *nn,int *a,ptrdiff_t *ma,int *d,ptrdiff_t *md,
	  double *X,int *k, int *ck, int *ks,int *m,int *p, ptrdiff_t *n,double **S,int ns,int *sr,
	  int *soff,double *sp,
	  int *nx, int *ts, int *dt, int *nt,double *v,int *qc,int *nthreads) {

  double *A,*A2,**A1,*Aaa,*IAaa,*Ap,*Aaap,*IA,*IAp,*ba,*bp,*rp,one=1.0,zero=0.0,*dp0,*dp1,*dp2,
    *dp3,xx,*L,**beta1,**mu1,**P,*wij,*wp,**ba1,**IAdAIA,*B,*ba1p,**L1,*IAdAIAp,***beta2,*mu2,
    *V2,*U,*ba2,*NCVth,*NCV1th,*NCV2th,*ba2p,*Arp,*Alp,*IAdAIArp,*work,***L2,***U2,*Vl,*xbdwork,*rsd1;
  int ncs=0,dum,i1,i,j,M,q,r,info=1,*k1p,*k2p,*a1p,*a2p,tid=0,maxM,maxM2,*xbiwork,
    *p1,ione=1,l,*cs,srl,*ind,*a1,*a2,nchunk,*ichunk,ch,ch_start,*piv,ncvth=1,*thlim,*l1c,**l2c,*iwork;
  ptrdiff_t nb,nu,nu_size=0,*mk,ii,jj,kk,kk0,target_nk,max_chunk,max_nb,*xbpwork,space[3];
  char uplo='U',ntrans='N',side='L';
  cs =  (int *)CALLOC(*nt,sizeof(int));
  thlim = (int *)CALLOC(ncvth+1,sizeof(int));
  if (ncvth>1) {
    NCVth = (double *)CALLOC(ncvth,sizeof(double));
    NCV1th = (double *)CALLOC(ncvth*ns,sizeof(double));
    NCV2th = (double *)CALLOC(ncvth*ns*ns,sizeof(double));
  } else {
    NCVth = NCV;NCV1th = NCV1; NCV2th = NCV2;
  }

  Xbdspace(space,m,p,n,nx,dt,nt);
  xbiwork = (int *) CALLOC((size_t) space[0],sizeof(int));
  xbpwork = (ptrdiff_t *) CALLOC((size_t)space[1],sizeof(ptrdiff_t));
  xbdwork = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)space[2],sizeof(double));

  P = (double **)CALLOC(ns,sizeof(double *));
  L1 = (double **)CALLOC(ns,sizeof(double *));
  beta1 = (double **)CALLOC(ns,sizeof(double *)); beta2 = (double ***)CALLOC(ns,sizeof(double **));
  L2 = (double ***)CALLOC(ns,sizeof(double **));U2 = (double ***)CALLOC(ns,sizeof(double **));
  l1c = (int *)CALLOC(ns,sizeof(int *));
  l2c = (int **)CALLOC(ns,sizeof(int **));
  A1 = (double **)CALLOC(ns,sizeof(double *));
  mu1 = (double **)CALLOC(ns,sizeof(double *));
  rsd1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *nn,sizeof(double *));
  IAdAIA = (double **)CALLOC(ns,sizeof(double *));ba1 = (double **)CALLOC(ns,sizeof(double *));
  L = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *pg * *pg,sizeof(double));
  Vl = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *pg * *pg,sizeof(double));
  V2 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *pg * *pg,sizeof(double));
  U = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *pg * *pg,sizeof(double));
  mu2 = (double *)CALLOC(*n,sizeof(double));
  for (dp1=L,dp2=L+ *pg * *pg,dp3=G;dp1<dp2;dp1++,dp3++) *dp1 = *dp3;
  work = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *pg * 2,sizeof(double)); iwork = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) *pg ,sizeof(int));

  mtrf(L,L,pg,pg,&ione,&zero,work,iwork);

  for (l=0;l<ns;l++) {
    srl = sr[l]; if (srl<0) srl = -srl;
    P[l] = (double *)CALLOC(srl * *pg,sizeof(double));
    beta2[l] = (double **)CALLOC(ns,sizeof(double *));
    L2[l] = (double **)CALLOC(ns,sizeof(double *));
    U2[l] = (double **)CALLOC(ns,sizeof(double *));
    l2c[l] = (int *)CALLOC(ns,sizeof(int *));
    mu1[l] = (double *)CALLOC(*n,sizeof(double));
    if (sr[l]>0) {
      F77_CALL(dgemm)(&ntrans,&ntrans,pg,sr+l,sr+l,&one,G+soff[l] * *pg,pg,S[l],sr+l,&zero,P[l],pg FCONE FCONE);
    } else {
      for (dp1=G+soff[l] * *pg,dp2=P[l],j=0;j<srl;j++) {
	xx = S[l][j];
	for (dp3=dp2 + *pg;dp2<dp3;dp2++,dp1++) *dp2 = *dp1 * xx;
      }
    }
    beta1[l] = db + l * *pg;
    xx = -sp[l];
    F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,pg,&srl,&xx,P[l],pg,beta+soff[l],&ione,&zero,beta1[l],&ione FCONE);
    ncs=0;xx = sp[l];
    Xbd(mu1[l],beta1[l],X,k,ks,m,p,n,nx,ts,dt,nt,v,qc,&ione,cs,&ncs,xbiwork,xbpwork,xbdwork);

    for (r=0;r<=l;r++) {

      if (sr[r]>sr[l]) i=sr[r]; else i=sr[l];
      for (dp1=V2,dp2=V2 + i*i;dp1<dp2;dp1++) *dp1 = 0.0;
      xx = sp[r]*sp[l];
      F77_CALL(dgemm)(&ntrans,&ntrans,sr+l,sr+r,sr+l,&xx,S[l],sr+l,P[r]+soff[l],pg,&zero,V2,&i FCONE FCONE);
      xx = 1e-14;
      if (l==r) q = 1; else q = 0;
      j = -1; mtrf(V2,U,&i,&j,&q,&xx,work,iwork);
      L2[l][r] = L2[r][l] = (double *)CALLOC(*pg * j,sizeof(double));

      F77_CALL(dgemm)(&ntrans,&ntrans,pg,&j,sr+l,&one,G+soff[l] * *pg,pg,V2,&i,&zero,L2[l][r],pg FCONE FCONE);
      dp0=L2[l][r];dp1=dp0+j * *pg;dp3=V2;
      if (!q) {
	U2[l][r] = U2[r][l] = (double *)CALLOC(*pg * j,sizeof(double));
	F77_CALL(dgemm)(&ntrans,&ntrans,pg,&j,sr+r,&one,G+soff[r] * *pg,pg,U,&i,&zero,U2[l][r],pg FCONE FCONE);
      }
      l2c[r][l]=l2c[l][r] = j;

      beta2[r][l] = beta2[l][r] =  (double *)CALLOC(*pg,sizeof(double));

      xx = -sp[l];
      F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,pg,&srl,&xx,P[l],pg,beta1[r]+soff[l],&ione,&zero,beta2[l][r],&ione FCONE);
      xx = -sp[r];
      q = sr[r]; if (q<0) q = -q;
      F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,pg,&q,&xx,P[r],pg,beta1[l]+soff[r],&ione,&one,beta2[l][r],&ione FCONE);
      if (l==r) for (bp=beta1[r],dp1=beta2[l][r],dp2=dp1 + *pg;dp1<dp2;bp++,dp1++) *dp1 += *bp;
    }

    xx = sp[l];
    for (dp1 = S[l],dp2= S[l] + sr[l]*sr[l],dp3=Vl;dp1<dp2;dp1++,dp3++) *dp3 = *dp1 *xx;
    j = -1; mtrf(Vl,Vl,sr+l,&j,&ione,&zero,work,iwork); l1c[l] = j;
    L1[l] = (double *)CALLOC(*pg * j,sizeof(double));
    F77_CALL(dgemm)(&ntrans,&ntrans,pg,&j,sr+l,&one,G+soff[l] * *pg,pg,Vl,sr+l,&zero,L1[l],pg FCONE FCONE);

  }

  *NCV=0.0;

  mk = (ptrdiff_t *)CALLOC(*nn,sizeof(ptrdiff_t));
  for (ii=kk = -1,i=0;i<*nn;i++) {
    q = ma[i]-kk; kk = ma[i];
    ii = mk[i] = ii + q*(q+1)/2;
  }

  for (M=nb=j=kk=i=0;i<*nn;i++) {
    dum = mk[i]-kk+1; kk = mk[i]+1; if (j<dum) j = dum;
    dum = (int)(sqrt(8*dum+1)-1)/2;
    if (j<dum) j = dum;
    nb += dum;
  }
  Aaa = (double *)CALLOC(ncvth*j*j,sizeof(double));
  IAaa = (double *)CALLOC(ncvth*j*j,sizeof(double));
  B = (double *)CALLOC(ncvth*j*j,sizeof(double));
  rp = (double *)CALLOC(j*ncvth,sizeof(double));
  wij = (double *)CALLOC(j*ncvth,sizeof(double));
  piv = (int *)CALLOC(j*ncvth,sizeof(int));
  ba2 = (double *)CALLOC(j*ncvth,sizeof(double));
  maxM=j;maxM2=j*j;

  target_nk = *n * 2;
  max_chunk = 0;
  for (j=1,ii=-1,i=0;i<*nn;i++) {
    if (mk[i]-ii>target_nk) {
      if (mk[i-1]!=ii&&i>0) {
	if (mk[i-1]-ii>max_chunk) max_chunk = mk[i-1]-ii;
	ii = mk[i-1]; j++;
      } else {
	if (mk[i]-ii>max_chunk) max_chunk = mk[i]-ii;
	ii=mk[i]; if (i < *nn-1) j++;
      }
    }
  }
  nchunk = j;
  if (nchunk==1) max_chunk = mk[*nn-1]+1;
  ichunk = (int *)CALLOC(nchunk,sizeof(int));
  max_nb=0;
  for (j=0,jj=ii=-1,i=0;i<*nn;i++) {
    if (mk[i]-ii>target_nk) {
      if (mk[i-1]!=ii&&i>0) {
	ii = mk[i-1];
	if (ma[i-1]-jj>max_nb) max_nb = ma[i-1]-jj;
	jj = ma[i-1];
	ichunk[j] = i-1; j++;
      } else {
	ii = mk[i];ichunk[j]=i; j++;
	if (ma[i]-jj>max_nb) max_nb = ma[i]-jj;
	jj = ma[i];
      }
    }
  }
  ichunk[j] = *nn-1;
  if (nchunk==1) max_nb = ma[*nn-1]+1;

  ba = (double *)CALLOC(max_nb,sizeof(double));

  for (l=0;l<ns;l++) {
    ba1[l] = (double *)CALLOC(max_nb,sizeof(double));
  }

  IA = (double *)CALLOC(max_chunk,sizeof(double));
  for (l=0;l<ns;l++) IAdAIA[l]  = (double *)CALLOC(max_chunk,sizeof(double));
  a1 = (int *)CALLOC(max_chunk,sizeof(int));a2 = (int *)CALLOC(max_chunk,sizeof(int));
  ind = (int *)CALLOC(max_chunk,sizeof(int));

  for (ch=0;ch<nchunk;ch++) {
    if (ch==0) {
      ch_start = kk = 0;
      nu = mk[ichunk[ch]]+1;
    } else {
      ch_start=ichunk[ch-1]+1;
      kk = ma[ch_start-1]+1;
      nu = mk[ichunk[ch]] - mk[ichunk[ch-1]] ;
    }

    for (k1p=a1,k2p=a2,i=ch_start;i<=ichunk[ch];i++) {
      M = ma[i]-kk+1;
      a1p = a + kk;
      for (j=0;j<M;j++,a1p++) for (a2p = a+kk,q=0;q<=j;q++,k1p++,k2p++,a2p++) {
	*k1p = *a1p;
	*k2p = *a2p;
      }
      kk = ma[i] + 1;
    }

    upair(a1,a2,ind,&nu);

    if (ch&&nu>nu_size) {
      nu_size = nu;
      for (l=0;l<ns;l++) A1[l] = (double *)REALLOC(A1[l],nu*sizeof(double));
      A = (double *)REALLOC(A,nu*sizeof(double));A2 = (double *)REALLOC(A2,nu*sizeof(double));
    } else {
      nu_size = nu;
      for (l=0;l<ns;l++) A1[l] = (double *)CALLOC(nu,sizeof(double));
      A = (double *)CALLOC(nu,sizeof(double));A2 = (double *)CALLOC(nu,sizeof(double));
    }

    j=1;
    diagXLLtXt(A,L,X,k,ks,m,p,n,nx,ts,dt,nt,v,qc,pg,pg,
	       a1,a2,&nu,nthreads);

    if (ch==0) kk0=0; else kk0 = mk[ichunk[ch-1]]+1;
    thread_lim(ch_start,ichunk[ch],thlim,ncvth);
#ifdef OPENMP_ON
#pragma omp parallel for private(i,j,q,tid,ii,jj,kk,bp,IAp,Aaap,i1,dum,wp,dp1,dp2,xx,p1,info,M) num_threads(ncvth)
#endif
    for (tid=0;tid<ncvth;tid++)
    for (i=thlim[tid];i<thlim[tid+1];i++) {

      if (i==0) bp=ba; else {
        if (ch_start==0) bp = ba + ma[i-1]+1; else bp = ba + ma[i-1]- ma[ch_start-1];
      }
      if (i==0) {
	ii=jj=kk=0;
	IAp = IA;
      } else {
        kk = mk[i-1]+1;
        ii = ma[i-1]+1;
        jj = md[i-1]+1;
	IAp = IA + kk - kk0;
      }
      M = (int) ma[i]-ii+1;

      for (p1 = ind+kk-kk0,Aaap = Aaa+maxM2*tid,j=0;j<M;j++,Aaap += M-j) for (q=0;q<=j;q++,Aaap++,p1++) *Aaap = -A[*p1];
      fill_lt(Aaa+maxM2*tid,M);
      for (wp=wij+maxM*tid,j=0;j<M;j++) wp[j] = w[a[ii+j]];
      for (Aaap=Aaa+maxM2*tid,wp=wij+maxM*tid,dp1=wp+M;wp<dp1;wp++) for (dp2=Aaap+M;Aaap<dp2;Aaap++) *Aaap *= *wp;
      for (Aaap=Aaa+maxM2*tid,j=0;j<M;j++,Aaap += M+1) *Aaap += 1;
      if (M==1) {
        *IAp = *(wij+maxM*tid) / *(Aaa+maxM2*tid);
        *bp = *IAp * rsd[a[ii]];
        IAp++;
      } else {

        F77_CALL(dgetrf)(&M,&M,Aaa+maxM2*tid,&M,piv+tid*maxM,&info);
        F77_CALL(dgetri)(&M,Aaa+maxM2*tid,&M,piv+tid*maxM,rp+tid*maxM,&M,&info);
	for (Aaap=Aaa+maxM2*tid,j=0;j<M;j++) for (wp=wij+tid*maxM,dp1=wp+M;wp<dp1;Aaap++,wp++) *Aaap *= *wp;

        for (dp1=rp+tid*maxM,j=0;j<M;j++,dp1++) *dp1 = rsd[a[ii+j]];
        F77_CALL(dsymv)(&uplo,&M,&one,Aaa+tid*maxM2,&M,rp+tid*maxM,&ione,&zero,bp,&ione FCONE);

        for (Aaap = Aaa+tid*maxM2,j=0;j<M;j++,Aaap += M-j) for (q=0;q<=j;q++,IAp++,Aaap++) *IAp = *Aaap;

      }

      q = md[i]-jj+1;
      for (dum=j=0;j<q;j++) {
        i1 = d[jj+j];
        while (i1 > a[ii+dum] && dum<M) dum++;
	xx = bp[dum];
        if (xx!=0.0) NCVth[tid] += xx*xx/w[i1];
	if (j==0) rsd1[i] = xx;
      }

    }

    for (l=0;l<ns;l++) {
      srl = sr[l]; if (srl<0) srl = -srl;

      j=1;diagXLLtXt(A1[l],L1[l],X,k,ks,m,p,n,nx,ts,dt,nt,v,qc,pg,l1c+l,
		     a1,a2,&nu,nthreads);
      for (dp1=A1[l],dp2=dp1+nu;dp1<dp2;dp1++) *dp1 = - *dp1;
      if (ch==0) kk0=0; else kk0= mk[ichunk[ch-1]]+1;

#ifdef OPENMP_ON
#pragma omp parallel for private(i,j,q,tid,ii,jj,kk,bp,ba1p,IAp,Ap,Aaap,IAdAIAp,i1,dum,dp1,dp2,xx,p1,M,side) num_threads(ncvth)
#endif
       for (tid=0;tid<ncvth;tid++)
       for (i=thlim[tid];i<thlim[tid+1];i++) {
	if (i==0) {
	  bp=ba;ba1p=ba1[l];
	} else {
          if (ch_start==0) {
	    bp = ba + ma[i-1]+1;
	    ba1p = ba1[l] + ma[i-1]+1;
	  } else {
	    bp = ba + ma[i-1]- ma[ch_start-1];
	    ba1p = ba1[l] + ma[i-1] - ma[ch_start-1];
	  }
        }
        if (i==0) {
	  ii=jj=kk=0;
	  IAp = IA;IAdAIAp = IAdAIA[l];
        } else {
          kk = mk[i-1]+1;
          ii = ma[i-1]+1;
          jj = md[i-1]+1;
	  IAp = IA + kk - kk0;
	  IAdAIAp = IAdAIA[l] + kk - kk0;
        }

	M = (int) ma[i]-ii+1;

        for (p1 = ind+kk-kk0,Ap=A1[l],Aaap = Aaa+tid*maxM2,j=0;j<M;j++,Aaap += M-j) for (q=0;q<=j;q++,Aaap++,p1++) *Aaap = Ap[*p1];
        fill_lt(Aaa+tid*maxM2,M);

	for (Aaap = IAaa+tid*maxM2,j=0;j<M;j++,Aaap += M-j) for (q=0;q<=j;q++,IAp++,Aaap++) *Aaap = *IAp;
        side = 'L';
        F77_CALL(dsymm)(&side,&uplo,&M,&M,&one,IAaa+tid*maxM2,&M,Aaa+tid*maxM2,&M,&zero,B+tid*maxM2,&M FCONE FCONE);
        side = 'R';
        F77_CALL(dsymm)(&side,&uplo,&M,&M,&one,IAaa+tid*maxM2,&M,B+tid*maxM2,&M,&zero,Aaa+tid*maxM2,&M FCONE FCONE);
        for (dp2=rp+tid*maxM,dp1=mu1[l],j=0;j<M;j++,dp2++) *dp2 = -dp1[a[ii+j]];
        F77_CALL(dsymv)(&uplo,&M,&one,IAaa+tid*maxM2,&M,rp+tid*maxM,&ione,&zero,ba1p,&ione FCONE);
        F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,&M,&M,&one,B+tid*maxM2,&M,bp,&ione,&one,ba1p,&ione FCONE);

        for (Aaap = Aaa+tid*maxM2,j=0;j<M;j++,Aaap += M-j) for (q=0;q<=j;q++,IAdAIAp++,Aaap++) {
          *IAdAIAp = *Aaap;
        }

        q = md[i]-jj+1;
        for (dum=j=0;j<q;j++) {
          i1 = d[jj+j];
          while (i1 > a[ii+dum] && dum<M) dum++;
	  xx = ba1p[dum] * bp[dum];
          if (xx!=0.0) NCV1th[tid*ns+l] += 2*xx/w[i1];
        }

      }

      for (r=0;r<=l;r++) {
        ncs=0;
        Xbd(mu2,beta2[l][r],X,k,ks,m,p,n,nx,ts,dt,nt,v,qc,&ione,cs,&ncs,xbiwork,xbpwork,xbdwork);

      	if (l==r) {
	  diagXLLtXt(A2,L2[l][r],X,k,ks,m,p,n,nx,ts,dt,nt,v,qc,
			  pg,l2c[l]+r,a1,a2,&nu,nthreads);
	  for (dp1=A1[l],dp2=dp1+nu,dp3=A2;dp1<dp2;dp1++,dp3++) *dp3 += *dp3 + *dp1;
        } else diagXLUtXt(A2,L2[l][r],U2[l][r],X,k,ks,m,p,n,nx,ts,dt,nt,v,qc,
			  pg,l2c[l]+r,a1,a2,&nu,nthreads);

        if (ch==0) kk0=0; else kk0= mk[ichunk[ch-1]]+1;
#ifdef OPENMP_ON
#pragma omp parallel for private(i,j,q,tid,ii,jj,kk,bp,ba1p,ba2p,IAp,Alp,Arp,Aaap,IAdAIAp,IAdAIArp,i1,dum,dp1,dp2,xx,p1,M) num_threads(ncvth)
#endif
	for (tid=0;tid<ncvth;tid++)
        for (i=thlim[tid];i<thlim[tid+1];i++) {
	  if (i==0) {
	    bp=ba;ba1p=ba1[l];ba2p=ba1[r];
	  } else {
            if (ch_start==0) {
	      bp = ba + ma[i-1]+1;
	      ba1p = ba1[l] + ma[i-1]+1;
	      ba2p = ba1[r] + ma[i-1]+1;
	    } else {
	      bp = ba + ma[i-1]- ma[ch_start-1];
	      ba1p = ba1[l] + ma[i-1] - ma[ch_start-1];
	      ba2p = ba1[r] + ma[i-1] - ma[ch_start-1];
	    }
          }
          if (i==0) {
	    ii=jj=kk=0;
	    IAp = IA;IAdAIAp = IAdAIA[l];IAdAIArp = IAdAIA[r];
          } else {
            kk = mk[i-1]+1;
            ii = ma[i-1]+1;
            jj = md[i-1]+1;
	    IAp = IA + kk - kk0;
	    IAdAIAp = IAdAIA[l] + kk - kk0;
	    IAdAIArp = IAdAIA[r] + kk - kk0;
          }

	  M = (int) ma[i]-ii+1;

          for (Aaap = IAaa+tid*maxM2,j=0;j<M;j++,Aaap += M-j) for (q=0;q<=j;q++,IAp++,Aaap++) *Aaap =  *IAp;
          for (p1 = ind+kk-kk0,Aaap = Aaa+tid*maxM2,j=0;j<M;j++,Aaap += M-j) for (q=0;q<=j;q++,Aaap++,p1++) *Aaap = A2[*p1];

	  F77_CALL(dsymv)(&uplo,&M,&one,Aaa+tid*maxM2,&M,bp,&ione,&zero,B+tid*maxM2,&ione FCONE);
	  F77_CALL(dsymv)(&uplo,&M,&one,IAaa+tid*maxM2,&M,B+tid*maxM2,&ione,&zero,ba2+tid*maxM,&ione FCONE);
	  for (dp2=rp+tid*maxM,dp1=mu2,j=0;j<M;j++,dp2++) *dp2 = -dp1[a[ii+j]];
	  F77_CALL(dsymv)(&uplo,&M,&one,IAaa+tid*maxM2,&M,rp+tid*maxM,&ione,&one,ba2+tid*maxM,&ione FCONE);

	  for (p1 = ind+kk-kk0,Alp=A1[l],Aaap = Aaa+tid*maxM2,j=0;j<M;j++,Aaap += M-j) for (q=0;q<=j;q++,Aaap++,p1++) *Aaap = Alp[*p1];
          F77_CALL(dsymv)(&uplo,&M,&one,Aaa+tid*maxM2,&M,bp,&ione,&zero,B+tid*maxM2,&ione FCONE);

	  for (Aaap = Aaa+tid*maxM2,j=0;j<M;j++,Aaap += M-j) for (q=0;q<=j;q++,IAdAIArp++,Aaap++) *Aaap =  *IAdAIArp;
          F77_CALL(dsymv)(&uplo,&M,&one,Aaa+tid*maxM2,&M,B+tid*maxM2,&ione,&one,ba2+tid*maxM,&ione FCONE);

	  for (dp2=rp+tid*maxM,dp1=mu1[l],j=0;j<M;j++,dp2++) *dp2 = -dp1[a[ii+j]];
	  F77_CALL(dsymv)(&uplo,&M,&one,Aaa+tid*maxM2,&M,rp+tid*maxM,&ione,&one,ba2+tid*maxM,&ione FCONE);

	  for (p1 = ind+kk-kk0,Arp=A1[r],Aaap = Aaa+tid*maxM2,j=0;j<M;j++,Aaap += M-j) for (q=0;q<=j;q++,Aaap++,p1++) *Aaap = Arp[*p1];
	  F77_CALL(dsymv)(&uplo,&M,&one,Aaa+tid*maxM2,&M,bp,&ione,&zero,B+tid*maxM2,&ione FCONE);

	  for (Aaap = Aaa+tid*maxM2,j=0;j<M;j++,Aaap += M-j) for (q=0;q<=j;q++,IAdAIAp++,Aaap++) *Aaap =  *IAdAIAp;
          F77_CALL(dsymv)(&uplo,&M,&one,Aaa+tid*maxM2,&M,B+tid*maxM2,&ione,&one,ba2+tid*maxM,&ione FCONE);

	  for (dp2=rp+tid*maxM,dp1=mu1[r],j=0;j<M;j++,dp2++) *dp2 = -dp1[a[ii+j]];
	  F77_CALL(dsymv)(&uplo,&M,&one,Aaa+tid*maxM2,&M,rp+tid*maxM,&ione,&one,ba2+tid*maxM,&ione FCONE);

	  q = md[i]-jj+1;
          for (dum=j=0;j<q;j++) {
            i1 = d[jj+j];
            while (i1 > a[ii+dum]&&dum<M) dum++;
	    xx = ba1p[dum] * ba2p[dum] + bp[dum]*ba2[dum+tid*maxM];
            if (xx!=0.0) NCV2th[tid*ns*ns+l+r*ns] += 2 * xx/w[i1];
          }

        }
      }
    }
  }
  if (ncvth>1) {
    for (i=0;i<ncvth;i++) {
      *NCV += NCVth[i];
      for (j=0;j<ns;j++) NCV1[j] += NCV1th[i*ns+j];
      for (j=0;j<ns;j++) for (q=0;q<=j;q++) NCV2[j+q*ns] += NCV2th[i*ns*ns+j+q*ns];
    }
  }
  for (j=0;j<ns;j++) for (q=0;q<=j;q++) NCV2[q+j*ns]=NCV2[j+q*ns];
  for (j=0;j<*nn;j++) rsd[j] = rsd1[j];

  for (l=0;l<ns;l++) {
    for (r=0;r<=l;r++) {
      FREE(beta2[l][r]);FREE(L2[l][r]);if (r<l) FREE(U2[l][r]);
    }
    FREE(beta2[l]);FREE(L2[l]);FREE(U2[l]);
    FREE(P[l]);FREE(L1[l]);FREE(A1[l]);FREE(ba1[l]);
    FREE(mu1[l]);FREE(IAdAIA[l]);
    FREE(l2c[l]);
  }
  FREE(l1c);FREE(l2c);FREE(L);FREE(V2);FREE(U);FREE(Vl);FREE(P);FREE(beta1);FREE(L1);FREE(A1);
  FREE(ba1);FREE(mu1);FREE(rsd1);FREE(IAdAIA);FREE(piv);FREE(work);FREE(iwork);
  FREE(B);FREE(cs);FREE(mk);FREE(A);FREE(Aaa);FREE(IAaa);FREE(IA);FREE(ba);FREE(rp);
  FREE(beta2);FREE(L2);FREE(U2);FREE(mu2);FREE(wij);FREE(A2);FREE(ba2);FREE(ind);FREE(a1);FREE(a2);FREE(thlim);
  if (ncvth>1) {
    FREE(NCV1th);FREE(NCV2th);
    FREE(NCVth);
  }
  FREE(xbiwork);FREE(xbpwork);FREE(xbdwork);
}

# --- End of mgcv/src/discrete.c ---

# --- Start of mgcv/src/gdi.c ---

#include <stdlib.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <math.h>

#ifdef _OPENMP
#include <omp.h>
#endif

#include <R.h>
#include "mgcv.h"

#define ANSI

double trBtAB(double *A,double *B,int *n,int*m)

{ double tr=0.0,x,*p,*p1,*p2;
  int j,k;
  for (j=0;j<*m;j++)
    for (k=0;k<*n;k++) {
      p = A + *n * k;p2 = p + *n;
      p1 = B + *n * j;
      x = B[k + j * *n];
      for (;p<p2;p++,p1++) tr += *p * *p1 * x;
    }
  return(tr);
}

void multSk(double *y,double *x,int *xcol,int k,double *rS,int *rSncol,int *q,double *work)

{ int i,off,nc,bt,ct;
  double *rSk;
  off=0;
  for (i=0;i<k;i++) off += *q * rSncol[i];
  rSk = rS + off;
  nc = rSncol[k];
  bt=1;ct=0;
  mgcv_mmult(work,rSk,x,&bt,&ct,&nc,xcol,q);
  bt=0;
  mgcv_mmult(y,rSk,work,&bt,&ct,q,xcol,&nc);
}

double diagABt(double *d,double *A,double *B,int *r,int *c)

{ int j;
  double tr=0.0,*pa,*pb,*p1,*pd;
  if (*c>0) {
    for (pa=A,pb=B,p1=pa + *r,pd=d;pa<p1;pa++,pb++,pd++) *pd = *pa * *pb;
    for (j=1;j < *c;j++)
    for (p1=d + *r,pd=d;pd<p1;pa++,pb++,pd++) *pd += *pa * *pb;

    for (pd=d,p1=d + *r;pd < p1;pd++) tr += *pd;
  }
  return(tr);
}

double trAB(double *A,double *B,int *n, int *m)

{ double *p,*pa,*pb,tr=0.0;
  int i,j;
  for (pa=A,pb=B,j=0;j<*m;j++,pb++)
    for (p=pb,i=0;i<*n;i++,p += *m,pa++) tr+= *p * *pa;
  return(tr);
}

void get_bSb(double *bSb,double *bSb1, double *bSb2,double *sp,double *E,
             double *rS,int *rSncol,int *Enrow, int *q,int *M,int *M0,
             double *beta, double *b1, double *b2,int *deriv)

{ double *Sb,*Skb,*work,*work1,*p1,*p0,*p2,xx;
  int i,j,bt,ct,one=1,m,k,rSoff,mk,km,Mtot;
  j = *q; for (i=0;i<*M;i++) if (rSncol[i]>j) j = rSncol[i];
  j += *M0;

  work = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)j,sizeof(double));
  Sb = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*q,sizeof(double));
  bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(work,E,beta,&bt,&ct,Enrow,&one,q);
  bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(Sb,E,work,&bt,&ct,q,&one,Enrow);

  for (*bSb=0.0,i=0;i<*q;i++) *bSb += beta[i] * Sb[i];

  if (*deriv <=0) {FREE(work);FREE(Sb);return;}

  work1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)j,sizeof(double));
  Skb = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*M * *q,sizeof(double));
  for (p1=Skb,rSoff=0,i=0;i<*M;i++) {

     bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(work,rS + rSoff ,beta,&bt,&ct,rSncol+i,&one,q);
     for (j=0;j<rSncol[i];j++) work[j] *= sp[i];
     bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(p1,rS + rSoff ,work,&bt,&ct,q,&one,rSncol+i);
     rSoff += *q * rSncol[i];

     for (xx=0.0,j=0;j<*q;j++,p1++) xx += beta[j] * *p1;
     bSb1[i + *M0] = xx;
  }
  for (i=0;i<*M0;i++) bSb1[i] = 0.0;
  Mtot = *M + *M0;

  if (*deriv>1)  for (m=0;m < Mtot;m++) {
     bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(work1,E,b1 + m * *q,&bt,&ct,Enrow,&one,q);
     bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(work,E,work1,&bt,&ct,q,&one,Enrow);

    for (k=m;k < Mtot;k++) {
      km= k * Mtot + m ;
      mk= m * Mtot + k ;

      for (xx=0.0,p0=Sb,p1=Sb + *q;p0<p1;p0++,b2++) xx += *b2 * *p0;
      bSb2[km] = 2*xx;

      for (xx=0.0,p0=b1+ k  * *q,p1=p0 + *q,p2=work;p0<p1;p0++,p2++) xx += *p2 * *p0;
      bSb2[km] += 2*xx;

      if (k >= *M0) {
        for (xx=0.0,p0=Skb + (k- *M0) * *q,p1=p0 + *q,p2= b1+ m  * *q;p0<p1;p0++,p2++)
             xx += *p2 * *p0;
        bSb2[km] += 2*xx;
      }

      if (m >= *M0) {
        for (xx=0.0,p0=Skb + (m - *M0) * *q,p1=p0 + *q,p2= b1 + k  * *q;p0<p1;p0++,p2++)
             xx += *p2 * *p0;
        bSb2[km] += 2*xx;
      }

      if (k==m) bSb2[km] += bSb1[k]; else bSb2[mk] = bSb2[km];
    }
  }

  bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(work,b1,Sb,&bt,&ct,&Mtot,&one,q);
  for (i=0;i<Mtot;i++) bSb1[i] += 2*work[i];

  FREE(Sb);FREE(work);FREE(Skb);FREE(work1);

}

double frobenius_norm(double *X,int *r, int *c)

{ double fnorm=0.0,*p1;
  int n;
  n = *r * *c;
  for (p1=X+n;X<p1;X++) fnorm += *X * *X;
  return(sqrt(fnorm));
}

void pivoter(double *x,int *r,int *c,int *pivot, int *col, int *reverse)

{ double *dum,*px,*pd,*pd1,*p,*p1;
  int *pi,*pi1,i,j;
  if (*col) {
     dum = (double *) CALLOC((size_t)*c,sizeof(double));
     if (*reverse)
       for (i=0;i< *r;i++) {
	 for (px=x+i,pi=pivot,pi1=pi+*c;pi<pi1;pi++,px+=*r) dum[*pi]= *px;
         for (px=x+i,pd=dum,pd1=dum+*c;pd<pd1;pd++,px += *r) *px = *pd;
       } else
       for (i=0;i< *r;i++) {
	 for (px = x+i,pd=dum,pd1=dum + *c,j=0;pd < pd1;pd++,j++)  *pd = px[pivot[j] * *r];
	 for (px=x+i,pd=dum,pd1=dum+*c;pd<pd1;pd++,px += *r) *px = *pd;
     }
  } else {
    dum = (double *) CALLOC((size_t)*r,sizeof(double));
    if (*reverse)
    for (p=x,j=0;j<*c;j++,p += *r) {
      for (pi=pivot,pi1=pi+*r,p1=p;pi<pi1;pi++,p1++) dum[*pi] = *p1;
      for (pd=dum,pd1=dum+*r,p1=p;pd<pd1;pd++,p1++) *p1 = *pd;

    } else
      for (p=x,j=0;j<*c;j++,p += *r) {
	for (pi=pivot,pi1=pi+*r,pd=dum;pi<pi1;pd++,pi++) *pd = p[*pi];
        for (pd=dum,pd1=dum+*r,p1=p;pd<pd1;pd++,p1++) *p1 = *pd;
    }
  }
  FREE(dum);
}

double qr_ldet_inv(double *X,int *r,double *Xi,int *get_inv)

{ double *tau,ldet,*p,*Qt;
  int *pivot,i,True=1,j,False=0;

  pivot = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)*r,sizeof(int));
  tau = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*r,sizeof(double));

  mgcv_qr(X,r,r,pivot,tau);

  for (ldet=0.0,p=X,i=0;i<*r;i++,p += *r+1) ldet += log(fabs(*p));

  if (*get_inv) {

    Qt = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*r * *r,sizeof(double));
    for (p=Qt,i=0;i<*r;i++,p += *r+1) *p = 1.0;
    mgcv_qrqy(Qt,X,tau,r,r,r,&True,&True);

    mgcv_backsolve(X,r,r,Qt,Xi,r,&False);

    for (p=Xi,j=0;j<*r;j++,p += *r) {

      for (i=0;i<*r;i++) tau[pivot[i]] = p[i];
      for (i=0;i<*r;i++) p[i] = tau[i];

    }
    FREE(Qt);
  }
  FREE(pivot);FREE(tau);
  return(ldet);
}

void get_detS2(double *sp,double *sqrtS, int *rSncol, int *q,int *M, int * deriv,
               double *det, double *det1, double *det2, double *d_tol,
               double *r_tol,int *fixed_penalty)

{ double *R,*work,*tau,*rS1,*rS2, *S,*Si,*Sb,*B,*Sg,*p,*p1,*p2,*p3,*p4,*frob,max_frob,x,*spf,Rcond;
  int *pivot,iter,i,j,k,bt,ct,rSoff,K,Q,Qr,*gamma,*gamma1,*alpha,r,max_col,Mf,tot_col=0,left,tp;

  if (*fixed_penalty) {
    Mf = *M + 1;
    spf = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)Mf,sizeof(double));
    for (i=0;i<*M;i++) spf[i]=sp[i];
    spf[*M]=1.0;
  }
  else {spf=sp;Mf = *M;}

  if (*deriv) {
    for (j=i=0;i<Mf;i++) j += rSncol[i];
    tot_col=j;
    j *= *q;
    rS1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) j,sizeof(double));
    rS2 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) j,sizeof(double));
    for (p=rS1,p3=rS2,p1=rS1+j,p2=sqrtS;p<p1;p++,p2++,p3++) *p3 = *p = *p2;
  } else {rS1=rS2=NULL;}

  max_col = *q;
  for (i=0;i<Mf;i++) if (rSncol[i]>max_col) max_col=rSncol[i];

  p = Si = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*q * max_col * Mf,sizeof(double));

  for (rSoff=i=0;i<Mf;p+= *q * *q,rSoff+=rSncol[i],i++) {
    bt=0;ct=1;mgcv_mmult(p,sqrtS+rSoff * *q,sqrtS+rSoff * *q,&bt,&ct,q,q,rSncol+i);
  }

  K = 0;Q = *q;
  frob =  (double *)CALLOC((size_t)Mf,sizeof(double));
  gamma = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)Mf,sizeof(int));
  gamma1 = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)Mf,sizeof(int));
  alpha = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)Mf,sizeof(int));
  for (i=0;i<Mf;i++) gamma[i] = 1;

  S = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) Q * Q,sizeof(double));
  Sb = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) Q * Q,sizeof(double));
  pivot = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) Q,sizeof(int));
  tau = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) Q,sizeof(double));
  work = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(4 * Q),sizeof(double));

  Sg = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) Q * Q,sizeof(double));
  B = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) Q * max_col,sizeof(double));
  R = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) Q * Q,sizeof(double));

  iter =0;
  while(1) {
    iter ++;

    max_frob=0.0;
    for (p=Si,i=0;i<Mf;i++,p += *q * Q)
      if (gamma[i]) {
        frob[i] = frobenius_norm(p,q,&Q);
        if (frob[i] *spf[i] >max_frob) max_frob=frob[i]  * spf[i];
    }

    for (i=0;i<Mf;i++) {
      if (gamma[i]) {
        if (frob[i]  * spf[i] > max_frob * *d_tol) {
          alpha[i] = 1;gamma1[i] = 0;
        } else {
          alpha[i] = 0;gamma1[i] = 1;
        }
      } else {
        alpha[i] = gamma1[i] = 0;
      }
    }

    for (p=Sb,p1=p + *q * Q;p<p1;p++) *p=0.0;
    for (p=Si,i=0;i<Mf;i++,p += *q * Q) if (alpha[i]) {
      x = frob[i];
      for (p1=p,p2=Sb,p3=p + *q * Q;p1<p3;p1++,p2++) *p2 += *p1 / x;
    }
    for (i=0;i<*q;i++) pivot[i]=0;
    mgcv_qr(Sb, &Q, q ,pivot,tau);

    r = Q;
    R_cond(Sb,&Q,&r,work,&Rcond);
    while (*r_tol * Rcond > 1) { r--;R_cond(Sb,&Q,&r,work,&Rcond);}
    Qr = Q-r;

    if (Q==r) {
      if (iter==1 ) {
        for (p=Si,i=0;i<Mf;i++,p += Q*Q) {
          x = spf[i];
          for (p1=p,p2=S,p3=p+Q*Q;p1<p3;p1++,p2++) *p2 += *p1 * x;
        }
        break;
      } else break;
    }

    for (p=Sb,p1=p + *q * Q;p<p1;p++) *p = 0.0;
    for (p=Si,i=0;i<Mf;i++,p += *q * Q) if (alpha[i]) {
      x = spf[i];
      for (p1=p,p2=Sb,p3=p+ *q * Q;p1<p3;p1++,p2++) *p2 += *p1 * x;
    }
    for (i=0;i<*q;i++) pivot[i]=0;
    mgcv_qr(Sb, &Q, q ,pivot,tau);

    for (p=R,p1=R + *q * r;p<p1;p++) *p=0.0;
    for (i=0;i<r;i++) for (j=i;j<*q;j++) R[i + pivot[j] * r] = Sb[i + j * Q];

    for (p=Sg,p1=p + *q * Q;p<p1;p++) *p=0.0;
    for (p=Si,i=0;i<Mf;i++,p += *q * Q) if (gamma1[i]) {
      x = spf[i];
      for (p1=p,p2=Sg,p3=p+ *q * Q;p1<p3;p1++,p2++) *p2 += *p1 * x;
    }

    left=1;tp=1;
    mgcv_qrqy(Sg,Sb,tau,&Q,q,&Q,&left,&tp);

    for (i=0;i<Q;i++) for (j=0;j<*q;j++) S[i+K + j * *q] = Sg[i + j * Q];

    for (i=0;i<r;i++) for (j=0;j<*q;j++) S[i+K + j * *q] += R[i + j * r];

    for (p1=p=Si,i=0;i<Mf;i++,p += *q * Q,p1 += *q *Qr) if (gamma1[i]) {
      left=1;tp=1;
      mgcv_qrqy(p,Sb,tau,&Q,q,&Q,&left,&tp);
      p2=p+r;p3=p1;
      for (j=0;j<*q;j++,p2+=r) for (k=0;k<Qr;k++,p3++,p2++) *p3 = *p2;
    }

    if (*deriv) {

      for (i=0;i<Q;i++) for (j=0;j<tot_col;j++) rS2[i+Q*j] = rS1[K + i + *q * j];

      left=1;tp=1;
      mgcv_qrqy(rS2,Sb,tau,&Q,&tot_col,&Q,&left,&tp);

      for (i=0;i<Q;i++) for (j=0;j<tot_col;j++) rS1[K + i + *q * j] = rS2[i+Q*j];

      for (p=rS1,k=0;k<Mf;p +=rSncol[k] * *q, k++) if (alpha[k]) {
        for (i=K+r;i<*q;i++) for (j=0;j<rSncol[k];j++) p[i + j * *q] = 0.0;
      }
    }

    K = K + r; Q = Qr;
    for (i=0;i<Mf;i++) gamma[i] = gamma1[i];
  }

  for (i=0;i<*q;i++) for (j=0;j<*q;j++) R[i + *q * j] = S[j + *q * i];

  *det = qr_ldet_inv(R,q,B,deriv);

  if (*deriv) {

    bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(Si,B,sqrtS,&bt,&ct,q,&tot_col,q);

    for (p=Si,p1=rS1,i=0;i<*M;p += *q *rSncol[i],p1+= *q *rSncol[i],i++) {
      for (x=0.0,p2=p1,p3=p,p4=p1+ *q*rSncol[i];p2<p4;p2++,p3++) x += *p2 * *p3;
      det1[i] = x*sp[i];
    }
  }

  if (*deriv==2) {
    for (p=Si,p1=rS2,p2 = p1 + *q * tot_col;p1<p2;p1++,p++) *p1 = *p;

    for (p1=Si,p=rS2,p2=rS1,i=0;i<*M;p2+= *q * rSncol[i], p += *q *rSncol[i],i++,p1 += *q * *q) {
      bt=0;ct=1;mgcv_mmult(p1,p,p2,&bt,&ct,q,q,rSncol+i);
    }

    for (i=0;i<*M;i++) for (j=i;j<*M;j++)
      det2[i + *M * j] = det2[j + *M * i] = -sp[i]*sp[j]*trAB(Si + *q * *q *i,Si + *q * *q *j,q,q);
    for (i=0;i<*M;i++) det2[i + *M * i] += det1[i];
  }
  FREE(R);
  FREE(work);
  FREE(frob);
  FREE(gamma);
  FREE(gamma1);
  FREE(alpha);
  FREE(S);
  FREE(Sb);
  FREE(Sg);
  if (*deriv) { FREE(rS1);FREE(rS2);}
  if (*fixed_penalty) {FREE(spf);}
  FREE(Si);
  FREE(B);
  FREE(pivot);FREE(tau);
}

void get_stableS(double *S,double *Qf,double *sp,double *sqrtS, int *rSncol, int *q,int *M, int * deriv,
               double *det, double *det1, double *det2, double *d_tol,
               double *r_tol,int *fixed_penalty)

{ double *rS, *Un, *U, *Si,*Sb,*B,*C,*Sg,*p,*p1,*p2,*p3,*frob,*ev,max_frob,x,*spf;
  int iter,i,j,k,bt,ct,rSoff,K,Q,Qr,*gamma,*gamma1,*alpha,True=1,False=0,r,max_col,Mf,n_gamma1;

  if (*fixed_penalty) {
    Mf = *M + 1;
    spf = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)Mf,sizeof(double));
    for (i=0;i<*M;i++) spf[i]=sp[i];
    spf[*M]=1.0;
  }
  else {spf=sp;Mf = *M;}

  rS = sqrtS;

  p = Si = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*q * *q * Mf,sizeof(double));
  max_col = *q;
  for (rSoff=i=0;i<Mf;p+= *q * *q,rSoff+=rSncol[i],i++) {
    bt=0;ct=1;mgcv_mmult(p,sqrtS+rSoff * *q,sqrtS+rSoff * *q,&bt,&ct,q,q,rSncol+i);
    if (rSncol[i]>max_col) max_col=rSncol[i];
  }

  K = 0;
  Q = *q;
  frob =  (double *)CALLOC((size_t)Mf,sizeof(double));
  gamma = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)Mf,sizeof(int));
  gamma1 = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)Mf,sizeof(int));
  alpha = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)Mf,sizeof(int));
  for (i=0;i<Mf;i++) gamma[i] = 1;

  U=Sb = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) Q * Q,sizeof(double));

  Sg = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) Q * Q,sizeof(double));
  ev = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) Q,sizeof(double));
  B = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) Q * max_col,sizeof(double));
  C = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) Q * max_col,sizeof(double));

  iter =0;
  while(1) {
    iter ++;

    max_frob=0.0;
    for (p=Si,i=0;i<Mf;i++,p += Q * Q)
      if (gamma[i]) {
        frob[i] = frobenius_norm(p,&Q,&Q);
        if (frob[i] *spf[i] >max_frob) max_frob=frob[i]  * spf[i];
    }

    n_gamma1=0;
    for (i=0;i<Mf;i++) {
      if (gamma[i]) {
        if (frob[i]  * spf[i] > max_frob * *d_tol) {
          alpha[i] = 1;gamma1[i] = 0;
        } else {
          alpha[i] = 0;gamma1[i] = 1; n_gamma1++;
        }
      } else {
        alpha[i] = gamma1[i] = 0;
      }
    }

    if (n_gamma1) {
      for (p=Sb,p1=p+Q*Q;p<p1;p++) *p=0.0;
      for (p=Si,i=0;i<Mf;i++,p += Q*Q) if (alpha[i]) {
        x = frob[i];
        for (p1=p,p2=Sb,p3=p+Q*Q;p1<p3;p1++,p2++) *p2 += *p1 / x;
      }
      mgcv_symeig(Sb,ev,&Q,&False,&False,&False);

      r=1;
      while(r<Q&&(ev[Q-r-1]>ev[Q-1] * *r_tol)) r++;
    } else {
      r=Q;
    }

    if (Q==r) {
      if (iter==1 ) {
        for (p=Si,i=0;i<Mf;i++,p += Q*Q) {
          x = spf[i];
          for (p1=p,p2=S,p3=p+Q*Q;p1<p3;p1++,p2++) *p2 += *p1 * x;
        }

        for (p=Qf,p1=p+Q*Q;p<p1;p++) *p=0.0;
        for (p=Qf,i=0;i<Q;i++,p+=Q+1) *p=1.0;
        break;
      } else break;
    }

    for (p=Sb,p1=p+Q*Q;p<p1;p++) *p = 0.0;
    for (p=Si,i=0;i<Mf;i++,p += Q*Q) if (alpha[i]) {
      x = spf[i];
      for (p1=p,p2=Sb,p3=p+Q*Q;p1<p3;p1++,p2++) *p2 += *p1 * x;
    }

    mgcv_symeig(Sb,ev,&Q,&False,&True,&True);

    if (iter==1) for (p=U,p1=Qf,p2 = p+Q*Q;p<p2;p++,p1++) *p1 = *p;
    else {
      bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(B,Qf+K * *q,U,&bt,&ct,q,&Q,&Q);
      for (p=Qf + K * *q,p1=Qf + *q * *q,p2=B;p<p1;p++,p2++) *p = *p2;
    }

    for (p=Sg,p1=p+Q*Q;p<p1;p++) *p=0.0;
    for (p=Si,i=0;i<Mf;i++,p += Q*Q) if (gamma1[i]) {
      x = spf[i];
      for (p1=p,p2=Sg,p3=p+Q*Q;p1<p3;p1++,p2++) *p2 += *p1 * x;
    }

    if (K>0) {

      for (j=0;j<Q;j++) for (i=0;i<K;i++) C[i + K * j] = S[i + *q * (j+K)];

      bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(B,C,U,&bt,&ct,&K,&Q,&Q);

      for (j=0;j<Q;j++) for (i=0;i<K;i++) S[i + *q * (j+K)]= S[j + K + *q * i] = B[i + K * j];
    }

    bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(B,Sg,U,&bt,&ct,&Q,&Q,&Q);
    bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(C,U,B,&bt,&ct,&Q,&Q,&Q);

    for (i=0;i<r;i++) C[i+i * Q] += ev[i];

    for (j=0;j<Q;j++) for (i=0;i<Q;i++) S[i + K + *q * (j+K)] = C[i + Q * j];

     for (p=rS,k=0;k<*M;p += rSncol[k] * *q,k++) if (alpha[k]) {

        for (i=0;i<Q;i++) for (j=0;j<rSncol[k];j++) C[i + Q * j] = p[i + K + *q * j];
        bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(B,U,C,&bt,&ct,&r,rSncol+k,&Q);
        for (i=0;i<r;i++) for (j=0;j<rSncol[k];j++) p[i + K + *q * j] = B[i + r * j];
        for (i=K+r;i<K+Q;i++) for (j=0;j<rSncol[k];j++) p[i + *q * j] = 0.0;
      } else if (gamma1[k]) {
        for (i=0;i<Q;i++) for (j=0;j<rSncol[k];j++) C[i + Q * j] = p[i + K + *q * j];

        bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(B,U,C,&bt,&ct,&Q,rSncol+k,&Q);
        for (i=0;i<Q;i++) for (j=0;j<rSncol[k];j++) p[i + K + *q * j] = B[i + Q * j];
      }

    Qr = Q - r;Un = U + r * Q;

    for (p1=p=Si,i=0;i<Mf;i++,p += Q*Q,p1 +=Qr*Qr) if (gamma1[i]) {
      bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(B,Un,p,&bt,&ct,&Qr,&Q,&Q);
      bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(p1,B,Un,&bt,&ct,&Qr,&Qr,&Q);
    }

    K = K + r; Q = Qr;
    for (i=0;i<Mf;i++) gamma[i] = gamma1[i];
  }

  for (p=S,p1=S + *q * *q,p2=C;p<p1;p++,p2++) *p2 = *p;
  *det = qr_ldet_inv(C,q,B,deriv);

  if (*deriv) {
   for (p=rS,i=0;i<*M;p += *q *rSncol[i],i++) det1[i] = trBtAB(B,p,q,rSncol+i)*sp[i];
  }

  if (*deriv==2) {
    for (p1=Si,p=rS,i=0;i<*M;p += *q *rSncol[i],i++,p1 += *q * *q) {
      bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(C,B,p,&bt,&ct,q,rSncol+i,q);
      bt=0;ct=1;mgcv_mmult(p1,C,p,&bt,&ct,q,q,rSncol+i);
    }
    for (i=0;i<*M;i++) for (j=i;j<*M;j++)
      det2[i + *M * j] = det2[j + *M * i] = -sp[i]*sp[j]*trAB(Si + *q * *q *i,Si + *q * *q *j,q,q);
    for (i=0;i<*M;i++) det2[i + *M * i] += det1[i];
  }

  FREE(frob);
  FREE(gamma);
  FREE(gamma1);
  FREE(alpha);
  FREE(Sb);
  FREE(Sg);
  if (*fixed_penalty) {FREE(spf);}
  FREE(Si);
  FREE(ev);
  FREE(B);
  FREE(C);
}

void get_ddetXWXpS(double *det1,double *det2,double *P,double *K,double *sp,
      double *rS,int *rSncol,double *Tk,double *Tkm,int *n,int *q,int *r,int *M,int *M0,
		   int *deriv,int nthreads)

{ double *diagKKt,xx,*KtTK,*PtrSm,*PtSP,*trPtSP,*work,*pdKK,*p1,*pTkm;
  int m,k,bt,ct,j,one=1,km,mk,*rSoff,deriv2,max_col,Mtot,work_n;
  int tid;
  if (nthreads<1) nthreads = 1;

  Mtot = *M0 + *M;

  if (*deriv==2) deriv2=1; else deriv2=0;

  if (*deriv) {
    diagKKt = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
    xx = diagABt(diagKKt,K,K,n,r);
  } else {
      return;
  }

  work_n = *n; if (work_n < *r) work_n = *r;
  work =  (double *)CALLOC((size_t) work_n * nthreads,sizeof(double));
  tid=0;

  if (deriv2) {
    KtTK = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*r * *r * Mtot),sizeof(double));
    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp parallel private(k,j,tid) num_threads(nthreads)
    #endif
    {
      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
      #pragma omp for
      #endif
      for (k=0;k < Mtot;k++) {
	#ifdef OPENMP_ON
        tid = omp_get_thread_num();
	#endif
        j = k * *r * *r;
        getXtWX(KtTK+ j,K,Tk + k * *n,n,r,work + *n * tid);
      }
    }
  } else { KtTK=(double *)NULL;}

  bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(det1,Tk,diagKKt,&bt,&ct,&Mtot,&one,n);

  max_col = *q;
  for (j=0;j<*M;j++) if (max_col<rSncol[j]) max_col=rSncol[j];

  PtrSm = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*r * max_col * nthreads),sizeof(double));
  trPtSP = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *M,sizeof(double));

  if (deriv2) {
    PtSP = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*M * *r * *r ),sizeof(double));
  } else { PtSP = (double *) NULL;}

  rSoff =  (int *)CALLOC((size_t)*M,sizeof(int));
  if (*M>0) {
    rSoff[0] = 0;for (m=0;m < *M-1;m++) rSoff[m+1] = rSoff[m] + rSncol[m];
  }
  tid = 0;
  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  #pragma omp parallel private(m,bt,ct,tid) num_threads(nthreads)
  #endif
  {
    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp for
    #endif
    for (m=0;m < *M;m++) {
      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
      tid = omp_get_thread_num();
      #endif
      bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(PtrSm + tid * *r * max_col,P,rS+rSoff[m] * *q,&bt,&ct,r,rSncol+m,q);

      trPtSP[m] = sp[m] * diagABt(work + work_n * tid,PtrSm + tid * *r * max_col,
                                 PtrSm + tid * *r * max_col,r,rSncol+m);
      det1[m + *M0] += trPtSP[m];
      if (deriv2) {
        bt=0;ct=1;mgcv_mmult(PtSP+ m * *r * *r,PtrSm + tid * *r * max_col,
                            PtrSm+ tid * *r * max_col ,&bt,&ct,r,r,rSncol+m);
      }
    }
  }
  FREE(rSoff);

  if (deriv2)
  {

    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp parallel for private(m,k,km,mk,xx,tid,pdKK,p1,pTkm) num_threads(nthreads)
    #endif
    for (m=0;m < Mtot;m++) {
      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
      tid = omp_get_thread_num();
      #endif
      if (m==0) pTkm = Tkm; else pTkm = Tkm + (m * Mtot - (m*(m-1))/2) * *n;
      for (k=m;k < Mtot;k++) {
        km=k * Mtot + m;mk=m * Mtot + k;

        for (xx=0.0,pdKK=diagKKt,p1=pdKK + *n;pdKK<p1;pdKK++,pTkm++) xx += *pTkm * *pdKK;
        det2[km] = xx;

        det2[km] -= diagABt(work + work_n * tid,KtTK + k * *r * *r,KtTK+ m * *r * *r,r,r);

        if (k >= *M0 && k==m) det2[km] += trPtSP[m - *M0];

        if (m >= *M0) det2[km] -= sp[m - *M0]*diagABt(work + work_n * tid,KtTK + k * *r * *r,PtSP + (m - *M0) * *r * *r,r,r);

        if (k >= *M0) det2[km] -= sp[k - *M0]*diagABt(work + work_n * tid,KtTK + m * *r * *r,PtSP + (k - *M0) * *r * *r,r,r);

        if (k >= *M0 && m >= *M0) det2[km] -= sp[m - *M0]*sp[k - *M0]*
                                  diagABt(work + work_n * tid,PtSP + (k - *M0) * *r * *r,PtSP + (m - *M0) * *r * *r,r,r);

        det2[mk] = det2[km];
      }
    }
  }

  if (deriv2) {FREE(PtSP);FREE(KtTK);}
  FREE(diagKKt);FREE(work);
  FREE(PtrSm);FREE(trPtSP);

}

void get_trA2(double *trA,double *trA1,double *trA2,double *P,double *K,double *sp,
	      double *rS,int *rSncol,double *Tk,double *Tkm,double *w,int *n,int *q,
              int *r,int *M,int *deriv,int *nt)

{ double *diagKKt,*diagKKtKKt,xx,*KtTK,*KtTKKtK,*KKtK,*KtK,*work,*pTk,*pTm,*pdKKt,*pdKKtKKt,*p0,*p1,*p2,*p3,*pd,
    *PtrSm,*PtSP,*KPtrSm,*diagKPtSPKt,*diagKPtSPKtKKt,*PtSPKtK, *KtKPtrSm, *KKtKPtrSm,*Ip,*IpK;
  int i,m,k,bt,ct,j,one=1,km,mk,*rSoff,deriv2,neg_w=0,tid=0,n_work;
  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
    Rprintf("get_trA2 (d=%d)...",*deriv);
  #endif
  if (*deriv==2) deriv2=1; else deriv2=0;

  Ip = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
  for (p0=w,p1=p0+ *n,p2=Ip;p0<p1;p0++,p2++) if (*p0 < 0) {*p2 = -1.0;neg_w=1;} else *p2 = 1.0;

  diagKKt = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
  *trA = diagABt(diagKKt,K,K,n,r);
  if (neg_w) {
    for (*trA=0.0,p0=diagKKt,p1=p0 + *n,p2=Ip;p0<p1;p0++,p2++) *trA += *p2 * *p0;
  }
  if (!*deriv) {
    FREE(Ip);FREE(diagKKt);
    #ifdef OMP_REPORT
    Rprintf("done\n");
    #endif
    return;
  }

  if (*n > *r) n_work = *n; else n_work = *r;
  work =  (double *)CALLOC((size_t)n_work * *nt,sizeof(double));

  KtK = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*r * *r,sizeof(double));
  if (neg_w) {
    IpK = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *r * *n,sizeof(double));
    for (p0=IpK,p3=K,i=0;i<*r;i++)
      for (p1=Ip,p2=p1 + *n;p1<p2;p1++,p0++,p3++) *p0 = *p1 * *p3;
  } else {
    IpK = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *r * *n,sizeof(double));
    for (p0=IpK,p1=K,p2=K+ *n * *r;p1<p2;p0++,p1++) *p0 = *p1;

  }

  bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_pmmult(KtK,K,IpK,&bt,&ct,r,r,n,nt);
  if (neg_w) FREE(IpK); else FREE(IpK);
  KKtK = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n * *r,sizeof(double));
  bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_pmmult(KKtK,K,KtK,&bt,&ct,n,r,r,nt);

  diagKKtKKt = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
  xx = diagABt(diagKKtKKt,KKtK,K,n,r);

  if (deriv2) {
    KtTK = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*r * *r * *M),sizeof(double));
    KtTKKtK = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*r * *r * *M),sizeof(double));
    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp parallel private(k,j,tid) num_threads(*nt)
    #endif
    {
      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
      #pragma omp for
      #endif
      for (k=0;k < *M;k++) {
        #ifdef OPENMP_ON
        tid = omp_get_thread_num();
        #endif
        j = k * *r * *r;
        getXtWX(KtTK+ j,K,Tk + k * *n,n,r,work + tid * n_work);
        bt=ct=0;mgcv_mmult(KtTKKtK + k * *r * *r ,KtTK + j,KtK,&bt,&ct,r,r,r);
      }
    }
  } else { KtTK=KtTKKtK=(double *)NULL;}

  bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(trA1,Tk,diagKKt,&bt,&ct,M,&one,n);
  bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(work,Tk,diagKKtKKt,&bt,&ct,M,&one,n);
  for (i=0;i<*M;i++) trA1[i] +=  - work[i];

  if (deriv2) for (m=0;m < *M;m++) for (k=m;k < *M;k++){
     km=k * *M + m;mk=m * *M + k;

     for (xx=0.0,pdKKt=diagKKt,pdKKtKKt=diagKKtKKt,p1=pdKKt + *n;pdKKt<p1;pdKKt++,pdKKtKKt++,Tkm++)
          xx += *Tkm * (*pdKKt - *pdKKtKKt);
     trA2[km] = xx;

     trA2[km] -= 2*diagABt(work,KtTK + k * *r * *r,KtTK+ m * *r * *r,r,r);

     xx = 2*diagABt(work,KtTK+k * *r * *r,KtTKKtK+m * *r * *r,r,r);

     trA2[km] += xx;

     trA2[mk] = trA2[km];
  }

  if (deriv2) {FREE(KtTKKtK);FREE(KtTK);}

  FREE(diagKKtKKt);FREE(diagKKt);

  PtrSm = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*r * *q * *nt),sizeof(double));
  KPtrSm = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*n * *q * *nt),sizeof(double));
  diagKPtSPKt = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*n * *M),sizeof(double));
  if (deriv2) {
    PtSP = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*M * *r * *r ),sizeof(double));
    PtSPKtK = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*M * *r * *r ),sizeof(double));
    KtKPtrSm = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*r * *q * *nt),sizeof(double));
    KKtKPtrSm = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*n * *q * *nt),sizeof(double));
    diagKPtSPKtKKt = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*n * *M),sizeof(double));
  } else {  KKtKPtrSm=PtSPKtK= PtSP=KtKPtrSm=diagKPtSPKtKKt=(double *)NULL; }

  rSoff =  (int *)CALLOC((size_t)*M,sizeof(int));
  rSoff[0] = 0;for (m=0;m < *M-1;m++) rSoff[m+1] = rSoff[m] + rSncol[m];
  tid = 0;
  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  #pragma omp parallel private(m,bt,ct,tid,xx,p0,p1,p2) num_threads(*nt)
  #endif
  {
    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp for
    #endif
    for (m=0;m < *M;m++) {
      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
      tid = omp_get_thread_num();
      #endif
      bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(PtrSm + *r * *q * tid,P,rS+rSoff[m] * *q,&bt,&ct,r,rSncol+m,q);
      bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(KPtrSm + *n * *q * tid,K,PtrSm + *r * *q * tid ,&bt,&ct,n,rSncol+m,r);
      if (deriv2) {
        bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(KtKPtrSm + *r * *q * tid,KtK,PtrSm + *r * *q * tid,&bt,&ct,r,rSncol+m,r);
        bt=0;ct=1;mgcv_mmult(PtSP+ m * *r * *r,PtrSm + *r * *q * tid,PtrSm + *r * *q * tid,&bt,&ct,r,r,rSncol+m);

        bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(KKtKPtrSm + *n * *q * tid,KKtK,PtrSm + *r * *q * tid,&bt,&ct,n,rSncol+m,r);
        bt=0;ct=1;mgcv_mmult(PtSPKtK + m * *r * *r,PtrSm + *r * *q * tid,KtKPtrSm+ *r * *q * tid,&bt,&ct,r,r,rSncol+m);
        xx = diagABt(diagKPtSPKtKKt+ m * *n,KPtrSm + *n * *q * tid,KKtKPtrSm + *n * *q * tid,n,rSncol+m);
      }
      xx = sp[m] * diagABt(diagKPtSPKt+ m * *n,KPtrSm + *n * *q * tid,KPtrSm + *n * *q * tid,n,rSncol+m);
      if (neg_w) {
        for (xx=0.0,p0=diagKPtSPKt+m * *n,p1=p0 + *n,p2=Ip;p0<p1;p0++,p2++) xx += *p0 * *p2;
        xx *= sp[m];
      }
      trA1[m] -= xx;
      if (deriv2) trA2[m * *M + m] -=xx;
    }
  }
  FREE(rSoff);

  if (!deriv2) {
    FREE(PtrSm);FREE(KPtrSm);FREE(diagKPtSPKt);
    FREE(work);FREE(KtK);FREE(KKtK);
    #ifdef OMP_REPORT
    Rprintf("done\n");
    #endif
    return;
  }

   for (m=0;m < *M;m++) for (k=m;k < *M;k++){
     km=k * *M + m;mk=m * *M + k;

     pTk = Tk + k * *n;
     for (xx=0.0,pd = diagKPtSPKtKKt + m * *n,p1=pd + *n;pd < p1;pd++,pTk++) xx += *pd * *pTk;
     trA2[km] += 2*sp[m] *xx;

     pTm = Tk + m * *n;
     for (xx=0.0,pd = diagKPtSPKtKKt + k * *n,p1=pd + *n;pd < p1;pd++,pTm++) xx += *pd * *pTm;
     trA2[km] += 2*sp[k] *xx;

     pTk = Tk + k * *n;
     for (xx=0.0,pd = diagKPtSPKt + m * *n,p1=pd + *n;pd < p1;pd++,pTk++) xx += *pd * *pTk;
     trA2[km] -= sp[m] *xx;

     pTm = Tk + m * *n;
     for (xx=0.0,pd = diagKPtSPKt + k * *n,p1=pd + *n;pd < p1;pd++,pTm++) xx += *pd * *pTm;
     trA2[km] -= sp[k] *xx;

     trA2[km] += 2 * sp[k]*sp[m]*diagABt(work,PtSP + m * *r * *r,PtSPKtK + k * *r * *r,r,r);

     trA2[mk] =trA2[km];
   }

   FREE(PtrSm);FREE(KPtrSm);FREE(PtSP);FREE(KtKPtrSm);FREE(diagKPtSPKt);
   FREE(diagKPtSPKtKKt);FREE(work);FREE(KtK);FREE(KKtK);FREE(PtSPKtK);FREE(KKtKPtrSm);
   FREE(Ip);
   #ifdef OMP_REPORT
    Rprintf("done\n");
   #endif
}

void rc_prod(double *y,double *z,double *x,int *xcol,int *n)

{ int i;
  double *pz,*pz1;
  pz1 = z + *n;
  for (i=0;i < *xcol;i++)
      for (pz=z;pz<pz1;pz++,y++,x++) *y = *pz * *x;
}

void Rinv(double *Ri,double *R,int *c,int *r, int *ri)

{ int i,j,k,eye;
  double xx,*rc;
  rc=Ri;
  for (i=0;i<*c;i++) {
      for (eye=1,k=i;k>=0;k--) {
	  for (xx=0.0,j=k+1;j <=i;j++) xx += R[k + j * *r] * rc[j];
          rc[k]=(eye-xx)/R[k + k * *r];
          eye=0;
      }
      for (k=i+1;k<*c;k++) rc[k]=0.0;
      rc += *ri;
  }
}

void pearson2(double *P, double *P1, double *P2,
              double *y,double *mu,double *V, double *V1,double *V2,double *g1,double *g2,
              double *p_weights,double *eta1, double *eta2,int n,int M,int deriv, int deriv2)

{ double resid,xx,*Pe1,*Pe2,*pp,*p1,*p0,*v2,*Pi1,*Pi2;
  int i,k,m,n_2dCols=0,one=1;
  if (deriv) {
    Pe1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)n,sizeof(double));
    Pi1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) n * M,sizeof(double));
    if (deriv2) {
      n_2dCols = (M * (1 + M))/2;
      Pe2 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)n,sizeof(double));
      v2 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)n,sizeof(double));
      Pi2 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)n_2dCols*n,sizeof(double));
    } else {Pe2=v2=Pi2=NULL;}
  } else {Pi1 = Pe2 = v2 = Pe1 = Pi2 = NULL;}
  *P=0.0;
  for (i=0; i < n;i++) {
    resid = y[i]-mu[i];
    xx = resid*p_weights[i]/V[i];
    *P += xx*resid;
    if (deriv) {
      Pe1[i] = - xx* (2 + resid*V1[i])/g1[i];
      if (deriv2) {
        Pe2[i] = - Pe1[i]*g2[i]/g1[i] +
	  (2*p_weights[i]/V[i]+2*xx*V1[i] - Pe1[i]*V1[i]*g1[i] - xx*resid*(V2[i]-V1[i]*V1[i]))/(g1[i]*g1[i]);
      }
    }
  }

  if (deriv) {
    rc_prod(Pi1,Pe1,eta1,&M,&n);
      if (deriv2) {
        rc_prod(Pi2,Pe1,eta2,&n_2dCols,&n);
        for (pp=Pi2,m=0;m < M;m++) for (k=m;k < M;k++) {
	    rc_prod(Pe1,eta1 + n *  m,eta1 + n * k,&one,&n);
            rc_prod(v2,Pe2,Pe1,&one,&n);
            p1=v2 + n;
            for (p0=v2;p0<p1;p0++,pp++) *pp += *p0;
        }
      }
  }

  if (deriv) {
    pp = Pi1;
    for (k=0;k<M;k++) { xx=0.0; for (i=0;i<n;i++,pp++) xx += *pp;P1[k] = xx;}
    if (deriv2) {
        for (pp=Pi2,m=0;m < M;m++) for (k=m;k < M;k++) {
          xx=0.0;
          for (i=0;i<n;i++,pp++) xx += *pp;
          P2[k*M+m] = P2[m*M+k] = xx;
        }
    }
  }

  if (deriv) {
    FREE(Pe1);FREE(Pi1);
    if (deriv2) {
      FREE(Pe2);FREE(Pi2);FREE(v2);
    }
  }

}

void applyP(double *y,double *x,double *R,double *Vt,int neg_w,int nr,int r,int c,int right)

{ double *x1;
  int bt,ct;
  if (neg_w) {
    x1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)r*c,sizeof(double));
    if (right) {
      mgcv_backsolve(R,&nr,&r,x,x1, &c,&right);
      bt=0;ct=1;mgcv_mmult(y,x1,Vt,&bt,&ct,&c,&r,&r);
    } else {
      bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(x1,Vt,x,&bt,&ct,&r,&c,&r);
      mgcv_backsolve(R,&nr,&r,x1,y, &c,&right);
    }
    FREE(x1);
  } else mgcv_backsolve(R,&nr,&r,x,y, &c,&right);
}

void applyPt(double *y,double *x,double *R,double *Vt,int neg_w,int nr,int r,int c,int right)

{ double *x1;
  int bt,ct;
  if (neg_w) {
    x1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)r*c,sizeof(double));
    if (right) {
      bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(x1,x,Vt,&bt,&ct,&c,&r,&r);
      mgcv_forwardsolve(R,&nr,&r,x1,y, &c,&right);
    } else {
      mgcv_forwardsolve(R,&nr,&r,x,x1, &c,&right);
      bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(y,Vt,x1,&bt,&ct,&r,&c,&r);
    }
    FREE(x1);
  } else mgcv_forwardsolve(R,&nr,&r,x,y, &c,&right);
}

void ift1(double *R,double *Vt,double *X,double *rS,double *beta,double *sp,double *w,
         double *dwdeta,double *b1, double *b2,double *eta1,double *eta2,
	  int *n,int *r, int *M,int *rSncol,int *deriv2,int *neg_w,int *nr)

{ int n_2dCols,i,j,k,one=1,bt,ct;
  double *work,*Skb,*pp,*p0,*p1,*work1;
  i = *n; if (i < *r) i = *r;
  work = (double *) CALLOC((size_t)i,sizeof(double));
  work1 = (double *) CALLOC((size_t)i,sizeof(double));
  Skb = (double *) CALLOC((size_t)*r,sizeof(double));
  n_2dCols = (*M * (1 + *M))/2;
  for (i=0;i<*M;i++) {
    multSk(Skb,beta,&one,i,rS,rSncol,r,work);
    for (j=0;j<*r;j++) Skb[j] *= -sp[i];
    applyPt(work,Skb,R,Vt,*neg_w,*nr,*r,1,0);
    applyP(b1 + i * *r,work,R,Vt,*neg_w,*nr,*r,1,0);
  }

  bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(eta1,X,b1,&bt,&ct,n,M,r);

  if (*deriv2) {
    pp = b2;
    for (i=0;i<*M;i++) for (k=i;k<*M;k++) {
      p0 = eta1 + *n * i;p1 = eta1 + *n * k;
      for (j=0;j<*n;j++,p0++,p1++) work[j] = - *p0 * *p1 * dwdeta[j];
      bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(Skb,X,work,&bt,&ct,r,&one,n);
      multSk(work,b1+k* *r,&one,i,rS,rSncol,r,work1);
      for (j=0;j<*r;j++) Skb[j] += -sp[i]*work[j];
      multSk(work,b1+i* *r,&one,k,rS,rSncol,r,work1);
      for (j=0;j<*r;j++) Skb[j] += -sp[k]*work[j];
      applyPt(work,Skb,R,Vt,*neg_w,*nr,*r,1,0);
      applyP(pp,work,R,Vt,*neg_w,*nr,*r,1,0);
      if (i==k) for (j=0;j< *r;j++) pp[j] += b1[i * *r + j];
      pp += *r;
    }

    bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(eta2,X,b2,&bt,&ct,n,&n_2dCols,r);
  }

  FREE(work);FREE(Skb);FREE(work1);
}

void ift2(double *R,double *Vt,double *X,double *rS,double *beta,double *sp,double *theta,
          double *Det_th,double *Det2_th,double *Det3,double *Det_th2,double *b1, double *b2,
          double *eta1,double *eta2,
	  int *n,int *r, int *M,int *n_theta,int *rSncol,int *deriv2,int *neg_w,int *nr)

{ int n_2dCols,i,j,k,one=1,bt,ct,ntot,kk;
  double *work,*Db_th,*pp,*p0,*p1,*work1,n_work;
  if (*n < *r) n_work = *r; else n_work = *n;
  work = (double *) CALLOC((size_t)n_work,sizeof(double));
  work1 = (double *) CALLOC((size_t)n_work,sizeof(double));
  Db_th = (double *) CALLOC((size_t)*r,sizeof(double));
  ntot = *M + *n_theta;
  n_2dCols = (ntot  * (1 + ntot))/2;

  for (i=0;i < ntot ;i++) {
    if (i < *n_theta) {
      bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(Db_th,X,Det_th + i * *n,&bt,&ct,r,&one,n);
      for (j=0;j<*r;j++) Db_th[j] *= -.5;
    } else {
      multSk(Db_th,beta,&one,i - *n_theta,rS,rSncol,r,work);
      for (j=0;j<*r;j++) Db_th[j] *=  - sp[i - *n_theta];
    }

    applyPt(work,Db_th,R,Vt,*neg_w,*nr,*r,1,0);
    applyP(b1 + i * *r,work,R,Vt,*neg_w,*nr,*r,1,0);
  }

  bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(eta1,X,b1,&bt,&ct,n,&ntot,r);

  if (*deriv2) {
    pp = b2;
    kk = 0;
    for (i=0;i<ntot;i++) for (k=i;k<ntot;k++) {

      p0 = eta1 + *n * i;p1 = eta1 + *n * k;
      for (j=0;j<*n;j++,p0++,p1++) work[j] = - *p0 * *p1 * Det3[j];
      bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(Db_th,X,work,&bt,&ct,r,&one,n);

      if (k < *n_theta) {
        p0 = Det2_th + *n * k;
        p1 = eta1 + *n * i;
        for (j=0;j<*n;j++,p0++,p1++) work[j] = *p0 * *p1 ;
        bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(work1,X,work,&bt,&ct,r,&one,n);
      } else {
        multSk(work1,b1+ i * *r,&one,k - *n_theta,rS,rSncol,r,work);
        for (j=0;j<*r;j++) work1[j] *=  sp[k - *n_theta] * 2;
      }
      for (j=0;j<*r;j++) Db_th[j] -=  work1[j];

      if (i < *n_theta) {
        p0 = Det2_th + *n * i;
        p1 = eta1 + *n * k;
        for (j=0;j<*n;j++,p0++,p1++) work[j] = *p0 * *p1 ;
        bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(work1,X,work,&bt,&ct,r,&one,n);
      } else {
        multSk(work1,b1+ k * *r,&one,i - *n_theta,rS,rSncol,r,work);
        for (j=0;j<*r;j++) work1[j] *=  sp[i - *n_theta] * 2;
      }
      for (j=0;j<*r;j++) Db_th[j] -=  work1[j];

      if (i< *n_theta && k < *n_theta) {
        p0 = Det_th2 + *n * kk;
        bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(work,X,p0,&bt,&ct,r,&one,n);
        for (j=0;j<*r;j++) Db_th[j] -=  work[j];
	kk++;
      } else if (i==k) {
        multSk(work1,beta,&one,i - *n_theta,rS,rSncol,r,work);
        for (j=0;j<*r;j++) Db_th[j] -= work1[j] * sp[i - *n_theta] *2 ;
      }
      for (j=0;j<*r;j++) Db_th[j] *= .5;
      applyPt(work,Db_th,R,Vt,*neg_w,*nr,*r,1,0);
      applyP(pp,work,R,Vt,*neg_w,*nr,*r,1,0);
      pp += *r;
    }
    bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(eta2,X,b2,&bt,&ct,n,&n_2dCols,r);
  }

  FREE(work);FREE(Db_th);FREE(work1);
}

void drop_cols(double *X, int r, int c,int *drop, int n_drop)

{ int k,j,j0,j1;
  double *p,*p1,*p2;
  if (n_drop<=0) return;
  if (n_drop) {
    for (k=0;k<n_drop;k++) {
      j = drop[k]-k;
      j0 = drop[k]+1;
      if (k<n_drop-1) j1 = drop[k+1]; else j1 = c;
      for (p=X + j * r,p1=X + j0 * r,p2=X + j1 * r;p1<p2;p++,p1++) *p = *p1;
    }
  }
}

void drop_rows(double *X,int r, int c,int *drop,int n_drop)

{ int i,j,k;
  double *Xs;
  if (n_drop<=0) return;
  Xs=X;
  for (j=0;j<c;j++) {
    for (i=0;i<drop[0];i++,X++,Xs++) *X = *Xs;
    Xs++;
    for (k=1;k<n_drop;k++) {
      for (i=drop[k-1]+1;i<drop[k];i++,X++,Xs++) *X = *Xs;
      Xs++;
    }
    for (i=drop[n_drop-1]+1;i<r;i++,X++,Xs++) *X = *Xs;
  }
}

void undrop_rows(double *X,int r,int c,int *drop,int n_drop)

{ double *Xs;
  int i,j,k;
  if (n_drop <= 0) return;
  Xs = X + (r-n_drop)*c - 1;
  X += r*c - 1;
  for (j=c-1;j>=0;j--) {
    for (i=r-1;i>drop[n_drop-1];i--,X--,Xs--) *X = *Xs;
    *X = 0.0;X--;
    for (k=n_drop-1;k>0;k--) {
      for (i=drop[k]-1;i>drop[k-1];i--,X--,Xs--) *X = *Xs;
      *X = 0.0;X--;
    }
    for (i=drop[0]-1;i>=0;i--,X--,Xs--) *X = *Xs;
  }
}

double MLpenalty1(double *det1,double *det2,double *Tk,double *Tkm,double *nulli, double *X,
double *R,double *Q, int *nind,double *sp,double *rS,int *rSncol,int *q,int *n,
		  int *Ms,int *M,int *M0,int *neg_w,double *rank_tol,int *deriv,
                  int *nthreads,int *type) {

  double *RU1,*tau,*work,*Ri,*Qb=NULL,*K,*P,*IQ,*IQQ,*Vt,*XU1=NULL,
         *d,*p0,*p1,*p2,*p3,ldetXWXS,ldetI2D=0.0;
  int ScS,bt,ct,qM,*pivot,i,j,k,left,tp,n_drop=0,*drop,False=0;

  drop = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)*Ms,sizeof(int));
  for (i=0;i < *q;i++) if (nulli[i]>0.0) { drop[n_drop] = i;n_drop++; }

  for (ScS=0.0,i=0;i<*M;i++) ScS += rSncol[i];

  qM = *q - n_drop;

  RU1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *q * *q ,sizeof(double));
  for (p1=RU1,p2=R,p3=R+ *q * *q;p2 < p3;p1++,p2++) *p1 = *p2;

  drop_cols(RU1,*q,*q,drop,n_drop);

  tau=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)qM,sizeof(double));
  pivot=(int *)CALLOC((size_t)qM,sizeof(int));

  mgcv_qr(RU1,q,&qM,pivot,tau);

  Ri =  (double *)CALLOC((size_t) qM * qM,sizeof(double));
  Rinv(Ri,RU1,&qM,q,&qM);

  if (*type==0||*neg_w) {
    Qb = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *q * qM,sizeof(double));
    for (i=0;i< qM;i++) Qb[i * *q + i] = 1.0;
    left=1;tp=0;mgcv_qrqy(Qb,RU1,tau,q,&qM,&qM,&left,&tp);
  } else {
    XU1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n * *q,sizeof(double));
    for (p1=XU1,p2=X,p3=X + *n * *q;p2 < p3;p1++,p2++) *p1 = *p2;
    drop_cols(XU1,*n,*q,drop,n_drop);
  }
  FREE(tau);

  K = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n * qM,sizeof(double));
  P = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) qM * qM,sizeof(double));

  if (*neg_w) {
    if (*neg_w < *q+1) k = *q+1; else k = *neg_w;
    IQ = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) k * *q,sizeof(double));
    for (i=0;i< *neg_w;i++) {
      p0 = IQ + i;p1 = Q + nind[i];
      for (j=0;j<*q;j++,p0+=k,p1+= *n) *p0 = *p1;
    }

    IQQ = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) k * qM,sizeof(double));
    bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(IQQ,IQ,Qb,&bt,&ct,&k,&qM,q);
    FREE(IQ);

    Vt = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) qM * qM,sizeof(double));
    d = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) qM,sizeof(double));
    mgcv_svd_full(IQQ,Vt,d,&k,&qM);
    FREE(IQQ);
    for (i=0;i<qM;i++) {
      d[i] = 1 - 2*d[i]*d[i];
      if (d[i]<=0) d[i]=0.0;
      else {
        ldetI2D += log(d[i]);
        d[i] = 1/sqrt(d[i]);
      }
    }

    for (p0=Vt,i=0;i<qM;i++)
    for (p1=d,p2=d+qM;p1<p2;p1++,p0++) *p0 *= *p1;

    bt=0;ct=1;mgcv_mmult(P,Ri,Vt,&bt,&ct,&qM,&qM,&qM);

    if (*type==1) {
      bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(K,XU1,P,&bt,&ct,n,&qM,&qM);
    } else {
      work = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *q * qM,sizeof(double));
      bt=0;ct=1;mgcv_mmult(work,Qb,Vt,&bt,&ct,q,&qM,&qM);

      bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(K,Q,work,&bt,&ct,n,&qM,q);
      FREE(work);
    }
    FREE(d);FREE(Vt);

  } else {
    ldetI2D = 0.0;

    for (p0=P,p1=Ri,p2=Ri+ qM * qM;p1<p2;p0++,p1++) *p0 = *p1;

    if (*type==1) {
      bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(K,XU1,P,&bt,&ct,n,&qM,&qM);
    } else {
      bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(K,Q,Qb,&bt,&ct,n,&qM,q);
    }
  }

  FREE(Ri);

  for (ldetXWXS=0.0,i=0;i<qM;i++) ldetXWXS += log(fabs(RU1[i + i * *q]));
  ldetXWXS *= 2;

  ldetXWXS += ldetI2D;

  FREE(RU1);

  drop_rows(rS,*q,ScS,drop,n_drop);
  pivoter(rS,&qM,&ScS,pivot,&False,&False);

  if (*type==0||*neg_w) FREE(Qb); else FREE(XU1);
  FREE(pivot);

  if (*deriv)
    get_ddetXWXpS(det1,det2,P,K,sp,rS,rSncol,Tk,Tkm,n,&qM,&qM,M,M0,deriv,*nthreads);

  FREE(P);FREE(K);FREE(drop);
  return(ldetXWXS);
}

int icompare (const void * a, const void * b)

{
  return ( *(int*)a - *(int*)b );
}

void gdiPK(double *work,double *X,double *E,double *Es,double *rS,double *U1,double *z,double *raw,double *R,
           double *Rh,double *nulli,double *dev_hess,double *P, double *K,double *Vt,double *PKtz,double *Q1,
           int *nind,int *pivot1,int *drop,
           int *n,int *q,int *Mp,int neg_w, int *nt,int *Enrow,int *rank,int *n_drop,int deriv2,int ScS, int *REML,
      double *rank_tol,double *ldetXWXS,int *type)

{ int i,j,k,*pivot,nt1,nr,left,tp,bt,ct,True=1,False=0,one=1,rr;
  double *zz=NULL,*WX,*tau,*R1,Rnorm,Enorm,Rcond,*Q=NULL,*tau1,*Ri,ldetI2D,*IQ,*d,*p0,*p1,*p2,*p3,*p4,xx,norm1,norm2;
  nt1 = *nt;

  if (*type==0) {
    zz = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
    for (i=0;i< *n;i++) {
      zz[i] = z[i]*raw[i];
    }
    for (i=0;i<neg_w;i++) { k=nind[i]; zz[k] = -zz[k];}
  }

  WX = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) ( (*n + *nt * *q) * *q),sizeof(double));
  for (j=0;j<*q;j++)
  { for (i=0;i<*n;i++)
    { k = i + *n * j;
      WX[k]=raw[i]*X[k];
    }
  }

  tau=(double *)CALLOC((size_t) *q * (*nt + 1),sizeof(double));

  pivot=(int *)CALLOC((size_t)*q,sizeof(int));

  mgcv_pqr(WX,n,q,pivot,tau,&nt1);

  rr = *q; if (*n < rr) rr = *n;
  R1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)rr * *q,sizeof(double));

  getRpqr(R1,WX,n,q,&rr,&nt1);

  pivoter(R1,&rr,q,pivot,&True,&True);

  Rnorm = frobenius_norm(R1,&rr,q);
  Enorm =  frobenius_norm(Es,Enrow,q);
  nr = rr + *Enrow;
  for (j=0;j<*q;j++) {
    for (i=0;i< rr;i++) R[i + nr * j] = R1[i + rr * j]/Rnorm;
    for (i=0;i< *Enrow;i++) R[i + rr + nr * j] = Es[i + *Enrow * j]/Enorm;
  }

  tau1=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)*q,sizeof(double));

  mgcv_qr(R,&nr,q,pivot1,tau1);

  *rank = *q;if (nr < *rank) *rank = nr;
  R_cond(R,&nr,rank,work,&Rcond);
  while (*rank_tol * Rcond > 1) { (*rank)--;R_cond(R,&nr,rank,work,&Rcond);}

  for (i=0;i<*q - *Mp;i++) nulli[i] = -1.0;
  for (i= *q - *Mp;i < *q;i++) nulli[i] = 1.0;
  *n_drop = *q - *rank;
  if (*n_drop) {
    for (i=0;i < *n_drop;i++) drop[i] = pivot1[*rank+i];
    qsort(drop,*n_drop,sizeof(int),icompare);

    drop_cols(R1,rr,*q,drop,*n_drop);
    drop_cols(E,*Enrow,*q,drop,*n_drop);
    drop_cols(X,*n,*q,drop,*n_drop);
    drop_rows(rS,*q,ScS,drop,*n_drop);
    drop_rows(nulli,*q,1,drop,*n_drop);
  }

  for (j=0;j < *rank;j++) {
    for (i=0;i< rr;i++) R[i + nr * j] = R1[i + rr * j];
      for (i=0;i< *Enrow;i++) R[i + rr + nr * j] = E[i + *Enrow * j];
  }

  mgcv_qr(R,&nr,rank,pivot1,tau1);

  i=1;pivoter(nulli,rank,&i,pivot1,&False,&False);

  if (deriv2) {
    pivoter(R1,&rr,rank,pivot1,&True,&False);
    getXtX(dev_hess,R1,&rr,rank);
  }

  if (neg_w || *type==0) {
    Q = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) nr * *rank,sizeof(double));
    for (i=0;i < *rank;i++) Q[i * nr + i] = 1.0;
    left=1;tp=0;mgcv_qrqy(Q,R,tau1,&nr,rank,rank,&left,&tp);

    for (i=0;i<rr;i++) for (j=0;j < *rank;j++) Q1[i + rr * j] = Q[i + nr * j];
    tp=0;mgcv_pqrqy(Q1,WX,tau,n,&rr,rank,&tp,&nt1);

  }

  Ri =  (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *rank * *rank,sizeof(double));
  Rinv(Ri,R,rank,&nr,rank);

  ldetI2D = 0.0;
  if (neg_w) {
    if (neg_w < *rank + 1) k = *rank + 1; else k = neg_w;
    IQ = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) k * *rank,sizeof(double));
    for (i=0;i < neg_w;i++) {
      p0 = IQ + i;p1 = Q1 + nind[i];
      for (j=0;j < *rank;j++,p0+=k,p1+= *n) *p0 = *p1;
    }

    d = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *rank,sizeof(double));
    mgcv_svd_full(IQ,Vt,d,&k,rank);
    FREE(IQ);

    if (deriv2) {

      p1=P;
      for (j=0;j < *rank;j++,p1 += *rank) {
        p0 = R + j * nr;
        for (p2=Vt,p3=p1,p4=p1 + *rank;p3<p4;p3++,p2++) *p3 = *p2 * *p0;
        p0++;
        for (k=1;k<=j;k++,p0++)
	  for (p3=p1;p3<p4;p3++,p2++) *p3 += *p2 * *p0;
      }

      for (p0=P,j=0;j < *rank;j++) for (p1=d,p2=d + *rank;p1<p2;p1++,p0++) *p0 *= *p1;

      getXtX(K,P,rank,rank);
      for (p0=dev_hess,p1=p0+ *rank * *rank,p2=K;p0<p1;p0++,p2++) *p0 += -2 * *p2;
    }

    for (i=0;i < *rank;i++) {
      work[i] = d[i] = 1 - 2*d[i]*d[i];
      if (d[i]<=0) work[i] = d[i] = 0.0;
      else {
        ldetI2D += log(d[i]);
        work[i] = sqrt(d[i]);
        d[i] = 1/work[i];
      }
    }

    for (p0=P,i=0;i < *rank;i++) {
      for (p1 = R + i*nr,p2 = p1 + i;p1 <= p2;p1++,p0++) *p0 = *p1;
      p2 = p0 + *rank - i - 1;
      for (;p0<p2;p0++) *p0 = 0.0;
    }

    bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_pmmult(Rh,Vt,P,&bt,&ct,rank,rank,rank,nt);

    for (p0=Rh,i=0;i < *rank;i++)
    for (p1=work,p2=work + *rank;p1<p2;p1++,p0++) *p0 *= *p1;

    for (p0=Vt,i=0;i < *rank;i++)
    for (p1=d,p2=d + *rank;p1<p2;p1++,p0++) *p0 *= *p1;

    bt=0;ct=1;mgcv_pmmult(P,Ri,Vt,&bt,&ct,rank,rank,rank,nt);

    if (*type==0) mgcv_pmmult(K,Q1,Vt,&bt,&ct,n,rank,rank,nt);

    FREE(d);
  } else {
    if (*type==0) {
      for (p0=K,p1=Q1,j=0;j< *rank;j++,p1 += *n)
      for (p2 = p1,p3=p1 + *n;p2<p3;p0++,p2++) *p0 = *p2;
    }

    for (p0=P,p1=Ri,j=0;j < *rank;j++,p0+= *rank)
    for (p2=p0,p3=p0 + *rank;p2<p3;p1++,p2++) *p2 = *p1;

    for (p0=Rh,i=0;i < *rank;i++) {
      for (p1 = R + i*nr,p2 = p1 + i;p1 <= p2;p1++,p0++) *p0 = *p1;
      p2 = p0 + *rank - i - 1;
      for (;p0<p2;p0++) *p0 = 0.0;
    }
  }

  if (*REML>0) {
    for (*ldetXWXS=0.0,i=0;i < *rank;i++) *ldetXWXS += log(fabs(R[i + i * nr]));
    *ldetXWXS *= 2;
    *ldetXWXS += ldetI2D;
  }

  pivoter(rS,rank,&ScS,pivot1,&False,&False);

  pivoter(E,Enrow,rank,pivot1,&True,&False);

  pivoter(X,n,rank,pivot1,&True,&False);

  if (*type==1) {
    applyP(K,X,R,Vt,neg_w,nr,*rank,*n,1);
  } else {

    bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(work,K,zz,&bt,&ct,rank,&one,n);
    mgcv_mmult(work + *q *2,Q1,zz,&bt,&ct,rank,&one,n);
  }

  if (*type==1) {
    bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(work,K,z,&bt,&ct,rank,&one,n);
    applyP(PKtz,work,R,Vt,neg_w,nr,*rank,1,0);
  } else {

    for (i=0;i<*n;i++) zz[i] = raw[i] * raw[i] * z[i];
    for (i=0;i<neg_w;i++) { k=nind[i]; zz[k] = -zz[k];}
    bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(work + *q,X,zz,&bt,&ct,rank,&one,n);
    for (norm1=norm2=0,i=0;i < *rank;i++) {

      for (xx=0,j=0;j<=i;j++) xx += R[j + nr * i] * work[*q * 2 + j];
      xx -= work[i + *q];
      norm1 += xx*xx; norm2 += work[i + *q] * work[i + *q];
    }
    if (norm1 > *rank_tol * norm2) {
      applyPt(zz,work + *q,R,Vt,neg_w,nr,*rank,1,0);
      applyP(PKtz,zz,R,Vt,neg_w,nr,*rank,1,0);
    } else applyP(PKtz,work,R,Vt,neg_w,nr,*rank,1,0);
  }

  FREE(WX);FREE(tau);FREE(Ri);FREE(R1);
  FREE(tau1);
  if (neg_w || *type==0) FREE(Q);
  FREE(pivot);
  if (*type==0) FREE(zz);
}

void gdi2(double *X,double *E,double *Es,double *rS,double *U1,
	  double *sp,double *theta,double *z,double *w,double *wz,double *wf,
          double *Dth,double *Det,double *Det2,double *Dth2,double *Det_th,
          double *Det2_th,double *Det3,double *Det_th2,
          double *Det4, double *Det3_th, double *Det2_th2,
          double *beta,double *b1,double *w1,
          double *D1,double *D2,double *P0,double *P1,double *P2,
          double *ldet, double *ldet1,double *ldet2,double *rV,
          double *rank_tol,int *rank_est,
	  int *n,int *q, int *M,int *n_theta, int *Mp,int *Enrow,int *rSncol,int *deriv,
	  int *fixed_penalty,int *nt,int *type,double *dVkk)

{ double *work,*p0,*p1,*p2,*p3,*p4,*p5,*p6,*p7,*K=NULL,
    *Vt,*b2=NULL,*P,xx=0.0,*eta1=NULL,*eta2=NULL,
    *PKtz,*wi=NULL,*w2=NULL,*Tk=NULL,*Tkm=NULL,
    *dev_hess=NULL,*R,*Rh,*raw,*Q1,*Q,*nulli,*WX,*tau,*R1;
  int i,j,k,*pivot1,ScS,*pi,rank,*pivot,
    ntot,n_2dCols=0,n_drop,*drop,tp,
    n_work,deriv2,neg_w=0,*nind,nr,True=1,False=0,ML=0;

  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  int m;
  m = omp_get_num_procs();
  if (*nt > m || *nt < 1) *nt = m;
  omp_set_num_threads(*nt);
  #else
  *nt = 1;
  #endif

  if (*ldet<0) ML=1;

  if (*deriv==2) deriv2=1; else deriv2=0;

  ScS=0;for (pi=rSncol;pi<rSncol + *M;pi++) ScS+= *pi;

  PKtz = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*q,sizeof(double));
  nulli = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*q,sizeof(double));
  drop = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)*q,sizeof(int));
  raw = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(double));
  n_work = (4 * *n + 2 * *q) * *M + 2 * *n;
  k = 5 * *q; if (n_work < k) n_work = k;
  k = (*M * (1 + *M))/2 * *n;
  if (n_work < k) n_work = k;
  work = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) n_work,sizeof(double));

  if (*n < *q) nr = *n + *Enrow; else nr = *q + *Enrow;

  R = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*q * nr,sizeof(double));
  Rh = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*q * *q,sizeof(double));
  pivot1=(int *)CALLOC((size_t)*q,sizeof(int));
  if (deriv2) dev_hess = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *q * *q,sizeof(double)); else dev_hess=NULL;
  K = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n * *q,sizeof(double));
  P = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *q * *q,sizeof(double));
  Q1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n * *q,sizeof(double));

  for (i=0;i< *n;i++)
    if (w[i]<0) { neg_w++;raw[i] = sqrt(-w[i]);}
    else if (w[i]>0) raw[i] = sqrt(w[i]);
    else { *type=1; }

  if (neg_w) {
    Vt = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *q * *q,sizeof(double));
    nind = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)neg_w,sizeof(int));
    k=0;for (i=0;i< *n;i++) if (w[i]<0) { nind[k]=i;k++;}
  } else { nind = (int *)NULL; Vt = (double *)NULL;}

  if (*type==1) z=wz;
  gdiPK(work,X,E,Es,rS,U1,z,raw,
        R,Rh,nulli,dev_hess,P,K,Vt,PKtz,Q1,
        nind,pivot1,drop,
        n,q,Mp,neg_w,nt,Enrow,
        &rank,&n_drop,
        deriv2,ScS,&True,
      rank_tol,ldet,type);

  FREE(raw);

  ntot = *M + *n_theta;
  n_2dCols = (ntot  * (1 + ntot))/2;
  if (*deriv) {
    eta1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n * ntot,sizeof(double));
    if (deriv2) {
      b2 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) rank * n_2dCols,sizeof(double));
      eta2 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n * n_2dCols,sizeof(double));
    }
    ift2(R,Vt,X,rS,PKtz,sp,theta,
          Det_th,Det2_th,Det3,Det_th2,
          b1,b2,eta1,eta2,
          n,&rank,M,n_theta,rSncol,&deriv2,&neg_w,&nr);

    if (*M>0) {
      i=0;mgcv_mmult(work,Rh,b1 + *n_theta * rank,&i,&i,&rank,M,&rank);

      getXtX(dVkk,work,&rank,M);
    }

    for (p4 = Dth,p0=D1,p1=eta1,i=0;i < *n_theta;i++,p0++) {
