      for (*p0=0.0,p2 = Det,p3=Det + *n;p2<p3;p2++,p1++,p4++) *p0 += *p1 * *p2 + *p4;
    }
    for (;i<ntot;i++,p0++) {
      for (*p0=0.0,p2 = Det,p3=Det + *n;p2<p3;p2++,p1++) *p0 += *p1 * *p2;
    }

    if (deriv2) {
       p0 = eta2;p6 = Dth2;
       for (i=0;i<ntot;i++) for (k=i;k<ntot;k++) {
           p1 = eta1 + i * *n; p2 = eta1 + k * *n;
           p3 = Det2; p4 = Det; p5 = Det + *n;
           for (xx=0.0;p4<p5;p0++,p1++,p2++,p3++,p4++) xx +=  *p3 * *p1 * *p2 + *p4 * *p0;
           if (k < *n_theta) {
	      for (j=0;j<*n;j++,p6++) xx += *p6;
           }
           if (i < *n_theta) {
             p1 = Det_th + *n * i;p2 = p1 + *n;
             p3 = eta1 + *n *k;
             for (;p1<p2;p1++,p3++) xx += *p1 * *p3;
           }
           if (k < *n_theta) {
             p1 = Det_th + *n * k;p2 = p1 + *n;
             p3 = eta1 + *n *i;
             for (;p1<p2;p1++,p3++) xx += *p1 * *p3;
           }
           D2[i + ntot * k] = D2[k + ntot * i] = xx;
       }
    }
  }

  if (*deriv) {

    p0 = w1;
    p3 = Det2_th;p4 = eta1;
    for (i=0;i<ntot;i++) {
      p1=Det3;p2 = p1 + *n;
      if (i < *n_theta) {
	for (;p1<p2;p0++,p1++,p3++,p4++) *p0 = (*p1 * *p4 + *p3 ) * .5;
      } else {
        for (;p1<p2;p0++,p1++,p4++) *p0 = *p1 * *p4 * .5;
      }
    }
    if (deriv2) {
      p0 = w2 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)  *n * n_2dCols,sizeof(double));
      p1 = Det2_th2;p2 = eta2;
      for (i=0;i<ntot;i++) for (k=i;k<ntot;k++) {
      	p3 = Det3;p4 = Det4;
        p5 = eta1 + i * *n;p6 = eta1 + k * *n;p7 = p3 + *n;
        for (;p3<p7;p0++,p2++,p3++,p4++,p5++,p6++)
	   *p0 = *p4 * *p5 * *p6 + *p3 * *p2;
        if (i < *n_theta) {
           p0 -= *n;p3 = Det3_th + i * *n;
           p4 = eta1 + k * *n;p5 = p0 + *n;
           for (;p0 < p5;p0++,p3++,p4++) *p0 += *p3 * *p4;
        }
        if (k < *n_theta) {
           p0 -= *n;p3 = Det3_th + k * *n;
           p4 = eta1 + i * *n;p5 = p0 + *n;
           for (;p0 < p5;p0++,p3++,p4++) *p0 += *p3 * *p4;
           if (i < *n_theta) {
             p0 -= *n;
             for (;p0 < p5;p0++,p1++) *p0 += *p1;
           }
	}
        p0 -= *n;p5 = p0 + *n;
        for (;p0 < p5;p0++) *p0 *= .5;
      }
    }

    if (*type==0) {

      wi=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
      for (i=0;i< *n;i++) { wi[i]=1/fabs(w[i]);}

      Tk = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n * ntot,sizeof(double));
      rc_prod(Tk,wi,w1,&ntot,n);
      if (deriv2) {
        Tkm = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)  *n * n_2dCols,sizeof(double));
        rc_prod(Tkm,wi,w2,&n_2dCols,n);
      }
      FREE(wi);
    } else {
      Tk = w1;
      Tkm = w2;
    }
  }

  if (ML) {

    for (p0=R,p1=R,j=0;j<rank;j++,p1+=nr) {
      for (p2=p1,i=0;i<=j;i++,p0++,p2++) *p0 = *p2;
      for (i=j+1;i<rank;i++,p0++) *p0 = 0.0;
    }
  }

  get_bSb(P0,P1,P2,sp,E,rS,rSncol,Enrow,&rank,M,n_theta,PKtz,b1,b2,deriv);

  if (ML) {
    *ldet = MLpenalty1(ldet1,ldet2,Tk,Tkm,nulli,X,R,Q1,nind,sp,rS,rSncol,
		       &rank,n,Mp,M,n_theta,&neg_w,rank_tol,deriv,nt,type);

  } else get_ddetXWXpS(ldet1,ldet2,P,K,sp,rS,rSncol,Tk,Tkm,n,&rank,&rank,M,n_theta,deriv,*nt);

  if (*deriv) {

    for (j = ntot - 1;j>=0;j--) {
      p0 = b1 + rank * j;
      for (i=0;i< rank;i++) beta[pivot1[i]] = p0[i];
      undrop_rows(beta,*q,1,drop,n_drop);
      p1 = b1 + *q * j;
      for (p0=beta,p2=p0 + *q;p0<p2;p0++,p1++) *p1 = *p0;
    }
  }

  for (i=0;i< rank;i++) beta[pivot1[i]] = PKtz[i];
  undrop_rows(beta,*q,1,drop,n_drop);

  nr = *n + *Enrow;
  WX = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) ( (nr + *nt * rank) * rank),sizeof(double));
  for (p0=w,p1=w + *n,p2=wf;p0<p1;p0++,p2++) *p0 = sqrt(*p2);
  for (p3=X,p0 = WX,i=0;i<rank;i++) {
    for (p1=w,p2=w+*n;p1<p2;p1++,p0++,p3++) *p0 = *p3 * *p1;
    for (j=0;j<*Enrow;j++,E++,p0++) *p0 = *E;
  }

  pivot = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)rank,sizeof(int));
  tau = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)rank*(*nt+1),sizeof(double));

  mgcv_pqr(WX,&nr,&rank,pivot,tau,nt);
  R1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)rank*rank,sizeof(double));
  getRpqr(R1,WX,&nr,&rank,&rank,nt);
  Rinv(P,R1,&rank,&rank,&rank);
  FREE(R1);
  Q = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) nr * rank,sizeof(double));
  for (i=0;i< rank;i++) Q[i * rank + i] = 1.0;
  tp=0;mgcv_pqrqy(Q,WX,tau,&nr,&rank,&rank,&tp,nt);
  for (p1=Q,p0=K,j=0;j<rank;j++,p1 += *Enrow) for (i=0;i<*n;i++,p1++,p0++) *p0 = *p1;
  FREE(Q);FREE(WX);FREE(tau);
  pivoter(P,&rank,&rank,pivot,&False,&True);
  FREE(pivot);
  for (p1=P,i=0;i < rank; i++) for (j=0;j<rank;j++,p1++) rV[pivot1[j] + i * rank] = *p1;
  undrop_rows(rV,*q,rank,drop,n_drop);
  p0 = rV + *q * rank;p1 = rV + *q * *q;
  for (p2=p0;p2<p1;p2++) *p2 = 0.0;

  for (p0=X,p1=K,p2=K + rank * *n;p1<p2;p0++,p1++) *p0 = *p1;

  for (p0 = X + rank * *n,p1 = X + *q * *n;p0<p1;p0++) *p0 = 0.0;

  *rank_est = rank;

  if (*deriv) {
    FREE(eta1);
    if (*type==0) FREE(Tk);
    if (deriv2) {
      FREE(b2);FREE(eta2);FREE(w2);
      if (*type==0) FREE(Tkm);
      FREE(dev_hess);
    }
  }
  if (neg_w) {
    FREE(Vt);FREE(nind);
  }
  FREE(PKtz);FREE(nulli);FREE(drop);
  FREE(work);FREE(R);FREE(Rh);FREE(pivot1);FREE(K);
  FREE(P);FREE(Q1);

}

void gdi1(double *X,double *E,double *Es,double *rS,double *U1,
	  double *sp,double *z,double *w,double *wf,double *alpha,double *mu,double *eta, double *y,
	 double *p_weights,double *g1,double *g2,double *g3,double *g4,double *V0,
	  double *V1,double *V2,double *V3,double *beta,double *b1,double *w1,
         double *D1,double *D2,double *P0, double *P1,double *P2,double *trA,
    double *trA1,double *trA2,double *rV,double *rank_tol,double *conv_tol, int *rank_est,
	 int *n,int *q, int *M,int *Mp,int *Enrow,int *rSncol,int *deriv,
	  int *REML,int *fisher,int *fixed_penalty,int *nt,double *dVkk)

{ double *WX,*tau,*work,*p0,*p1,*p2,*p3,*K=NULL,
    *R1,*Vt,xx,*b2,*P,*Q,
    *af1=NULL,*af2=NULL,*a1,*a2,*eta1=NULL,*eta2=NULL,
    *PKtz,*v1,*v2,*wi,*w2,*pw2,*Tk,*Tkm,*Tfk=NULL,*Tfkm=NULL,
         *pb2, *dev_grad,*dev_hess=NULL,
    ldetXWXS=0.0,reml_penalty=0.0,bSb=0.0,*R,*Rh,
    *alpha1,*alpha2,*raw,*Q1,*nulli;
  int i,j,k,*pivot=NULL,*pivot1,ScS,*pi,rank,tp,bt,ct,iter=0,m,one=1,
    n_2dCols=0,n_b2,n_drop,*drop,nt1,
      n_eta1=0,n_eta2=0,n_work,deriv2,neg_w=0,*nind,nr,True=1,False=0;

  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  m = omp_get_num_procs();
  if (*nt > m || *nt < 1) *nt = m;
  omp_set_num_threads(*nt);
  #else
  *nt = 1;
  #endif
  nt1 = *nt;

  if (*deriv==2) deriv2=1; else deriv2=0;

  ScS=0;for (pi=rSncol;pi<rSncol + *M;pi++) ScS+= *pi;

  PKtz = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*q,sizeof(double));
  nulli = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*q,sizeof(double));
  drop = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)*q,sizeof(int));
  raw = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(double));
  n_work = (4 * *n + 2 * *q) * *M + 2 * *n;
  k = 5 * *q; if (n_work < k) n_work = k;
  k = (*M * (1 + *M))/2 * *n; if (n_work < k) n_work = k;

  work = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) n_work,sizeof(double));

  if (*n < *q) nr = *n + *Enrow; else nr = *q + *Enrow;

  R = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*q * nr,sizeof(double));
  Rh = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*q * *q,sizeof(double));
  pivot1=(int *)CALLOC((size_t)*q,sizeof(int));
  if (deriv2) dev_hess = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *q * *q,sizeof(double)); else dev_hess=NULL;
  K = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n * *q,sizeof(double));
  P = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *q * *q,sizeof(double));
  Q1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n * *q,sizeof(double));

  for (i=0;i< *n;i++)
    if (w[i]<0) { neg_w++;raw[i] = sqrt(-w[i]);}
    else raw[i] = sqrt(w[i]);

  if (neg_w) {
    Vt = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *q * *q,sizeof(double));
    nind = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)neg_w,sizeof(int));
    k=0;for (i=0;i< *n;i++) if (w[i]<0) { nind[k]=i;k++;}
  } else { nind = (int *)NULL; Vt = (double *)NULL;}

  gdiPK(work,X,E,Es,rS,U1,z,raw,
        R,Rh,nulli,dev_hess,P,K,Vt,PKtz,Q1,
        nind,pivot1,drop,
        n,q,Mp,neg_w,nt,Enrow,
        &rank,&n_drop,
        deriv2,ScS,REML,
        rank_tol,&ldetXWXS,&False);

  FREE(raw);

  if (*deriv) {
    n_2dCols = (*M * (1 + *M))/2;
    n_b2 = rank * n_2dCols;
    b2 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)n_b2,sizeof(double));

    n_eta1 = *n * *M;
    eta1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)n_eta1,sizeof(double));
    Tk = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)n_eta1,sizeof(double));

    n_eta2 = *n * n_2dCols;
    eta2 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)n_eta2,sizeof(double));
    Tkm = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)n_eta2,sizeof(double));

    w2 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)n_eta2,sizeof(double));

    v1 = work;v2=work + *n * *M;

    a1=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
    a2=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
    alpha1=alpha2 =(double *)NULL;
    if (*fisher) {

      for (i=0;i< *n;i++) a1[i] = -  w[i] *(V1[i] + 2*g2[i])/g1[i];

      for (i=0;i< *n;i++)
        a2[i] = a1[i]*(a1[i]/w[i]-g2[i]/g1[i]) - w[i]*(V2[i]-V1[i]*V1[i] + 2*g3[i]-2*g2[i]*g2[i])/(g1[i]*g1[i]) ;

    } else {

      alpha1 = (double *) CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
      alpha2 = (double *) CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
      for (i=0;i< *n;i++) {
        xx = V2[i]-V1[i]*V1[i]+g3[i]-g2[i]*g2[i];
        alpha1[i] = (-(V1[i]+g2[i]) + (y[i]-mu[i])*xx)/alpha[i];
        alpha2[i] = (-2*xx + (y[i]-mu[i])*(V3[i]-3*V1[i]*V2[i]+2*V1[i]*V1[i]*V1[i]+g4[i]-3*g3[i]*g2[i]+2*g2[i]*g2[i]*g2[i]))/alpha[i];
      }

      for (i=0;i<*n;i++) a1[i] = w[i]*(alpha1[i]-V1[i]-2*g2[i])/g1[i];

      for (i=0;i<*n;i++) a2[i] = a1[i]*(a1[i]/w[i]-g2[i]/g1[i]) -
                                 w[i]*(alpha1[i]*alpha1[i] - alpha2[i] + V2[i]-V1[i]*V1[i] + 2*g3[i]-2*g2[i]*g2[i])/(g1[i]*g1[i]) ;

      if (! *REML) {
        af1=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
        af2=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));

        for (i=0;i< *n;i++) af1[i] = -  wf[i] *(V1[i] + 2*g2[i])/g1[i];

        for (i=0;i< *n;i++)
        af2[i] = af1[i]*(af1[i]/wf[i]-g2[i]/g1[i]) - wf[i]*(V2[i]-V1[i]*V1[i] + 2*g3[i]-2*g2[i]*g2[i])/(g1[i]*g1[i]) ;
      }

      FREE(alpha1);FREE(alpha2);

    }

    for (i=0;i< *n ;i++) v1[i] = -2*p_weights[i]*(y[i]-mu[i])/(V0[i]*g1[i]);
    dev_grad=(double *)CALLOC((size_t) rank,sizeof(double));
    bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(dev_grad,X,v1,&bt,&ct,&rank,&one,n);

    if (deriv2) {
       for (p0=dev_hess,p1=p0 + rank * rank;p0<p1;p0++) *p0 *= 2.0;
    }

  }
  else {
    v1=v2=b1=(double *)NULL;
    a1=a2=dev_grad=w2=b2=(double *)NULL;
    Tk=Tkm=(double *)NULL;
  }

  if (*deriv) {

    ift1(R,Vt,X,rS,PKtz,sp,w,a1,b1,b2,eta1,eta2,n,&rank,M,rSncol,&deriv2,&neg_w,&nr);
    if (*M>0) {
      i=0;mgcv_mmult(work,Rh,b1,&i,&i,&rank,M,&rank);

      getXtX(dVkk,work,&rank,M);
    }

    rc_prod(w1,a1,eta1,M,n);
    if (deriv2) {
      rc_prod(w2,a1,eta2,&n_2dCols,n);
      for (pw2=w2,m=0;m < *M;m++) for (k=m;k < *M;k++) {
        rc_prod(v1,eta1 + *n * m,eta1 + *n * k,&one,n);
        rc_prod(v2,a2,v1,&one,n);
        p1=v2 + *n;
        for (p0=v2;p0<p1;p0++,pw2++) *pw2 += *p0;
      }
    }

    wi=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
    for (i=0;i< *n;i++) { wi[i]=1/fabs(w[i]);}

    rc_prod(Tk,wi,w1,M,n);
    if (deriv2) rc_prod(Tkm,wi,w2,&n_2dCols,n);

    if (! *REML && ! *fisher) {
      rc_prod(w1,af1,eta1,M,n);
        Tfk = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)n_eta1,sizeof(double));
        Tfkm = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)n_eta2,sizeof(double));
      if (deriv2) {
        rc_prod(w2,af1,eta2,&n_2dCols,n);
        for (pw2=w2,m=0;m < *M;m++) for (k=m;k < *M;k++) {
          rc_prod(v1,eta1 + *n * m,eta1 + *n * k,&one,n);
          rc_prod(v2,af2,v1,&one,n);
          p1=v2 + *n;
          for (p0=v2;p0<p1;p0++,pw2++) *pw2 += *p0;
        }
      }
      for (i=0;i< *n;i++) { wi[i]=1/wf[i];}
      rc_prod(Tfk,wi,w1,M,n);
      if (deriv2) rc_prod(Tfkm,wi,w2,&n_2dCols,n);
      FREE(af1);FREE(af2);
    }
    else {Tfk = Tfkm = NULL;}

    bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(D1,b1,dev_grad,&bt,&ct,M,&one,&rank);

    if (deriv2) {
      getXtMX(D2,b1,dev_hess,&rank,M,v1);

      for (pb2=b2,m=0;m < *M;m++) for (k=m;k < *M;k++) {
          p1 = dev_grad + rank;
          for (xx=0.0,p0=dev_grad;p0<p1;p0++,pb2++) xx += *p0 * *pb2;
          D2[k * *M + m] += xx;
          D2[m * *M + k] = D2[k * *M + m];
      }
    }

  } else {wi=NULL;}

  if (*REML<0) {

    for (p0=R,p1=R,j=0;j<rank;j++,p1+=nr) {
      for (p2=p1,i=0;i<=j;i++,p0++,p2++) *p0 = *p2;
      for (i=j+1;i<rank;i++,p0++) *p0 = 0.0;
    }
  } else { FREE(R);FREE(Q1);FREE(nind); }

  if (*REML) {
    get_bSb(&bSb,trA1,trA2,sp,E,rS,rSncol,Enrow,&rank,M,&False,PKtz,b1,b2,deriv);
    if (*deriv) for (p2=D2,p1=trA2,i = 0; i< *M;i++) {
        D1[i] += trA1[i];
        if (deriv2) for (j=0;j<*M;j++,p1++,p2++) *p2 += *p1;
    }
  }

  pearson2(P0,P1,P2,y,mu,V0,V1,V2,g1,g2,p_weights,eta1,eta2,*n,*M,*deriv,deriv2);

  if (*REML) {
    j = *n - *Mp;
    i = 0;
    if (i) {
      *P0 += bSb;
       if (*deriv) for (p0=trA1,p1 = P1,p2 = P1 + *M;p1<p2;p1++,p0++) *p1 += *p0;
       if (*deriv>1) for (p0=trA2,p1 = P2,p2 = P2 + *M * *M;p1<p2;p1++,p0++) *p1 += *p0;
    }
    *P0 /= j;

    if (*deriv) for (p1 = P1,p2 = P1 + *M;p1<p2;p1++) *p1 /= j;
    if (*deriv>1) for (p1 = P2,p2 = P2 + *M * *M;p1<p2;p1++) *p1 /= j;
  }

  if (*deriv) {

    for (j = *M-1;j>=0;j--) {
      p0 = b1 + rank * j;
      for (i=0;i< rank;i++) beta[pivot1[i]] = p0[i];
      undrop_rows(beta,*q,1,drop,n_drop);
      p1 = b1 + *q * j;
      for (p0=beta,p2=p0 + *q;p0<p2;p0++,p1++) *p1 = *p0;
    }
  }

  for (i=0;i< rank;i++) beta[pivot1[i]] = PKtz[i];
  undrop_rows(beta,*q,1,drop,n_drop);

  if (*REML>0) {

    reml_penalty = ldetXWXS;
    get_ddetXWXpS(trA1,trA2,P,K,sp,rS,rSncol,Tk,Tkm,n,&rank,&rank,M,&False,deriv,*nt);
  }

  if (*REML<0) {

    reml_penalty =  MLpenalty1(trA1,trA2,Tk,Tkm,nulli,X,R,Q1,nind,sp,rS,rSncol,
			       &rank,n,Mp,M,&False,&neg_w,rank_tol,deriv,nt,&False);

    FREE(R);FREE(Q1);FREE(nind);
  }

  if (neg_w) FREE(Vt);
  FREE(work);FREE(PKtz);

  if (*deriv) {

    FREE(eta1);
    FREE(eta2);
    FREE(a1);FREE(a2);FREE(wi);FREE(dev_grad);

    FREE(w2);FREE(b2);

    if (deriv2) { FREE(dev_hess);}
  }

  if (*fisher) {
    wf = w;Tfk=Tk;Tfkm=Tkm;
  } else {

    nr = *n + *Enrow;

    WX = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) ( (nr + *nt * rank) * rank),sizeof(double));
    for (p0=w,p1=w + *n,p2=wf;p0<p1;p0++,p2++) *p0 = sqrt(*p2);
    for (p3=X,p0 = WX,i=0;i<rank;i++) {
      for (p1=w,p2=w+*n;p1<p2;p1++,p0++,p3++) *p0 = *p3 * *p1;
      for (j=0;j<*Enrow;j++,E++,p0++) *p0 = *E;
    }

    pivot = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)rank,sizeof(int));

    tau = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)rank*(*nt+1),sizeof(double));

    mgcv_pqr(WX,&nr,&rank,pivot,tau,&nt1);

    R1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)rank*rank,sizeof(double));
    getRpqr(R1,WX,&nr,&rank,&rank,&nt1);

    Rinv(P,R1,&rank,&rank,&rank);
    FREE(R1);

    Q = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) nr * rank,sizeof(double));

    for (i=0;i< rank;i++) Q[i * rank + i] = 1.0;
    tp=0;mgcv_pqrqy(Q,WX,tau,&nr,&rank,&rank,&tp,&nt1);

    for (p1=Q,p0=K,j=0;j<rank;j++,p1 += *Enrow) for (i=0;i<*n;i++,p1++,p0++) *p0 = *p1;
    FREE(Q);FREE(WX);FREE(tau);
    if (*deriv)  pivoter(rS,&rank,&ScS,pivot,&False,&False);

  }

  if (*REML) i=0; else i = *deriv;
  get_trA2(trA,trA1,trA2,P,K,sp,rS,rSncol,Tfk,Tfkm,wf,n,&rank,&rank,M,&i,nt);

  if (!*fisher) {
    pivoter(P,&rank,&rank,pivot,&False,&True);
    FREE(pivot);
  }

  for (p1=P,i=0;i < rank; i++) for (j=0;j<rank;j++,p1++) rV[pivot1[j] + i * rank] = *p1;
  undrop_rows(rV,*q,rank,drop,n_drop);
  p0 = rV + *q * rank;p1 = rV + *q * *q;
  for (p2=p0;p2<p1;p2++) *p2 = 0.0;

  for (p0=X,p1=K,p2=K + rank * *n;p1<p2;p0++,p1++) *p0 = *p1;

  for (p0 = X + rank * *n,p1 = X + *q * *n;p0<p1;p0++) *p0 = 0.0;

  *rank_est = rank;

  FREE(drop);
  FREE(nulli);
  FREE(pivot1);
  FREE(P);FREE(K);
  FREE(Rh);
  if (*deriv) {
    FREE(Tk);FREE(Tkm);
    if (! *REML && ! *fisher) { FREE(Tfk);FREE(Tfkm);}
  }

  if (*REML) {*rank_tol = reml_penalty;*conv_tol = bSb;}

  *deriv = iter;
}

void R_cond(double *R,int *r,int *c,double *work,double *Rcondition)

{ double kappa,*pm,*pp,*y,*p,ym,yp,pm_norm,pp_norm,y_inf=0.0,R_inf=0.0;
  int i,j,k;

  pp=work;work+= *c;pm=work;work+= *c;
  y=work;work+= *c;p=work;
  for (i=0;i<*c;i++) p[i] = 0.0;
  for (k=*c-1;k>=0;k--) {
      yp = (1-p[k])/R[k + *r *k];
      ym = (-1-p[k])/R[k + *r *k];
      for (pp_norm=0.0,i=0;i<k;i++) { pp[i] = p[i] + R[i + *r * k] * yp;pp_norm += fabs(pp[i]);}
      for (pm_norm=0.0,i=0;i<k;i++) { pm[i] = p[i] + R[i + *r * k] * ym;pm_norm += fabs(pm[i]);}
      if (fabs(yp)+pp_norm >= fabs(ym)+pm_norm) {
	  y[k]=yp;
          for (i=0;i<k;i++) p[i] = pp[i];
      } else {
          y[k]=ym;
          for (i=0;i<k;i++) p[i] = pm[i];
      }
      kappa=fabs(y[k]);
      if (kappa>y_inf) y_inf=kappa;
  }
  for (i=0;i<*c;i++) {
    for (kappa=0.0,j=i;j<*c;j++) kappa += fabs(R[i + *r * j]);
    if (kappa>R_inf) R_inf = kappa;
  }
  kappa=R_inf*y_inf;
  *Rcondition=kappa;
}

void pls_fit1(double *y,double *X,double *w,double *wy,double *E,double *Es,int *n,int *q,int *rE,double *eta,
              double *penalty,double *rank_tol,int *nt,int *use_wy)

{ int i,j,k,rank,one=1,*pivot,*pivot1,left,tp,neg_w=0,*nind,bt,ct,nr,rr,n_drop=0,*drop,True=1,False=0,nz;
  double *z,*WX,*tau,Rcond,xx,zz,zz1,*work,*Q,*Q1,*IQ,*raw,*d,*Vt,*p0,*p1,
    *R1,*tau1,Rnorm,Enorm,*R,*Xp;
  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  int m;

  m = omp_get_num_procs();
  if (*nt > m || *nt < 1) *nt = m;
  omp_set_num_threads(*nt);
  #else
  *nt = 1;
  #endif
  rr = *q;if (rr>*n) rr = *n;
  nr = rr + *rE;
  nz = *n; if (nz<nr) nz=nr;
  z = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) nz,sizeof(double));
  raw = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(double));

  for (i=0;i< *n;i++)
    if (w[i]<0) { neg_w++;raw[i] = sqrt(-w[i]);}
    else raw[i] = sqrt(w[i]);

  if (neg_w) {
    nind = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)neg_w,sizeof(int));
    k=0;for (i=0;i< *n;i++) if (w[i]<0) { nind[k]=i;k++;}
  } else { nind = (int *)NULL;}

  for (i=0;i< *n;i++) {
    z[i] = y[i]*raw[i];
  }

  for (i=0;i<neg_w;i++) {k=nind[i];z[k] = -z[k];}

  WX = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) ( (*n + *nt * *q) * *q),sizeof(double));
  for (Xp=X,p0=WX,j=0;j<*q;j++) {
    for (p1=raw,i=0;i<*n;i++,p1++,p0++,Xp++) {
      *p0 = *Xp * *p1;

    }
  }

  tau=(double *)CALLOC((size_t) *q * (*nt + 1),sizeof(double));

  pivot=(int *)CALLOC((size_t)*q,sizeof(int));

  mgcv_pqr(WX,n,q,pivot,tau,nt);

  R1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) rr * *q,sizeof(double));

  getRpqr(R1,WX,n,q,&rr,nt);

  pivoter(R1,&rr,q,pivot,&True,&True);

  Rnorm = frobenius_norm(R1,&rr,q);
  Enorm =  frobenius_norm(Es,rE,q);

  R = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*q * nr,sizeof(double));
  for (j=0;j<*q;j++) {
    for (i=0;i< rr;i++) R[i + nr * j] = R1[i + rr * j]/Rnorm;
    for (i=0;i< *rE;i++) R[i + rr + nr * j] = Es[i + *rE * j]/Enorm;
  }

  tau1=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)*q,sizeof(double));
  pivot1=(int *)CALLOC((size_t)*q,sizeof(int));
  mgcv_qr(R,&nr,q,pivot1,tau1);

  work = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(4 * *q),sizeof(double));
  rank = *q; if (nr < rank) rank = nr;
  R_cond(R,&nr,&rank,work,&Rcond);
  while (*rank_tol * Rcond > 1) { rank--;R_cond(R,&nr,&rank,work,&Rcond);}

  n_drop = *q - rank;
  if (n_drop) {
    drop = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)n_drop,sizeof(int));
    for (i=0;i<n_drop;i++) drop[i] = pivot1[rank+i];
    qsort(drop,n_drop,sizeof(int),icompare);

    drop_cols(R1,rr,*q,drop,n_drop);
    drop_cols(E,*rE,*q,drop,n_drop);
  } else {drop=NULL;}

  for (j=0;j<rank;j++) {
    for (i=0;i< rr;i++) R[i + nr * j] = R1[i + rr * j];
      for (i=0;i< *rE;i++) R[i + rr + nr * j] = E[i + *rE * j];
  }
  FREE(R1);
  mgcv_qr(R,&nr,&rank,pivot1,tau1);

  if (neg_w) {
    Q = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) nr * rank,sizeof(double));
    for (i=0;i< rank;i++) Q[i * nr + i] = 1.0;
    left=1;tp=0;mgcv_qrqy(Q,R,tau1,&nr,&rank,&rank,&left,&tp);

    Q1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n * rank,sizeof(double));

    for (i=0;i<rr;i++) for (j=0;j<rank;j++) Q1[i + rr * j] = Q[i + nr * j];
    tp=0;mgcv_pqrqy(Q1,WX,tau,n,&rr,&rank,&tp,nt);

    FREE(Q);

    if (neg_w < rank+1) k = rank+1; else k = neg_w;
    IQ = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) k * rank,sizeof(double));
    for (i=0;i<neg_w;i++) {
      p0 = IQ + i;p1 = Q1 + nind[i];
      for (j=0;j<rank;j++,p0+=k,p1+= *n) *p0 = *p1;
    }
    FREE(Q1);

    Vt = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) rank * rank,sizeof(double));
    d = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)  rank,sizeof(double));
    mgcv_svd_full(IQ,Vt,d,&k,&rank);
    FREE(IQ);
    for (i=0;i<rank;i++) {
      d[i] = 1 - 2*d[i]*d[i];
      if (d[i]< - *rank_tol) {
        *n = -1;
        FREE(Vt);FREE(d);FREE(pivot);FREE(tau);
        FREE(nind);FREE(raw);FREE(z);FREE(work);FREE(WX);
        FREE(tau1);FREE(pivot1);FREE(R);if (n_drop) FREE(drop);
        return;
      }
      if (d[i]<=0) d[i]=0.0; else d[i] = 1/d[i];
    }

  } else {Vt = d = (double *)NULL; }

  if (!*use_wy) {

    tp=1;mgcv_pqrqy(z,WX,tau,n,&rr,&one,&tp,nt);
    j = rank;
    for (i=j;i<nz;i++) z[i]=0.0;
    left=1,tp=1;
    mgcv_qrqy(z,R,tau1,&nr,&one,&rank,&left,&tp);

    for (i=rank;i < nz;i++) z[i]=0.0;

    for (i=0;i<rank;i++) y[i] = work[i+*q] = z[i];

    if (neg_w) {
       bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(work,Vt,z,&bt,&ct,&rank,&one,&rank);
       for (i=0;i<rank;i++) work[i] *= d[i];
       bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(z,Vt,work,&bt,&ct,&rank,&one,&rank);
       for (i=0;i<rank;i++) y[i] = z[i];
    }

    left=1,tp=0; mgcv_qrqy(z,R,tau1,&nr,&one,&rank,&left,&tp);

    for (*penalty=0.0,i=rr;i<nr;i++) *penalty += z[i]*z[i];

    for (i=rank;i < *n;i++) z[i]=0.0;

    tp=0;mgcv_pqrqy(z,WX,tau,n,&rr,&one,&tp,nt);

    for (i=0;i<*n;i++) eta[i] = z[i]/raw[i];
  }

  bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(work,X,wy,&bt,&ct,q,&one,n);
  drop_rows(work,*q,1,drop,n_drop);
  pivoter(work,&rank,&one,pivot1,&False,&False);

  if (!*use_wy) {
    for (zz=zz1=0,i=0;i<rank;i++) {
      for (xx=0,j=0;j<=i;j++) xx += R[j + nr * i] * work[*q + j];
      xx -= work[i];
      zz1 += xx*xx; zz += work[i]*work[i];
    }
    if (zz1 > *rank_tol * zz) {
      *use_wy = 1;
    }
  }

  if (*use_wy) {
    for (k=0;k<rank;k++) {
      for (xx=0.0,j=0;j < k;j++) xx += R[j + nr * k]*z[j];
      z[k] = (work[k] - xx)/R[k + nr * k];
    }
    if (neg_w) {
      bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(work,Vt,z,&bt,&ct,&rank,&one,&rank);
      for (i=0;i<rank;i++) work[i] *= d[i];
      bt=1;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(z,Vt,work,&bt,&ct,&rank,&one,&rank);
    }
    for (i=0;i<rank;i++) y[i] = z[i];
  }

  for (k=rank-1;k>=0;k--) {
      for (xx=0.0,j=k+1;j < rank;j++) xx += R[k + nr * j]*z[j];
      z[k] = (y[k] - xx)/R[k + nr * k];
  }

  for (i=0;i< rank;i++) y[pivot1[i]] = z[i];

  undrop_rows(y,*q,1,drop,n_drop);

  if (*use_wy) {

    bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(eta,X,y,&bt,&ct,n,&one,q);
    bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_mmult(work,E,y,&bt,&ct,rE,&one,q);
    for (*penalty=0.0,i=0;i < *rE;i++) *penalty += work[i]*work[i];
  }

  FREE(z);
  FREE(WX);FREE(tau);FREE(pivot);FREE(raw);
  FREE(R);FREE(pivot1);FREE(tau1);  FREE(work);
  if (n_drop) FREE(drop);
  if (neg_w) { FREE(nind);FREE(d);FREE(Vt);}
}

# --- End of mgcv/src/gdi.c ---

void R_init_mgcv(DllInfo *dll)
{
    R_registerRoutines(dll, CEntries, CallMethods, NULL, NULL);
    R_useDynamicSymbols(dll, FALSE);
    R_RegisterCCallable("mgcv","mgcv_pmmult2", (DL_FUNC) &mgcv_pmmult2);
    R_RegisterCCallable("mgcv","pls_fit1", (DL_FUNC) &pls_fit1);
    R_RegisterCCallable("mgcv","gdi2", (DL_FUNC) &gdi2);
}

# --- Start of mgcv/src/magic.c ---

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <math.h>

#ifdef _OPENMP
#include <omp.h>
#endif

#include "general.h"
#include "mgcv.h"
#include "matrix.h"

double ***array3d(int ni,int nj,int nk)

{ double ***a,***p,**p1,*p2;
  int j;
  a=(double ***)CALLOC((size_t)(ni),sizeof(double **));
  *a=(double **)CALLOC((size_t)(ni*nj),sizeof(double *));
  **a=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)(ni*nj*nk),sizeof(double));
  p2 = **a; p1= *a;p=a;
  for (p=a;p<a+ni;p++)
  { *p = p1;
    for (j=0;j<nj;j++,p2+=nk,p1++) *p1 = p2;
  }
  return(a);
}

void free3d(double ***a)
{ FREE(**a);FREE(*a);FREE(a);
}

double **array2d(int ni,int nj)

{ double **a,*p,**dum;
  a=(double **)CALLOC((size_t)ni,sizeof(double *));
  *a=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)(ni*nj),sizeof(double));
  for (p= *a,dum=a;dum<a+ni;dum++,p+=nj) *dum = p;
  return(a);
}

void free2d(double **a) {FREE(*a);FREE(a);}

void fit_magic(double *X,double *sp,double **S,double *H,double *gamma,double *scale,
               int *control,double rank_tol,double yy,double *y0,double *y1,double *U1,
               double *V,double *d,double *b,double *score,double *norm,double *delta,int *rank,
               double *norm_const,int *n_score,int *nt)

{ double *St,*p,*p1,xx,*R,*Vt,*a,trA,yAy,yAAy;
  int i,j,k,m,n,q,rank_S=-1,r;
  m=control[4];n=control[1];q=control[2];

  St=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)(q*q),sizeof(double));
  if (control[3])
  for (p=St;p<St+q*q;p++,H++) *p = *H;
  for (k=0;k<m;k++) { xx=exp(sp[k]);for (p=St,p1=S[k];p<St+q*q;p++,p1++) *p += *p1 * xx;}

  if (m>0||control[3]) mroot(St,&rank_S,&q);
  else rank_S=0;

  r=rank_S+q;
  R=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)(r*q),sizeof(double));
  getRpqr(R,X,&n,&q,&r,nt);

  for (j=0;j<q;j++) for (i=q;i<r;i++) R[i+r*j]=St[(i-q)+rank_S*j];

  a=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)q,sizeof(double));
  Vt=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)(q*q),sizeof(double));
  mgcv_svd_full(R,Vt,d,&r,&q);

  *rank=q;xx=d[0]*rank_tol;
  while(d[*rank-1]<xx) (*rank)--;

  for (i=0;i<q;i++) for (j=0;j< *rank;j++) V[i+q*j]=Vt[j+q*i];

  for (i=0;i<q;i++) for (j=0;j< *rank;j++) U1[i+q*j]=R[i+r*j];

  for (i=0;i< *rank;i++) { for (xx=0.0,j=0;j<q;j++) xx += U1[j + i*q]*y0[j];y1[i]=xx;}

  for (yAy=0.0,i=0;i< *rank;i++) yAy += y1[i]*y1[i];
  for (i=0;i<q;i++) { for (xx=0.0,j=0;j< *rank;j++) xx += U1[i + q*j]*y1[j];b[i]=xx;}
  for (yAAy=0.0,i=0;i<q;i++) yAAy += b[i]*b[i];
  *norm=yy-2*yAy+yAAy;
  if (*norm<0.0) *norm=0.0;

  for (trA=0.0,i=0;i<q* *rank;i++) trA += U1[i]*U1[i];

  for (i=0;i<*rank;i++) a[i]=y1[i]/d[i];
  for (i=0;i<q;i++) { for (xx=0.0,j=0;j< *rank;j++) xx += V[i + q*j]*a[j];b[i]=xx;}

  n = *n_score;
  xx = n - *gamma * trA;*delta=xx;
  if (control[0]) {*score = n* (*norm+*norm_const)/(xx*xx);*scale= (*norm + *norm_const)/(n-trA);}
  else {*score = (*norm + *norm_const) / n - 2* *scale / n * xx + *scale; }
  FREE(a);FREE(Vt);FREE(R);FREE(St);
}

double *crude_grad(double *X,double *sp,double **Si,double *H,double *gamma,double *scale,
               int *control,double rank_tol,double yy,double *y0,double *y1,double *U1,
               double *V,double *d,double *b,double *score,double *norm,double *delta,int *rank,
		   double *norm_const,int *n_score,int *nt)

{ double ftol=1e-6,*grad,sc1,sc0,ds;
  int i;
  fit_magic(X,sp,Si,H,gamma,scale,control,rank_tol,yy,y0,y1,U1,V,d,b,&sc0,norm,delta,rank,norm_const,n_score,nt);
  grad=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)control[4],sizeof(double));
  for (i=0;i<control[4];i++)
  { ds=fabs(sp[i])*ftol;
    sp[i] += ds;
    fit_magic(X,sp,Si,H,gamma,scale,control,rank_tol,yy,y0,y1,U1,V,d,b,&sc1,norm,delta,rank,norm_const,n_score,nt);
    grad[i]=(sc1-sc0)/ds;sp[i] -= ds;
  }
  return(grad);
}

double **crude_hess(double *X,double *sp,double **Si,double *H,double *gamma,double *scale,
               int *control,double rank_tol,double yy,double *y0,double *y1,double *U1,
               double *V,double *d,double *b,double *score,double *norm,double *delta,int *rank,
		    double *norm_const,int *n_score,int *nt)

{ int m,i,j;
  double *g0,*g1,**hess,ftol=1e-4,ds;
  m=control[4];
  hess=array2d(m,m);
  g0=crude_grad(X,sp,Si,H,gamma,scale,control,rank_tol,yy,y0,y1,U1,V,d,b,score,norm,delta,rank,norm_const,n_score,nt);
  for (i=0;i<m;i++)
  { ds=fabs(sp[i])*ftol;
    sp[i] += ds;
    g1=crude_grad(X,sp,Si,H,gamma,scale,control,rank_tol,yy,y0,y1,U1,V,d,b,score,norm,delta,rank,norm_const,n_score,nt);
    for (j=0;j<m;j++) hess[i][j] = (g1[j]-g0[j])/ds;
    sp[i] -= ds;
  }
  return(hess);
}

void magic_gH(double *U1U1,double **M,double **K,double *VS,double **My,double **Ky,double **yK,double **hess,
              double *grad,double *dnorm,double *ddelta,double *sp,double **d2norm,double **d2delta,double *S,
              double *U1,double *V,double *d,double *y1,int rank,int q,int m,int *cS,int *cucS,int gcv,double *gamma,double *scale,
              double norm,double delta,int n,double *norm_const)

{ double *p,*p1,*p2,*p3,*p4,xx,xx1,x1,x2,*VSi;
  int i,j,*ip,bt,ct,r,c,tid=0;
  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("magic_gH");
  #endif

  getXtX(U1U1,U1,&q,&rank);

  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  #pragma omp parallel private(i,j,ip,bt,ct,c,r,p,p1,p2,p3,p4,xx,tid,VSi)
  #endif
  {
    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp for
    #endif
    for (i=0;i<m;i++) {
      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
      tid = omp_get_thread_num();
      #endif
      ip = cS + i;
      p = S + cucS[i] * q;
      bt=1;ct=0;r=rank;c= *ip;
      VSi = VS + tid * q * q;
      mgcv_mmult(VSi,V,p,&bt,&ct,&r,&c,&q);
      for (p2=VSi,j=0;j<*ip;j++)
      for (p1=d;p1<d+rank;p1++,p2++) *p2 /= *p1;
      bt=1;ct=0;r= *ip;c= rank;
      mgcv_mmult(M[i],VSi,U1U1,&bt,&ct,&r,&c,&rank);
      bt=ct=0;r=c=rank;
      mgcv_mmult(K[i],VSi,M[i],&bt,&ct,&r,&c,ip);
      bt=0;ct=1;r=c=rank;
      mgcv_mmult(M[i],VSi,VSi,&bt,&ct,&r,&c,ip);
      for (p1=My[i],p2=M[i];p1<My[i]+rank;p1++)
      { for (xx=0.0,p3=y1;p3<y1+rank;p3++,p2++) xx += *p3 * *p2;*p1 = xx;}
      for (p1=yK[i],p2=K[i];p1<yK[i]+rank;p1++)
      { for (xx=0.0,p3=y1;p3<y1+rank;p3++,p2++) xx += *p3 * *p2;*p1 = xx;}
      for (p1=Ky[i],p2=K[i];p1<Ky[i]+rank;p1++,p2++)
      { for (xx=0.0,p4=p2,p3=y1;p3<y1+rank;p3++,p4+=rank) xx += *p3 * *p4;*p1 = xx;}
    }
  }

  for (i=0;i<m;i++)
  { for (xx=0.0,p=K[i];p<K[i]+rank*rank;p+=rank+1) xx += *p;ddelta[i]= *gamma * xx * exp(sp[i]);
    for (j=0;j<=i;j++)
    { for (xx=0.0,p=M[j],p1=K[i];p<M[j]+rank*rank;p++,p1++) xx += *p * *p1;
      d2delta[j][i]=d2delta[i][j]= - *gamma*2*exp(sp[i]+sp[j])*xx;
    }
    d2delta[i][i]+=ddelta[i];

    for (xx=0.0,p=y1,p1=Ky[i],p2=My[i];p<y1+rank;p++,p1++,p2++) xx += *p *( *p2 - *p1);
    dnorm[i] =2*exp(sp[i])*xx;
    for (j=0;j<=i;j++)
    { for (xx=0.0,p1=My[i],p2=My[j],p3=Ky[i],p4=Ky[j],p=yK[i];p1<My[i]+rank;p++,p1++,p2++,p3++,p4++)
      xx += (*p1 * *p4 + *p2 * *p3 - 2 * *p1 * *p2 + *p * *p2);
      d2norm[i][j]=d2norm[j][i]=  xx*2*exp(sp[i]+sp[j]);
    }
    d2norm[i][i]+=dnorm[i];
  }

  if (gcv)
  { norm += *norm_const;
    xx = n/(delta*delta);xx1= xx*2*norm/delta;
    x1 = -2*xx/delta;x2=3*xx1/delta;
    for (i=0;i<m;i++)
    { grad[i]=xx*dnorm[i]-xx1*ddelta[i];
      for (j=0;j<=i;j++)
      hess[i][j]=hess[j][i]=x1*(ddelta[j]*dnorm[i]+ddelta[i]*dnorm[j])+
                 xx*d2norm[i][j]+x2*ddelta[i]*ddelta[j] -
                 xx1*d2delta[i][j];
    }
  } else
  { for (i=0;i<m;i++)
    { grad[i]=( dnorm[i] - 2* *scale*ddelta[i])/ n;
      for (j=0;j<=i;j++) hess[i][j]=hess[j][i]=(d2norm[i][j]-2* *scale*d2delta[i][j])/ n;
    }
  }
  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("end\n");
  #endif
}

void magic(double *y,double *X,double *sp0,double *def_sp,double *S,double *H,double *L,double *lsp0,
           double *gamma,double *scale,int *control,int *cS,double *rank_tol,double *tol,double *b,
           double *rV,double *norm_const,int *n_score,int *nt)

{ int *pi,*pivot,q,n,autoinit,ScS,m,mp,i,j,tp,k,use_sd=0,rank,converged,iter=0,ok,*cucS,
    gcv,try,fit_call=0,step_fail=0,max_half,*spok,def_supplied,use_dsyevd=0,
    L_exists,True=1,False=0;
  double *sp=NULL,*p,*p1,*p2,*tau,xx,*y1,*y0,yy,**Si=NULL,*work,score,*sd_step,*n_step,*U1,*V,*d,**M,**K,
         *VS,*U1U1,**My,**Ky,**yK,*dnorm,*ddelta,**d2norm,**d2delta,norm,delta,*grad,**hess,*nsp,
    min_score,*step,d_score=1e10,*ev=NULL,*u,msg=0.0,Xms,*rSms,*bag,*bsp,sign,*grad1,*u0,*R;
  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  m = omp_get_num_procs();
  if (*nt > m || *nt < 1) *nt = m;
  omp_set_num_threads(*nt);
  #else
  *nt = 1;
  #endif

  gcv=control[0];q=control[2];n=control[1];m=control[4];max_half=control[5];mp=control[6];

  tau=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)q *(1 + *nt),sizeof(double));
  pivot=(int *)CALLOC((size_t)q,sizeof(int));

  mgcv_pqr(X,&n,&q,pivot,tau,nt);

  cucS = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)m,sizeof(int));
  for (i=1;i<m;i++) cucS[i] = cucS[i-1] + cS[i-1];
  ScS=0;for (pi=cS;pi<cS+m;pi++) ScS+= *pi;

  work=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)q,sizeof(double));
  for (p=S,i=0;i<ScS;i++,p+=q)
  { for (pi=pivot,p2=work;p2<work+q;pi++,p2++) *p2 = p[*pi];
    for (p1=p,p2=work;p1<p+q;p1++,p2++) *p1 = *p2;
  }

  if (control[3])
  { for (j=0;j<q;j++)
     { for (i=0;i<q;i++) work[i]=H[pivot[i]+q*j];
      for (i=0;i<q;i++) H[i+q*j]=work[i];
    }
    for (i=0;i<q;i++)
    { for (j=0;j<q;j++) work[j]=H[i+pivot[j]*q];
      for (j=0;j<q;j++) H[i+q*j]=work[j];
    }
  }

  y0=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)n,sizeof(double));
  for (p=y,p1=y0;p<y+n;p++,p1++) *p1 = *p;
  tp=1;i=1;
  mgcv_pqrqy(y0,X,tau,&n,&q,&i,&tp,nt);

  for (yy=0.0,p=y;p<y+n;p++) yy += *p * *p;

  if (m>0)
  { Si=array2d(m,q*q);
    i=0;j=1;
    for (p=S,k=0;k<m;p+=cS[k]*q,k++)  mgcv_mmult(Si[k],p,p,&i,&j,&q,&q,cS+k);
  }

  if (mp<0) { L_exists=0;mp=m;} else L_exists=1;

  if (m>0) sp = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)m,sizeof(double));

  autoinit=0;for (p=sp0;p<sp0+mp;p++) if (*p <=0.0) { autoinit=1;break;}
  def_supplied=1; for (p=def_sp;p<def_sp+mp;p++) if (*p <=0.0) { def_supplied=0;break;}

  if (L_exists&&!def_supplied)
    error(_("magic requires smoothing parameter starting values if L supplied"));

  R = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)q*q,sizeof(double));
  getRpqr(R,X,&n,&q,&q,nt);

  if (m>0&&!def_supplied)
  { rSms=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)m,sizeof(double));

    Xms=0.0;for (j=0;j<q;j++) for (i=0;i<=j;i++) { xx=R[i+q*j];Xms+=xx*xx;}
    p=S;Xms/=n*q;
    for (i=0;i<m;i++)
    { for (xx=0.0,p=Si[i];p<Si[i]+q*q;p+=q+1) xx += *p;
      rSms[i]=xx/(q*cS[i]);
      def_sp[i]=Xms/rSms[i];
    }
  } else { Xms=0.0;rSms=NULL;}

  if (autoinit) for (i=0;i<mp;i++) sp0[i]=log(def_sp[i]);
  else for (i=0;i<mp;i++) sp0[i]=log(sp0[i]);

  if (L_exists) {
    i=0;j=1;if (mp) mgcv_mmult(sp,L,sp0,&i,&i,&m,&j,&mp);
    for (p=sp,p1=lsp0,p2=sp+m;p<p2;p++,p1++) *p += *p1;
  } else {
    for (i=0;i<m;i++) sp[i]=sp0[i];
  }

  y1=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)q,sizeof(double));
  U1=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)(q*q),sizeof(double));
  V=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)(q*q),sizeof(double));
  d=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)q,sizeof(double));
  if (mp>0)
  { M=array2d(m,q*q);K=array2d(m,q*q);
    VS=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)(q * q * *nt),sizeof(double));
    My=array2d(m,q);Ky=array2d(m,q);yK=array2d(m,q);
    hess=array2d(m,m);
    grad=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)mp,sizeof(double));
    grad1=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)m,sizeof(double));
    dnorm=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)m,sizeof(double));
    ddelta=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)m,sizeof(double));
    nsp=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)mp,sizeof(double));
    d2norm=array2d(m,m);d2delta=array2d(m,m);
    ev=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)mp,sizeof(double));
    u=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)(m*m),sizeof(double));
    u0=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)(m*mp),sizeof(double));
    U1U1=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)(q*q),sizeof(double));
    spok=(int *)CALLOC((size_t)m,sizeof(int));

    bsp=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)m,sizeof(double));
    bag=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)m,sizeof(double));
  } else
  { M=K=My=Ky=yK=hess=d2norm=d2delta=NULL;
    u0=VS=grad1=grad=dnorm=ddelta=nsp=ev=u=U1U1=bsp=bag=NULL;
    spok=NULL;
  }

  fit_magic(X,sp,Si,H,gamma,scale,control,*rank_tol,yy,y0,y1,U1,V,d,b,&score,&norm,&delta,&rank,norm_const,n_score,nt);
  fit_call++;

  if (mp>0&&!autoinit)
  { magic_gH(U1U1,M,K,VS,My,Ky,yK,hess,grad1,dnorm,ddelta,sp,d2norm,d2delta,S,
             U1,V,d,y1,rank,q,m,cS,cucS,gcv,gamma,scale,norm,delta,*n_score,norm_const);
    xx=1e-4*(1+fabs(score));
    ok=1;

    if (L_exists) {
      i=0;j=1;mgcv_mmult(grad,L,grad1,&j,&i,&mp,&j,&m);
    } else {
      p = grad;grad=grad1;grad1=p;
    }

    for (i=0;i<mp;i++) if (fabs(grad[i])<xx)
    { sp0[i]=log(def_sp[i]);ok=0;

    }

    if (L_exists) {
      i=0;j=1;mgcv_mmult(sp,L,sp0,&i,&i,&m,&j,&mp);
      for (p=sp,p1=lsp0,p2=sp+m;p<p2;p++,p1++) *p += *p1;
    } else {
      for (i=0;i<m;i++) sp[i]=sp0[i];
    }

    if (!ok) {
      fit_magic(X,sp,Si,H,gamma,scale,control,*rank_tol,yy,y0,y1,
      U1,V,d,b,&score,&norm,&delta,&rank,norm_const,n_score,nt);
      fit_call++;
    }
  }

  min_score=score;
  sd_step=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)mp,sizeof(double));
  n_step=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)mp,sizeof(double));

  if (autoinit&&!def_supplied)
  {
    for (p=S,k=0;k<m;k++)
    { for (j=0;j<cS[k];j++)
      { for (xx=0.0,i=0;i<q;i++,p++) xx += *p * b[i];
        d[j]=xx;
      }
      for (xx=0.0,j=0;j<cS[k];j++) xx+= d[j]*d[j];
      sd_step[k]=log(*scale * cS[k]/xx)-sp[k];
    }
    use_sd=1;

  }

  if (mp>0)
  { converged=0;iter=0;
    while (!converged)
    { iter++;
      if (iter>400) error(_("magic, the gcv/ubre optimizer, failed to converge after 400 iterations."));
      if (iter>1||(autoinit&&!def_supplied)) ok=1;
      else ok=0;
      try=0;
      if (use_sd) step=sd_step; else step=n_step;
      while (ok)
      { try++; if (try==4&&!use_sd) {use_sd=1;step=sd_step;}
        for (i=0;i<mp;i++) nsp[i]=sp0[i]+step[i];
        if (L_exists) {
          i=0;j=1;mgcv_mmult(sp,L,nsp,&i,&i,&m,&j,&mp);
          for (p=sp,p1=lsp0,p2=sp+m;p<p2;p++,p1++) *p += *p1;
        } else {
          for (i=0;i<m;i++) sp[i]=nsp[i];
        }
        fit_magic(X,sp,Si,H,gamma,scale,control,*rank_tol,yy,y0,y1,
                  U1,V,d,b,&score,&norm,&delta,&rank,norm_const,n_score,nt);
        fit_call++;
        if (score<min_score)
        { ok=0;
          d_score=min_score-score;
          min_score=score;
          for (i=0;i<mp;i++) sp0[i]=nsp[i];
        } else
        for (i=0;i<mp;i++) step[i]/=2;
        if (try==(max_half-1)&&ok)
        for (i=0;i<mp;i++) step[i]=0.0;
        if (try==max_half) {ok=0;}
      }
      if (iter>3)
      { converged=1;
        if (d_score> *tol*(1+min_score)) converged=0;
        for (xx=0.0,i=0;i<mp;i++) xx+=grad[i]*grad[i];
	xx=sqrt(xx);
        if (xx>pow(*tol,1/3.0)*(1+fabs(min_score))) converged=0;
        if (try==max_half) converged=1;
        if (converged) { msg=sqrt(xx*xx/mp);if (try==max_half) step_fail=1;}
      }

      { if (L_exists) {
          i=0;j=1;mgcv_mmult(sp,L,sp0,&i,&i,&m,&j,&mp);
          for (p=sp,p1=lsp0,p2=sp+m;p<p2;p++,p1++) *p += *p1;
        } else {
          for (i=0;i<m;i++) sp[i]=sp0[i];
        }
        magic_gH(U1U1,M,K,VS,My,Ky,yK,hess,grad1,dnorm,ddelta,sp,d2norm,d2delta,S,
		 U1,V,d,y1,rank,q,m,cS,cucS,gcv,gamma,scale,norm,delta,*n_score,norm_const);

        for (i=0;i<m;i++) for (j=0;j<m;j++) u[i+m*j]=hess[i][j];
	if (L_exists) {
          i=0;j=1;mgcv_mmult(grad,L,grad1,&j,&i,&mp,&j,&m);
          mgcv_mmult(u0,L,u,&j,&i,&mp,&m,&m);
          mgcv_mmult(u,u0,L,&i,&i,&mp,&mp,&m);
        } else {
          p = grad;grad=grad1;grad1=p;
        }

        mgcv_symeig(u,ev,&mp,&use_dsyevd,&True,&False);
        use_sd=0;for (p=ev;p<ev+mp;p++) if (*p<0.0) {use_sd=1;break;}
        if (!use_sd)
        { for (i=0;i<mp;i++) { for (xx=0.0,j=0;j<mp;j++) xx+=u[j+mp*i]*grad[j];sd_step[i]=xx/ev[i];}
          for (i=0;i<mp;i++) { for (xx=0.0,j=0;j<mp;j++) xx+=u[i+mp*j]*sd_step[j];n_step[i]= -xx;}
          for (xx=fabs(n_step[0]),i=1;i<mp;i++) if (fabs(n_step[i])>xx) xx=fabs(n_step[i]);
          if (xx>5.0)
          { xx=5.0/xx;for (i=0;i<mp;i++) n_step[i]*=xx;}
        }
        for (xx=fabs(grad[0]),i=1;i<mp;i++) if (xx<fabs(grad[i])) xx=fabs(grad[i]);
        for (i=0;i<mp;i++) sd_step[i]= -grad[i]/xx;
      }
    }

    for (k=0;k<mp;k++)
    { ok=5;xx=2.0;
      if (grad[k]<0.0) sign=1; else sign=-1;
      while (ok)
      { sp0[k] += sign*xx;
        if (L_exists) {
          i=0;j=1;mgcv_mmult(sp,L,sp0,&i,&i,&m,&j,&mp);
          for (p=sp,p1=lsp0,p2=sp+m;p<p2;p++,p1++) *p += *p1;
        } else {
          for (i=0;i<m;i++) sp[i]=sp0[i];
        }
        ok--;
        fit_magic(X,sp,Si,H,gamma,scale,control,*rank_tol,yy,y0,y1,U1,V,d,b,&score,&norm,&delta,&rank,norm_const,n_score,nt);
        if (score<min_score)
        { min_score=score;
        } else
        { ok=0;sp0[k] += -sign*xx;}
      }
    }

    if (L_exists) {
      i=0;j=1; mgcv_mmult(sp,L,sp0,&i,&i,&m,&j,&mp);
      for (p=sp,p1=lsp0,p2=sp+m;p<p2;p++,p1++) *p += *p1;
    } else {
      for (i=0;i<m;i++) sp[i]=sp0[i];
    }
    fit_magic(X,sp,Si,H,gamma,scale,control,*rank_tol,yy,y0,y1,U1,V,d,b,&score,&norm,&delta,&rank,norm_const,n_score,nt);

    free2d(M);free2d(K);free2d(My);free2d(Ky);free2d(yK);free2d(hess);
    free2d(d2norm);free2d(d2delta);FREE(U1U1);FREE(rSms);FREE(u);
    FREE(VS);FREE(grad);FREE(dnorm);FREE(ddelta);FREE(nsp);FREE(ev);
    FREE(bsp);FREE(bag);FREE(spok);FREE(grad1);FREE(u0);
  }

  for (p2=V,p1=d;p1<d+rank;p1++)
  for (j=0;j<q;j++,p2++) *p2 /= *p1;

  for (p1=V,p2=rV;p2<rV+q*rank;p2+=q)
  for (pi=pivot;pi<pivot+q;pi++,p1++) p2[*pi] = *p1;

  for (i=0;i<q;i++) d[i]=b[i];
  for (i=0;i<q;i++) b[pivot[i]]=d[i];
  for (i=0;i<mp;i++) sp0[i]=exp(sp0[i]);
  *gamma = score;
  *tol = msg;
  control[0]=rank;
  control[1]=1-step_fail;
  control[2]=1-use_sd;
  control[3]=iter;
  control[4]=fit_call;

  if (m>0) {FREE(sp);free2d(Si);}

  for (i=0;i<q;i++) {
    for (j=0;j<i;j++) V[i + q * j] = 0.0;
    for (j=i;j<q;j++) V[i + q * j] = R[i + q * j];
  }

  pivoter(V,&q,&q,pivot,&True,&True);
  for (p1=X,p2=V,p=V+q*q;p2<p;p1++,p2++) *p1 = *p2;

  FREE(tau);FREE(pivot);FREE(work);FREE(y0);FREE(y1);
  FREE(U1);FREE(V);FREE(d);FREE(sd_step);
  FREE(n_step);FREE(R);FREE(cucS);

}

# --- End of mgcv/src/magic.c ---

# --- Start of mgcv/src/mat.c ---

#include <stdlib.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <float.h>

#ifdef _OPENMP
#include <omp.h>
#endif

#include "mgcv.h"
#include <R.h>

void mgcv_omp(int *a) {
#ifdef OPENMP_ON
  *a=1;
#else
  *a=0;
#endif
}

void rpmat(double *A,int n) {

  int i,j;
  for (i=0;i<n;i++) {
    Rprintf("\n");
    for (j=0;j<n;j++) Rprintf("%7.2g  ",A[i + n * j]);
  }
  Rprintf("\n");
}

void dump_mat(double *M,int *r,int*c,const char *path) {

  FILE *mf;
  mf = fopen(path,"wb");
  if (mf == NULL) {
    Rprintf("\nFailed to open file\n");
    return;
  }
  fwrite(r,sizeof(int),1,mf); fwrite(c,sizeof(int),1,mf);
  fwrite(M,sizeof(double),*r * *c,mf);
  fclose(mf);
}

void read_mat(double *M,int *r,int*c,char *path) {

 size_t j;
 FILE *mf;
 mf = fopen("/home/sw283/tmp/badmat.dat","rb");
 if (mf == NULL) {
    Rprintf("\nFailed to open file\n");
    return;
 }
 if (*r < 1) {
   j=fread(r,sizeof(int),1,mf); j=fread(c,sizeof(int),1,mf);
 } else {
   j=fread(r,sizeof(int),1,mf); j=fread(c,sizeof(int),1,mf);
   j=fread(M,sizeof(double),*r * *c,mf);
   if (j!= *r * *c)  Rprintf("\nfile dim problem\n");
 }
 fclose(mf);
}

void up2lo(double * A, int n) {

  double *pu,*pl,*plf;
  int i;
  for (i=0;i<n;i++)
    for (plf = A + i * (ptrdiff_t) n, pu=pl=plf + i,plf=plf+n,pl++,pu+=n;pl<plf;pl++,pu+=n) *pl = *pu;

}

void row_squash(double *X,int rnew,int rold,int col) {

  int c;
  double *Xnew,*Xold,*X1;
  Xnew = X;
  for (c=0;c<col;c++)
    for (Xold = X + c * (ptrdiff_t) rold,X1=Xold+rnew;Xold<X1;Xold++,Xnew++) *Xnew = *Xold;
}

void tile_ut(int n,int *m,int *K, int *C,int *R,int *B) {

  double x,dn=0.0;
  int i,N,kk,l,nb,r,c;
  (*m) ++;
  while (dn<1 && *m>1) { (*m)--;dn = n/(double) *m; }
  N = (*m * (*m + 1))/2;

  K[0]=0;
  for (x=0.0,i=1;i < *m;i++) { x+=dn; K[i] = (int) floor(x);}
  K[*m] = n;
  if (*m % 2) {
    kk=l=nb=B[0]=C[0]=R[0]=0;i=1;
    for (r=0;r < *m;r++) for (c=r+1;c < *m;c++,i++) {
      if (kk==(*m - 1)/2) {
	  kk=0;l++;
	  C[i]=R[i]=l;
	  nb++;B[nb]=i;i++;
      }
      C[i] = c;R[i]=r;kk++;
    }
  } else {
    nb=l=kk=B[0]=i=0;
    for (r=0;r < *m;r++) for (c=r+1;c < *m;c++) {
	if (kk == *m/2) {kk=0;nb++;B[nb]=i;}
	if (kk==0 && l < *m) {
          R[i]=C[i]=l;l++;i++;R[i]=C[i]=l;l++;i++;kk++;
	  if (kk == *m/2) { kk = 0;nb++;B[nb]=i;}
	}
        R[i]=r;C[i]=c;kk++;i++;
    }
  }
  B[*m] = N;
}

void pdtrmm(int *n,int *q,double *alpha, double *A,int *lda,double *D,int *ldd,int *nt,int *iwork,double *work) {

  int i,j,m,N,*K,*C,*R,*B,*off,r,c,nr,nc,ldt;
  double *p0,*p1,*p2,*p3,*p4,*p5,zero=0.0;
  char side ='L',nope='N',up='U';
  m = *nt;N = (m * (m + 1))/2;
  K = iwork;iwork += m+1;C = iwork;iwork += N;
  R = iwork; iwork += N;B = iwork;iwork += m+1;off = iwork;
  tile_ut(*n,&m,K,C,R,B);
  N = (m * (m + 1))/2;
  off[0] = 0;
  for (i=1;i<N;i++) off[i] = off[i-1] + K[R[i-1]+1] - K[R[i-1]];

  ldt = off[N-1] + K[R[N-1]+1] - K[R[N-1]];

  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  #pragma omp parallel for private(i,j,r,c,nr,p0,p1,p2,p3,p4,p5,nc) num_threads(m)
  #endif
  for (i=0;i<m;i++) {
    for (j=B[i];j<B[i+1];j++) {
      r = R[j]; c = C[j]; nr = K[r+1] - K[r];
      if (r==c) {

	for (p0=D+K[c],p1=work+off[j],p2 = D + (ptrdiff_t) *ldd * *q;p0<p2;p0 += *ldd,p1 += ldt)
	  for (p3=p0,p4=p3+nr,p5=p1;p3<p4;p3++,p5++) *p5 = *p3;

        F77_CALL(dtrmm)(&side,&up,&nope,&nope,&nr,q,alpha,A + K[r] + K[c] * (ptrdiff_t) *lda,
			lda,work+off[j],&ldt FCONE FCONE FCONE FCONE);

      } else {
  	nc = K[c+1] - K[c];
	F77_CALL(dgemm)(&nope,&nope,&nr,q,&nc,alpha,A + K[r] + K[c] * (ptrdiff_t) *lda,
			lda,D + K[c],ldd,&zero,work+off[j],&ldt FCONE FCONE);
      }
    }
  }

  for (p0 = D,p2 = D + (ptrdiff_t) *ldd * *q;p0<p2;p0 += *ldd) for (p1=p0,p3=p0 + *n;p1<p3;p1++) *p1 = 0.0;
  for (i=0;i<N;i++) {
    r = R[i];nr = K[r+1]-K[r];
    for (p0=work + off[i],p1 = D+K[r];p1<p2;p0 += ldt,p1 += *ldd)
      for (p3=p0,p4=p1,p5=p4 + nr;p4<p5;p4++,p3++) *p4 += *p3;
  }

}

void pdsyrk(int *n,int *k,double *a,double *A,int *lda,double *b,double *D, int *ldd,int *work,int *nt) {

  int i,j,N,m,*K,*C,*R,*B,l,r,c,nb;
  char uplo = 'U',trans = 'T',ntrans = 'N';
  m = *nt;N = (m * (m + 1))/2;
  K = work;work += m+1;C = work;work += N;
  R = work; work += N;B = work;
  tile_ut(*n,&m,K,C,R,B);

  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  #pragma omp parallel for private(i,j,r,c,nb,l) num_threads(m)
  #endif
  for (i=0;i<m;i++) {
    for (j=B[i];j<B[i+1];j++) {
      r=R[j];c=C[j];
      if (r==c) {
	nb = K[r+1]-K[r];
        F77_CALL(dsyrk)(&uplo,&trans,&nb,k,a,A + (ptrdiff_t) *lda * K[r],lda,b,
			D + (ptrdiff_t) *ldd * K[c] + K[r],ldd FCONE FCONE);
      } else {
        nb = K[r+1]-K[r]; l = K[c+1]-K[c];
	F77_CALL(dgemm)(&trans,&ntrans,&nb,&l,k,a,A + (ptrdiff_t)*lda * K[r],lda,A + *lda * K[c],lda,b,
			D + (ptrdiff_t) *ldd * K[c] + K[r],ldd FCONE FCONE);

      }
    }
  }

}

void mgcv_tensor_mm(double *X,double *T,int *d,int *m,int *n) {

  ptrdiff_t start, i,j,k, tp=1, xp=0,pd;
  double *Xj,*Xj1,*Xk, *Tk,*p,*p1,*p2;

  for (i=0;i<*m;i++) { xp += d[i];tp *= d[i];}
  Xk = X + (xp-d[*m-1]) * *n;
  Tk = T + (tp-d[*m-1]) * *n;

  p = Xk;p1 = Xk + *n * (ptrdiff_t) d[*m-1];p2 = Tk;
  for (;p<p1;p++,p2++) *p2 = *p;
  pd = d[*m-1];
  for (i = *m - 2;i>=0;i--) {
    Xk -= *n * (ptrdiff_t) d[i];
    Xj = Xk;
    start = tp - pd * d[i];
    p = T + start * *n;
    for (j=0;j<d[i];j++) {
      p1 = Tk;
      Xj1 = Xj + *n;

      for (k=0;k<pd;k++) for (p2 = Xj;p2<Xj1;p2++,p1++,p++) *p = *p1 * *p2;
      Xj += *n;
    }
    pd *= d[i];
    Tk = T + (tp - pd) * *n;
  }
}

SEXP mgcv_tmm(SEXP x,SEXP t,SEXP D,SEXP M, SEXP N) {

  double *X,*T;
  int *d,*m,*n;
  X = REAL(x);T=REAL(t);
  d = INTEGER(D);
  m = INTEGER(M);
  n = INTEGER(N);
  mgcv_tensor_mm(X,T,d,m,n);
  return(R_NilValue);
}

void mgcv_mmult0(double *A,double *B,double *C,int *bt,int *ct,int *r,int *c,int *n)

{ double xx,*bp,*cp,*cp1,*cp2,*cp3,*ap,*ap1;
  int cr,i,j;
  if (*bt)
  { if (*ct)
    {

      for (i=0;i<*r;i++)
      { cp1 = C + *c;

        xx = *B;
        for (ap=A,cp=C;cp<cp1;cp++,ap+= *r) { *ap = *cp; *cp *= xx;}
        B++;cp2=cp1;
        for (j=1;j< *n;j++,B++)
	    for (xx= *B,cp=C;cp<cp1;cp++,cp2++) *cp += xx * *cp2;

        for (ap=A,cp=C;cp<cp1;cp++,ap+= *r) { xx = *ap; *ap = *cp; *cp = xx;}
        A++;
      }
    } else {
      cr= *n;cp2 = C + *c * (ptrdiff_t) cr;
      for (ap=A,cp1=C;cp1< cp2;cp1+=cr) for (bp=B,i=0;i< *r;i++,ap++)
      { for (xx=0.0,cp=cp1,cp3=cp1+ *n;cp< cp3;cp++,bp++) xx += *cp * *bp;
        *ap=xx;
      }
    }
  } else
  { if (*ct)
    { cp = C;
      for (j=0;j< *c;j++)
      { ap1 = A + *r;C=cp;xx = *C;bp=B;
        for (ap=A;ap<ap1;ap++,bp++) *ap = xx * *bp ;
        C += *c;
        for (i=1;i< *n;i++,C+= *c)
	for (xx=*C,ap=A;ap<ap1;ap++,bp++) *ap += xx * *bp;
        A=ap1;cp++;
      }
    } else
    { for (j=0;j< *c;j++)
      { ap1 = A + *r;xx = *C;bp=B;
        for (ap=A;ap<ap1;ap++,bp++) *ap = xx * *bp ;
        C++;
        for (i=1;i< *n;i++,C++)
	for (xx=*C,ap=A;ap<ap1;ap++,bp++) *ap += xx * *bp;
        A=ap1;
      }
    }
  }
}

void mgcv_mmult(double *A,double *B,double *C,int *bt,int *ct,int *r,int *c,int *n) {

  char transa='N',transb='N';
  int lda,ldb,ldc;
  double alpha=1.0,beta=0.0;
  if (*r<=0||*c<=0||*n<=0) return;
  if (B==C) {
    if (*bt&&(!*ct)&&(*r==*c)) { getXtX(A,B,n,r);return;}
    else if (*ct&&(!*bt)&&(*r==*c)) { getXXt(A,B,c,n);return;}
  }
  if (*bt) {
    transa = 'T';
    lda = *n;
  } else  lda = *r;

  if (*ct) {
    transb = 'T';
    ldb = *c;
  } else ldb = *n;

  ldc = *r;
  F77_CALL(dgemm)(&transa,&transb,r,c,n, &alpha,
		B, &lda,C, &ldb,&beta, A, &ldc FCONE FCONE);
}

SEXP wdiag(SEXP a,SEXP IND,SEXP B) {

  int r,k,*ind,*ik;
  double *A,*b;
  A = REAL(a); b = REAL(B); ind = INTEGER(IND);
  r = nrows(a); k = length(B);
  for (ik = ind + k;ind < ik;ind++, b++) {
    k = *ind - 1; A[k+k*r] = *b;
  }
  return(R_NilValue);
}

SEXP mrow_sum(SEXP x,SEXP M, SEXP K) {

  int i,j,p,n,nm,*m,*k,*kp,*p1;
  double *X,xx,*B;
  SEXP b;
  nm = length(M);
  X = REAL(x);
  M = PROTECT(coerceVector(M,INTSXP));
  K = PROTECT(coerceVector(K,INTSXP));
  m = INTEGER(M); k = INTEGER(K);
  p = ncols(x); n = nrows(x);
  b = PROTECT(allocMatrix(REALSXP,nm,p));
  B = REAL(b);
  for (j=0;j<p;j++,X+=n) {
    for (kp=k,i=0;i<nm;i++) {
      for(xx=0.0,p1=k+m[i];kp<p1;kp++) xx += X[*kp];
      *B = xx;B++;
    }
  }
  UNPROTECT(3);
  return(b);
}

SEXP mgcv_madi(SEXP a, SEXP b,SEXP ind,SEXP diag) {

  int dia,*ii,ij,n,i,j,k;
  double *A,*B;
  SEXP kr;
  dia = asInteger(diag);n = nrows(a); k = length(ind);
  ind = PROTECT(coerceVector(ind,INTSXP));
  b = PROTECT(coerceVector(b,REALSXP));
  a = PROTECT(coerceVector(a,REALSXP));
  ii = INTEGER(ind);
  A = REAL(a);
  B = REAL(b);
   if (dia==0) {
    for (j=0;j<k;j++) {
      ij = (ii[j]-1)*n-1;
      for (i=0;i<k;i++,B++) A[ii[i] + ij] += *B;
    }
  } else if (dia > 0) {
    for (i=0;i<k;i++,B++) {
      ij = (ii[i]-1)*(n+1); A[ij] += *B;
    }
  } else {
    for (i=0;i<k;i++) {
      ij = (ii[i]-1)*(n+1);
        A[ij] += *B;
     }
  }

  PROTECT(kr=allocVector(REALSXP,1));
  REAL(kr)[0] = 1.0;
  UNPROTECT(4);
  return(kr);
}

SEXP mgcv_pmmult2(SEXP b, SEXP c,SEXP bt,SEXP ct, SEXP nthreads) {

  double *A,*B,*C;
  int r,col,n,nt,Ct,Bt;
  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  int m;
  #endif

  SEXP a;

  nt = asInteger(nthreads);
  Bt = asInteger(bt);
  Ct = asInteger(ct);

  if (Bt) {
    r = ncols(b);
    n = nrows(b);
  } else {
    r = nrows(b);
    n = ncols(b);
  }

  if (Ct) col = nrows(c);
  else col = ncols(c);

  B = REAL(b);C=REAL(c);
  a = PROTECT(allocMatrix(REALSXP,r,col));
  A = REAL(a);
  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  m = omp_get_num_procs();
  if (nt > m || nt < 1) nt = m;

  #else

  nt = 1;
  #endif
  mgcv_pmmult(A,B,C,&Bt,&Ct,&r,&col,&n,&nt);
  UNPROTECT(1);
  return(a);
}

int mgcv_bchol0(double *A,int *piv,int *n,int *nt,int *nb) {

  int i,j,k,l,q,r=-1,*pk,*pq,jb,n1,m,N,*a,b;
  double tol=0.0,*dots,*pd,*p1,*Aj,*Aq0,*Aj0,*Aj1,*Ajn,*Ail,xmax,x,*Aq,*Ajj,*Aend;
  dots = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(double));
  for (pk = piv,i=0;i < *n;pk++,i++) *pk = i;
  jb = *nb;
  n1 = *n + 1;
  Ajn = A;
  m = *nt;if (m<1) m=1;if (m>*n) m = *n;
  a = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) (*nt+1),sizeof(int));
  a[m] = *n;
  for (k=0;k<*n;k+= *nb) {
    if (*n - k  < jb) jb = *n - k ;
    for (pd = dots + k,p1 = dots + *n;pd<p1;pd++) *pd = 0;
    for (j=k;j<k+jb;j++,Ajn += *n) {
      pd = dots + j;Aj = Ajn + j; Aj1 = Aj - 1;
      xmax = -1.0;q=j;p1 = dots + *n;
      if (j>k) for (;pd<p1;pd++,Aj1 += *n) *pd += *Aj1 * *Aj1;
      for (l=j,pd = dots + j;pd<p1;pd++,Aj += n1,l++) {
        x = *Aj - *pd;
        if (x>xmax) { xmax = x;q=l;}
      }
      if (j==0) tol = *n * xmax * DBL_EPSILON;
      Aq = A + *n * q + q;

      if (*Aq - dots[q]<tol) {r = j;break;}

      pd = dots + j;p1 = dots + q;
      x = *pd;*pd = *p1;*p1 = x;

      pk = piv + j;pq = piv +q;
      i = *pk;*pk = *pq;*pq = i;

      Aend = A + *n * *n;
      Aj = Ajn + j; Aq = Ajn + q;
      for (;Aj<Aend;Aj += *n,Aq += *n) {
        x = *Aj;*Aj = *Aq;*Aq = x;
      }

      Aj = Ajn; Aq = A + *n * q;Aend = Aj + *n;
      for (;Aj < Aend;Aj++,Aq++)  {
        x = *Aj;*Aj = *Aq;*Aq = x;
      }

      Ajj = Ajn + j;

      *Ajj = sqrt(*Ajj - *pd);
      Aend = A + *n * *n;
      if (j > k&&j < *n) {
        Aj = Ajn + *n;
        Aq = Aj + k;
        Aj += j;
        Aj1 = Ajn + k;
        for (;Aj<Aend;Aj += *n,Aq += *n) {
	  for (pd = Aj1,p1=Aq;pd < Ajj;pd++,p1++) *Aj -= *pd * *p1;
          /

    if (r > 0) break;

    if (k + jb < *n) {

      N = *n - j;
      if (m > N) { m = N;a[m] = *n; }
      *a = j;
      x = (double) N;x = x*x / m;

      for (i=1;i < m;i++) a[i] = round(N - sqrt(x*(m-i)))+j;
      for (i=1;i <= m;i++) {
          if (a[i]<=a[i-1]) a[i] = a[i-1]+1;
      }
      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
#pragma omp parallel private(b,i,l,Aj,Aend,Aq,Aj1,Ail,Aj0,Aq0) num_threads(m)
      #endif
      {
        #ifdef OPENMP_ON
        #pragma omp for
        #endif
        for (b=0;b<m;b++)
	for (i=a[b];i<a[b+1];i++) {
          Aj0 = A + i * *n;
          Aq0 = Aj0 + k;
          Aend = Aj0 + j;
          Ail = Aj1 = Aj0 + i;
          Aj0 += k;
	  for (l=i;l<*n;l++,Aq0 += *n,Aj1++,Ail += *n) {

            Aj = Aj0;
            Aq = Aq0;
            for (;Aj < Aend;Aj++,Aq++) *Aj1 -= *Aq * *Aj;

            *Ail = *Aj1;
          }
	}
      }
    }
  }
  if (r<0) r = *n;
  FREE(dots);

  for (Ajn=A,j=0;j<*n;j++,Ajn += *n) {
    Aj = Ajn;Aend = Aj + *n;
    if (j<r) Aj += j+1; else Aj += r;
    for (;Aj<Aend;Aj++) *Aj = 0.0;
  }
  FREE(a);
  return(r);
}

int mgcv_bchol(double *A,int *piv,int *n,int *nt,int *nb) {

  int i,j,k,l,q,r=-1,*pk,*pq,jb,n1,m,N,*a,one=1,*work;
  double tol=0.0,*dots,*pd,*p1,*Aj,*Aj1,*Ajn,xmax,x,*Aq,*Ajj,*Aend,alpha=-1.0,beta=1.0;
  char uplo = 'L',trans='N';
  dots = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(double));
  work = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) *nt * (*nt+3) + 2,sizeof(int));
  for (pk = piv,i=0;i < *n;pk++,i++) *pk = i;
  jb = *nb;
  n1 = *n + 1;
  Ajn = A;
  m = *nt;if (m<1) m=1;if (m>*n) m = *n;
  a = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) (*nt+1),sizeof(int));
  a[m] = *n;
  for (k=0;k<*n;k+= *nb) {
    if (*n - k  < jb) jb = *n - k ;
    for (pd = dots + k,p1 = dots + *n;pd<p1;pd++) *pd = 0;
    for (j=k;j<k+jb;j++,Ajn += *n) {
      pd = dots + j;Aj = Ajn + j; Aj1 = Aj - 1;
      xmax = -1.0;q=j;p1 = dots + *n;
      if (j>k) for (;pd<p1;pd++,Aj1 += *n) *pd += *Aj1 * *Aj1;
      for (l=j,pd = dots + j;pd<p1;pd++,Aj += n1,l++) {
        x = *Aj - *pd;
        if (x>xmax) { xmax = x;q=l;}
      }
      if (j==0) tol = *n * xmax * DBL_EPSILON;
      Aq = A + *n * q + q;
      if (*Aq - dots[q]<tol) {r = j;break;}

      pd = dots + j;p1 = dots + q;
      x = *pd;*pd = *p1;*p1 = x;

      pk = piv + j;pq = piv +q;
      i = *pk;*pk = *pq;*pq = i;

      Aj = Ajn + j;
      x =  *Aj; *Aj = *Aq;
      *Aq = x;

      N = q-j-1;
      if (N>0) {
        Aj += *n;
        Aq = A + q * *n + j + 1;
        F77_CALL(dswap)(&N,Aj,n,Aq,&one);
      }

      N = *n-q-1;
      if (N>0) {
        Aq = A + (q+1) * *n + q;
        Aj = A + (q+1) * *n + j;
        F77_CALL(dswap)(&N,Aj,n,Aq,n);
      }

      N = j;
      if (N>0) {
        Aq = A + q * *n;
        Aj = Ajn;
        F77_CALL(dswap)(&N,Aj,&one,Aq,&one);
      }

      Ajj = Ajn + j;
      *Ajj = sqrt(*Ajj - *pd);
      Aend = A + *n * *n;
      if (j > k&&j < *n) {

	trans='T';N = *n - j - 1;i=j-k;
        F77_CALL(dgemv)(&trans,&i,&N,&alpha,A+(j+1) * *n + k,n,A + j * *n + k,&one,&beta,A + (j+1) * *n+j,n FCONE);
      }
      if (j < *n) {
        Aj = Ajj; x = *Aj;Aj += *n;
        for (;Aj<Aend;Aj += *n) *Aj /= x;
      }
    }
    if (r > 0) break;

    if (k + jb < *n) {
      N = *n - j ;
      i = j - k;
      trans = 'T';uplo='U';
      if (0) F77_CALL(dsyrk)(&uplo,&trans,&N, &i, &alpha,A+j * *n + k,n,&beta,A + j * *n + j,n FCONE FCONE);
      else pdsyrk(&N,&i,&alpha,A+j * *n + k,n,&beta,A + j * *n + j,n,work,nt);

    }
  }
  if (r<0) r = *n;
  FREE(dots);

  for (Ajn=A,j=0;j<*n;j++,Ajn += *n) {
    Aj = Ajn;Aend = Aj + *n;
    if (j<r) Aj += j+1; else Aj += r;
    for (;Aj<Aend;Aj++) *Aj = 0.0;
  }
  FREE(a);FREE(work);
  return(r);
}

int mgcv_pchol(double *A,int *piv,int *n,int *nt) {

  int i,j,k,r,q,n1,*pk,*pq,kn,qn,*a,N,m,b;
  double *Aj,*Ak,*Aq,*Aend,x,Ajk,Akk,thresh=0.0;
  if (*nt < 1) *nt = 1;
  if (*nt > *n) *nt = *n;
  m = *nt;
  a = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) (*nt+1),sizeof(int));
  a[0] = 0;a[m] = *n;
  r = 0;n1 = *n + 1;
  for (pk = piv,i=0;i < *n;pk++,i++) *pk = i;
  for (pk=piv,k=0;k< *n;k++,pk++) {
    kn = k * *n;

    Ak = A + kn + k;x = *Ak;q=k;Ak+=n1;
    for (i=k+1;i < *n;i++,Ak+=n1) if (*Ak>x) {x = *Ak;q=i;}
    qn = q * *n;
    if (k==0) thresh = *n * x * DBL_EPSILON;
    if (x>thresh) {
      r++;

      pq = piv + q;i = *pq; *pq = *pk;*pk = i;

      Ak = A + kn + k;Aq = A + qn + q;
      x = *Ak;*Ak = *Aq;*Aq = x;

      Ak++; Aend = Aq; Aq = A + q + kn + *n;
      for (;Aq<Aend;Ak++,Aq += *n) {
        x = *Ak;*Ak = *Aq;*Aq = x;
      }

      Ak = A + k;Aend=Ak + kn;Aq = A + q;
      for (;Ak < Aend;Ak += *n,Aq += *n) {x = *Aq;*Aq = *Ak;*Ak = x;}

      Ak = A + kn; Aq = A + qn+q+1;Aend = Ak + *n;Ak+=q+1;
      for (;Ak<Aend;Ak++,Aq++) {x = *Aq;*Aq = *Ak;*Ak = x;}

      Ak = A + kn + k;*Ak = sqrt(*Ak);Akk = *Ak;Ak++;
      for (;Ak<Aend;Ak++) *Ak /= Akk;

      N = *n - k - 1;
      if (m > N) { m = N;a[m] = *n; }
      (*a)++;
      x = (double) N;x = x*x / m;

      for (i=1;i < m;i++) a[i] = round(N - sqrt(x*(m-i)))+k+1;
      for (i=1;i <= m;i++) {
        if (a[i]<=a[i-1]) a[i] = a[i-1]+1;
      }

      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
      #pragma omp parallel private(b,j,Aj,Aend,Ak,Ajk) num_threads(m)
      #endif
      {
        #ifdef OPENMP_ON
        #pragma omp for
        #endif
        for (b=0;b<m;b++)
	  for (j=a[b];j<a[b+1];j++) {
	    Aj = A + *n * j + j;
            Ak = A + kn; Aend = Ak + *n;Ak += j;Ajk = *Ak;
            for (;Ak < Aend;Ak++,Aj++) *Aj -= *Ak * Ajk;
          }
      }
    } else break;
  }

  Aend = A + *n * *n;
  for (Ak = A + r * *n;Ak<Aend;Ak++) *Ak = 0.0;

  a[0] = 0;a[*nt] = *n;
  x = (double) *n;x = x*x / *nt;

  for (i=1;i < *nt;i++) a[i] = round(*n - sqrt(x*(*nt-i)));
  for (i=1;i <= *nt;i++) {
    if (a[i]<=a[i-1]) a[i] = a[i-1]+1;
  }
  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  #pragma omp parallel private(b,i,Ak,Aend,Aq) num_threads(*nt)
  #endif
  {
    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp for
    #endif
    for (b=0;b < *nt;b++)
      for (i=a[b];i<a[b+1];i++) {
        Ak = A + i * *n; Aend = Ak + *n;Ak += i;
        Aq = Ak + *n;
        Ak ++;
        for (;Ak<Aend;Ak++,Aq += *n) {
          *Aq = *Ak;*Ak=0.0;
        }
      }
  }

  FREE(a);
  return(r);
}

SEXP mgcv_Rpchol(SEXP Amat,SEXP PIV,SEXP NT,SEXP NB) {

  int n,nt,*piv,r,*rrp,nb;
  double *A;
  SEXP rr;
  nb = asInteger(NB);
  nt = asInteger(NT);
  n = nrows(Amat);
  A = REAL(Amat);
  piv = INTEGER(PIV);

  r = mgcv_bchol(A,piv,&n,&nt,&nb);

  rr = PROTECT(allocVector(INTSXP, 1));
  rrp = INTEGER(rr);
  *rrp = r;
  UNPROTECT(1);
  return(rr);
}

int bpqr(double *A,int n,int p,double *tau,int *piv,int nb,int nt) {

  int jb,pb,i,j,k=0,m,*p0,nb0,q,one=1,ok_norm=1,*mb,*kb,rt,nth,min_np;
  double *cn,*icn,x,*a0,*a1,*F,*Ak,*Aq,*work,tol,xx,done=1.0,dmone=-1.0,dzero=0.0;
  char trans='T',nottrans='N';
#ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("bpqr...");
#endif
  tol = pow(DBL_EPSILON,.8);
  mb = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) nt,sizeof(int));
  kb = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) nt,sizeof(int));
  for (p0=piv,i=0;i<p;i++,p0++) *p0 = i;
  work = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) nb,sizeof(double));

  cn =(double *)CALLOC((size_t) p,sizeof(double));
  icn =(double *)CALLOC((size_t) p,sizeof(double));
  for (a0=A,i=0;i<p;i++) {
    for (x=0.0,a1 = a0 + n;a0<a1;a0++) x += *a0 * *a0;
    cn[i] = icn[i] = x;
  }
  if (p<nb) nb = p;
  nb0 = nb;
  jb=0;
  pb = p;
  F = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p * nb0,sizeof(double));
  if (n<p)  min_np = n; else min_np = p;
  while (jb < min_np) {
    nb = p-jb;if (nb>nb0) nb = nb0;
    for (a0=F,a1=F+nb*pb;a0<a1;a0++) *a0 = 0.0;
    for (j=0;j<nb;j++) {
      k = jb + j;
      a0 = cn + k;x = *a0;q=k;a0++;
      for (i=k+1;i<p;i++,a0++) if (*a0>x) { x = *a0;q=i; }
      if (q!=k) {
        i = piv[q];piv[q]=piv[k];piv[k] = i;
        x = cn[q];cn[q]=cn[k];cn[k] = x;
        x = icn[q];icn[q]=icn[k];icn[k] = x;
        Aq = A + q * (ptrdiff_t) n;Ak = A + k * (ptrdiff_t) n;a1 = Aq + n;
        for (;Aq<a1;Aq++,Ak++) { x = *Aq; *Aq = *Ak; *Ak = x;}
        Aq = F + q - jb;Ak = F + j;a1 = F + nb * (ptrdiff_t) pb;
        for (;Aq<a1;Aq+=pb,Ak+=pb) { x = *Aq; *Aq = *Ak; *Ak = x;}
      }

      m = n-k;Ak = A + (ptrdiff_t)n * k + k;
      if (j) {
        q = m ;
        rt = q/nt;if (rt*nt < q) rt++;
        nth = nt; while (nth>1&&(nth-1)*rt>q) nth--;
        kb[0] = k;
        for (i=0;i<nth-1;i++) { mb[i] = rt;kb[i+1]=kb[i]+rt;}
        mb[nth-1]=q-(nth-1)*rt;
        #ifdef OPENMP_ON
        #pragma omp parallel private(i) num_threads(nth)
        #endif
        {
          #ifdef OPENMP_ON
          #pragma omp for
          #endif
          for (i=0;i<nth;i++) {

            F77_CALL(dgemv)(&nottrans, mb+i, &j,&dmone,A+jb*(ptrdiff_t)n+kb[i],&n,F+j,&pb,&done,
	                    A + (ptrdiff_t)n*k + kb[i], &one FCONE);
          }
        }
      }

      xx = *Ak;
      Ak++;
      F77_CALL(dlarfg)(&m,&xx,Ak,&one,tau+k);
      Ak--;*Ak = 1.0;

      if (k<p-1) {
        q = p - k - 1 ;
        rt = q/nt;if (rt*nt < q) rt++;
        nth = nt; while (nth>1&&(nth-1)*rt>q) nth--;
        kb[0] = j+1;
        for (i=0;i<nth-1;i++) { mb[i] = rt;kb[i+1]=kb[i]+rt;}
        mb[nth-1]=q-(nth-1)*rt;
        {
          #ifdef OPENMP_ON
          #pragma omp parallel for private(i) num_threads(nth)
          #endif
          for (i=0;i<nth;i++) {
        	  F77_CALL(dgemv)(&trans, &m, mb+i,tau+k,A+(kb[i]+jb) * (ptrdiff_t) n+k,&n,
                          Ak,&one,&dzero,F+kb[i]+(ptrdiff_t)j*pb, &one FCONE);
          }
        }
      }

      if (j>0) {
        q = j ;
        rt = q/nt;if (rt*nt < q) rt++;
        nth = nt; while (nth>1&&(nth-1)*rt>q) nth--;
        kb[0] = jb;
        for (i=0;i<nth-1;i++) { mb[i] = rt;kb[i+1]=kb[i]+rt;}
        mb[nth-1]=q-(nth-1)*rt;
        #ifdef OPENMP_ON
        #pragma omp parallel private(i) num_threads(nth)
        #endif
        {
          #ifdef OPENMP_ON
          #pragma omp for
          #endif
          for (i=0;i<nth;i++) {
  	    F77_CALL(dgemv)(&trans, &m, mb+i,&dmone,A+kb[i]* (ptrdiff_t)n+k,&n,Ak,&one,
			    &dzero,work+kb[i]-jb, &one FCONE);
           }
        }
        q = pb ;
        rt = q/nt;if (rt*nt < q) rt++;
        nth = nt; while (nth>1&&(nth-1)*rt>q) nth--;
        kb[0] = 0;
        for (i=0;i<nth-1;i++) { mb[i] = rt;kb[i+1]=kb[i]+rt;}
        mb[nth-1]=q-(nth-1)*rt;
        for (i=0;i<nth;i++) {
	  F77_CALL(dgemv)(&nottrans, mb+i, &j,tau+k,F+kb[i],&pb,work,&one,&done,F+(ptrdiff_t)j*pb+kb[i], &one FCONE);
        }
      }

      if (k<p-1) {
        q = pb-j-1 ;
        rt = q/nt;if (rt*nt < q) rt++;
        nth = nt; while (nth>1&&(nth-1)*rt>q) nth--;
        kb[0] = j+1;
        for (i=0;i<nth-1;i++) { mb[i] = rt;kb[i+1]=kb[i]+rt;}
        mb[nth-1]=q-(nth-1)*rt;
        q=j+1;
        #ifdef OPENMP_ON
        #pragma omp parallel private(i) num_threads(nth)
        #endif
        {
          #ifdef OPENMP_ON
          #pragma omp for
          #endif
          for (i=0;i<nth;i++) {
	    F77_CALL(dgemv)(&nottrans, mb+i, &q,&dmone,F+kb[i],&pb,A+(ptrdiff_t)jb*n+k,
	                    &n,&done,A+(kb[i]+jb)*(ptrdiff_t)n+k, &n FCONE);
          }
        }
      }
      *Ak = xx;

      ok_norm = 1;
      if (k < p-1) {
        Ak += n;
        a0 = cn + k + 1;a1=cn + p;Aq = icn + k + 1;
        for (;a0<a1;a0++,Aq++,Ak+=n) {
          *a0 -= *Ak * *Ak;
          if (*a0<*Aq*tol) ok_norm = 0;
        }
      }
      if (!ok_norm) {
        j++;
        nb = j;
        break;
      }
    }
    j--;

    m = n - k - 1 ;
    if (m>0 && k<p-1) {

      rt = m/nt;if (rt*nt < m) rt++;
      nth = nt; while (nth>1&&(nth-1)*rt>m) nth--;
      kb[0] = k+1;
      for (i=0;i<nth-1;i++) { mb[i] = rt;kb[i+1]=kb[i]+rt;}
      mb[nth-1]=m-(nth-1)*rt;
      rt = p - k - 1;
      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
      #pragma omp parallel private(i,Ak,Aq) num_threads(nth)
      #endif
      {
        #ifdef OPENMP_ON
        #pragma omp for
        #endif
        for (i=0;i<nth;i++) {
          Ak = A + (k+1)*(ptrdiff_t) n + kb[i];Aq = A + jb * (ptrdiff_t) n + kb[i];

          F77_CALL(dgemm)(&nottrans,&trans,mb+i,&rt,&nb,&dmone,Aq,&n,F+j+1,&pb,&done,Ak,&n FCONE FCONE);
        }
      }
    }

    if (!ok_norm) {
      for (i = k+1;i<p; i++) {
        if (cn[i]<icn[i]*tol) {
          x=0.0; Ak = A + i * (ptrdiff_t)n;
          Aq = Ak + n;
          Ak += k+1;
          for (;Ak<Aq;Ak++) x += *Ak * *Ak;
          cn[i] = icn[i] = x;
        }
      }
    }
    pb -= nb;
    jb += nb;
  }
  FREE(F); FREE(mb); FREE(kb);
  FREE(cn);
  FREE(icn);
  FREE(work);
#ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("done\n");
#endif
  return(min_np);
}

int mgcv_piqr(double *x,int n, int p, double *beta, int *piv, int nt) {

  int i,k,r,nh,j,one=1,cpt,nth,cpf,ii;
  double *c,*p0,*p1,*p2,xx,tau,*work,zz,*v,*z,*z1,br;

  #ifndef OPENMP_ON
  nt = 1;
  #endif
  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("mgcv_piqr...");
  #endif
  c =(double *)CALLOC((size_t) p,sizeof(double));
  work =(double *)CALLOC((size_t) (p*nt),sizeof(double));
  k=0;tau=0.0;

  for (p0=x,i=0;i<p;i++) {
    piv[i] = i;
    for (xx=0.0,p1=p0 + n;p0<p1;p0++) xx += *p0 * *p0;
    c[i] = xx;
    if (xx>tau) {tau=xx;k=i;}
  }
  r = -1;
  nh = n;

  while (tau > 0) {
    r++;
    i=piv[r]; piv[r] = piv[k];piv[k] = i;

    xx = c[r];c[r] = c[k];c[k] = xx;
    for (p0 = x + n * r, p1 = x + (ptrdiff_t)n * k,p2 = p0 + n;p0<p2;p0++,p1++) {
          xx = *p0; *p0 = *p1; *p1 = xx;
    }

    p0 = x + r * n + r;
    p1 = p0 + 1;
    xx = *p0;
    F77_CALL(dlarfg)(&nh,&xx,p1,&one,beta+r);

    *p0 = 1.0;
    j=p-r-1;

    if (j) {
          cpt = j / nt;
          if (cpt * nt < j) cpt++;
          nth = j/cpt;
          if (nth * cpt < j) nth++;
          cpf = j - cpt * (nth-1);
    } else nth=cpf=cpt=0;

    br = beta[r];

    { j = cpt;
      if (j) {
        #ifdef OPENMP_ON
        #pragma omp parallel for private(i,j,p1,v,z,z1,zz,ii) num_threads(nt)
        #endif
        for (i=0;i<nth;i++) {
	    if (i == nth-1) j = cpf; else j = cpt;
            p1 = p0 + n + n * cpt *i;
            z1=p1+nh;
            for (ii =0;ii<j;ii++,p1+=n,z1+=n) {

            for (zz=0.0,v=p0,z=p1;z<z1;z++,v++) zz += *z * *v * br;
            for (z=p1,v=p0;z<z1;z++,v++) *z -= zz * *v;
	   }

        }
      }
    }
    nh--;
    *p0 = xx;

    k = r + 1;
    for (tau=0.0,p0+=n,i=r+1;i<p;i++,p0+=n) {
          c[i] -= *p0 * *p0;
          if (c[i]>tau) {
            tau = c[i];
            k=i;
          }
     }
     if (r==n-1) tau = 0.0;

    }

  FREE(c); FREE(work);
  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("done\n");
  #endif
  return(r+1);
}

SEXP mgcv_Rpiqr(SEXP X, SEXP BETA,SEXP PIV,SEXP NT, SEXP NB) {

  int n,p,nt,*piv,r,*rrp,nb;
  double *x,*beta;
  SEXP rr;
  nt = asInteger(NT);nb = asInteger(NB);
  n = nrows(X);
  p = ncols(X);
  x = REAL(X);beta = REAL(BETA);
  piv = INTEGER(PIV);
  r = bpqr(x,n,p,beta,piv,nb,nt);

  rr = PROTECT(allocVector(INTSXP, 1));
  rrp = INTEGER(rr);
  *rrp = r;

  UNPROTECT(1);
  return(rr);

}

void qradd(double *Q,double *R,double *a,int n,int p) {

  double *p0,*p1,*Rjj,*Rji,tau,s,c,ai,Qj;
  int i,j;

  for (p0=Q+n-1,p1=Q+n*n;p0<p1;p0+=n) *p0=0.0;
  for (p0=Q+n*(n-1);p0<p1;p0++) *p0=0.0;
  p1--;*p1=1.0;Rjj = R;
  for (j=0;j<p;j++,a++,Rjj+=p+1) if (*a) {
    if (fabs(*a)>fabs(*Rjj)) {
      tau = - *Rjj / *a; s = 1/sqrt(1+tau*tau); c = s*tau;
    } else {
      tau = - *a / *Rjj; c = 1/sqrt(1+tau*tau); s = c*tau;
    }
    *Rjj = c * *Rjj - s * *a; *a = 0.0;
    Rji = Rjj + p;p0 = a + 1;
    for (i=j+1;i<p;i++,Rji+=p,p0++) {
      ai = c * *p0 + s * *Rji;
      *Rji = c * *Rji - s * *p0;
      *p0 = ai;
    }
    p0 = Q + n*j;p1 = Q + n*(n-1);
    for (i=0;i<n;i++,p0++,p1++) {
      Qj = *p0 * c - *p1 * s;
      *p1 = *p1 * c + *p0 * s;
      *p0 = Qj;
    }
  }
}

void qrdrop(double *Q,double *R,int k,int n,int p) {

  double qk,*p0,*p1,*p2,*rn,*qn,*qj,tau,s,c,Qj,Rji,rnp=0.0;
  int i,j;

  for (j=0;j<n;j++) {
    p0 = Q + n * j;p2 = p0 + n;p0 += k; p1 = p0 + 1;
    qk = *p0;
    for (;p1<p2;p0++,p1++) *p0 = *p1;
    p2--; *p2 = qk;
  }
  rn = R + 1;
  if (n==p) rnp = R[p*p-1];
  qn = Q + n * n - 1; qj = qn - n;
  for (j=n-2;j>=0;j--,qj -= n) if (*qj) {
    if (fabs(*qn)>fabs(*qj)) {
      tau = - *qj / *qn; s = 1/sqrt(1+tau*tau); c = s*tau;
    } else {
      tau = - *qn / *qj; c = 1/sqrt(1+tau*tau); s = c*tau;
    }
    /

    p0 = Q + j * n; p1 = Q + (n-1)*n; p2 = p1 + n;
    for (;p1<p2;p0++,p1++) {
      Qj = *p0 * s + *p1 * c;
      *p1 = *p0 * c - *p1 * s;
      *p0 = Qj;
    }

    if (j<p) {
      p1 = R + (p-1) * p + j;
      Rji = rnp * c + *p1 * s;
      rnp = *p1 * c - rnp * s;
      *p1 = Rji;p1 -= p;p0 = rn + p-2;
      for (i=p-2;i>=j;i--,p1 -= p,p0--) {
        Rji = *p0 * c + *p1 * s;
	*p0 = *p1 * c - *p0 * s;
	*p1 = Rji;
      }
    }
  }
  for (i=0;i<p-1;i++) rn[i] = 0.0;
  if (n==p) R[p*p-1] = 0.0;
}

SEXP QRdrop(SEXP q,SEXP r,SEXP K) {

  int n,p,*k;
  double *Q,*R;
  n = nrows(q); p = nrows(r);
  k = INTEGER(K);Q = REAL(q); R = REAL(r);
  qrdrop(Q,R,*k,n,p);
  return(R_NilValue);
}

SEXP QRadd(SEXP q,SEXP r,SEXP A) {

  int n,p;
  double *Q,*R,*a;
  n = nrows(q); p = nrows(r);
  a = REAL(A);Q = REAL(q); R = REAL(r);
  qradd(Q,R,a,n,p);
  return(R_NilValue);
}

void mgcv_pmmult(double *A,double *B,double *C,int *bt,int *ct,int *r,int *c,int *n,int *nt) {

  char transa='N',transb='N';
  int lda,ldb,ldc,cpt,cpf,c1,i,nth;
  double alpha=1.0,beta=0.0;
  if (*r<=0||*c<=0||*n<=0) return;
  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("mgcv_pmmult...");
  #endif
  if (B==C) {
    if (*bt&&(!*ct)&&(*r==*c)) { getXtX(A,B,n,r);return;}
    else if (*ct&&(!*bt)&&(*r==*c)) { getXXt(A,B,c,n);return;}
  }
  #ifndef OPENMP_ON
  *nt = 1;
  #endif
  if (*nt == 1) {
    mgcv_mmult(A,B,C,bt,ct,r,c,n);
    return;
  }
  if (*bt) {
    transa = 'T';
    lda = *n;
  } else  lda = *r;

  if (*ct) {
    transb = 'T';
    ldb = *c;
  } else ldb = *n;

  ldc = *r;

  if (*ct) {
    if (*bt) {
      cpt = *r / *nt;
      if (cpt * *nt < *r) cpt++;
      nth = *r/cpt;
      if (nth * cpt < *r) nth++;
      cpf = *r - cpt * (nth-1);
      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
      #pragma omp parallel private(i,c1) num_threads(nth)
      #endif
      {

        #ifdef OPENMP_ON
        #pragma omp for
        #endif
        for (i=0;i<nth;i++) {
          if (i == nth-1) c1 = cpf; else c1 = cpt;

          if (c1>0) F77_CALL(dgemm)(&transa,&transb,&c1,c,n, &alpha,B + i * (ptrdiff_t) cpt * *n, n ,
				    C, c,&beta, A + i * (ptrdiff_t) cpt * *c, &c1 FCONE FCONE);
        }
      }

      row_block_reorder(A,r,c,&cpt,bt);
    } else {
      cpt = *r / *nt;
      if (cpt * *nt < *r) cpt++;
      nth = *r/cpt;
      if (nth * cpt < *r) nth++;
      cpf = *r - cpt * (nth-1);

      row_block_reorder(B,r,n,&cpt,bt);
      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
      #pragma omp parallel private(i,c1) num_threads(nth)
      #endif
      {

        #ifdef OPENMP_ON
        #pragma omp for
        #endif
        for (i=0;i<nth;i++) {
          if (i == nth-1) c1 = cpf;else c1=cpt;
          if (c1>0) F77_CALL(dgemm)(&transa,&transb,&c1,c,n, &alpha,B + i * (ptrdiff_t) cpt * *n,
				    &c1,C,c,&beta, A + i * (ptrdiff_t) cpt * *c, &c1 FCONE FCONE);
        }
      }

      row_block_reorder(B,r,n,&cpt,ct);
      row_block_reorder(A,r,c,&cpt,ct);
    }
  } else {
    cpt = *c / *nt;
    if (cpt * *nt < *c) cpt++;
    nth = *c/cpt;
    if (nth * cpt < *c) nth++;
    cpf = *c - cpt * (nth-1);
    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp parallel private(i,c1) num_threads(*nt)
    #endif
    {

      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
      #pragma omp for
      #endif
      for (i=0;i< nth;i++) {
        if (i == nth-1) c1 = cpf;else c1=cpt;
        if (c1>0) F77_CALL(dgemm)(&transa,&transb,r,&c1,n, &alpha,B, &lda,C + i * (ptrdiff_t) *n * cpt,
				  &ldb,&beta, A + i * (ptrdiff_t) *r * cpt, &ldc FCONE FCONE);
      }
    }
  }
  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("done\n");
  #endif
}

void pcrossprod(double *B, double *A,int *R, int *C,int *nt,int *nb) {

  int M,N,nf,nrf,kmax,kk,i,r,c,k,bn,an,cn;
  ptrdiff_t as,bs,cs;
  char uplo = 'U',trans='T',ntrans='N';
  double alpha=1.0,beta=1.0;
  M = ceil(((double) *C)/ *nb);
  N = ceil(((double) *R)/ *nb);
  if (M==1) {
    beta = 0.0;
    F77_CALL(dsyrk)(&uplo,&trans,C,R,&alpha,A,R,&beta,B,C FCONE FCONE);
  } else {
    nf = *C - (M-1) * *nb;
    nrf = *R - (N-1) * *nb;
    kmax = (M+1)*M/2;
    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp parallel for private(kk,i,r,c,bn,bs,k,as,an,beta,cs,cn) num_threads(*nt)
    #endif
    for (kk=0;kk<kmax;kk++) {
      i=kk;r=0;while (i >= M-r) { i -= M - r; r++;}; c = r + i;
      if (r==M-1) bn = nf; else bn = *nb;
      bs = r * (ptrdiff_t) *nb;
      if (c==r) {
        for (k=0;k<N;k++) {
          as = k * (ptrdiff_t) *nb;
          if (k==N-1) an = nrf; else an = *nb;

          if (k) beta=1.0; else beta = 0.0;
          F77_CALL(dsyrk)(&uplo,&trans,&bn,
			&an,
                        &alpha,A + as + (ptrdiff_t) *R * bs,
                        R,
                        &beta,B + bs + (ptrdiff_t) *C * bs,C FCONE FCONE);
        }
      } else {
	cs = c * (ptrdiff_t) *nb;
        if (c==M-1) cn = nf; else cn = *nb;
        for (k=0;k<N;k++) {
          as = k * (ptrdiff_t) *nb;
          if (k==N-1) an = nrf; else an = *nb;

          if (k) beta=1.0; else beta = 0.0;
          F77_CALL(dgemm)(&trans,&ntrans,
                          &bn,&cn,&an,
                          &alpha,A + *R * bs + as,
                          R,A + *R * cs + as,R,&beta, B + bs + *C * cs, C FCONE FCONE);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  for (r=0; r < *C;r++) for (c=0;c < r;c++) B[r + *C * c] = B[c + *C * r];
}

SEXP mgcv_Rpcross(SEXP A, SEXP NT,SEXP NB) {

  int c,r,nt,nb;
  double *A0,*B0;
  SEXP B;
  nt = asInteger(NT);
  nb = asInteger(NB);
  r = nrows(A);c = ncols(A);
  A0 = REAL(A);
  B = PROTECT(allocMatrix(REALSXP,c,c));
  B0 = REAL(B);

  pcrossprod(B0,A0,&r,&c,&nt,&nb);

  UNPROTECT(1);
  return(B);
}

void getXtX0(double *XtX,double *X,int *r,int *c)

{ double *p0,*p1,*p2,*p3,*p4,x;
  int i,j;
  for (p0=X,i=0;i<*c;i++,p0 += *r)
  for (p1=X,j=0;j<=i;j++,p1 += *r) {
    for (x=0.0,p2=p0,p3=p1,p4=p0 + *r;p2<p4;p2++,p3++) x += *p2 * *p3;
    XtX[i + j * *c] = XtX[j + i * *c] = x;
  }
}

void getXtX(double *XtX,double *X,int *r,int *c)

{ double alpha=1.0,beta=0.0;
  int i,j;
  char uplo = 'L',trans='T';
  F77_CALL(dsyrk)(&uplo,&trans,c, r, &alpha,X,r,&beta,XtX,c FCONE FCONE);

  for (i=0;i<*c;i++)
  for (j=0;j<i;j++)  XtX[j + i * *c] = XtX[i + j * *c];

}

void getXXt(double *XXt,double *X,int *r,int *c)

{ double alpha=1.0,beta=0.0;
  ptrdiff_t ii,jj;
  char uplo = 'L',trans='N';
  F77_CALL(dsyrk)(&uplo,&trans,r, c, &alpha,X,r,&beta,XXt,r FCONE FCONE);

  for (ii=0;ii<*r;ii++)
  for (jj=0;jj<ii;jj++)  XXt[jj + ii * *r] = XXt[ii + jj * *r];

}

void getXtWX0(double *XtWX, double *X,double *w,int *r,int *c,double *work)

{ int i,j;
  double *p,*p1,*p2,*pX0,*pX1,xx;
  pX0=X;
  for (i=0;i< *c;i++) {
    p2 = work + *r;
    for (p=w,p1=work;p1<p2;p++,p1++,pX0++) *p1 = *pX0 * *p;
    for (pX1=X,j=0;j<=i;j++) {
      for (xx=0.0,p=work;p<p2;p++,pX1++) xx += *p * *pX1;
      XtWX[i * *c + j] = XtWX[j * *c + i] = xx;
    }
  }
}

void getXtWX(double *XtWX, double *X,double *w,int *r,int *c,double *work)

{ int i,j,one=1;
  char trans='T';
  double *p,*p1,*p2,*pX0,xx=0.0,*w2,alpha=1.0,beta=0.0;
  pX0=X;
  w2 = XtWX;
  for (i=0;i< *c;i++) {
    p2 = work + *r;

    for (p=w,p1=work;p1<p2;p++,p1++,pX0++) *p1 = *pX0 * *p;

    j = i+1;
    F77_CALL(dgemv)(&trans, r, &j,&alpha,X, r,work,&one,&beta,w2, &one FCONE);
    if (i==0) xx = w2[0];
    else for (j=0;j<=i;j++) XtWX[i * *c + j] = w2[j];
  }

  if (*r * *c > 0) *XtWX = xx;
  for (i=0;i< *c;i++) for (j=0;j<i;j++) XtWX[j * *c + i] =  XtWX[i * *c + j];
}

void getXtMX(double *XtMX,double *X,double *M,int *r,int *c,double *work)

{ int i,j;
  double *p,*p1,*p2,*pX0,*pX1,xx,*pM;
  pX0=X;
  for (i=0;i< *c;i++) {

    p2 = work + *r;pM=M;
    for (p1=work;p1<p2;pM++,p1++) *p1 = *pX0 * *pM;
    pX0++;
    for (j=1;j< *r;j++,pX0++)
    for (p1=work;p1<p2;pM++,p1++) *p1 += *pX0 * *pM;

    for (pX1=X,j=0;j<=i;j++) {
      for (xx=0.0,p=work;p<p2;p++,pX1++) xx += *p * *pX1;
      XtMX[i * *c + j] = XtMX[j * *c + i] = xx;
    }
  }
}

void vcorr(double *dR,double *Vr,double *Vb,int *p,int *M) {

  double *Vi,*ViV,*p0,*p1,*p2,zero=0.0,one=1.0,x;
  int i,j,k;
  char trans = 'N';
  k = *p * *M; if (k<0) k = -k;
  Vi = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) k,sizeof(double));
  ViV = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) k,sizeof(double));
  if (*M>0) {
    for (i=0;i<*p;i++) {
      for (p0=Vi,k=0;k<*M;k++) {

        p1 = dR + k * *p * *p + i * *p;
        p2 = p1 + i + 1;
        for (;p1<p2;p1++,p0++) *p0 = *p1;
      }

      k = i + 1;
      F77_CALL(dgemm)(&trans,&trans,&k,M,M, &one,
  		Vi, &k, Vr , M, &zero, ViV, &k FCONE FCONE);
      for (j=i;j<*p;j++) {
        for (x=0.0,p0=ViV,k=0;k<*M;k++) {

          p1 = dR + k * *p * *p + j * *p;
          p2 = p1 + i + 1;
          for (;p1<p2;p1++,p0++) x += *p0 * *p1;
        }
        Vb[i + *p * j] = Vb[j + *p * i] = x;
      }
    }
  } else {
    *M = - *M;
    for (i=0;i<*p;i++) {
      for (p0=Vi,k=0;k<*M;k++) {

        p1 = dR + k * *p * *p + i ;
        p2 = p1 + *p * *p;
        p1 += i * *p;
        for (;p1<p2;p1+= *p,p0++) *p0 = *p1;
      }

      k = *p - i;
      F77_CALL(dgemm)(&trans,&trans,&k,M,M, &one,
  		Vi, &k, Vr , M, &zero, ViV, &k FCONE FCONE);
      for (j=i;j<*p;j++) {
        for (x=0.0,p0=ViV,k=0;k<*M;k++) {
          p0 += (j-i);

          p1 = dR + k * *p * *p + j;
          p2 = p1 + *p * *p;
          p1 += j * *p;
          for (;p1<p2;p1 += *p,p0++) x += *p0 * *p1;
        }
        Vb[i + *p * j] = Vb[j + *p * i] = x;
      }
    }
  }
  FREE(Vi);FREE(ViV);
}

void dchol(double *dA, double *R, double *dR,int *p) {

  int i,j,k;
  double x,*pdRi,*pdRj,*pRi,*pRj,*pend;
  for (i=0;i < *p;i++) for (j=i;j < *p;j++) {
    k = i * *p;pdRi = dR + k; pRi = R + k;
    k = j * *p; pdRj = dR + k; pRj = R + k;pend = pRj + i;
    for (x=0.0;pRj<pend;pRi++,pRj++,pdRi++,pdRj++) x += *pRi * *pdRj + *pRj * *pdRi;
    k = i + j * *p;
    if (j>i) dR[k] = (dA[k] - x - R[k]*dR[i + i * *p])/R[i + i * *p];
    else  dR[k] = (dA[k] - x)*.5/R[i + i * *p];
  }
}

SEXP mgcv_chol_down(SEXP r,SEXP ru,SEXP N,SEXP K, SEXP UT) {

  double *R,*Rup;
  int *n,*k,*ut;
  R = REAL(r);Rup=REAL(ru);
  n = INTEGER(N);
  k = INTEGER(K);
  ut = INTEGER(UT);
  chol_down(R,Rup,n,k,ut);
  return(R_NilValue);
}

static inline double hypote(double x, double y) {

  double t;
  x = fabs(x);y=fabs(y);
  if (y>x) { t = x;x = y; y = t;}
  if (x==0) return(y); else t = y/x;
  return(x*sqrt(1+t*t));
}

void chol_down(double *R,double *Rup, int *n,int *k,int *ut) {

  int i,n1;
  double x,*Ri1,*Ri,*Rj,c,s,*Re,*ca,*sa,*sp,*cp;
  n1 = *n-1;
  if (*ut) {
      ca = R + 2;sa = ca + *n;
      for (i=0;i<n1;i++) {
	Ri = Rup + i * n1;
	if (i < *k) {
	  Rj = R + i * *n;
	  Re = Rj + i;
	} else {
	  Rj = R + (i+1) * *n;
	  Re = Rj + *k;
        }
	for (;Rj<=Re;Ri++,Rj++) *Ri=*Rj;

	if (i >= *k) {

          Re = Rup + i * n1 + i;
	  Ri1=Ri;Ri--;
	  for (cp=ca,sp=sa;Ri<Re;cp++,sp++,Ri++,Ri1++,Rj++) {
            *Ri1 = - *sp * *Ri  + *cp * *Rj;
            *Ri = *cp * *Ri + *sp * *Rj;
	  }

	  x = sqrt(*Ri * *Ri + *Rj * *Rj);
