          c = *Ri / x; s = *Rj / x;*Ri = x;
	  if (i<n1-1) { *cp = c;*sp=s;}
	}
      }

      Re = R + *n;
      for (;ca<Re;ca++,sa++) *ca = *sa = 0.0;
  } else {

    for (i=0;i< *k;i++) {
      for (Ri=Rup+i*n1,Rj=R+i * *n,Re=Ri+*k;Ri<Re;Ri++,Rj++) *Ri = *Rj;
    }

    for (i=0;i<=*k;i++) {
      for (Ri=Rup+i*n1,Re=Ri+n1,Ri+= *k,Rj=R+i * *n+ *k+1;Ri<Re;Ri++,Rj++) *Ri = *Rj;
    }
    for (i = *k;i<n1;i++) {
      Ri = Rup + i  + i * n1;
      Rj = R + (i+1) + (i+1) * *n;

      x = hypote(*Ri,*Rj);
      c = *Ri / x; s = *Rj / x;*Ri = x;
      Rj++;Ri++;Ri1=Ri+n1;Re = Rup + (i+1) * n1;
      for (;Ri<Re;Rj++,Ri++,Ri1++) {
	*Ri1 = -s * *Ri  + c * *Rj;
        *Ri = c * *Ri + s * *Rj;
      }
    }
  }

}

void chol_up(double *R,double *u, int *n,int *up,double *eps) {

  double c0,s0,*c,*s,z,*x,z0,*c1;
  int j,j1,n1;
  n1 = *n - 1;
  if (*up) for (j1=-1,j=0;j<*n;j++,u++,j1++) {
    z = *u;
    x = R + *n * j;
    c = R + 2;s = R + *n + 2;
    for (c1=c+j1;c<c1;c++,s++,x++) {
      z0 = z;
      z = *c * z - *s * *x;
      *x = *s * z0 + *c * *x;
    }
    if (j) {

      z0 = z;
      z = c0 * z - s0 * *x;
      *x = s0 * z0 + c0 * *x;
      x++;
      if (j<n1) {*c = c0; *s = s0;}
    }

    z0 = hypote(z,*x);
    c0 = *x/z0; s0 = z/z0;

    *x = s0 * z + c0 * *x;
  } else  for (j1=-1,j=0;j<*n;j++,u++,j1++) {
    z = *u;
    x = R + *n * j;
    c = R + 2;s = R + *n + 2;
    for (c1=c+j1;c<c1;c++,s++,x++) {
      z0 = z;
      z =  *c * z - *s * *x;
      *x = -*s * z0 + *c * *x;
    }
    if (j) {

      z0 = z;
      z = c0 * z - s0 * *x;
      *x = -s0 * z0 + c0 * *x;
      x++;
      if (j<n1) {*c = c0; *s = s0;}
    }

    z0 = z / *x;
    if (fabs(z0)>=1) {

      if (*n>1) R[1] = -2.0;
      return;
    }
    if (z0 > 1 - *eps) z0 = 1 - *eps;
    c0 = 1/sqrt(1-z0*z0);s0 = c0 * z0;

    *x = -s0 * z + c0 * *x;
  }

  c = R + 2;s = R + *n + 2;
  for (x = c + *n - 2;c<x;c++,s++) *c = *s = 0.0;
}

SEXP mgcv_chol_up(SEXP r,SEXP U,SEXP N,SEXP UP,SEXP EPS) {

  double *R,*u,*eps;
  int *n,*up;
  R = REAL(r);u=REAL(U);
  eps = REAL(EPS);
  n = INTEGER(N);
  up = INTEGER(UP);
  chol_up(R,u,n,up,eps);
  return(R_NilValue);
}

void mgcv_chol(double *a,int *pivot,int *n,int *rank)

{ double *work,*p1,*p2,*p,*p3,tol=-1.0;
  int info=1;
  char uplo='U';
  work=(double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n * 2,sizeof(double));

  F77_CALL(dpstrf)(&uplo,n,a,n,pivot,rank,&tol,work,&info FCONE);

  for (p2=a + *n,p1=a+1,p3=a+ *n * *n;p2<p3;p1+= *n+1,p2+= *n) for (p=p1;p<p2;p++) *p=0.0;
  FREE(work);
}

void mroot(double *A,int *rank,int *n)

{ int *pivot,erank,i,j;
  double *pi,*pj,*p0,*p1,*B;
  pivot=(int *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(int));
  mgcv_chol(A,pivot,n,&erank);
  if (*rank<=0) *rank = erank;

  B=CALLOC((size_t)(*n * *n),sizeof(double));

  for (p0=A,p1=B,i=0;i< *n;i++,p0+= *n,p1+= *n)
  for (pi=p0,pj=p1;pi<=p0+i;pi++,pj++) { *pj = *pi; *pi=0.0;}

  for (p0=B,i=0;i< *n;p0+= *n,i++)
  for (j=pivot[i],pj=p0,pi=A + (j-1)* *n;pj<=p0+i;pi++,pj++) *pi = *pj;

  for (pi=A,p0=A,i=0;i< *n;i++,p0+= *n)
  for (pj=p0;pj<p0+ *rank;pj++,pi++) *pi = *pj;
  FREE(pivot);FREE(B);
}

void mtrf(double *A, double *B,int *n,int *rank,int *psd,double *tol,double *work,int *iwork) {

  int info=1,i,j,*piv;
  char uplo='L';
  double *p,*p1,max=0.0,row_max,x;

  piv=iwork;
  if (*psd) {
    x = -1.0;
    F77_CALL(dpstrf)(&uplo,n,A,n,piv,&j,&x,work,&info FCONE);
    if (*rank<=0) *rank=j;
    for (i=0;i<*n;i++,piv++) *piv += -1;
    piv=iwork;

    if (j < *rank) for (p = A + j* *n,p1 = A + *rank * *n;p<p1;p++) *p = 0.0;

    for (j=0;j<*rank;j++) {
      for (i=0;i<j;i++) work[i] = 0.0;
      for (p=A+j * *n,i=j;i<*n;i++) work[i] = p[i];
      for (i=0;i<*n;i++) p[piv[i]] = work[i];
    }
  } else {
    F77_CALL(dgetrf)(n,n,A,n,piv,&info);

    for (i=0;i<*n;i++) { work[i] = (double) piv[i];piv[i]=i;}
    for (i=0;i<*n;i++) { j = (int) work[i]-1;info = piv[j];piv[j]=piv[i];piv[i]=info;}

    *rank = 0;
    for (p=A,p1=A+ *n * *n;p<p1;p++) { x=fabs(*p); if (x > max) max=x;}
    for (i=0;i<*n;i++) {
      row_max=0.0;
      for (p=A+i* *n+i,j=i;j<*n;j++,p += *n) {
        x = fabs(*p); if (x > row_max) row_max=x;
      }
      if (row_max > max * *tol) {

	for (j=0;j<i;j++,B++) *B = 0.0;
	for (p1=p=A+i* *n + i, j=i;j<*n;j++,B++,p+= *n) *B = *p;
        *p1 = 1.0;

	for (j=0;j<i;j++) work[j] = 0.0;
        for (p=A+i * *n,j=i;j<*n;j++) work[j] = p[j];
        for (p=A+ *rank * *n,j=0;j<*n;j++) p[piv[j]] = work[j];
	(*rank)++;
      }
    }
  }
}

void mgcv_svd_full(double *x,double *vt,double *d,int *r,int *c)

{
  char jobu='O',jobvt='A';
  int lda,ldu,ldvt,lwork,info,*iwork=NULL,fast=1;
  double work1,*work,*u=NULL;
  ldu=lda= *r;ldvt = *c;
  lwork=-1;

  if (fast) {
    iwork = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) 8 * *c,sizeof(int));
    F77_CALL(dgesdd)(&jobu, r, c, x, &lda, d, u, &ldu, vt,&ldvt,
			     &work1, &lwork,iwork, &info FCONE);
  } else F77_CALL(dgesvd)(&jobu,&jobvt, r, c, x, &lda, d, u, &ldu, vt,&ldvt,
  		   &work1, &lwork, &info FCONE FCONE);
  lwork=(int)floor(work1);
  if (work1-lwork>0.5) lwork++;
  work=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)lwork,sizeof(double));

  if (fast) {
    F77_CALL(dgesdd)(&jobu, r, c, x, &lda, d, u, &ldu, vt,&ldvt,
			     work, &lwork,iwork, &info FCONE);
    FREE(iwork);
  } else F77_CALL(dgesvd)(&jobu,&jobvt, r, c, x, &lda, d, u, &ldu, vt,&ldvt,
  		   work, &lwork, &info FCONE FCONE);
  FREE(work);
}

void mgcv_td_qy(double *S,double *tau,int *m,int *n, double *B,int *left,int *transpose)

{ char trans='N',side='R',uplo='U';
  int nq,lwork=-1,info;
  double *work,work1;
  if (*left) { side = 'L';nq = *m;} else nq = *n;
  if (*transpose) trans = 'T';

  F77_CALL(dormtr)(&side,&uplo,&trans,m,n,S,&nq,tau,B,m,&work1,&lwork,&info FCONE FCONE FCONE);
  lwork=(int)floor(work1);if (work1-lwork>0.5) lwork++;
  work=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)lwork,sizeof(double));

  F77_CALL(dormtr)(&side,&uplo,&trans,m,n,S,&nq,tau,B,m,work,&lwork,&info FCONE FCONE FCONE);
  FREE(work);
}

void tri_chol(double *ld,double *sd,int *n,int *info) {

  F77_CALL(dpttrf)(n,ld,sd,info);
}

void band_chol(double *B,int *n,int *k,int *info) {

  char uplo='L';
  int kd;
  kd = *k - 1;
  F77_CALL(dpbtrf)(&uplo,n,&kd,B,k,info FCONE);

}

void mgcv_tri_diag(double *S,int *n,double *tau)

{ int lwork=-1,info;
  double *work,work1,*e,*d;
  char uplo='U';
  d = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
  e = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*n-1),sizeof(double));

  F77_CALL(dsytrd)(&uplo,n,S,n,d,e,tau,&work1,&lwork,&info FCONE);
  lwork=(int)floor(work1);if (work1-lwork>0.5) lwork++;
  work=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)lwork,sizeof(double));

  F77_CALL(dsytrd)(&uplo,n,S,n,d,e,tau,work,&lwork,&info FCONE);
  FREE(work);FREE(d);FREE(e);
}

void mgcv_pbsi0(double *R, int *n,int *nt) {

  int j,info,jb,nb=50;
  char left = 'L',right = 'R',up='U',ntrans='N',diag='N';
  double d1 = 1.0,m1= -1.0;
  for (j=0;j< *n;j+=nb) {
    jb = *n - j; if (jb>nb) jb=nb;
    if (j) {

      F77_CALL(dtrmm)(&left,&up,&ntrans,&diag,&j,&jb,&d1,R,n,R + j * *n,n FCONE FCONE FCONE FCONE);

      F77_CALL(dtrsm)(&right,&up,&ntrans,&diag,&j,&jb,&m1,R + *n * j + j,n,R + j * *n,n FCONE FCONE FCONE FCONE);
    }

    F77_CALL(dtrti2)(&up,&diag,&jb,R + *n * j + j,n,&info FCONE FCONE);
  }

}

void mgcv_pbsi1(double *R, int *n,int *nt) {

  int j,info,jb,nb=50,*iwork;
  char left = 'L',right = 'R',up='U',ntrans='N',diag='N';
  double d1 = 1.0,m1= -1.0,*work;
  iwork = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)3 * (*nt *(*nt+1))/2 + 2 * *nt + 2,sizeof(int));
  work = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) nb * ((ptrdiff_t) *n + *nt + 1) * (*nt + 1)/2,sizeof(double));
  for (j=0;j< *n;j+=nb) {
    jb = *n - j; if (jb>nb) jb=nb;
    if (j) {

      if (0) F77_CALL(dtrmm)(&left,&up,&ntrans,&diag,&j,&jb,&d1,R,n,R + j * *n,n FCONE FCONE FCONE FCONE);
                                         else pdtrmm(&j,&jb,&d1,R,n,R + j * *n,n,nt,iwork,work);

      F77_CALL(dtrsm)(&right,&up,&ntrans,&diag,&j,&jb,&m1,R + *n * j + j,n,R + j * *n,n FCONE FCONE FCONE FCONE);
    }

    F77_CALL(dtrti2)(&up,&diag,&jb,R + *n * j + j,n,&info FCONE FCONE);
  }
  FREE(work);FREE(iwork);
}

void mgcv_pbsi(double *R,int *r,int *nt) {

  int i,j,k,r1,*a,b;
  double x,*d,*dk,*z,*zz,*z1,*rr,*Rjj,*r2;
  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("mgcv_pbsi...");
  #endif
  d = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *r,sizeof(double));
  if (*nt < 1) *nt = 1;
  if (*nt > *r) *nt = *r;

  a = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) (*nt+1),sizeof(int));
  a[0] = 0;a[*nt] = *r;
  x = (double) *r;x = x*x*x / *nt;

  for (i=1;i < *nt;i++) a[i] = round(pow(x*i,1/3.0));
  for (i=*nt-1;i>0;i--) {
    if (a[i]>=a[i+1]) a[i] = a[i+1]-1;
  }
  r1 = *r + 1;
  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  #pragma omp parallel private(b,i,j,k,zz,z,z1,rr,Rjj,dk) num_threads(*nt)
  #endif
  {
    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp for
    #endif
    for (b=0;b< *nt;b++) {
      for (i=a[b];i<a[b+1];i++) {
        k = *r - i -1;
        Rjj = rr = R + *r * (ptrdiff_t) i + i;
        dk = d + k;
        *dk = 1 / *rr;
        z = R + *r * (ptrdiff_t)k + k + 1;
        rr -= i;
        for (zz=z,z1 = z+i;zz<z1;zz++,rr++) *zz = *rr * *dk;
        for (j=i-1;j>=0;j--) {
          Rjj -= r1;
          dk = z + j;
          *dk /= - *Rjj;
          for (zz=z,z1=z+j,rr=Rjj-j;zz<z1;zz++,rr++) *zz += *rr * *dk;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  x = (double) *r;x = x*x / *nt;

  for (i=1;i < *nt;i++) a[i] = round(sqrt(x*i));
  for (i=*nt-1;i>0;i--) {
    if (a[i]>=a[i+1]) a[i] = a[i+1]-1;
  }

  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
#pragma omp parallel private(b,i,k,zz,rr,r2) num_threads(*nt)
  #endif
  {
    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp for
    #endif
    for (b=0;b<*nt;b++) {
      for (i=a[b];i<a[b+1];i++) {
        k = *r - i - 1;
        zz = R + i * (ptrdiff_t) *r + i;
        *zz = d[k];
        zz -= i;

        for (rr = R + k * (ptrdiff_t)*r + k + 1, r2 = rr + i;rr<r2;rr++,zz++) {*zz = *rr;*rr = 0.0;}
      }
    }
  }
  FREE(d);FREE(a);
  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("done\n");
  #endif
}

SEXP mgcv_Rpbsi(SEXP A, SEXP NT) {

  int nt,r;
  double *R;
  nt = asInteger(NT);
  r = nrows(A);
  R = REAL(A);
  mgcv_pbsi1(R,&r,&nt);
  return(R_NilValue);
}

void mgcv_PPt1(double *A,double *R,int *r,int *nt) {

  int i,j,k,ik,nb=50,ib,m,*s;
  char right='R',up='U',trans='T',no='N';
  double one=1.0,di,x;

  s = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) *nt + 1,sizeof(int));
  for (i=0;i<*r;i++) for (j=i;j<*r;j++) A[i + j * *r] = R[i + j * *r];
  for (i=0;i< *r;i+=nb) {
    ib= *r-i; if (ib>nb) ib = nb;

    F77_CALL(dtrmm)(&right,&up,&trans,&no,&i,&ib,&one,A + *r * i + i,r,A + *r * i, r FCONE FCONE FCONE FCONE);

    F77_CALL(dlauu2)(&up,&ib,A + *r * i + i,r,&j FCONE);
    if (i+ib < *r) {
      j = *r - i - ib;
      m = *nt;while (5*m>i && m>1) m--;
      di = i / (double) m;
      s[0]=0; for (x=0.0,k=1;k<m;k++) { x+= di;s[k] = (int)floor(x);} s[m] = i;

      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
      #pragma omp parallel for private(k,ik) num_threads(m)
      #endif
      for (k=0;k<m;k++) {
	ik = s[k+1] - s[k];
        F77_CALL(dgemm)(&no,&trans,&ik,&ib,&j,&one,A + s[k] + (i+ib)* *r,r,A + i + (i+ib) * *r,
	                r,&one,A +  i * *r + s[k],r FCONE FCONE);
      }

      F77_CALL(dsyrk)(&up,&no,&ib,&j,&one,A+i + (i+ib) * *r,r,&one,A+ i * *r + i, r FCONE FCONE);
    }
  }
  FREE(s);

  for (i=0;i<*r;i++) for (j=i+1;j<*r;j++) A[j + i * *r] =  A[i + j * *r];
}

void mgcv_PPt(double *A,double *R,int *r,int *nt) {

  double x,*ru,*rl,*r1,*ri,*rj,*Aji,*Aij;
  int *a,i,j,b;
  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("mgcv_PPt...");
  #endif
  if (*nt < 1) *nt = 1;
  if (*nt > *r) *nt = *r;
  a = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) (*nt+1),sizeof(int));
  a[0] = 0;a[*nt] = *r;

  x = (double) *r;x = x*x / *nt;

  for (i=1;i < *nt;i++) a[i] = round(*r - sqrt(x*(*nt-i)));
  for (i=1;i <= *nt;i++) {
    if (a[i]<=a[i-1]) a[i] = a[i-1]+1;
  }

  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  #pragma omp parallel private(b,i,ru,rl,r1) num_threads(*nt)
  #endif
  {
    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp for
    #endif
    for (b=0;b<*nt;b++) {
      for (i=a[b];i<a[b+1];i++) {
        ru = rl = R + i * (ptrdiff_t)*r + i;
        ru += *r;

        for (r1=rl + *r - i,rl++;rl<r1;rl++,ru += *r) *rl = *ru;
      }
    }
  }

  x = (double) *r;x = x*x*x / *nt;

  for (i=1;i < *nt;i++) a[i] = round(*r - pow(x*(*nt-i),1/3.0));
  for (i=1;i <= *nt;i++) {
    if (a[i]<=a[i-1]) a[i] = a[i-1]+1;
  }
  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  #pragma omp parallel private(x,b,i,j,ru,rl,r1,rj,ri,Aij,Aji) num_threads(*nt)
  #endif
  {
    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp for
    #endif
    for (b=0;b< *nt;b++) {
      for (i=a[b];i<a[b+1];i++) {
        Aij = Aji = A + i * (ptrdiff_t)*r + i;
        rl = ru =  R + i * (ptrdiff_t)*r + i;
        r1 = R + (i+1) * (ptrdiff_t)*r;
        for (j=i;j < *r;j++,Aij += *r,Aji++,ru++,rl+= *r + 1) {

          for (x=0.0,rj=rl,ri=ru;ri < r1;rj++,ri++) x += *rj * *ri;
          *Aij = *Aji = x;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  x = (double) *r;x = x*x / *nt;

  for (i=1;i < *nt;i++) a[i] = round(*r - sqrt(x*(*nt-i)));
  for (i=1;i <= *nt;i++) {
    if (a[i]<=a[i-1]) a[i] = a[i-1]+1;
  }
  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  #pragma omp parallel private(b,i,rl,r1) num_threads(*nt)
  #endif
  {
    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp for
    #endif
    for (b=0;b<*nt;b++) {
      for (i=a[b];i<a[b+1];i++) {
        rl = R + i * (ptrdiff_t)*r + i;

        for (r1=rl + *r - i,rl++;rl<r1;rl++) *rl = 0.0;
      }
    }
  }
  FREE(a);
  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("done\n");
  #endif
}

SEXP mgcv_RPPt(SEXP a,SEXP r, SEXP NT) {

  int nt,n;
  double *R,*A;
  nt = asInteger(NT);
  n = nrows(a);
  A = REAL(a);
  R = REAL(r);
  mgcv_PPt1(A,R,&n,&nt);
  return(R_NilValue);
}

void mgcv_backsolve0(double *R,int *r,int *c,double *B,double *C, int *bc)

{ int i,j,k;
  double x,*pR,*pC;
  for (j=0;j<*bc;j++) {
    for (i = *c-1;i>=0;i--) {
      x = 0.0;

      pR = R + i + (i+1) * (ptrdiff_t)*r;pC = C + j * (ptrdiff_t)*c + i + 1;
      for (k=i+1;k<*c;k++,pR+= *r,pC++) x += *pR * *pC;
      C[i + (ptrdiff_t)j * *c] = (B[i + (ptrdiff_t)j * *c] - x)/R[i + (ptrdiff_t)*r * i];
    }
  }
}
void mgcv_forwardsolve0(double *R,int *r,int *c,double *B,double *C, int *bc)

{ int i,j,k;
  double x;
  for (j=0;j<*bc;j++) {
    for (i = 0;i< *c;i++) {
      x=0.0;
      for (k=0;k<i;k++) x+= C[k + (ptrdiff_t)j * *c] * R[k + (ptrdiff_t)i * *r];
      C[i + (ptrdiff_t)j * *c] = (B[i + (ptrdiff_t)j * *c] - x) / R[i + (ptrdiff_t)i * *r];
    }
  }
}

void mgcv_backsolve(double *R,int *r,int *c,double *B,double *C, int *bc,int *right)

{ double *pR,*pC,alpha=1.0;
  int n,m;
  char side='L',uplo='U',transa='N',diag='N';
  if (*right) { side = 'R';m = *bc,n= *c;} else {m = *c;n= *bc;}
  for (pC=C,pR=pC+ *bc * (ptrdiff_t)*c;pC<pR;pC++,B++) *pC = *B;
  F77_CALL(dtrsm)(&side,&uplo,&transa, &diag,&m, &n, &alpha,R, r,C,&m FCONE FCONE FCONE FCONE);
}

 void mgcv_forwardsolve(double *R,int *r,int *c,double *B,double *C, int *bc,int *right)

{ double *pR,*pC,alpha=1.0;
  int m,n;
  char side='L',uplo='U',transa='T',diag='N';
  if (*right) { side='R';m = *bc;n= *c; } else {m = *c;n= *bc;}
  for (pC=C,pR=pC+ *bc * (ptrdiff_t) *c;pC<pR;pC++,B++) *pC = *B;
  F77_CALL(dtrsm)(&side,&uplo,&transa, &diag,&m, &n, &alpha,R, r,C,&m FCONE FCONE FCONE FCONE);
}

void mgcv_pforwardsolve(double *R,int *r,int *c,double *B,double *C, int *bc,int *nt)

{ double *pR,*pC,alpha=1.0;
  int cpt,cpf,nth,i,cp;
  char side='L',uplo='U',transa='T',diag='N';
  cpt = *bc / *nt;
  if (cpt * *nt < *bc) cpt++;
  nth = *bc/cpt;
  if (nth * cpt < *bc) nth++;
  cpf = *bc - cpt * (nth-1);
  for (pC=C,pR=pC+ *bc * (ptrdiff_t)*c;pC<pR;pC++,B++) *pC = *B;

  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  #pragma omp parallel for private(i,cp) num_threads(nth)
  #endif
  for (i=0;i<nth;i++) {
    if (i==nth-1) cp = cpf; else cp = cpt;
    F77_CALL(dtrsm)(&side,&uplo,&transa, &diag,c, &cp, &alpha,R, r,
		    C + i * (ptrdiff_t) cpt * *c,c FCONE FCONE FCONE FCONE);
  }
}

void mgcv_pbacksolve(double *R,int *r,int *c,double *B,double *C, int *bc,int *nt)

{ double *pR,*pC,alpha=1.0;
  int cpt,cpf,nth,i,cp;
  char side='L',uplo='U',transa='N',diag='N';
  cpt = *bc / *nt;
  if (cpt * *nt < *bc) cpt++;
  nth = *bc/cpt;
  if (nth * cpt < *bc) nth++;
  cpf = *bc - cpt * (nth-1);
  for (pC=C,pR=pC+ *bc * (ptrdiff_t)*c;pC<pR;pC++,B++) *pC = *B;

  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  #pragma omp parallel for private(i,cp) num_threads(nth)
  #endif
  for (i=0;i<nth;i++) {
    if (i==nth-1) cp = cpf; else cp = cpt;
    F77_CALL(dtrsm)(&side,&uplo,&transa, &diag,c, &cp, &alpha,R, r,
		    C + i * (ptrdiff_t) cpt * *c,c FCONE FCONE FCONE FCONE);
  }
}

SEXP mgcv_Rpforwardsolve(SEXP R, SEXP B,SEXP NT) {

  int c,r,nt,bc;
  double *R0,*b,*C0;
  SEXP C;
  nt = asInteger(NT);
  r = nrows(R);c = ncols(R);
  R0 = REAL(R);
  bc = ncols(B);
  b = REAL(B);
  C = PROTECT(allocMatrix(REALSXP,c,bc));
  C0 = REAL(C);

  mgcv_pforwardsolve(R0,&r,&c,b,C0,&bc,&nt);

  UNPROTECT(1);
  return(C);
}

SEXP mgcv_Rpbacksolve(SEXP R, SEXP B,SEXP NT) {

  int c,r,nt,bc;
  double *R0,*b,*C0;
  SEXP C;
  nt = asInteger(NT);
  r = nrows(R);c = ncols(R);
  R0 = REAL(R);
  bc = ncols(B);
  b = REAL(B);
  C = PROTECT(allocMatrix(REALSXP,c,bc));
  C0 = REAL(C);

  mgcv_pbacksolve(R0,&r,&c,b,C0,&bc,&nt);

  UNPROTECT(1);
  return(C);
}

void row_block_reorder(double *x,int *r,int *c,int *nb,int *reverse) {

  ptrdiff_t *a,*s,k,nbf=0,i,j,q,si,ti,ns,ns_main,ns_extra;
  double *x0,*x1,tmp,*extra=NULL;
  k = *r / *nb;
  if (*r > *nb * k) {
    nbf = *r - *nb * k;k++;
  }

  ns = k * *c;
  if (nbf) {
    ns_main = (*r * *c) / *nb;
    ns_extra = ns - ns_main;
    extra = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) (*nb * ns_extra),sizeof(double));
    x0 = extra + *nb * ns_extra - 1;
    x1 = x + *r * *c -1 ;

    if (*reverse) {

      for (i=ns-1;i >= ns_main;i--) {
        x0 -= *nb - nbf;
        for (j=0;j<nbf;j++,x0--,x1--) *x0 = *x1;
      }
      x0 = x + ns_main * *nb -1;
      for (;i >= ns - *c;i--) {
        x0 -= *nb - nbf;
        for (j=0;j<nbf;j++,x0--,x1--) *x0 = *x1;
      }
    } else {

      for (i=ns-1;i>=ns_main;i--) {
        if ((i+1)%k) {
          for (j = 0;j < *nb;j++,x0--,x1--) *x0 = *x1;
        } else {
          x0 -= (*nb - nbf);
          for (j = 0;j < nbf;j++,x0--,x1--) *x0 = *x1;
        }
      }

      x0 = x + ns_main * *nb - 1;
      for (;i>=0;i--) {
        if ((i+1)%k) {
          for (j = 0;j < *nb;j++,x0--,x1--) *x0 = *x1;
        } else {
          x0 -= (*nb - nbf);
          for (j = 0;j < nbf;j++,x0--,x1--) *x0 = *x1;
        }
      }
    }
  } else {
    ns_main = ns;ns_extra=0;
  }

  a = (ptrdiff_t *) CALLOC((size_t) (k * *c),sizeof(ptrdiff_t));

  s = (ptrdiff_t *) CALLOC((size_t) (k * *c),sizeof(ptrdiff_t));
  for (i=0;i<k * *c;i++) s[i] = a[i] = i;
  ti=0;
  for (i=0;i<k;i++) for (j=0;j<*c;j++,ti++) {
      if (*reverse) si = s[(ti%k) * *c + ti/k];
      else si = s[i + j * k];
      if (ti < ns_main) x0 = x + ti * *nb;
      else x0 = extra + (ti - ns_main) * *nb;
      if (si < ns_main) x1 = x + si * *nb;
      else x1 = extra + (si - ns_main) * *nb;

      for (q=0;q < *nb;q++,x0++,x1++) { tmp = *x0;*x0 = *x1;*x1 = tmp;}

      q = a[si]; a[si] = a[ti]; a[ti] = q;
      s[a[si]] = si;s[a[ti]]=ti;
  }

  if (nbf) {
    if (*reverse) {
      si = k;
      x0 = x + *r;
      x1 = x + k * *nb;
      for (;si<ns;si++) {
        if (si == ns_main) x1 = extra;
        if ((si+1)%k) {
          for (i=0; i < *nb;i++,x0++,x1++) *x0 = *x1;
        } else {
          for (i=0;i<nbf;i++,x0++,x1++) *x0 = *x1;
          x1 += *nb - nbf;
        }
      }
    } else {
      x1 = x0 = x + (k-1) * *c * *nb;
      si = *c * (k-1);
      for (i=0;i < *c;i++,si++,x1+=(*nb-nbf)) {
        if (si == ns_main) x1 = extra;
        for (j=0;j<nbf;j++,x0++,x1++) *x0 = *x1;
      }
    }
  }

  FREE(a); FREE(s);if (nbf) FREE(extra);
}

int get_qpr_k(int *r,int *c,int *nt) {

  #ifdef OPENMP_ON
  int k;double kd,fkd,x,ckd;
  kd = sqrt(*r/(double)*c);
  if (kd <= 1.0) k = 1; else
    if (kd > *nt) k = *nt; else {
      fkd = floor(kd);ckd = ceil(kd);
      if (fkd>1) x =  *r / fkd + fkd * *c; else x = *r;
      if (*r / ckd + ckd * *c < x)
        k = (int)ckd; else k = (int)fkd;
    }
  return(k);
  #else
  return(1);
  #endif
}

void getRpqr(double *R,double *x,int *r, int *c,int *rr,int *nt) {

  int i,j,n,rows;
  double *Rs;
  Rs = x;n = *r;
  rows = *c; if (rows > *rr) rows = *rr;
  for (i=0;i<rows;i++) for (j=0;j<*c;j++) if (i>j) R[i + *rr * j] = 0; else
	R[i + *rr * j] = Rs[i + n * j];
}

void getRpqr0(double *R,double *x,int *r, int *c,int *rr,int *nt) {

  int i,j,k,n;
  double *Rs;
  k = get_qpr_k(r,c,nt);
  if (k==1) {
    Rs = x;n = *r;
  } else {
    n = k * *c;
    Rs = x + *r * *c;
  }
  for (i=0;i<*c;i++) for (j=0;j<*c;j++) if (i>j) R[i + *rr * j] = 0; else
	R[i + *rr * j] = Rs[i + n * j];
}

void mgcv_pqrqy0(double *b,double *a,double *tau,int *r,int *c,int *cb,int *tp,int *nt) {

  int i,j,k,l,left=1,n,nb,nbf,nq,True=1,False=0;
  double *x0,*x1,*Qb;
  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("mgcv_pqrqy0...");
  #endif
  k = get_qpr_k(r,c,nt);
  if (k==1) {
    if (*tp == 0 ) {
      x0 = b + *r * *cb -1;
      x1 = b + *c * *cb -1;
      for (j= *cb;j>0;j--) {

        x0 -= *r - *c;
        for (i = *c;i>0;i--,x0--,x1--) {
          *x0 = *x1;
          if (x0!=x1) *x1 = 0.0;
        }
      }
    }
    mgcv_qrqy(b,a,tau,r,cb,c,&left,tp);
    if (*tp) {
      x1 = x0 = b;
      for (i=0;i < *cb;i++,x1 += *r - *c)
	for (j=0;j < *c;j++,x0++,x1++) *x0 = *x1;
    }
    return;
  }

  nb = (int)ceil(*r/(double)k);
  nbf = *r - (k-1)*nb;
  Qb = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) (k * *c * *cb),sizeof(double));
  nq = *c * k;
  if (*tp) {

    if (*cb > 1) {
      row_block_reorder(b,r,cb,&nb,&False);
    }
    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp parallel private(i,j,l,n,x1) num_threads(k)
    #endif
    {
      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
      #pragma omp for
      #endif
      for (i=0;i<k;i++) {
        if (i == k-1) n = nbf; else n = nb;
        x1 = b+ i * nb * *cb;
        mgcv_qrqy(x1,a + i * nb * *c,tau + i * *c,&n,cb,c,&left,tp);

        for (j = 0; j < *c;j++) for (l=0;l< *cb;l++) Qb[j + i * *c + nq * l] = x1[j + n * l];
      }
    }

    mgcv_qrqy(Qb,a + *r * *c,tau + k * *c,&nq,cb,c,&left,tp);

    x0 = b;
    x1 = Qb;
    for (i=0;i<*cb;i++) {
      for (j=0;j<*c;j++,x0++,x1++) *x0 = *x1;
      x1 += (k-1) * *c;
    }
  } else {

    x0=Qb;
    x1=b;
    for (i=0;i<*cb;i++) {
      for (j=0;j<*c;j++,x0++,x1++) {
        *x0 = *x1;
        *x1=0.0;
      }
      x0 += (k-1) * *c;
    }

    mgcv_qrqy(Qb,a + *r * *c,tau + k * *c,&nq,cb,c,&left,tp);

    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp parallel private(i,j,l,n,x0,x1) num_threads(k)
    #endif
    {
      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
      #pragma omp for
      #endif
      for (i=0;i<k;i++) {
        if (i == k-1) n = nbf; else n = nb;
        x0 = b + nb * *cb * i;
        x1 = Qb + i * *c;
        for (j=0;j < *cb;j++) {
          for (l=0;l< *c;l++,x0++,x1++) *x0 = *x1;
          x0 += n - *c;
          x1 += nq - *c;
        }
        x1 = b+ i * nb * *cb;
        mgcv_qrqy(x1,a + i * nb * *c,tau + i * *c,&n,cb,c,&left,tp);
      }
    }

    if (*cb>1) row_block_reorder(b,r,cb,&nb,&True);
  }
  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("done\n");
  #endif
  FREE(Qb);
}

void mgcv_pqrqy(double *b,double *a,double *tau,int *r,int *c,int *cb,int *tp,int *nt) {

  int i,j,ki,k,left=1,nth;
  double *x0,*x1,*aii,*p0;

  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("mgcv_pqrqy...");
  #endif

  if (*tp == 0 ) {
    x0 = b + *r * *cb -1;
    x1 = b + *c * *cb -1;
    for (j= *cb;j>0;j--) {

      x0 -= *r - *c;
      for (i = *c;i>0;i--,x0--,x1--) {
        *x0 = *x1;
        if (x0!=x1) *x1 = 0.0;
      }
    }
  }
  if (*cb==1 || *nt==1) mgcv_qrqy(b,a,tau,r,cb,c,&left,tp);
  else {

    aii = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*c,sizeof(double));
    for (k=*r+1,x0=aii,x1=aii + *c,p0=a;x0<x1;x0++,p0 += k) { *x0 = *p0;*p0 = 1.0; }
    nth = *nt;if (nth > *cb) nth = *cb;
    k = *cb/nth;
    if (k*nth < *cb) k++;
    if (k*(nth-1) >= *cb) nth--;
    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp parallel private(i,j,ki) num_threads(nth)
    #endif
    {
      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
      #pragma omp for
      #endif
      for (i=0;i<nth;i++) {
        j = i * k;
        if (i==nth-1) ki = *cb - j; else ki = k;

        mgcv_qrqy0(b + j * *r,a,tau,r,&ki,c,&left,tp);
      }
    }
    for (k=*r+1,x0=aii,x1=aii + *c,p0=a;x0<x1;x0++,p0 += k) *p0= *x0;
    FREE(aii);
  }
  if (*tp) {
    x1 = x0 = b;
    for (i=0;i < *cb;i++,x1 += *r - *c)
    for (j=0;j < *c;j++,x0++,x1++) *x0 = *x1;
  }

  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("done\n");
  #endif
  return;
}

void mgcv_pqr(double *x,int *r, int *c,int *pivot, double *tau, int *nt) {

  if (*nt==1) mgcv_qr(x,r,c,pivot,tau); else {

    bpqr(x,*r,*c,tau,pivot,30,*nt);
  }
}

void mgcv_pqr0(double *x,int *r, int *c,int *pivot, double *tau, int *nt) {

  int i,j,k,l,*piv,nb,nbf,n,True=1,False=0,nr;
  double *R,*R1,*xi;
  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("mgcv_pqr0...");
  #endif
  k = get_qpr_k(r,c,nt);

  if (k==1) mgcv_qr(x,r,c,pivot,tau); else {
    nb = (int)ceil(*r/(double)k);
    nbf = *r - (k-1)*nb;

    row_block_reorder(x,r,c,&nb,&False);
    piv = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) (k * *c),sizeof(int));
    R = x + *r * *c ;
    nr = *c * k;
    #ifdef OPENMP_ON
    #pragma omp parallel private(i,j,l,n,xi,R1) num_threads(k)
    #endif
    {
      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
      #pragma omp for
      #endif
      for (i=0;i<k;i++) {
        if (i==k-1) n = nbf; else n = nb;
        xi = x + nb * i * *c;
        mgcv_qr(xi,&n,c,piv + i * *c,tau + i * *c);

        R1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*c * *c),sizeof(double));
        for (l=0;l<*c;l++) for (j=l;j<*c;j++) R1[l + *c * j] = xi[l + n * j];

        pivoter(R1,c,c,piv + i * *c,&True,&True);
        for (l=0;l<*c;l++) for (j=0;j<*c;j++) R[i * *c +l + nr *j] = R1[l+ *c * j];
        FREE(R1);
      }
    }
    FREE(piv);
    n = k * *c;
    mgcv_qr(R,&n,c,pivot,tau + k * *c);
  }
  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("done\n");
  #endif
}

void mgcv_qr(double *x, int *r, int *c,int *pivot,double *tau)

{ int info,lwork=-1,*ip;
  double work1,*work;

  F77_CALL(dgeqp3)(r,c,x,r,pivot,tau,&work1,&lwork,&info);
  lwork=(int)floor(work1);if (work1-lwork>0.5) lwork++;
  work=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)lwork,sizeof(double));

  F77_CALL(dgeqp3)(r,c,x,r,pivot,tau,work,&lwork,&info);
  FREE(work);

  for (ip=pivot;ip < pivot + *c;ip++) (*ip)--;

}

void mgcv_qr2(double *x, int *r, int *c,int *pivot,double *tau)

{ int info,*ip,i;
  double *work;
  work=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)*r,sizeof(double));

  F77_CALL(dgeqr2)(r,c,x,r,tau,work,&info);
  FREE(work);

  for (i=0,ip=pivot;ip < pivot + *c;ip++,i++) *ip = i;

}

void mgcv_qrqy0(double *b,double *a,double *tau,int *r,int *c,int *k,int *left,int *tp) {

  char side='L';
  int lda,lwork=-1,incv=1,ri,i0,i1,ii,i;
  double *work,*v;

  if (! *left) {
    side='R';lda = *c;lwork = *r;
  } else {
    lda= *r;lwork = *c;
  }

  work=(double *)calloc((size_t)lwork,sizeof(double));
  if ((*left && ! *tp)||(! *left && *tp)) {
    i0 = *k - 1;i1=-1;ii=-1;
  } else {
    i0 = 0;i1 = *k;ii=1;
  }
  for (i=i0;i!=i1;i+=ii) {
    v = a + lda * i + i;
    ri = *r - i;
    F77_CALL(dlarf)(&side,&ri,c,v,&incv,tau+i,b+i,r,work FCONE);
  }
  free(work);
  }

void mgcv_qrqy(double *b,double *a,double *tau,int *r,int *c,int *k,int *left,int *tp)

{ char side='L',trans='N';
  int lda,lwork=-1,info;
  double *work,work1;

  if (! *left) { side='R';lda = *c;} else lda= *r;
  if ( *tp) trans='T';

  F77_CALL(dormqr)(&side,&trans,r,c,k,a,&lda,tau,b,r,&work1,&lwork,&info FCONE FCONE);
  lwork=(int)floor(work1);if (work1-lwork>0.5) lwork++;
  work=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)lwork,sizeof(double));

  F77_CALL(dormqr)(&side,&trans,r,c,k,a,&lda,tau,b,r,work,&lwork,&info FCONE FCONE);
  FREE(work);
}

void update_qr(double *Q,double *R,int *n, int *q,double *lam, int *k)

{ double *x,*work,c,s,r,x0,x1,m,*xip,*xjp,*riip,*rijp,*Qp,*wp;
  x=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)*q,sizeof(double));
  work=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
  x[*k] = *lam;

  for (Qp=Q+ *k * *n,riip=R+ *k * *q + *k,xip=x+ *k ;xip< x+ *q;xip++,riip+= *q+1)
  {
    x0= *xip;
    x1= *riip;
    m = fabs(x0);s=fabs(x1); if (s>m) m=s;
    x1/=m;x0/=m;
    r=sqrt(x0*x0+x1*x1);
    c=x1/r;s=x0/r;
    *riip=m*r;

    for (rijp=riip + *q,xjp=xip+1;xjp<x+ *q;xjp++,rijp+= *q)
    { x1= *rijp;

      *rijp = c*x1 - s* *xjp;
      *xjp = s*x1 + c* *xjp;
    }

    for (wp=work;wp<work+ *n;wp++,Qp++)
    { x1 = *Qp;

      *Qp = c*x1-s* *wp;
      *wp = s*x1+c* *wp;
    }
  }
  FREE(x);FREE(work);
}

void mgcv_symeig(double *A,double *ev,int *n,int *use_dsyevd,int *get_vectors,
                 int *descending)

{ char jobz='V',uplo='L',range='A';
  double work1,*work,dum1=0,abstol=0.0,*Z,*dum2,x,*p,*p1,*p2,*Acopy;
  int lwork = -1,liwork = -1,iwork1,info,*iwork,dumi=0,n_eval=0,*isupZ,i,j,k,debug=0;
  if (debug && *get_vectors) {
    Acopy = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*n * *n),sizeof(double));
    for (p2=Acopy,p=A,p1=A+ *n * *n;p<p1;p++,p2++) *p2 = *p;
  }

  if (*get_vectors) jobz='V'; else jobz='N';
  if (*use_dsyevd)
  { F77_CALL(dsyevd)(&jobz,&uplo,n,A,n,ev,&work1,&lwork,&iwork1,&liwork,&info FCONE FCONE);
    lwork=(int)floor(work1);if (work1-lwork>0.5) lwork++;
    work=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)lwork,sizeof(double));
    liwork = iwork1;iwork= (int *)CALLOC((size_t)liwork,sizeof(int));
    F77_CALL(dsyevd)(&jobz,&uplo,n,A,n,ev,work,&lwork,iwork,&liwork,&info FCONE FCONE);
    FREE(work);FREE(iwork);
    if (*descending) for (i=0;i<*n/2;i++) {

	p = A + i * *n;
        p1 = A + *n * (*n - 1 - i);
        for (p2 = p + *n;p<p2;p++,p1++) {
          x = *p;*p=*p1;*p1=x;
        }
    }
  } else
  { Z=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*n * *n),sizeof(double));
    isupZ=(int *)CALLOC((size_t)(2 * *n),sizeof(int));
    F77_CALL(dsyevr)(&jobz,&range,&uplo,
		   n,A,n,&dum1,&dum1,&dumi,&dumi,
		   &abstol,&n_eval,ev,
    		   Z,n,isupZ, &work1,&lwork,&iwork1,&liwork,&info FCONE FCONE FCONE);
    lwork=(int)floor(work1);if (work1-lwork>0.5) lwork++;
    work=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)lwork,sizeof(double));
    liwork = iwork1;iwork= (int *)CALLOC((size_t)liwork,sizeof(int));
    F77_CALL(dsyevr)(&jobz,&range,&uplo,
		   n,A,n,&dum1,&dum1,&dumi,&dumi,
		   &abstol,&n_eval,ev,
    		     Z,n,isupZ, work,&lwork,iwork,&liwork,&info FCONE FCONE FCONE);
    FREE(work);FREE(iwork);

    if (*get_vectors) {
     p1 = A;
     if (*descending) {
        dum2 = Z + *n * (*n-1);
        for (work=dum2;work>=Z;work -= *n)
	  for (p=work;p<work + *n;p++,A++) *A = *p;
      } else {
        dum2 = Z + *n * *n;
        for (work=Z;work<dum2;work++,A++) *A = *work;
      }
    }
    A = p1;
    FREE(Z);FREE(isupZ);
  }
  if (*descending) for (i=0;i<*n/2;i++) {
       x = ev[i]; ev[i] = ev[*n-i-1];ev[*n-i-1] = x;
  }
  if (debug && *get_vectors) {
    p = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*n * *n),sizeof(double));
    getXtX(p,A,n,n);
    x=0.0;k=0;
    for (i=0;i<*n;i++) for (j=0;j<i;j++)  if (fabs(p[i + *n * j])>1e-14) {
	  x += fabs(p[i + *n * j]);k++;
    }
    Rprintf("**\n");
    j=k;
    if (k) Rprintf("Non orthogonal eigenvectors %d %g\n",k,x/k);
    x=0.0;k=0;
    for (i=0;i<*n;i++) if (fabs(p[i + *n * i]-1)>1e-14) {
	x += fabs(p[i + *n * i]-1);k++;
    }
    if (k) Rprintf("Eigenvectors not normalized %d %g\n",k,x/k);
    if (k+j>0) dump_mat(Acopy,n,n,"/home/sw283/tmp/badmat.dat");
    FREE(p);FREE(Acopy);
  }

}

void mgcv_trisymeig(double *d,double *g,double *v,int *n,int *getvec,int *descending)

{ char compz;
  double *work,work1,x,*dum1,*dum2;
  int ldz=0,info,lwork=-1,liwork=-1,*iwork,iwork1,i,j;

  if (*getvec) { compz='I';ldz = *n;} else { compz='N';ldz=1;}

  F77_CALL(dstedc)(&compz,n,
		   d, g,
		   v,
                   &ldz,
		   &work1, &lwork,
		   &iwork1, &liwork, &info FCONE);

   lwork=(int)floor(work1);if (work1-lwork>0.5) lwork++;
   work=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)lwork,sizeof(double));
   liwork = iwork1;
   iwork= (int *)CALLOC((size_t)liwork,sizeof(int));

   F77_CALL(dstedc)(&compz,n,
		   d, g,
		   v,
                   &ldz,
		   work, &lwork,
		   iwork, &liwork, &info FCONE);

   if (*descending) {
     for (i=0;i<*n/2;i++) {
       x = d[i]; d[i] = d[*n-i-1];d[*n-i-1] = x;
       if (*getvec) {
         dum1 = v + *n * i;dum2 = v + *n * (*n-i-1);
         for (j=0;j<*n;j++,dum1++,dum2++) {
           x = *dum1;*dum1 = *dum2;*dum2 = x;
         }
       }
     }
   }

   FREE(work);FREE(iwork);
   *n=info;
}

void Rlanczos(double *A,double *U,double *D,int *n, int *m, int *lm,double *tol,int *nt) {

  int biggest=0,f_check,i,k,kk,ok,l,j,vlength=0,ni,pi,converged,incx=1,ri,ci=0,cir=0,one=1;
  double **q,*v=NULL,bt,xx,yy,*a,*b,*d,*g,*z,*err,*p0,*p1,*zp,*qp,normTj,eps_stop,max_err,alpha=1.0,beta=0.0;
  unsigned long jran=1,ia=106,ic=1283,im=6075;

  char uplo='U',trans='T';
  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("Rlanczos");
  #endif

  #ifndef OPENMP_ON
  *nt = 1;
  #endif
  if (*nt > *n) *nt = *n;

  eps_stop = *tol;

  if (*lm<0) { biggest=1;*lm=0;}
  f_check = (*m + *lm)/2;
  if (f_check<10) f_check =10;
  kk = (int) floor(*n/10); if (kk<1) kk=1;
  if (kk<f_check) f_check = kk;

  q=(double **)CALLOC((size_t)(*n+1),sizeof(double *));

  q[0]=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
  b=q[0];bt=0.0;
  for (i=0;i < *n;i++)
  { jran=(jran*ia+ic) % im;
    xx=(double) jran / (double) im -0.5;
    b[i]=xx;xx=-xx;bt+=b[i]*b[i];
  }
  bt=sqrt(bt);
  for (i=0;i < *n;i++) b[i]/=bt;

  a=(double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(double));
  b=(double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(double));
  g=(double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(double));
  d=(double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(double));
  z=(double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(double));
  err=(double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(double));
  for (i=0;i< *n;i++) err[i]=1e300;

  if (*nt>1) {
    ci = *n / *nt;
    cir = *n - ci * (*nt - 1);
    if (cir>ci) {
      ci++;
      *nt = (int)ceil(*n/ci);
      cir = *n - ci * (*nt - 1);
    }
    if (cir == 0) { (*nt)--;cir=ci; }
  }

  for (j=0;j< *n;j++) {

    if (*nt>1) {
      #ifdef OPENMP_ON
      #pragma omp parallel private(i,ri) num_threads(*nt)
      #endif
      {
	#ifdef OPENMP_ON
	#pragma omp for
	#endif
        for (i=0;i<*nt;i++) {
          if (i < *nt-1) ri = ci; else ri = cir;

          F77_CALL(dgemv)(&trans,n,&ri,&alpha,A+i * ci * *n,n,q[j],
                        &one,&beta,z+i*ci,&one FCONE);
        }
      }
    } else F77_CALL(dsymv)(&uplo,n,&alpha,
		A,n,
		q[j],&incx,
		&beta,z,&incx FCONE);

    for (xx=0.0,qp=q[j],p0=qp+*n,zp=z;qp<p0;qp++,zp++) xx += *qp * *zp;
    a[j] = xx;

    if (!j)
    {
      for (zp=z,p0=zp+*n,qp=q[j];zp<p0;qp++,zp++) *zp -= xx * *qp;
    } else
    {
      yy=b[j-1];
      for (zp=z,p0=zp + *n,qp=q[j],p1=q[j-1];zp<p0;zp++,qp++,p1++) *zp -= xx * *qp + yy * *p1;

      for (i=0;i<=j;i++)
      {

        xx = -F77_CALL(ddot)(n,z,&incx,q[i],&incx);

        F77_CALL(daxpy)(n,&xx,q[i],&incx,z,&incx);
      }

      for (i=0;i<=j;i++)
      {

        xx = -F77_CALL(ddot)(n,z,&incx,q[i],&incx);

        F77_CALL(daxpy)(n,&xx,q[i],&incx,z,&incx);
      }

    }

    for (xx=0.0,zp=z,p0=zp+*n;zp<p0;zp++) xx += *zp * *zp;
    b[j]=sqrt(xx);

    if (j < *n-1)
    { q[j+1]=(double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(double));
      for (xx=b[j],qp=q[j+1],p0=qp + *n,zp=z;qp<p0;qp++,zp++) *qp = *zp/xx;
    }

    if (((j>= *m + *lm)&&(j%f_check==0))||(j == *n-1))
    { for (i=0;i<j+1;i++) d[i]=a[i];
      for (i=0;i<j;i++) g[i]=b[i];

      if (vlength) FREE(v);
      vlength=j+1;
      v = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(vlength*vlength),sizeof(double));

      kk = j + 1;
      mgcv_trisymeig(d,g,v,&kk,&one,&one);

      normTj=fabs(d[0]);if (fabs(d[j])>normTj) normTj=fabs(d[j]);

      for (k=0;k<j+1;k++)
      { err[k]=b[j]*v[k * vlength + j];
        err[k]=fabs(err[k]);
      }

      if (j >= *m + *lm)
      { max_err=normTj*eps_stop;
        if (biggest) {

          pi=ni=0;converged=1;
          while (pi+ni < *m) if (fabs(d[pi])>= fabs(d[j-ni])) {
              if (err[pi]>max_err) {converged=0;break;} else pi++;
	    } else {
              if (err[ni]>max_err) {converged=0;break;} else ni++;
            }

          if (converged) {
            *m = pi;
            *lm = ni;
            j++;break;
          }
        } else
        { ok=1;
          for (i=0;i < *m;i++) if (err[i]>max_err) ok=0;
          for (i=j;i > j - *lm;i--) if (err[i]>max_err) ok=0;
          if (ok)
          { j++;break;}
        }
      }
    }
  }

  for (k=0;k < *m;k++)
  { D[k]=d[k];
    for (l=0;l<j;l++)
    for (xx=v[l + k * vlength],p0=U + k * *n,p1 = p0 + *n,qp=q[l];p0<p1;p0++,qp++) *p0 += *qp * xx;
  }

  for (k= *m;k < *lm + *m;k++)
  { kk=j-(*lm + *m - k);
    D[k]=d[kk];
    for (l=0;l<j;l++)
    for (xx=v[l + kk * vlength],p0=U + k * *n,p1 = p0 + *n,qp=q[l];p0<p1;p0++,qp++) *p0 += *qp * xx;
  }

  FREE(a);
  FREE(b);
  FREE(g);
  FREE(d);
  FREE(z);
  FREE(err);
  if (vlength) FREE(v);
  for (i=0;i< *n+1;i++) if (q[i]) FREE(q[i]);
  FREE(q);
  *n = j;
  #ifdef OMP_REPORT
  Rprintf("done\n");
  #endif
}

SEXP spdev(SEXP A) {

  SEXP i_sym,x_sym,dim_sym,p_sym,MOD;
  int *dim,*Ai,*Ap,n,i,j,k,*mod;
  double *Ax,x,z,*dA,*csA;
  p_sym = install("p");
  dim_sym = install("Dim");
  i_sym = install("i");
  x_sym = install("x");
  dim = INTEGER(R_do_slot(A,dim_sym));
  n = dim[0];
  Ap = INTEGER(R_do_slot(A,p_sym));
  Ai = INTEGER(R_do_slot(A,i_sym));
  Ax = REAL(R_do_slot(A,x_sym));

  dA = (double *) CALLOC((size_t)n,sizeof(double));
  csA = (double *) CALLOC((size_t)n,sizeof(double));

  for (j=0;j<n;j++) {
    for (k=Ap[j];k<Ap[j+1];k++) {
      i = Ai[k];
      if (i==j) dA[j] = Ax[k]; else csA[j] += fabs(Ax[k]);
    }
  }
  PROTECT(MOD=allocVector(INTSXP,1));
  mod=INTEGER(MOD);*mod=0;

  for (j=0;j<n;j++) if (dA[j]<=0) { dA[j] = csA[j];(*mod)++;}

  for (j=0;j<n;j++) {
    for (k=Ap[j];k<Ap[j+1];k++) {
      i = Ai[k];
      if (i==j) Ax[k] = dA[j];
      x = sqrt(dA[i]*dA[j]); z = (dA[i] + dA[j])/2;

      if (z<x) x = z;
      if (Ax[k] > x) { Ax[k] = x; (*mod)++;} else if (Ax[k] < -x) { Ax[k] = -x; (*mod)++;}
    }
  }

  FREE(dA);FREE(csA);
  UNPROTECT(1);
  return(MOD);
}

SEXP dpdev(SEXP a) {

  int n,i,j,k,*mod;
  double *A,*dA,*csA,*pA,*p1,*p2,*pd,*pc,x,z;
  SEXP MOD;
  n = nrows(a);
  a = PROTECT(coerceVector(a,REALSXP));
  A = REAL(a);

  dA = (double *) CALLOC((size_t)n,sizeof(double));
  csA = (double *) CALLOC((size_t)n,sizeof(double));
  PROTECT(MOD=allocVector(INTSXP,1));
  mod=INTEGER(MOD);*mod=0;
  for (pd=dA,pc=csA,pA=A,j=0;j<n;j++,pd++,pc++) {
    for (p1=pA+j,p2=pA+n;pA<p1;pA++) *pc += fabs(*pA);
    *pd = *pA;pA++;
    for (;pA<p2;pA++) *pc += fabs(*pA);
  }

  for (j=0;j<n;j++) if (dA[j]<=0) {
      A[j+j*n] = dA[j] = csA[j];(*mod)++;
  }

  for (k=j=0;j<n;j++)
  for (i=0;i<n;i++,k++) {
    x = sqrt(dA[i]*dA[j]); z = (dA[i] + dA[j])/2;

    if (z<x) x = z;
    if (A[k] > x) { A[k] = x; (*mod)++;} else
    if (A[k] < -x) { A[k] = -x; (*mod)++;}
  }

  FREE(dA);FREE(csA);
  UNPROTECT(2);
  return(MOD);
}

# --- End of mgcv/src/mat.c ---

# --- Start of mgcv/src/matrix.c ---

#include <R.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdarg.h>
#include "mgcv.h"
#include "matrix.h"
#include "general.h"

#define ROUND(a) ((a)-(int)floor(a)>0.5) ? ((int)floor(a)+1):((int)floor(a))

matrix null_mat;

matrix initmat(int rows,int cols)

{ matrix A;int i;
  A.vec=0;
  A.M=(double **)CALLOC((size_t)rows,sizeof(double *));
  if ((cols==1)||(rows==1)) A.vec=1;
  if (A.M) A.M[0]=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)(cols*rows),sizeof(double));
  for (i=1;i<rows;i++) A.M[i]=A.M[0]+i*cols;

  A.mem=(long)(rows*cols*sizeof(double));
  A.original_r=A.r=rows;A.original_c=A.c=cols;

  A.V=A.M[0];
  return(A);
}

matrix initvec(int rows)

{ return(initmat(1,rows));}

void freemat(matrix A) {
  if (A.V) FREE(A.V);
  if (A.M) FREE(A.M);
}

void vmult(matrix *A,matrix *b,matrix *c,int t)

{ double **AM,*bV,*cV,*p;
  int i,j,cr,br;
  cr=c->r;br=b->r;
  AM=A->M;bV=b->V;cV=c->V;
  if (t)
  for (i=0;i<cr;i++)
  { *cV=0.0;
    for (j=0;j<br;j++) *cV += AM[j][i]*bV[j];
    cV++;
  } else
  for (i=0;i<cr;i++)
  { *cV=0.0;
    p=AM[i];
    for (j=0;j<br;j++)
    *cV += p[j]*bV[j];
    cV++;
  }
}

void mcopy(matrix *A,matrix *B)

{ int Ac;
  double *pA,*pB,**AM,**BM;
  if (A->r>B->r||A->c>B->c) error(_("Target matrix too small in mcopy"));
  BM=B->M;Ac=A->c;
  for (AM=A->M;AM<A->M+A->r;AM++)
  { pB= *BM;
    for (pA= *AM;pA< *AM+Ac; pA++) *(pB++) = *pA;
    BM++;
  }
}

void matmult(matrix C,matrix A,matrix B,int tA,int tB)

{ int i,j,k;
  double temp,*p,*p1,*p2,**CM,**AM,**BM;
  AM=A.M;BM=B.M;CM=C.M;
  if (tA)
  { if (tB)
    { if ((A.r!=B.c)||(A.c!=C.r)||(B.r!=C.c))
      { error(_("Incompatible matrices in matmult."));}
      for (i=0;i<A.c;i++) for (j=0;j<B.r;j++)
      { p2=CM[i]+j;(*p2)=0.0;p=BM[j];
	for (k=0;k<A.r;k++)
	(*p2)+=AM[k][i]*(*p++);
      }
    } else
    { if ((A.r!=B.r)||(A.c!=C.r)||(B.c!=C.c))
      { error(_("Incompatible matrices in matmult."));}
      for (i=0;i<A.c;i++)
      for (p=CM[i];p<(CM[i]+C.c);p++)
      (*p)=0.0;
      for (k=0;k<A.r;k++) for (i=0;i<A.c;i++)
      { temp=AM[k][i];p1=BM[k];
	for (p=CM[i];p<(CM[i]+B.c);p++)
	(*p)+=temp*(*p1++);
      }
    }
  } else
  { if (tB)
    { if ((A.c!=B.c)||(A.r!=C.r)||(B.r!=C.c))
      { error(_("Incompatible matrices in matmult."));}
      for (i=0;i<A.r;i++) for (j=0;j<B.r;j++)
      { p2=CM[i]+j;*p2=0.0;p1=BM[j];
	for (p=AM[i];p<(AM[i]+A.c);p++)
	(*p2)+=(*p)*(*p1++);
      }
    } else
    { if ((A.c!=B.r)||(C.r!=A.r)||(C.c!=B.c))
      { error(_("Incompatible matrices in matmult."));}
      for (i=0;i<A.r;i++) for (p=CM[i];p<(CM[i]+B.c);p++) *p=0.0;
      for (k=0;k<A.c;k++) for (i=0;i<A.r;i++)
      { p1=BM[k];temp=AM[i][k];
	     for (p=CM[i];p<(CM[i]+B.c);p++)
	     (*p)+=temp*(*p1++);
      }
    }
  }
}

void invert(matrix *A)

{ double **AM,*p,*p1,max,x;
  int *c,*rp,*cp,i,j,k,pr=0,pc=0,*d,cj,ck;
  if (A->r!=A->c) error(_("Attempt to invert() non-square matrix"));
  c=(int *)CALLOC((size_t)A->c,sizeof(int));
  d=(int *)CALLOC((size_t)A->c,sizeof(int));
  rp=(int *)CALLOC((size_t)A->c,sizeof(int));
  cp=(int *)CALLOC((size_t)A->c,sizeof(int));
  for (i=0;i<A->c;i++) { c[i]=i;d[i]=i;}
  AM=A->M;
  for (j=0;j<A->c;j++)
  { max=0.0;
    for (i=j;i<A->r;i++)
    { p=AM[i];
      for (k=j;k<A->c;k++)
      { x=p[c[k]];if (fabs(x)>max) { max=fabs(x);pr=i;pc=k;}}
    }

    p=AM[j];AM[j]=AM[pr];AM[pr]=p;
    k=c[j];c[j]=c[pc];c[pc]=k;
    rp[j]=pr;
    cp[j]=pc;
    cj=c[j];

    x=AM[j][cj];
    if (x==0.0) error(_("Singular Matrix passed to invert()"));
    for (p=AM[j];p<AM[j]+A->c;p++) *p/=x;
    AM[j][cj]=1.0/x;
    for (i=0;i<A->r;i++)
    { p=AM[i];p1=AM[j];
      if (i!=j)
      { x = -p[cj];
        for (k=0;k<j;k++)
        { ck=c[k];p[ck]+=x*p1[ck];}
        p[cj]=x*p1[cj];
        for (k=j+1;k<A->c;k++)
        { ck=c[k];p[ck]+=x*p1[ck];}
      }
    }
  }

  for (i=A->r-1;i>=0;i--)
  { if (cp[i]!=i)
    { p=AM[i];AM[i]=AM[cp[i]];AM[cp[i]]=p;
    }
  }

  for (j=0;j<A->c-1;j++)
  if (c[j]!=j)
  { if (c[j]<j) k=c[c[j]]; else k=c[j];
    for (i=0;i<A->r;i++)
    { p=AM[i];x=p[j];p[j]=p[k];p[k]=x;}
    d[k]=d[j];d[j]=c[j];
    c[d[k]]=k;
  }

  for (i=A->r-1;i>=0;i--)
  if (rp[i]!=i)
  { for (k=0;k<A->r;k++)
    { p=AM[k];x=p[i];p[i]=p[rp[i]];p[rp[i]]=x;}
  }

  FREE(c);FREE(rp);FREE(cp);FREE(d);
}

double dot(matrix a,matrix b)

{ int i,k=0;double c=0.0,*p,*p1;
  if (a.vec) { p1=b.V;for (p=a.V;p<a.V+a.c*a.r;p++) c+=(*p)*(*p1++);}
  else
  for (i=0;i<a.r;i++) for (p=a.M[i];p<(a.M[i]+a.c);p++)
  { c+=(*p)*b.M[k/b.c][k%b.c];k++;}
  return(c);
}

double enorm(matrix d)

{ double e=0.0,m=0.0,y,*p;
  int i;
  if (d.vec) for (p=d.V;p<d.V+d.r*d.c;p++) { y=fabs(*p); if (y>m) m=y; }
  else for (i=0;i<d.r;i++) for (p=d.M[i];p<d.M[i]+d.c;p++)
  { y=fabs(*p);if (y>m) m=y;}
  if (!m) return(0.0);
  if (d.vec) for (p=d.V;p<d.V+d.r*d.c;p++)
  { y= *p / m; e+=y*y;} else
  for (i=0;i<d.r;i++) for (p=d.M[i];p<(d.M[i]+d.c);p++)
  { y= *p / m;e+=y*y;}
  e=sqrt(e)*m;
  return(e);
}

void householder(matrix *u,matrix a,matrix b,int t1)

{ int i;double v,*aV,*bV,*uV;
  aV=a.V;bV=b.V;uV=u->V;
  u->r=t1+1;
  for (i=0;i<u->r;i++) uV[i]=aV[i]-bV[i];
  v=enorm((*u))/sqrt(2.0);
  for (i=0;i<u->r;i++) uV[i]/=v;
}

void Hmult(matrix C,matrix u)

{ double temp,*p,*p1,*uV,**CuM,**CM;
  int i,j;
  matrix Cu;
  Cu=initmat(C.r,u.c);
  uV=u.V;CuM=Cu.M;CM=C.M;
  for (i=0;i<Cu.r;i++)
  { p1=CuM[i];(*p1)=0.0;p=CM[i];
    for (j=0;j<u.r;j++) (*p1)+=(*p++)*uV[j];
  }
  for (i=0;i<Cu.r;i++)
  { temp=Cu.V[i];p=CM[i];
    for (j=0;j<u.r;j++)
    (*p++) -= temp*uV[j];
  }
  freemat(Cu);
}

void HQmult(matrix C,matrix U,int p,int t)

{ double *u,*CuV,**CM;
  matrix Cu;
  int i,j,k;
  if (p) Cu=initmat(C.c,1);else Cu=initmat(C.r,1);
  CuV=Cu.V;CM=C.M;
  if (p)
  { if (t)
    { for (k=0;k<U.r;k++)
      { u=U.M[k];
        for (i=0;i<C.c;i++)
	     { CuV[i]=0.0;
          for (j=0;j<C.r;j++) CuV[i]+=CM[j][i]*u[j];
	     }
	     for (i=0;i<C.r;i++) for (j=0;j<C.c;j++) CM[i][j] -= CuV[j]*u[i];
      }
    }  else
    { for (k=U.r-1;k>=0;k--)
      { u=U.M[k];
	     for (i=0;i<C.c;i++)
	     { CuV[i]=0.0;
	       for (j=0;j<C.r;j++) CuV[i]+=CM[j][i]*u[j];
	     }
	     for (i=0;i<C.r;i++) for (j=0;j<C.c;j++) CM[i][j] -= CuV[j]*u[i];
      }
    }
  } else
  { if (t)
    { for (k=U.r-1;k>=0;k--)
      { u=U.M[k];
	     for (i=0;i<C.r;i++)
	     { CuV[i]=0.0;
	       for (j=0;j<C.c;j++) CuV[i]+=CM[i][j]*u[j];
	     }
	     for (i=0;i<C.r;i++) for (j=0;j<C.c;j++) CM[i][j] -= CuV[i]*u[j];
      }
      } else
      { for (k=0;k<U.r;k++)
      { u=U.M[k];
	     for (i=0;i<C.r;i++)
	     { CuV[i]=0.0;
	       for (j=0;j<C.c;j++) CuV[i]+=CM[i][j]*u[j];
	     }
	     for (i=0;i<C.r;i++) for (j=0;j<C.c;j++) CM[i][j] -= CuV[i]*u[j];
      }
    }
  }
  freemat(Cu);
}

void QT(matrix Q,matrix A,int fullQ)

{ int i,j,Ar,Ac,k;
  double lsq,*p,*p1,**QM,**AM,g,x,m;
  QM=Q.M;AM=A.M;Ar=A.r;Ac=A.c;
  if (fullQ) for (i=0;i<Ac;i++)
  { p=QM[i];
    for (j=0;j<Ac;j++) if (i==j)
    p[j]=1.0; else p[j]=0.0;
  }
  if (Ar>0)
  { for (i=0;i<Ar;i++)
    {
      p=AM[i];
      m=0.0;for (j=0;j<Ac-i;j++) { x=p[j];x=fabs(x);if (x>m) m=x;}
      if (m) for (j=0;j<Ac-i;j++) p[j]/=m;
      lsq=0.0;for (j=0;j<Ac-i;j++) lsq+=p[j]*p[j];
      lsq=sqrt(lsq);
      if (p[Ac-i-1]<0.0) lsq= -lsq;
      p[Ac-i-1]+=lsq;
      if (lsq)
      g=1/(lsq*p[Ac-i-1]);
      else g=0.0;
      lsq*=m;
      for (j=i+1;j<Ar;j++)
      { x=0.0;p1=AM[j];
        for (k=0;k<Ac-i;k++) x+=p[k]*p1[k];
        x*=g;
        for (k=0;k<Ac-i;k++) p1[k] += -x*p[k];
      }
      if (fullQ)
      for (j=0;j<Q.r;j++)
      { x=0.0;p=AM[i];p1=QM[j];
        for (k=0;k<Ac-i;k++) x+=p[k]*p1[k];
        x*=g;
        for (k=0;k<Ac-i;k++) p1[k] += -x*p[k];
      } else
      { g=sqrt(g);
        p=QM[i];p1=AM[i];
        for (j=0;j<Ac-i;j++) p[j]=p1[j]*g;
        for (j=Ac-i;j<Ac;j++) p[j]=0.0;
      }
      AM[i][Ac-i-1]=-lsq;
      for (j=0;j<Ac-i-1;j++) AM[i][j]=0.0;
    }
  }
}

void OrthoMult(matrix *Q,matrix *A,int off,int rows,int t,int pre,int o_pre)

{ double au,*u,*a,**AtM=NULL,**AM,**QM;
  int i,j,k,Ar,Qc,kk;
  matrix At;
  if (o_pre) t=1-t;
  if (pre)
  { At=initmat(A->c,A->r);
    AM=A->M;AtM=At.M;
    for (i=0;i<A->r;i++) for (j=0;j<A->c;j++) AtM[j][i]=AM[i][j];
    t=1-t;
  } else At=*A;
  AM=At.M;QM=Q->M;Ar=At.r;Qc=Q->c;
  for (kk=0;kk<rows;kk++)
  { if (t) k=rows-1-kk; else k=kk;
    u=QM[k];
    for (i=0;i<Ar;i++)
    { a=AM[i];au=0.0;
      for (j=off+k;j<Qc;j++) au+=a[j]*u[j];
      for (j=off+k;j<Qc;j++) a[j] -= au*u[j];
    }
  }
  if (pre)
  { AM=A->M;
    for (i=0;i<At.r;i++) for (j=0;j<At.c;j++) AM[j][i]=AtM[i][j];
    freemat(At);
  }
}

void Rsolv(matrix *R,matrix *p,matrix *y, int transpose)

{ int i,j,k;
  double x,*pV,*yV,*RMi,**RM,*dum,**pM,**yM;
  pV=p->V;yV=y->V;
  if (y->r==1)
  { if (transpose)
    { RM=R->M;
      for (i=0;i<R->r;i++)
      { x=0.0;dum=pV;for (j=0;j<i;j++) { x+=RM[j][i] * *dum;dum++;}
        *dum=(yV[i]-x)/RM[i][i];
      }
    } else
    for (i=R->r-1;i>=0;i--)
    { RMi=R->M[i];
      x=0.0;for (j=i+1;j<R->r;j++) x+=RMi[j]*pV[j];
      pV[i]=(yV[i]-x)/RMi[i];
    }
  } else
  { pM=p->M;yM=y->M;
    if (transpose)
    { RM=R->M;
      for (k=0;k<p->c;k++)
      for (i=0;i<R->r;i++)
      { x=0.0;for (j=0;j<i;j++) x+=RM[j][i] * pM[j][k];
        pM[i][k]=(yM[i][k]-x)/RM[i][i];
      }
    } else
    for (k=0;k<p->c;k++)
    for (i=R->r-1;i>=0;i--)
    { RMi=R->M[i];
      x=0.0;for (j=i+1;j<R->r;j++) x+=RMi[j]*pM[j][k];
      pM[i][k]=(yM[i][k]-x)/RMi[i];
    }
  }
}

int QR(matrix *Q,matrix *R)

{ int i,j,k,n,Rr,Rc;
  double *u,t,z,**RM,*p,m;/

    if (m) for (i=k;i<Rr;i++) RM[i][k]/=m;

    t=0.0;for (i=k;i<Rr;i++) { z= RM[i][k];t+=z*z;}

    if (RM[k][k]>0.0) t = -sqrt(t);else t= sqrt(t);

    for (i=k+1;i<Rr;i++) { u[i]=RM[i][k];RM[i][k]=0.0;}
    z=RM[k][k];
    u[k]=RM[k][k]-t;RM[k][k]=t*m;
    t=t*t;t+=u[k]*u[k]-z*z;

    t=sqrt(t/2);
    if (t==0.0) {FREE(u);return(0);}
    for (p=u+k;p<u+Rr;p++) *p /= t;
    for (j=k+1;j<R->c;j++) {

      t=0.0;for (i=k;i<Rr;i++) t+=u[i] * RM[i][j];

      for (i=k;i<Rr;i++) RM[i][j] -= u[i]*t;
    }
    if (Q->r)
    { p=Q->M[k];
      for (i=k;i<Rr;i++) p[i]=u[i];
    }
  }
  FREE(u);
  return(1);
}

int real_elemcmp(const void *a,const void *b,int el)

{ static int k=0;
  double *na,*nb,*nak;
  if (el>0) { k=el;return(0);}
  na=(*(double **)a);nb=(*(double **)b);
  nak = na + k;
  for (;na<nak;na++,nb++) {
    if (*na < *nb) return(-1);
    if (*na > *nb) return(1);
  }
  return(0);
}

int melemcmp(const void *a,const void *b)

{ return(real_elemcmp(a,b,-1));
}

void msort(matrix a)

{ double z=0.0;
  real_elemcmp(&z,&z,a.c);
  qsort(a.M,(size_t)a.r,sizeof(a.M[0]),melemcmp);
}

void RArrayFromMatrix(double *a,int r,matrix *M)

{ int i,j;
  for (i=0;i<M->r;i++) for (j=0;j<M->c;j++) a[i+r*j]=M->M[i][j];
}

matrix Rmatrix(double *A,int r,int c)

{ int i,j;
  matrix M;
  M=initmat(r,c);
  for (i=0;i<r;i++) for (j=0;j<c;j++) M.M[i][j]=A[i+j*r];
  return(M);
}

#define round(a) ((a)-floor(a) <0.5 ? (int)floor(a):(int) floor(a)+1)

matrix getD(matrix h,int nak)

{ long i,j,n;
  matrix T,D,Res;
  n=h.r+1;
  T=initmat(n,n);D=initmat(n,n);Res=initmat(n,n);
  for (i=0;i<n;i++) for (j=0;j<n;j++)
  { T.M[i][j]=0.0;D.M[i][j]=0.0;}
  if (n==1L)
  { Res.M[0][0]=0.0;
  } else
  if (n==2L)
  { Res.M[0][0]=Res.M[1][0]=-1.0/h.V[0];
    Res.M[0][1]=Res.M[1][1]=1.0/h.V[0];
  } else
  { for (i=0;i<n;i++) T.M[i][i]=2.0;
    for (i=1;i<n-1;i++)
    { T.M[i][i-1]=h.V[i]/(h.V[i]+h.V[i-1]);
      T.M[i][i+1]=1.0-T.M[i][i-1];
      D.M[i][i-1]= -3.0*T.M[i][i-1]/h.V[i-1];
      D.M[i][i+1]=3.0*T.M[i][i+1]/h.V[i];
      D.M[i][i]= -(D.M[i][i+1]+D.M[i][i-1]);
    }
    if (!nak)
    { T.M[0][1]=1.0;D.M[0][0]= -3.0/h.V[0];D.M[0][1]= -D.M[0][0];}
    else
    { T.M[0][1]=2.0*(h.V[0]+h.V[1])/h.V[1];
      D.M[0][0]= -2.0*(3.0*h.V[0]+2.0*h.V[1])/
		(h.V[0]*(h.V[0]+h.V[1]));
      D.M[0][2]=2.0*h.V[0]*h.V[0]/
      (h.V[1]*h.V[1]*(h.V[0]+h.V[1]));
      D.M[0][1]= -D.M[0][0]-D.M[0][2];
    }
    T.M[n-1][n-2]=1.0;D.M[n-1][n-2]= -3.0/h.V[n-2];
    D.M[n-1][n-1]= -D.M[n-1][n-2];
    invert(&T);
    matmult(Res,T,D,0,0);
  }
  freemat(T);freemat(D);
  return(Res);
}

void MonoCon(matrix *A,matrix *b,matrix *x,int control,double lower,double upper )

{ long i,j,n;
  int up,lo,hi;
  double m;
  matrix h,D;
  h=initmat(x->r-1,1L);
  n=h.r;
  for (i=0;i<n;i++) h.V[i]=x->V[i+1]-x->V[i];
  D=getD(h,0);
  up=control/4;control=control%4;
  lo=control/2;control=control%2;
  hi=control;
  if (up) m= -1.0; else m=1.0;
  (*A)=initmat(4*n+hi+lo,n+1);
  for (i=0;i<n;i++)
  { for (j=0;j<n+1;j++)
    { if (j==i)
      { A->M[i][j]=(D.M[i][j]+3.0/h.V[i])*m;
	    A->M[i+n][j]=(D.M[i+1][j]+3.0/h.V[i])*m;
	    A->M[i+2*n][j]=m;
	    A->M[i+3*n][j]= -D.M[i][j]*m;
      } else
      if (j==(i+1))
      { A->M[i][j]=(D.M[i][j]-3.0/h.V[i])*m;
	    A->M[i+n][j]=(D.M[i+1][j]-3.0/h.V[i])*m;
	    A->M[i+2*n][j]= -m;
	    A->M[i+3*n][j]= -D.M[i][j]*m;
      } else
      { A->M[i][j]=D.M[i][j]*m;
	    A->M[i+n][j]=D.M[i+1][j]*m;
	    A->M[i+2*n][j]=0.0;
	    A->M[i+3*n][j]= -D.M[i][j]*m;
      }
    }
  }
  *b = initmat(A->r,1L);
  if (lo)
  { for (j=0;j<n+1;j++) A->M[4*n][j]=0.0;
    if (up) A->M[4*n][0]=1.0; else A->M[4*n][n]=1.0;
    b->V[4*n]=lower;
  }
  if (hi)
  { for (j=0;j<n+1;j++) A->M[4*n][j]=0.0;
    if (up) A->M[4*n+lo][n]=-1.0; else A->M[4*n+lo][0]=-1.0;
    b->V[4*n+lo] = -upper;
  }
  freemat(D);
  freemat(h);
}

void getFS(double *x,int n,double *S,double *F) {

  double *D,*ldB,*sdB,*h,*Di,*Di1,*Di2,*Fp,*Sp,a,b,c;
  int i,j,n1,n2;

  h = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(n-1),sizeof(double));
  for (i=1;i<n;i++) h[i-1] = x[i]-x[i-1];

  D = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(n*(n-2)),sizeof(double));
  n1 = n-1;n2=n-2;
  for (Di=D,Di1=D+n2,Di2=Di1+n2,i=0;i<n2;i++,Di+=n1,Di1+=n1,Di2+=n1) {
    *Di = 1/h[i];*Di2 = 1/h[i+1];*Di1 = - *Di - *Di2;
  }

  ldB = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(n2),sizeof(double));
  for (i=0;i<n2;i++) ldB[i] = (h[i]+h[i+1])/3;
  sdB = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(n2-1),sizeof(double));
  for (i=1;i<n2;i++) sdB[i-1] = h[i]/6;

  F77_CALL(dptsv)(&n2,&n,ldB,sdB,D,&n2,&i);

  Di=D;
  for (i=0;i<n;i++) {
    Fp = F+i;
    *Fp=0.0;Fp+=n;

    for (j=0;j<n2;j++,Fp+=n,Di++) *Fp = *Di;
    *Fp=0.0;
  }

  a = 1/h[0];
  for (Sp=S,Di=D,i=0;i<n;i++,Sp+=n,Di+=n2) *Sp = *Di * a;
  if (n>3) {
    a = -1/h[0] - 1/h[1];b = 1/h[1];
    for (Sp=S+1,Di1=D+1,Di=D,i=0;i<n;i++,Sp+=n,Di+=n2,Di1+=n2) *Sp = *Di * a + *Di1 * b;
    for (j=2;j<n2;j++) {
      a = 1/h[j-1];c = 1/h[j];b = -a -c;
      for (Sp=S+j,Di=D+j-2,Di1 = D +j-1,Di2=D + j,i=0;i<n;i++,Sp+=n,Di+=n2,Di1+=n2,Di2+=n2)
        *Sp = *Di * a + *Di1 * b + *Di2 * c;
    }
    j = n2;
    a = 1/h[j-1]; b = -1/h[j-1] - 1/h[j];
    for (Sp=S+n2,Di1=D+n2-1,Di=D+n2-2,i=0;i<n;i++,Sp+=n,Di+=n2,Di1+=n2) *Sp = *Di * a + *Di1 * b;
  } else {
    a = -1/h[0] - 1/h[1];
    for (Sp=S+1,Di=D,i=0;i<n;i++,Sp+=n,Di+=n2) *Sp = *Di * a;
  }
  j = n2;
  a = 1/h[j];
  for (Sp=S+n1,Di=D+n2-1,i=0;i<n;i++,Sp+=n,Di+=n2) *Sp = *Di * a;

  FREE(ldB);FREE(sdB);FREE(h);FREE(D);
}

void crspl(double *x,int *n,double *xk, int *nk,double *X,double *S, double *F,int *Fsupplied) {

  int i,j=0,k,extrapolate,jup,jmid;
  double xlast=0.0,h=0.0,xi,kmax,kmin,ajm,ajp,cjm,cjp,*Fp,*Fp1,*Xp,xj,xj1,xik;
  if (! *Fsupplied) getFS(xk,*nk,S,F);
  kmax = xk[*nk-1];kmin = xk[0];
  for (i=0;i<*n;i++) {
    xi = x[i];extrapolate=0;

    if (xi < kmin||xi>kmax) {
      extrapolate=1;
    } else if (i>0 && fabs(xlast-xi) < 2*h) {
      while (xi <= xk[j] && j > 0) j--;
      while (xi > xk[j+1] && j < *nk-2) j++;

      if (j<0) j=0;
      if (j > *nk-2) j = *nk - 2;

    } else {
      j=0;jup=*nk-1;
      while (jup-j>1) {
        jmid = (jup+j) >> 1;
        if (xi > xk[jmid]) j = jmid; else jup = jmid;
      }

    }

    if (extrapolate) {
      if (xi<kmin) {
        j = 0;
        h = xk[1] - kmin;
        xik = xi - kmin;
        cjm = -xik*h/3;
        cjp = -xik*h/6;
        Xp = X + i;
        for (Fp = F,Fp1 = F + *nk,k=0;k < *nk;k++,Xp += *n,Fp++,Fp1++) *Xp = cjm * *Fp + cjp * *Fp1 ;
        X[i] += 1 - xik/h;
        X[i + *n] += xik/h;
      } else {
        j = *nk-1;
        h = kmax - xk[j-1];
        xik = xi - kmax;
        cjm= xik*h/6;
        cjp = xik*h/3;
        Xp = X + i;
        for (Fp1 = F+ j * *nk,Fp = Fp1 - *nk,k=0;k < *nk;k++,Xp += *n,Fp++)
             *Xp = cjm * *Fp + cjp * *Fp1  ;
        X[i + *n * (*nk-2)] += - xik/h;
        X[i + *n * (*nk-1)] += 1+ xik/h;
      }
    } else {
      xj = xk[j];xj1=xk[j+1];
      h = xj1-xj;
      ajm = (xj1 - xi);ajp = (xi-xj);
      cjm = (ajm*(ajm*ajm/h - h))/6;
      cjp = (ajp*(ajp*ajp/h - h))/6;
      ajm /= h;ajp /= h;

      Xp = X + i;

      for (Fp = F+ j * *nk, Fp1 = F+(j+1)* *nk,k=0;k < *nk;k++,Xp += *n,Fp++,Fp1++)
        *Xp = cjm * *Fp + cjp * *Fp1;

      Xp = X + i + j * *n;
      *Xp += ajm; Xp += *n; *Xp += ajp;
    }

    xlast=xi;
  }

}

void MinimumSeparation(double *x,int *n, int *d,double *t,int *m,double *dist) {

  int one=1,*ni;
  kdtree_type kd;
  kd_tree(t,m,d,&kd);
  ni = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(int));
  k_newn_work(x,kd,t,dist,ni,n,m,d,&one);

  FREE(ni);
  free_kdtree(kd);
}

void MinimumSeparation_old(double *gx,double *gy,int *gn,double *dx,double *dy, int *dn,double *dist)

{
  double sep,xx,yy,*dum,*xdum,*ydum;
  int n,m;
  n = *gn;m = *dn;
  for (dum=dist;dum < dist + n; dum++,gx++,gy++)
  { xx= *gx - *dx;yy = *gy - *dy;*dum = xx*xx + yy*yy;
    for (xdum=dx+1,ydum=dy+1;xdum < dx + m;xdum++,ydum++)
    { xx= *gx - *xdum;yy = *gy - *ydum;sep = xx*xx + yy*yy;
      if (sep < *dum) *dum = sep;
    }
    *dum = sqrt(*dum);
  }
}

void RuniqueCombs(double *X,int *ind,int *r, int *c)

{ matrix B,Xd;
  int i,*ind1;
  B=Rmatrix(X,(long)(*r),(long)(*c));
  Xd=initmat(B.r,B.c+1);
  Xd.c--;mcopy(&B,&Xd);freemat(B);Xd.c++;
  for (i=0;i<Xd.r;i++) Xd.M[i][Xd.c-1]=(double)i;
  ind1=Xd_strip(&Xd);
  for (i=0;i<*r;i++) ind[i] = ind1[i];
  Xd.c--;
  RArrayFromMatrix(X,Xd.r,&Xd);
  *r = (int)Xd.r;
  freemat(Xd);FREE(ind1);

}

void RMonoCon(double *Ad,double *bd,double *xd,int *control,double *lower,double *upper,int *n)

{ int i;
  matrix x,A,b;
  x=initmat((long)*n,1L);
  for (i=0;i<x.r;i++) x.V[i]=xd[i];
  MonoCon(&A,&b,&x,*control,*lower,*upper);
  RArrayFromMatrix(Ad,A.r,&A);
  RArrayFromMatrix(bd,b.r,&b);

  freemat(x);freemat(A);freemat(b);

}

#include <stdlib.h>
