void *R_chk_calloc1(size_t nmemb,size_t size) {

  if (nmemb<=0) {
    Rprintf("adjusting %d memory allocation\n",(int) nmemb);
    nmemb++;
  }
  return(R_chk_calloc(nmemb,size));
}

void rksos(double *x,int *n,double *eps) {

  double dl1,xi,rk,xk,xx;
  int i,k;
  dl1 = acos(0)*2;
  dl1 = dl1*dl1/6;
  for (i=0;i< *n;i++) {
    xi = x[i];
    if (xi <= 0) {
      if (xi < -1) xi = -1;
      rk = 1.0 - dl1;
      xk = xi = xi/2 + 0.5;
      for (k=1;k<1000;k++) {
        xx = xk/(k*k);
        rk += xx;
        xk *= xi;
	if (xx < *eps) break;
      }
    } else {
      if (xi>1) xi=1;
      if (xi/2>=.5) rk=1.0; else
      rk = 1 - log(.5+xi/2)*log(.5-xi/2);
      xk = xi = .5 - xi/2;
      for (k=1;k<1000;k++) {
        xx = xk/(k*k);
        rk += -xx;
        xk *= xi;
	if (xk < *eps) break;
      }
    }
    x[i] = rk;
  }
}

void in_out(double *bx, double *by, double *break_code, double *x,double *y,int *in, int *nb, int *n)

{ double xx,yy,dum,x0,x1,y0,y1;
  int i,j,count,start,swap;
  for (i=0;i<*n;i++) {
      xx=x[i];yy=y[i];
      start=0;
      for (count=0,j=0;j<*nb;j++) {
	x0 = bx[j];
        if (x0 <= *break_code) start=j+1;
        else {
          if (j==*nb-1) x1=bx[start]; else x1 = bx[j+1];
          if (x1 <= *break_code) x1 = bx[start];
          if (x0!=x1) {
	    if (x1<x0) { dum=x0;x0=x1;x1=dum;swap=1;} else swap=0;
            if (x0<xx&&x1>=xx) {
	      y0 = by[j];
              if (j==*nb-1) y1=by[start]; else
              y1 = by[j+1];
              if (y1 <= *break_code) y1=by[start];
              if (y0<=yy&&y1<=yy) count++;
              else {
		if (!(y0>yy&&y1>yy)) {
                  if (swap) {dum=y0;y0=y1;y1=dum;}
                  dum = (xx-x0)*(y1-y0)/(x1-x0)+y0;
                  if (yy>=dum) count++;
		}
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
     if (count%2) in[i]=1;else in[i]=0;
  }
}

void psum(double *y, double *x,int *index,int *n) {

  int i;
  for (i=0;i< *n;i++) {
    y[index[i]-1] += x[i];
  }
}

double *forward_buf(double *buf,int *jal,int update)

{ double *buf2,*p,*p1,*p2;
  int n=1000;
  buf2 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*jal+n),sizeof(double));
  for (p=buf,p1=buf + *jal,p2=buf2;p<p1;p++,p2++) *p2 = *p;
  FREE(buf);
  if (update) *jal += n;
  return(buf2);
}

double *backward_buf(double *buf,int *jal,int *j0,int *j_lo,int *j_hi,int update)

{ int n=1000;
  double *buf2,*p,*p1,*p2;
  if (n > *j0-1) n = *j0 - 1;
  if (n==0) return(buf);
  buf2 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*jal+n),sizeof(double));
  for (p=buf,p1=buf + *jal,p2=buf2 + n;p<p1;p++,p2++) *p2 = *p;
  if (update) {
    *jal += n;
    *j_lo += n;
    *j_hi += n;
    *j0 -= n;
  }
  FREE(buf);
  return(buf2);
}

void tweedious(double *w,double *w1,double *w2,double *w1p,double *w2p,
	       double *w2pp,double *y,double *eps,int *n,
               double *th,double *rho,double *a, double *b)

{ int j_max,i,j_lo,j_hi,jb,jal,j0,j,ok,jal_lim=50000000,buffer_run_out=0,failed=0;
  double x,x1,x2,xx,ymax,ymin,alpha,*alogy,*p1,*p2,*p3,*p4,*p5,
    *wb,*wb1,/

  phi = exp(*rho);

  if (*th>0) {
      exp_th =  exp(- *th);

      x = 1 + exp_th;p = (*b + *a * exp_th)/x;
      x1 = x*x;dpth1 = exp_th*(*b - *a)/x1;
      dpth2 =  ((*a - *b)*exp_th+(*b - *a)*exp_th*exp_th)/(x1*x);
  } else {
      exp_th =  exp(*th);

      x = exp_th+1;p = (*b * exp_th + *a)/x;
      x1 = x*x;dpth1 = exp_th*(*b - *a)/x1;
      dpth2 = ((*a - *b)*exp_th*exp_th+(*b - *a)*exp_th)/(x*x1);
  }

  log_eps = log(*eps);
  onep = 1 - p;onep2 = onep * onep;
  alpha = (2 - p)/onep;

  w_base = alpha * log(p-1) + *rho/onep - log(2 - p);
  wp_base = (log(-onep) + *rho)/onep2 - alpha/onep + 1/(2 - p);
  wp2_base= 2*(log(-onep) + *rho)/(onep2*onep) - (3*alpha-2)/(onep2) + 1/((2 - p)*(2 - p));

  alogy = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
  logy1p2 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
  logy1p3 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));

  ymax = ymin = *y;
  *alogy = alpha * log(*y);
  *logy1p2 = log(*y)/(onep2);
  *logy1p3 = *logy1p2/onep;

  for (p1=y+1,p2=y+ *n,p3=alogy+1,p4=logy1p2+1,p5=logy1p3+1;p1<p2;p1++,p3++,p4++,p5++) {
    x = log(*p1);
    *p3 = alpha * x;
    *p4 = x/onep2;
    *p5 = *p4/onep;
    if (*p1 > ymax) ymax = *p1; else if (*p1 < ymin) ymin = *p1;
  }

  x = pow(ymin,2 - p)/(phi * (2 - p));
  j_lo = (int) floor(x);if (j_lo<1) j_lo = 1;

  x = pow(ymax,2 - p)/(phi * (2 - p));
  j_hi = (int) ceil(x);if (j_hi<j_lo) j_hi = j_lo;

  j0 = j_lo - 1000;if (j0<1) j0=1;
  jal = j_hi + 1000;

  jal -= j0-1;
  j_lo -= j0;
  j_hi -= j0;

  wb = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)jal,sizeof(double));

  wb1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)jal,sizeof(double));

  wp1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)jal,sizeof(double));

  wp2 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)jal,sizeof(double));

  wpp = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)jal,sizeof(double));

  for (jb=j_lo,j=j_lo+j0;jb <= j_hi;jb++,j++) {
    wb[jb] = j * w_base - lgamma((double)j+1) - lgamma(-j * alpha);
    wb1[jb] = -j/onep;
    xx = j/onep2;
    x = xx*digamma(-j*alpha);
    wp1[jb] = j * wp_base + x;
    xx = trigamma(-j*alpha) * xx * xx;
    wp2[jb] = j * wp2_base + 2*x/onep - xx;
    wpp[jb] = j /onep2;
  }

  for (i=0;i<*n;i++) {

    x = pow(y[i],2 - p)/(phi * (2 - p));
    j_max = (int) floor(x);
    if (x - j_max  > .5||j_max<1) j_max++;
    if (fabs(j_max-x)>1) {
      failed = 1;
      break;
    }
    j_max -= j0;

    j = j_max+j0;
    jalogy = j*alogy[i];
    wdW2d2W= wdlogwdp=dWpp=0.0;
    wi=w1i=w2i=0.0;

    if (j_max>j_hi) j_max = j_hi;
    if (j_max<j_lo) j_max = j_lo;
    wmax = wb[j_max] - jalogy;wmin = wmax + log_eps;

    ok = 0;
    for (j=j_max+j0,jb=j_max;jb<=j_hi;jb++,j++) {
      jalogy = j * alogy[i];
      wj = wb[jb] - jalogy;
      w1j = wb1[jb];
      wp1j = wp1[jb] - j * logy1p2[i];
      wp2j = wp2[jb] - 2 * j * logy1p3[i];

      wp2j = wp1j * dpth2 + wp2j * dpth1 * dpth1;
      wp1j *= dpth1;
      wppj = wpp[jb] * dpth1;

      wj_scaled =  exp(wj-wmax);
      wi += wj_scaled;

      w1i += wj_scaled * w1j;
      w2i += wj_scaled * w1j*w1j;

      x = wj_scaled*wp1j;
      wdlogwdp += x;
      x1 = wj_scaled*(wp1j*wp1j + wp2j);
      wdW2d2W += x1;

      x2 = wj_scaled*(wp1j*j/onep + wppj);
      dWpp += x2;

      if (wj < wmin) { ok=1;break;}

    }

    while (!ok) {
      for (;jb<jal;jb++,j++) {
        wb[jb] = j * w_base - lgamma((double)j+1) - lgamma(-j * alpha);
        wb1[jb] = -j/onep;
        xx = j/onep2;
        x = xx*digamma(-j*alpha);
        wp1[jb] = j * wp_base + x;
        xx = trigamma(-j*alpha) * xx * xx;
        wp2[jb] = j * wp2_base + 2*x/onep - xx;
        wpp[jb] = j /onep2;

        jalogy = j * alogy[i];
        wj = wb[jb] - jalogy;
        w1j = wb1[jb];
        wp1j = wp1[jb] - j * logy1p2[i];
        wp2j = wp2[jb] - 2 * j * logy1p3[i];

        wp2j = wp1j * dpth2 + wp2j * dpth1 * dpth1;
        wp1j *= dpth1;
        wppj = wpp[jb] * dpth1;

        wj_scaled =  exp(wj-wmax);
        wi += wj_scaled;

        w1i += wj_scaled * w1j;
        w2i += wj_scaled * w1j*w1j;

        x = wj_scaled*wp1j;
        wdlogwdp += x;
        x1 = wj_scaled*(wp1j*wp1j + wp2j);
        wdW2d2W += x1;

        x2 = wj_scaled*(wp1j*j/onep + wppj);
        dWpp += x2;

        if (wj < wmin) { ok=1;break;}

      }
      j_hi = jb; if (j_hi > jal-1) j_hi = jal-1;
      if (!ok) { if (jal<jal_lim) {

        wb = forward_buf(wb,&jal,0);
        wb1 = forward_buf(wb1,&jal,0);

        wp1 = forward_buf(wp1,&jal,0);
        wp2 = forward_buf(wp2,&jal,0);
        wpp = forward_buf(wpp,&jal,1);
	} else ok = buffer_run_out = 1;}
    }

    ok=0;
    for (j=j_max-1+j0,jb=j_max-1;jb>=j_lo;jb--,j--) {
        jalogy = j * alogy[i];
      wj = wb[jb] - jalogy;
      w1j = wb1[jb];
      wp1j = wp1[jb] - j * logy1p2[i];
      wp2j = wp2[jb] - 2 * j * logy1p3[i];

      wp2j = wp1j * dpth2 + wp2j * dpth1 * dpth1;
      wp1j *= dpth1;
      wppj = wpp[jb] * dpth1;

      wj_scaled =  exp(wj-wmax);
      wi += wj_scaled;

      w1i += wj_scaled * w1j;
      w2i += wj_scaled * w1j*w1j;

      x = wj_scaled*wp1j;
      wdlogwdp += x;
      x1 = wj_scaled*(wp1j*wp1j + wp2j);
      wdW2d2W += x1;

      x2 = wj_scaled*(wp1j*j/onep + wppj);
      dWpp += x2;

      if (wj < wmin) { ok=1;break;}
    }

    if (j<=1&&j_lo==0) ok=1;

    while (!ok) {
      for (jb=j_lo-1;jb>=0;jb--,j--) {
        wb[jb] = j * w_base - lgamma((double)j+1) - lgamma(-j * alpha);
        wb1[jb] = -j/onep;
        xx = j/onep2;
        x = xx*digamma(-j*alpha);
        wp1[jb] = j * wp_base + x;
        xx = trigamma(-j*alpha) * xx * xx;
        wp2[jb] = j * wp2_base + 2*x/onep - xx;
        wpp[jb] = j /onep2;

        jalogy = j * alogy[i];
        wj = wb[jb] - jalogy;
        w1j = wb1[jb];
        wp1j = wp1[jb] - j * logy1p2[i];
        wp2j = wp2[jb] - 2 * j * logy1p3[i];

        wp2j = wp1j * dpth2 + wp2j * dpth1 * dpth1;
        wp1j *= dpth1;
        wppj = wpp[jb] * dpth1;

        wj_scaled =  exp(wj-wmax);
        wi += wj_scaled;

        w1i += wj_scaled * w1j;
        w2i += wj_scaled * w1j*w1j;

        x = wj_scaled*wp1j;
        wdlogwdp += x;
        x1 = wj_scaled*(wp1j*wp1j + wp2j);
        wdW2d2W += x1;

        x2 = wj_scaled*(wp1j*j/onep + wppj);
        dWpp += x2;

        if (wj < wmin) { ok=1;break;}
      }

      if (j<=1) ok=1;

      j_lo = jb; if (j_lo<0) j_lo=0;
      if (!ok) { if (jal<jal_lim) {

        wb = backward_buf(wb,&jal,&j0,&j_lo,&j_hi,0);
        wb1 = backward_buf(wb1,&jal,&j0,&j_lo,&j_hi,0);

        wp1 = backward_buf(wp1,&jal,&j0,&j_lo,&j_hi,0);
        wp2 = backward_buf(wp2,&jal,&j0,&j_lo,&j_hi,0);
        wpp = backward_buf(wpp,&jal,&j0,&j_lo,&j_hi,1);
	} else ok = buffer_run_out = 1;}

    }

    w[i] = wmax + log(wi);
    w2[i] = w2i/wi - (w1i/wi)*(w1i/wi);
    w2p[i] = wdW2d2W/wi - (wdlogwdp/wi)*(wdlogwdp/wi);
    w2pp[i] = (w1i/wi)*(wdlogwdp/wi) + dWpp/wi;
    w1[i] = -w1i/wi;
    w1p[i] =  wdlogwdp/wi;

  }
  if (buffer_run_out) *eps = -1.0;
  if (failed) *eps = -2.0;
  FREE(alogy);FREE(wb);FREE(wb1);
  FREE(logy1p2);FREE(logy1p3);FREE(wp1);FREE(wp2);FREE(wpp);
}

void tweedious2(double *w,double *w1,double *w2,double *w1p,double *w2p,
	       double *w2pp,double *y,double *eps,int *n,
               double *th,double *rho,double *a, double *b)

{ int k,j_max,i,j,ok,incr,jal_lim=50000000,series_too_long=0,failed=0;;
  double x,x1,x2,xx,alpha,alogy,lgammaj1,
    wbj,wb1j,wp1jb,wp2jb,wppjb,
    wp1j,wp2j,wppj,dpth1,dpth2,
    w_base,wp_base,wp2_base,wj_scaled,wdlogwdp,
    wdW2d2W,dWpp,exp_th,
    wmax,wmin,
    wi,w1i,w2i,
    log_eps,wj,w1j,jalogy,onep,onep2,twop,logy1p2,logy1p3,p,phi;

  log_eps = log(*eps);

  for (i=0;i<*n;i++) {
    phi = exp(rho[i]);
    if (th[i]>0) {
        exp_th =  exp(-th[i]);
        x = 1 + exp_th;p = (*b + *a * exp_th)/x;
        x1 = x*x;dpth1 = exp_th*(*b - *a)/x1;
        dpth2 =  ((*a - *b)*exp_th+(*b - *a)*exp_th*exp_th)/(x1*x);
    } else {
        exp_th =  exp(th[i]);
        x = exp_th+1;p = (*b * exp_th + *a)/x;
        x1 = x*x;dpth1 = exp_th*(*b - *a)/x1;
        dpth2 = ((*a - *b)*exp_th*exp_th+(*b - *a)*exp_th)/(x*x1);
    }

    x = pow(y[i],2 - p)/(phi * (2 - p));
    j_max = (int) floor(x);
    if (x - j_max  > .5||j_max<1) j_max++;
    if (fabs(j_max-x)>1) {
      failed = 1;
      break;
    }

    j = j_max;
    onep = 1 - p;onep2 = onep * onep;
    twop = 2 - p;
    alpha = twop/onep;
    alogy = log(y[i]);
    logy1p2 = alogy/(onep2);
    logy1p3 = logy1p2/onep;
    alogy *= alpha;

    wdW2d2W= wdlogwdp=dWpp=0.0;
    wi=w1i=w2i=0.0;

    w_base = alpha * log(-onep) + rho[i]/onep - log(twop);
    wp_base = (log(-onep) + rho[i])/onep2 - alpha/onep + 1/twop;
    wp2_base= 2*(log(-onep) + rho[i])/(onep2*onep) - (3*alpha-2)/(onep2) + 1/(twop*twop);

    wmax = j * w_base - lgamma((double)j+1) - lgamma(-j * alpha) - j*alogy;
    wmin = wmax + log_eps;

    ok = 0;

    incr = 1;
    lgammaj1 = lgamma((double)j+1);
    k=0;
    while (!ok) {
      wbj = j * w_base - lgammaj1 - lgamma(-j * alpha);
      wb1j = -j/onep;
      xx = j/onep2;
      x = xx*digamma(-j*alpha);
      wp1jb = j * wp_base + x;

      xx = trigamma(-j*alpha) * xx * xx;
      wp2jb = j * wp2_base + 2*x/onep - xx;
      wppjb = j /onep2;

      jalogy = j * alogy;
      wj = wbj - jalogy;
      w1j = wb1j;
      wp1j = wp1jb - j * logy1p2;

      wp2j = wp2jb - 2 * j * logy1p3;

      wp2j = wp1j * dpth2 + wp2j * dpth1 * dpth1;
      wp1j *= dpth1;
      wppj = wppjb * dpth1;

      wj_scaled =  exp(wj-wmax);
      wi += wj_scaled;

      w1i += wj_scaled * w1j;
      w2i += wj_scaled * w1j*w1j;

      x = wj_scaled*wp1j;
      wdlogwdp += x;

      x1 = wj_scaled*(wp1j*wp1j + wp2j);
      wdW2d2W += x1;

      x2 = wj_scaled*(wp1j*j/onep + wppj);
      dWpp += x2;
      j += incr;
      if (incr>0) {
	lgammaj1 += log(j);
        if (wj < wmin) { j = j_max - 1;incr = -1;
	  if (j==0) ok=1;
          lgammaj1 = lgamma((double)j+1);
	}
      } else {
	lgammaj1 += -log(j+1);
        if (wj < wmin||j<1) ok=1;
      }
      k++;
      if (k>=jal_lim) ok = series_too_long = 1;
    }

    w[i] = wmax + log(wi);
    w2[i] = w2i/wi - (w1i/wi)*(w1i/wi);
    w2p[i] = wdW2d2W/wi - (wdlogwdp/wi)*(wdlogwdp/wi);
    w2pp[i] = (w1i/wi)*(wdlogwdp/wi) + dWpp/wi;
    w1[i] = -w1i/wi;
    w1p[i] =  wdlogwdp/wi;

  }
  if (series_too_long) *eps = -1.0;
  if (failed) *eps = -2.0;
}

void rwMatrix(int *stop,int *row,double *w,double *X,int *n,int *p,int *trans,double *work) {

  ptrdiff_t i,j,jump,start=0,end,off;
  double *X1p,*Xp,weight,*Xpe,*X1;

  X1 = work;
  jump = *n;
  off = *n * (ptrdiff_t) *p;
  for (X1p=X1,Xpe=X1p+off;X1p<Xpe;X1p++) *X1p = 0.0;
  for (i=0;i<*n;i++) {
    end = stop[i]+1;
    for (j=start;j<end;j++) {
      if (*trans) {
        X1p = X1 + row[j];
        Xp = X + i;
      } else {
        X1p = X1 + i;
        Xp = X + row[j];
      }
      weight = w[j];
      for (Xpe=Xp+off;Xp<Xpe;Xp+=jump,X1p+=jump) *X1p += weight * *Xp;
    }
    start = end;
  }

  for (Xp=X,X1p=X1,Xpe=Xp+off;Xp<Xpe;Xp++,X1p++) *Xp = *X1p;
}

# --- End of mgcv/src/misc.c ---

# --- Start of mgcv/src/mvn.c ---

#include <stdlib.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <math.h>

#include "mgcv.h"
#include <R.h>

void mvn_ll(double *y,double *X,double *XX,double *beta,int *n,int *lpi,
            int *m,double *ll,double *lb,double *lbb,double *dbeta,
            double *dH,int *deriv,int *nsp,int *nt) {

  double *R,*theta,ldetR,*Xl,*bl,oned=1.0,zerod=0.0,*p,*p1,*p2,*p3,xx,zz,yy,*yty,
    *mu,*Rymu,rip,*dtheta,*db,*deriv_theta,*yX,*yRX;
  ptrdiff_t i,j,k,l,q,r;
  int pl,one=1,bt,ct,nb,*din,ntheta,ncoef,*rri,*rci,ri,rj,ril,rjl,rik,rjk,rij,rjj;
  const char not_trans='N';
  ntheta = *m * (*m+1)/2;ncoef = lpi[*m-1];

  nb = ncoef + ntheta;

  R = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*m * *m,sizeof(double));
  theta = beta + lpi[*m-1];
  ldetR = 0.0;
  rri = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)ntheta,sizeof(int));
  rci = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)ntheta,sizeof(int));
  deriv_theta = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)ntheta,sizeof(double));
  for (k=0,i=0;i<*m;i++) {
    deriv_theta[k] = exp(theta[k]);
    R[i + *m * i] = deriv_theta[k];ldetR += theta[k];
    rri[k]=rci[k]=i;k++;
    for (j=i+1;j<*m;j++) {
      R[i + *m * j] = theta[k];
      deriv_theta[k] = 1.0;
      rri[k]=i;rci[k]=j;k++;
    }
  }

  mu  = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
  for (l=0;l<*m;l++) {
    if (l==0) { Xl = X;pl = lpi[0];bl=beta;}
    else { Xl = X + *n * lpi[l-1];pl = lpi[l]-lpi[l-1];bl = beta + lpi[l-1];}
    F77_CALL(dgemv)(&not_trans,n,&pl,&oned,Xl,n, bl, &one,&zerod, mu, &one FCONE);

    for (p=mu,p1= mu + *n,p2=y+l;p<p1;p++,p2 += *m) *p2 -= *p;
  }
  FREE(mu);

  Rymu =  (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n * *m,sizeof(double));
  bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_pmmult(Rymu,R,y,&bt,&ct,m,n,m,nt);

  for (*ll=0.0,p=Rymu,p1=p + *n * *m;p<p1;p++) *ll += *p * *p;
  *ll = - *ll/2 + ldetR * *n;

  p = lb;

  for (l=0;l<*m;l++) {
    if (l==0) { Xl = X;pl = lpi[0];}
    else { Xl = X + *n * lpi[l-1];pl = lpi[l]-lpi[l-1];}
    for (i=0;i<pl;i++) {
      *p = 0.0;
      for (j=0;j<*n;j++) {
        xx = Xl[j + *n * i];
        for (p1=R + l * *m,p2 = p1 + l,p3 = Rymu + *m *j;p1<=p2;p1++,p3++) *p += xx * *p1 * *p3;
      }
      p++;
    }
  }

  for (k=0,i=0;i<*m;i++) {

    xx = deriv_theta[k]; ;
    *p += *n;
    k++;

    for (zz=0.0,l=0,p1 = Rymu+i,p2=y+i;l<*n;l++,p1 += *m,p2 += *m) zz += *p1 * *p2 * xx;
    *p += -zz;
    p++;
    for (j=i+1;j<*m;j++) {
      k++;
      for (zz=0.0,l=0,p1 = Rymu+i,p2=y+j;l<*n;l++,p1 += *m,p2 += *m) zz += *p1 * *p2;
      *p += -zz;
      p++;
    }
  }

  din =  (int *)CALLOC((size_t)ncoef,sizeof(int));
  for (k=0,i=0;i<ncoef;i++) {
    if (i==lpi[k]) k++;
    din[i] = k;
  }

  for (i=0;i<ncoef;i++) for (j=0;j<=i;j++) {
     l=din[i];k=din[j];

     for (p=R+l * *m,p1=R+k * *m,rip=0.0,p2=p1+k;p1<=p2;p++,p1++) rip += *p * *p1;
     lbb[i + nb * j] = lbb[j + nb * i] = -XX[i + ncoef * j]*rip;
  }

  for (i=0;i<ncoef;i++) for (j=0;j<ntheta;j++) {
     ri = rri[j];
     rj = rci[j];
     l = din[i];
     xx = 0.0;
     zz = deriv_theta[j];

     if (l==rj) for (p = X + i* *n,p1=Rymu+ri,p2=p + *n;p<p2;p++,p1 += *m) xx += *p * *p1;
     xx *= zz;

     if (ri<=l) {
       for (yy=0.0,p = X + i* *n,p1=y+rj,p2=p + *n;p<p2;p++,p1 += *m) yy += *p * *p1;
       xx += yy * R[ri + *m * l]*zz;
     }
     lbb[i + nb * (j+ncoef)] = lbb[j + ncoef + nb * i] = xx;
  }

  for (k=0;k<ntheta;k++) for (l=0;l<=k;l++) {
      xx=0.0;
      if (k==l) {
        ri=rri[k];rj=rci[k];
        if (ri==rj) {

          for (zz=0.0,i=0,p=Rymu+ri,p2=y+ri;i<*n;i++,p += *m,p2+= *m) zz += *p * *p2;
          xx -= zz *  deriv_theta[k];
        }
      }

      ri=rri[k];rj=rci[k];
      zz = deriv_theta[k];

      ril=rri[l];rjl=rci[l];
      rik=rri[k];rjk=rci[k];
      if (ril==rik) {
	for (yy=0.0,i=0,p=y+rjl,p1=y+rjk;i<*n;i++,p+= *m, p1+= *m) yy += *p * *p1;
        yy *= zz;
        if (ril==rjl) yy *= deriv_theta[l];
        xx -= yy;
      }
      lbb[k + ncoef + nb * (l+ncoef)] = lbb[l + ncoef + nb * (k+ncoef)] = xx;
  }

  if (*deriv) {
    yX = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*m * ncoef,sizeof(double));
    bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_pmmult(yX,y,X,&bt,&ct,m,&ncoef,n,nt);
    yRX = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*m * ncoef,sizeof(double));
    bt=0;ct=0;mgcv_pmmult(yRX,Rymu,X,&bt,&ct,m,&ncoef,n,nt);
    yty = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*m * *m,sizeof(double));
    bt=0;ct=1;mgcv_pmmult(yty,y,y,&bt,&ct,m,m,n,nt);
    for (r=0;r< *nsp;r++) {
      db = dbeta + nb * r;
      dtheta = db + ncoef;

      for (i=0;i<ncoef;i++) for (j=0;j<=i;j++) {
	l = din[i];k = din[j];
	xx=0.0;p=R+l* *m;p1=R+k* *m;
        for (q=0;q<ntheta;q++) {
          ri=rri[q];rj=rci[q];
          if (rj==l) xx += p1[ri]*deriv_theta[q]*dtheta[q];
          if (rj==k) xx += p[ri]*deriv_theta[q]*dtheta[q];
        }

        dH[i + nb * j] = dH[j + nb * i] = -xx * XX[i +  ncoef * j];
      }

      for (i=0;i<ncoef;i++) for (j=0;j<ntheta;j++) {

        l=din[i];
	for (xx=0.0,q=0;q<ncoef;q++) {
          k=din[q];
          ri=rri[j];rj=rci[j];
          zz = deriv_theta[j];
          if (rj==l) xx += -R[ri + *m * k]*zz*XX[i + ncoef * q] * db[q];
          if (rj==k) xx += -R[ri + *m * l]*zz*XX[i + ncoef * q] * db[q];
	}

        rij=rri[j];rjj=rci[j];
        for (k=0;k<ntheta;k++) {
          zz=0.0;
          rik=rri[k];rjk=rci[k];
          if (rij==rik&&(l==rjj||l==rjk)) {
            if (l==rjj) zz +=  yX[rjk + i * *m] * deriv_theta[j] * deriv_theta[k];
            if (l==rjk) zz +=  yX[rjj + i * *m] * deriv_theta[j] * deriv_theta[k];
            if (k==j&&rik==rjk) {
               zz +=  deriv_theta[k] * yRX[rjj + *m * i];
            }
            xx += zz * dtheta[k];
          }
          if (k==j&&rik==rjk) xx += dtheta[k]* deriv_theta[k] * R[rjj + *m * l] * yX[rjj + *m * i];
        }
        dH[i + (j+ncoef) * nb] = dH[j+ncoef + i * nb] = xx;
      }

      for (j=0;j<ntheta;j++) for (k=j;k<ntheta;k++) {
        rij=rri[j];rjj=rci[j];rik=rri[k];rjk=rci[k];

	xx = 0.0;
        for (i=0;i<ncoef;i++) {
	  zz=0.0;l=din[i];
          if (rij==rik&&(l==rjj||l==rjk)) {
            if (l==rjj) zz +=  yX[rjk + i * *m] * deriv_theta[j] * deriv_theta[k];
            if (l==rjk) zz +=  yX[rjj + i * *m] * deriv_theta[j] * deriv_theta[k];
            if (k==j&&rik==rjk) {

              if (l==rjj) zz +=  deriv_theta[k] * yRX[rjj + *m * i];
            }
            xx += zz * db[i];
          }
          if (k==j&&rij==rjj) xx +=  db[i]*deriv_theta[k] * R[rjj + *m * l] * yX[rjj + *m * i];
        }
        for (i=0;i<ntheta;i++) {
	  ri = rri[i];rj=rci[i];zz=0.0;
          if (j==k&&ri==rij&&rjk==rik) zz += deriv_theta[j]*deriv_theta[i]*yty[rj * *m + rjj];
          if (i==k&&rik==rij&&rj==ri) zz += deriv_theta[j]*deriv_theta[i]*yty[rjk * *m + rjj];
          if (i==j&&rik==rij&&rj==ri) zz += deriv_theta[k]*deriv_theta[i]*yty[rjk * *m + rj];

          if (i==j&&j==k&&ri==rj) {
            for (yy=0.0,p=Rymu+ri,p1=y+ri,q=0;q<*n;p+= *m,p1+= *m,q++) yy += *p * *p1;
            zz += deriv_theta[k]*yy;
          }
          xx += -zz*dtheta[i];
        }
	dH[k + ncoef + (j+ncoef) * nb] = dH[j+ncoef + (k+ncoef) * nb] = xx;
      }

      dH += nb * nb;
    }
    FREE(yX);FREE(yRX);FREE(yty);
  }

  FREE(din); FREE(rri); FREE(rci);

  FREE(R);FREE(Rymu);FREE(deriv_theta);
}

SEXP mvnll(SEXP Y,SEXP x,SEXP xx,SEXP BETA,SEXP LPI, SEXP LL, SEXP LB,
	   SEXP LBB, SEXP DBETA, SEXP Dh, SEXP DERIV,SEXP NSP, SEXP NT) {

  double *y,*X,*XX,*ll,*lb,*lbb,*dbeta,*dH,*beta;
  int *lpi,n,nt,nsp,deriv,m;

  m = length(LPI);
  n = nrows(x);
  nt = asInteger(NT);
  nsp = asInteger(NSP);
  deriv = asInteger(DERIV);

  y = REAL(Y);
  X = REAL(x); XX = REAL(xx);
  lpi = INTEGER(LPI);
  ll = REAL(LL);lb = REAL(LB);lbb = REAL(LBB);
  beta = REAL(BETA);dbeta = REAL(DBETA);dH = REAL(Dh);
  mvn_ll(y,X,XX,beta,&n,lpi,&m,ll,lb,lbb,dbeta,dH,&deriv,&nsp,&nt);
  return(R_NilValue);
}

# --- End of mgcv/src/mvn.c ---

# --- Start of mgcv/src/ncv.c ---

#include <stdlib.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <math.h>

#ifdef _OPENMP
#include <omp.h>
#endif

#include "mgcv.h"
#include <R.h>

void minres0(double *R, double *u,double *b, double *x, int *p,int *m) {

  double *A,xx,zz,*work,workq;
  int p2,j,one=1,*ipiv,lwork=-1;
  char ntrans = 'N',trans='T',uplo='U',diag='N',side='L';
  p2 = *p * *p;
  A = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p2,sizeof(double));
  ipiv = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) *p,sizeof(int));
  for (j=0;j<p2;j++) A[j] = R[j];
  xx=1.0;
  F77_CALL(dtrmm)(&side,&uplo,&trans,&diag,p,p,&xx,R,p,A,p FCONE FCONE FCONE FCONE);
  zz = -1.0;
  F77_CALL(dsyrk)(&uplo,&ntrans,p,m,&zz,u,p,&xx,A,p FCONE FCONE);
  for (j=0;j<*p;j++) x[j] = b[j];
  F77_CALL(dsysv)(&uplo,p,&one,A,p,ipiv,x,p,&workq,&lwork,&j FCONE);
  lwork=floor(workq);if (lwork<workq) lwork++;
  work = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) lwork,sizeof(double));
  F77_CALL(dsysv)(&uplo,p,&one,A,p,ipiv,x,p,work,&lwork,&j FCONE);
  FREE(A);FREE(ipiv);FREE(work);
}

void woodbury(double *R, double *u,double *b, double *x, int *p,int *m,double *work,int *iwork) {

  char side = 'L',trans = 'T',ntrans='N',uplo='U',diag='N';
  double done=1.0,*RitU,dzero=0.0,*B,*p0,*v,xx;
  int i,pm,one=1,nwork;
  pm = *p * *m;
  RitU = work;
  work += pm;
  B = work; work += *m * *m;
  v = work; work += *m;
  nwork = *iwork;
  for (i=0;i<pm;i++) RitU[i] = u[i];
  F77_CALL(dtrsm)(&side,&uplo,&trans,&diag,p,m,&done,R,p,RitU,p FCONE FCONE FCONE FCONE);
  F77_CALL(dgemm)(&trans,&ntrans,m,m,p,&done,RitU,p,RitU,p,&dzero,B,m FCONE FCONE);
  for (p0=B,i=0;i < *m;i++,p0 += *m+1) *p0 += -1.0;
  for (i=0;i<*p;i++) x[i] = b[i];
  F77_CALL(dtrsv)(&uplo,&trans,&diag,p,R,p,x,&one FCONE FCONE FCONE);
  F77_CALL(dgemv)(&trans,p,m,&done,RitU,p,x,&one,&dzero,v,&one FCONE);
  F77_CALL(dsysv)(&uplo,m,&one,B,m,iwork,v,m,work,&nwork,&i FCONE);
  xx = -1.0;
  F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,p,m,&xx,RitU,p,v,&one,&done,x,&one FCONE);
  F77_CALL(dtrsv)(&uplo,&ntrans,&diag,p,R,p,x,&one FCONE FCONE FCONE);

}

void minres(double *R, double *u,double *b, double *x, int *p,int *m,double *work) {

  int one=1,i,j;
  double *v,*z,xx,zz,*u1,*dum,beta1,beta2,eta,epsilon,sig0,sig1,sig2,gamma0,gamma1,gamma2,
    *v1,*v2,alpha,delta,rho1,rho2,rho3,maxb,*w,*w1,*w2,*pp;
  char ntrans = 'N',trans='T',uplo='U',diag='N',side='L';
  u1 = work; work += *p * *m;
  v = work;work += *p;
  v1 = work;work += *p;
  v2 = work;work += *p;
  w = work;work += *p;
  w1 = work;work += *p;
  w2 = work;work += *p;
  z = work;work += *p;
  dum = work; work += *m;
  for (maxb=0.0,i=0;i<*p;i++) {
    xx = x[i] = b[i];maxb += xx*xx;
  }
  maxb = sqrt(maxb);
  F77_CALL(dtrsv)(&uplo,&trans,&diag,p,R,p,x,&one FCONE FCONE FCONE);
  xx = 1.0;
  for (i=0;i < *p * *m;i++) u1[i] = u[i];
  F77_CALL(dtrsm)(&side,&uplo,&trans,&diag,p,m,&xx,R,p,u1,p FCONE FCONE FCONE FCONE);

  zz = 0.0;
  F77_CALL(dgemv)(&trans,p,m,&xx,u1,p,x,&one,&zz,dum,&one FCONE);
  F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,p,m,&xx,u1,p,dum,&one,&zz,v1,&one FCONE);
  for (beta1=0.0,i=0;i<*p;i++) { xx=v1[i]; beta1 += xx*xx;}
  epsilon = eta = beta1 = sqrt(beta1);
  gamma0 = gamma1 = 1.0;sig0 = sig1 = 0.0;
  for (i=0;i < *p ;i++) v[i] = w1[i] = w[i] = 0.0;
  for (j=0;j<200;j++) {
    for (i=0;i<*p;i++) { v1[i] /= beta1;z[i]=v1[i];}
    xx = 1.0; zz = 0.0;
    F77_CALL(dgemv)(&trans,p,m,&xx,u1,p,v1,&one,&zz,dum,&one FCONE);
    zz = 1.0; xx = -1.0;
    F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,p,m,&xx,u1,p,dum,&one,&zz,z,&one FCONE);
    for (alpha=0.0,i=0;i<*p;i++) alpha += v1[i]*z[i];
    for (beta2=0.0,i=0;i<*p;i++) {
      xx = v2[i] = z[i] - alpha * v1[i] - beta1 * v[i];
      beta2 += xx*xx;
    }
    delta = gamma1 * alpha - gamma0 * sig1 * beta1;
    rho1 = sqrt(delta*delta+beta2);
    beta2 = sqrt(beta2);
    rho2 = sig1 * alpha + gamma0 * gamma1 * beta1;
    rho3 = sig0*beta1;
    gamma2 = delta/rho1;
    sig2 = beta2/rho1;
    xx = gamma2*eta;
    for (i=0;i<*p;i++) {
      w2[i] = (v1[i] - rho3*w[i] - rho2*w1[i])/rho1;
      x[i] += xx * w2[i];
    }
    epsilon *= fabs(sig2);
    if (epsilon<maxb*1e-10) break;
    eta *= -sig2;

    pp = v; v = v1; v1 = v2; v2 = pp;
    pp = w; w = w1; w1 = w2; w2 = pp;
    sig0 = sig1; sig1 = sig2; beta1 = beta2;
    gamma0 = gamma1; gamma1 = gamma2;
  }
  F77_CALL(dtrsv)(&uplo,&ntrans,&diag,p,R,p,x,&one FCONE FCONE FCONE);
  *m=j;
}

int CG(double *A,double *Mi,double *b, double *x,int n,double tol,double *cgwork) {

  int i,one=1,k;
  double *r,*z,*p,*r1,*z1,c1,c2,bmax=0.0,rmax,alpha,rz,r1z1,pAp,*dum,beta;
  char ntrans = 'N';
  p = cgwork;
  r = p + n;r1 = r + n;z=r1 + n;z1 = z + n;

  for (i=0;i<n;i++) {
    c1 = b[i]; r[i] = c1;
    c1 = fabs(c1);
    if (c1>bmax) bmax = c1;
  }
  c1 = -1.0;c2=1.0;
  F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,&n,&n,&c1,A,&n,x,&one,&c2,r,&one FCONE);
  c1 = 0.0;
  F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,&n,&n,&c2,Mi,&n,r,&one,&c1,z,&one FCONE);
  for (i=0;i<n;i++) p[i] = z[i];
  c1=1.0;c2=0.0;
  for (k=0;k<200;k++) {
    F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,&n,&n,&c1,A,&n,p,&one,&c2,z1,&one FCONE);
    for (rz=0.0,pAp=0.0,i=0;i<n;i++) {rz += r[i]*z[i];pAp += p[i] * z1[i];}
    if (pAp==0.0) {k=-k;break;}
    alpha = rz/pAp;
    for (rmax=0.0,i=0;i<n;i++) {
      x[i] += alpha * p[i];
      r1[i] = r[i] - alpha * z1[i];
      if (fabs(r1[i])>rmax) rmax = fabs(r1[i]);
    }
    if (rmax < tol*bmax) break;
    F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,&n,&n,&c1,Mi,&n,r1,&one,&c2,z1,&one FCONE);
    for (r1z1=0.0,i=0;i<n;i++) r1z1 += r1[i] * z1[i];
    if (rz==0.0) {k=-k;break;}
    beta = r1z1/rz;
    for (i=0;i<n;i++) p[i] = z1[i] + beta * p[i];
    dum = z1; z1 = z; z = dum;
    dum = r1; r1 = r; r = dum;
  }
  return(k);
}

SEXP nei_cov(SEXP v,SEXP d, SEXP d1, SEXP M, SEXP K) {

  int *m,*k,n,p,i,j,i0,i1=0,ii,q;
  double *D,*D1,*V,*Ds;
  M = PROTECT(coerceVector(M,INTSXP));
  K = PROTECT(coerceVector(K,INTSXP));
  m = INTEGER(M); k = INTEGER(K);
  V = REAL(v);
  D = REAL(d);D1 = REAL(d1);
  n = length(M);
  p = ncols(d);
  for (ii=0;ii<p*p;ii++) V[ii]=0.0;
  Ds = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p,sizeof(double));
  for (i=0;i<n;i++) {
    i0 = i1;i1 = m[i];
    j=k[i0];
    for (q=0;q<p;q++) Ds[q] = D1[j+q*n];
    for (ii=i0+1;ii<i1;ii++) {
      j = k[ii];
      for (q=0;q<p;q++) Ds[q] += D1[j+q*n];
    }

    for (ii=0,j=0;j<p;j++) for (q=0;q<p;q++,ii++) V[ii] += D[i+j*n]*Ds[q];
  }
  FREE(Ds);
  UNPROTECT(2);
  return(R_NilValue);
}

SEXP ncv(SEXP x, SEXP hi, SEXP W1, SEXP W2, SEXP DB, SEXP DW, SEXP rS, SEXP IND, SEXP MI, SEXP M, SEXP K,SEXP BETA, SEXP SP, SEXP ETA, SEXP DETA,SEXP DLET,SEXP DERIV) {

  SEXP S,kr;
  int maxn,i,nsp,n,p,*m,*k,j,l,ii,i0,ki,q,p2,one=1,deriv,kk,error=0,jj,nm,*ind,nth,*mi,io,io0,no;
  double *X,*g,*g1,*gp,*p1,*Hp,*Hi,*Xi,xx,*xip,*xip0,z,*Hd,w1ki,w2ki,*wXi,*d,*w1,*w2,*eta,
    *deta,*beta,*dg,*dgp,*dwX,*wp,*wp1,*db=NULL,*dw=NULL,*rSj,*sp,*d1,*dbp,*dH=NULL,*xp,*wxp,*bp,*bp1,*dwXi,*cgwork,*dlet=NULL;
  char trans = 'T',ntrans = 'N';
  M = PROTECT(coerceVector(M,INTSXP));
  MI = PROTECT(coerceVector(MI,INTSXP));
  IND = PROTECT(coerceVector(IND,INTSXP));
  K = PROTECT(coerceVector(K,INTSXP));
  deriv = asInteger(DERIV);
  mi = INTEGER(MI);m = INTEGER(M); k = INTEGER(K);ind = INTEGER(IND);
  nsp = length(rS);
  nth = ncols(DETA)-nsp;
  sp = REAL(SP);
  w1=REAL(W1);w2=REAL(W2);
  X = REAL(x);beta = REAL(BETA);
  Hi=REAL(hi);eta = REAL(ETA);deta = REAL(DETA);
  p = ncols(x); n = nrows(x);p2=p*p;
  no = length(IND);
  nm = length(M);
  Hp = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p2,sizeof(double));
  g = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) 3*p,sizeof(double));
  g1 = g + p;dg = g1 + p;
  d = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) 2*p,sizeof(double));
  cgwork = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p*5,sizeof(double));
  d1 = d + p;

  maxn = ii = 0;
  for (j=0;j<nm;j++) {
    i = m[j]; if (i-ii>maxn) maxn = i-ii; ii = i;
  }
  Xi = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p*maxn,sizeof(double));
  wXi = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p*maxn,sizeof(double));
  dwXi = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p*maxn,sizeof(double));
  Hd = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p2,sizeof(double));
  dwX = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p*n,sizeof(double));

  for (xip0 = X,xip=dwX,q=0;q<p;q++) for (wp=w2,wp1=wp+n;wp<wp1;wp++,xip++,xip0++) *xip = *xip0 * *wp;
  xx=1.0;z=0.0;
  F77_CALL(dgemm)(&trans,&ntrans,&p,&p,&n,&xx,X,&n,dwX,&n,&z,Hd,&p FCONE FCONE);
  for (j=0;j<nsp;j++) {
    S = VECTOR_ELT(rS, j);rSj = REAL(S);q = ncols(S);
    F77_CALL(dgemm)(&ntrans,&trans,&p,&p,&q,sp+j,rSj,&p,rSj,&p,&xx,Hd,&p FCONE FCONE);
  }
  if (deriv>0) {
    db=REAL(DB);dw=REAL(DW);dlet = REAL(DLET);
    dH = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p2*(nsp+nth),sizeof(double));
    for (j=0;j<nsp+nth;j++) {
      for (xip0 = X,xip=dwX,q=0;q<p;q++) for (wp=dw+n*j,wp1=wp+n;wp<wp1;wp++,xip++,xip0++) *xip = *xip0 * *wp;
      F77_CALL(dgemm)(&trans,&ntrans,&p,&p,&n,&xx,X,&n,dwX,&n,&z,dH+j*p2,&p FCONE FCONE);
      if (j>=nth) {
        S = VECTOR_ELT(rS, j-nth);
        rSj = REAL(S);q = ncols(S);
        F77_CALL(dgemm)(&ntrans,&trans,&p,&p,&q,sp+j-nth,rSj,&p,rSj,&p,&xx,dH+j*p2,&p FCONE FCONE);
      }
    }
  } else if (deriv<0) dlet = REAL(DLET);
  FREE(dwX);
  for (io=ii=0,i=0;i<nm;i++) {
    p1 = g + p;
    ki = k[ii];w1ki = w1[ki];w2ki = w2[ki];
    i0=ii;io0=io;
    for (xip0=xip=Xi,gp=g,xp=X,wxp=wXi;gp<p1;gp++,xp += n,xip += maxn,wxp += maxn) {
      xx = xp[ki];
      *gp = w1ki * xx;
      *xip = xx;
      *wxp = xx*w2ki;
    }
    q=1;
    for (xip0++,ii++;ii<m[i];ii++,xip0++,q++) {
      ki = k[ii];w1ki = w1[ki];w2ki = w2[ki];
      for (xip=xip0,gp=g,xp=X,wxp=wXi+q;gp<p1;gp++,xp += n,xip += maxn,wxp += maxn) {
	xx = xp[ki];
	*gp += w1ki * xx;
	*xip = xx;
	*wxp = xx*w2ki;
      }
    }

    for (j=0;j<p2;j++) Hp[j] = Hd[j];
    xx = -1.0;z=1.0;

    F77_CALL(dgemm)(&trans,&ntrans,&p,&p,&q,&xx,Xi,&maxn,wXi,&maxn,&z,Hp,&p FCONE FCONE);

    xx = 0.0;
    F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,&p,&p,&z,Hi,&p,g,&one,&xx,d,&one FCONE);
    kk=CG(Hp,Hi,g,d,p,1e-13,cgwork);
    if (kk>error) error=kk;

    for (;io<mi[i];io++) {
      for (xx=0.0,xip=X+ind[io],j=0;j<p;j++,xip += n) xx += *xip * (beta[j]-d[j]);
      eta[io] = xx;
    }
    if (deriv<0) for (xip=dlet+p*i,j=0;j<p;j++) xip[j] = d[j];

    if (deriv>0) for (l=0;l<nsp+nth;l++) {

      for (xx=0.0,xip=Xi,bp=db+p*l,bp1=bp+p;bp<bp1;bp++,xip+=maxn) xx += *xip * *bp;
      for (dgp=dg,xip=wXi,p1=dg+p;dgp < p1;dgp++,xip+= maxn) *dgp = - *xip * xx;
      jj = i0;
      if (l<nth) for (xx=dlet[l*n+k[jj]],dgp=dg,xip=Xi,p1=dg+p;dgp < p1;dgp++,xip+= maxn) *dgp = - *xip * xx;
      for (jj++,j=1;j<q;j++,jj++) {
        for (xx=0.0,xip=Xi+j,bp=db+p*l,bp1=bp+p;bp<bp1;bp++,xip+=maxn) xx += *xip * *bp;
        for (dgp=dg,xip=wXi+j,p1=dg+p;dgp < p1;dgp++,xip+= maxn) *dgp -= *xip * xx;
	if (l<nth) for (xx=dlet[l*n+k[jj]],dgp=dg,xip=Xi,p1=dg+p;dgp < p1;dgp++,xip+= maxn) *dgp = - *xip * xx;
      }

      for (j=0;j<p;j++) for (xip0=Xi+j*maxn,xip=dwXi+j*maxn,wp=dw+l*n,jj=i0;jj<m[i];jj++,xip0++,xip++) *xip = *xip0 * wp[k[jj]];
      z=1.0;xx=0.0;
      F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,&q,&p,&z,dwXi,&maxn,d,&one,&xx,g,&one FCONE);
      F77_CALL(dgemv)(&trans,&q,&p,&z,Xi,&maxn,g,&one,&xx,g1,&one FCONE);
      F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,&p,&p,&z,dH+l*p2,&p,d,&one,&xx,g,&one FCONE);
      for (j=0;j<p;j++) dg[j] += g1[j] - g[j];
      F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,&p,&p,&z,Hi,&p,dg,&one,&xx,d1,&one FCONE);
      kk=CG(Hp,Hi,dg,d1,p,1e-13,cgwork);
      if (kk>error) error=kk;
      for (io=io0;io<mi[i];io++) {
        for (xx=0.0,xip=X+ind[io],dbp=db+p*l,j=0;j<p;j++,xip += n) xx += *xip * (dbp[j]-d1[j]);
        deta[io+l*no] = xx;
      }
    }
  }
  FREE(Hp);FREE(Hd);
  FREE(g);FREE(d);FREE(cgwork);
  FREE(Xi);FREE(wXi);FREE(dwXi);
  if (deriv>0) FREE(dH);
  PROTECT(kr=allocVector(INTSXP,1));
  INTEGER(kr)[0] = error;
  UNPROTECT(5);
  return(kr);
}

SEXP Rncv(SEXP x, SEXP r, SEXP W1, SEXP W2, SEXP DB, SEXP DW, SEXP rS, SEXP IND, SEXP MI, SEXP M, SEXP K,SEXP BETA, SEXP SP, SEXP ETA,
	   SEXP DETA,SEXP DLET,SEXP DERIV,SEXP EPS,SEXP NT) {

  SEXP S,kr;
  int maxn,i,nsp,n,p,pg,*m,*k,j,l,ii,i0,ki,q,p2,one=1,deriv,*error,jj,nm,*ind,nth,*mi,io,io0,no,pdef,nddbuf,nwork = 0,
    nt,tid=0,pmaxn,*iwork=NULL,use_minres=1,niwork=0;
  double *X,*g,*g1,*gp,*p1,*R0,*R,*Xi,xx,*xip,*xip0,z,w1ki,w2ki,*wXi,*d,*w1,*w2,*eta,*p0,*p3,*ddbuf,*Rb,*work=NULL,
    *deta,*beta,*dg,*dgp,*dwX=NULL,*wp,*wp1,*db=NULL,*dw=NULL,*rSj,*sp,*d1,*dbp,*dH=NULL,*xp,*wxp,*bp,*bp1,*dwXi,*dlet=NULL,*dp,eps,alpha;
  char trans = 'T',ntrans = 'N',uplo='U',diag='N';
  M = PROTECT(coerceVector(M,INTSXP));
  MI = PROTECT(coerceVector(MI,INTSXP));
  IND = PROTECT(coerceVector(IND,INTSXP));
  K = PROTECT(coerceVector(K,INTSXP));
  deriv = asInteger(DERIV);
  nt = asInteger(NT);
  mi = INTEGER(MI);m = INTEGER(M); k = INTEGER(K);ind = INTEGER(IND);
  nsp = length(rS);
  nth = ncols(DETA)-nsp;
  sp = REAL(SP);
  w1=REAL(W1);w2=REAL(W2);
  X = REAL(x);beta = REAL(BETA);
  R=REAL(r);eta = REAL(ETA);deta = REAL(DETA);
  eps = asReal(EPS);
  p = ncols(x); n = nrows(x);p2=p*p;
  no = length(IND);
  nm = length(M);
  #ifndef _OPENMP
  nt = 1;
  #endif

  d = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) 2*p*nt,sizeof(double));
  d1 = d + p*nt;

  maxn = ii = 0;
  for (j=0;j<nm;j++) {
    i = m[j]; if (i-ii>maxn) maxn = i-ii; ii = i;
  }
  pmaxn = p*maxn;
  pg = p; if (pg<maxn) pg=maxn;
  g = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) (2*p+pg)*nt,sizeof(double));
  g1 = g + pg*nt;dg = g1 + p*nt;

  Xi = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) pmaxn*nt,sizeof(double));
  wXi = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) pmaxn*nt,sizeof(double));
  dwXi = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) pmaxn*nt,sizeof(double));
  R0 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p2*nt,sizeof(double));Rb = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p2*nt,sizeof(double));
  ddbuf = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) pmaxn*nt,sizeof(double));
  if (use_minres) {
    nwork =  p*(maxn+7)+maxn;
  } else {
    niwork = maxn;
    iwork = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)niwork*nt,sizeof(int));

    nwork = -1;z = 1.0;i=1;
    F77_CALL(dsysv)(&uplo,&niwork,&i,d,&niwork,iwork,d,&niwork,&xx,&nwork,&j FCONE);
    nwork = (int) ceil(xx);
    nwork += maxn*(p+1+maxn);
  }
  work = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) nwork*nt,sizeof(double));
  error = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) nt,sizeof(int));
  xx=1.0;z=0.0;

  if (deriv>0) {
    dwX = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p*n,sizeof(double));
    db=REAL(DB);dw=REAL(DW);dlet = REAL(DLET);
    dH = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p2*(nsp+nth),sizeof(double));
    for (j=0;j<nsp+nth;j++) {
      for (xip0 = X,xip=dwX,q=0;q<p;q++) for (wp=dw+n*j,wp1=wp+n;wp<wp1;wp++,xip++,xip0++) *xip = *xip0 * *wp;
      F77_CALL(dgemm)(&trans,&ntrans,&p,&p,&n,&xx,X,&n,dwX,&n,&z,dH+j*p2,&p FCONE FCONE);
      if (j>=nth) {
        S = VECTOR_ELT(rS, j-nth);
        rSj = REAL(S);q = ncols(S);
        F77_CALL(dgemm)(&ntrans,&trans,&p,&p,&q,sp+j-nth,rSj,&p,rSj,&p,&xx,dH+j*p2,&p FCONE FCONE);
      }
    }
    FREE(dwX);
  } else if (deriv<0) dlet = REAL(DLET);

  #ifdef _OPENMP

#pragma omp parallel for schedule(guided) private(i,ii,io,i0,io0,j,jj,l,ki,q,alpha,nddbuf,pdef,xx,z,wxp,xip,xp,xip0,p0,p1,p3,dp,gp,dgp,bp,bp1,wp,dbp,w1ki,w2ki,tid) num_threads(nt)
  #endif
  for (i=0;i<nm;i++) {
    if (!i) io=ii=0;else {io=mi[i-1];ii=m[i-1];}
    #ifdef _OPENMP
    tid = omp_get_thread_num();
    #endif
    p1 = g + tid*pg + p;
    ki = k[ii];w1ki = w1[ki];w2ki = w2[ki];
    i0=ii;io0=io;
    for (p0=R0+tid*p2,p3=R,j=0;j<p;j++,p0+=p,p3+=p) for (q=0;q<=j;q++) p0[q] = p3[q];
    alpha = sqrt(fabs(w2ki));
    nddbuf=0;
    for (dp=d+tid*p,xip0=xip=Xi+pmaxn*tid,gp=g+tid*pg,xp=X,wxp=wXi+pmaxn*tid;gp<p1;dp++,gp++,xp += n,xip += maxn,wxp += maxn) {
      xx = xp[ki];
      *gp = w1ki * xx;
      *xip = xx;
      *wxp = xx*w2ki;
      *dp = xx*alpha;
    }
    if (alpha<0) j=1; else j=0;
    chol_up(R0+p2*tid,d+tid*p,&p,&j,&eps);
    if (*(R0+p2*tid+1) < -0.5) {
      pdef=0;*(R0+p2*tid+1)=0.0;
      for (p0=R0+p2*tid,p3=R,j=0;j<p;j++,p0+=p,p3+=p) for (q=0;q<=j;q++) p0[q] = p3[q];
      for (p0=ddbuf+p*nddbuf+tid*pmaxn,p3=d+tid*p,j=0;j<p;j++) p0[j] = p3[j];
      nddbuf++;
    } else pdef=1;
    q=1;
    for (xip0++,ii++;ii<m[i];ii++,xip0++,q++) {
      ki = k[ii];w1ki = w1[ki];w2ki = w2[ki];alpha = sqrt(fabs(w2ki));
      for (dp=d+tid*p,xip=xip0,gp=g+pg*tid,xp=X,wxp=wXi+q+pmaxn*tid;gp<p1;dp++,gp++,xp += n,xip += maxn,wxp += maxn) {
	xx = xp[ki];
	*gp += w1ki * xx;
	*xip = xx;
	*wxp = xx*w2ki;
	*dp = xx*alpha;
      }
      if (alpha<0) {
	j=1;
      } else {
        for (p0=Rb+p2*tid,p3=R0+p2*tid,j=0;j<p;j++,p0+=p,p3+=p) for (l=0;l<=j;l++) p0[l] = p3[l];
        j=0;
      }
      chol_up(R0+p2*tid,d+tid*p,&p,&j,&eps);
      if (*(R0+p2*tid+1)< -0.5) {
	pdef=0;*(R0+p2*tid+1) = 0.0;
	for (p0=R0+tid*p2,p3=Rb+tid*p2,j=0;j<p;j++,p0+=p,p3+=p) for (l=0;l<=j;l++) p0[l] = p3[l];
        for (p0=ddbuf+p*nddbuf+pmaxn*tid,p3=d+p*tid,j=0;j<p;j++) p0[j] = p3[j];
        nddbuf++;
      }
    }

    if (pdef) {
      for (p0=d+tid*p,p3=g+tid*pg,j=0;j<p;j++) p0[j] = p3[j];
      F77_CALL(dtrsv)(&uplo,&trans,&diag,&p,R0+tid*p2,&p,d+tid*p,&one FCONE FCONE FCONE);
      F77_CALL(dtrsv)(&uplo,&ntrans,&diag,&p,R0+tid*p2,&p,d+tid*p,&one FCONE FCONE FCONE);
    } else {

      j = nddbuf;
      if (use_minres) minres(R0+p2*tid,ddbuf+tid*pmaxn,g+pg*tid,d+p*tid,&p,&j,work+tid*nwork); else {
        *(iwork+niwork*tid) = nwork;
	woodbury(R0+p2*tid,ddbuf+tid*pmaxn,g+pg*tid,d+p*tid,&p,&j,work+tid*nwork,iwork+niwork*tid);
      }
      error[tid]++;
    }
    for (;io<mi[i];io++) {
      for (p0=d+tid*p,xx=0.0,xip=X+ind[io],j=0;j<p;j++,xip += n) xx += *xip * (beta[j]-p0[j]);
      eta[io] = xx;
    }
    if (deriv<0) {
      for (p0=d+tid*p,p1=dlet+p*i,j=0;j<p;j++) p1[j] = p0[j];
    }

    if (deriv>0) for (l=0;l<nsp+nth;l++) {

      for (xx=0.0,xip=Xi+tid*pmaxn,bp=db+p*l,bp1=bp+p;bp<bp1;bp++,xip+=maxn) xx += *xip * *bp;
      for (dgp=dg+tid*p,xip=wXi+tid*pmaxn,p1=dgp+p;dgp < p1;dgp++,xip+= maxn) *dgp = - *xip * xx;
      jj = i0;
      if (l<nth) for (xx=dlet[l*n+k[jj]],dgp=dg+p*tid,xip=Xi+tid*pmaxn,p1=dgp+p;dgp < p1;dgp++,xip+= maxn) *dgp += - *xip * xx;
      for (jj++,j=1;j<q;j++,jj++) {
        for (xx=0.0,xip=Xi+j+pmaxn*tid,bp=db+p*l,bp1=bp+p;bp<bp1;bp++,xip+=maxn) xx += *xip * *bp;
        for (dgp=dg+tid*p,xip=wXi+j+tid*pmaxn,p1=dgp+p;dgp < p1;dgp++,xip+= maxn) *dgp -= *xip * xx;
	if (l<nth) for (xx=dlet[l*n+k[jj]],dgp=dg+tid*p,xip=Xi+j+tid*pmaxn,p1=dgp+p;dgp < p1;dgp++,xip+= maxn) *dgp += - *xip * xx;
      }

      for (j=0;j<p;j++) for (xip0=Xi+j*maxn+pmaxn*tid,xip=dwXi+j*maxn+tid*pmaxn,wp=dw+l*n,jj=i0;jj<m[i];jj++,xip0++,xip++) *xip = *xip0 * wp[k[jj]];
      z=1.0;xx=0.0;
      F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,&q,&p,&z,dwXi+pmaxn*tid,&maxn,d+tid*p,&one,&xx,g+tid*pg,&one FCONE);
      F77_CALL(dgemv)(&trans,&q,&p,&z,Xi+pmaxn*tid,&maxn,g+tid*pg,&one,&xx,g1+tid*p,&one FCONE);
      F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,&p,&p,&z,dH+l*p2,&p,d+tid*p,&one,&xx,g+tid*pg,&one FCONE);
      for (dgp = dg+tid*p,p0=g1+tid*p,p3=g+tid*pg,j=0;j<p;j++) dgp[j] += p0[j] - p3[j];
      if (pdef) {
	for (p0=d1+tid*p,p3=dg+tid*p,j=0;j<p;j++) p0[j] = p3[j];
        F77_CALL(dtrsv)(&uplo,&trans,&diag,&p,R0+tid*p2,&p,d1+tid*p,&one FCONE FCONE FCONE);
        F77_CALL(dtrsv)(&uplo,&ntrans,&diag,&p,R0+tid*p2,&p,d1+tid*p,&one FCONE FCONE FCONE);
      } else {
	j = nddbuf;
	if (use_minres) minres(R0+tid*p2,ddbuf+tid*pmaxn,dg+tid*p,d1+tid*p,&p,&j,work+nwork*tid); else {
          *(iwork+niwork*tid) = nwork;
	  woodbury(R0+tid*p2,ddbuf+tid*pmaxn,dg+tid*p,d1+tid*p,&p,&j,work+nwork*tid,iwork+niwork*tid);
	}
      }
      for (io=io0;io<mi[i];io++) {
        for (p0=d1+tid*p,xx=0.0,xip=X+ind[io],dbp=db+p*l,j=0;j<p;j++,xip += n) xx += *xip * (dbp[j]-p0[j]);
        deta[io+l*no] = xx;
      }
    }
  }
  FREE(R0);FREE(ddbuf);FREE(Rb);
  FREE(g);FREE(d);
  FREE(Xi);FREE(wXi);FREE(dwXi);
  if (deriv>0) FREE(dH);
  FREE(work);if (!use_minres) FREE(iwork);
  for (j=0,i=0;i<nt;i++) j += error[i];
  FREE(error);
  PROTECT(kr=allocVector(INTSXP,1));
  INTEGER(kr)[0] = j;
  UNPROTECT(5);
  return(kr);
}

static inline int i2f(int i,int j,int K) {

  int ii;
  if (i>j) { ii=j;j=i;i=ii;}
  ii = (i*(2*K-i+1))/2 + j-i;
  return(ii);
}

static inline int i3f(int i,int j,int k,int K) {

  int ii;
  while (!(k>=j&&j>=i)) {
    if (i>j) {ii=j;j=i;i=ii;}
    if (j>k) {ii=j;j=k;k=ii;}
  }
  ii = (i*(3*K*(K+1)+(i-1)*(i-3*K-2)))/6 + ((j-i)*(2*K+1-i-j))/2+k-j;
  return(ii);
}

SEXP ncvls(SEXP x,SEXP JJ,SEXP h,SEXP hi,SEXP dH,SEXP L1, SEXP L2,SEXP L3,SEXP IND, SEXP MI, SEXP M, SEXP K,SEXP BETA,
	   SEXP ETACV,SEXP DETACV,SEXP DETA,SEXP DB,SEXP DERIV) {

  double *X,*H,*Hi,*l1,*l2,*l3=NULL,*beta,*g,*Hp,xx,z,*d,*d1,*cgwork,*eta,*deta,v,*db=NULL,*dbp,*detacv=NULL,*dh;
  int **jj,*jjl,*jjq,*ind,*m,*k,n,p,nm,nlp,*plp,ii,i,j,i0,i1,l,ln,ki,p2,q,r,l2i,one=1,kk,nsp,iter1=0,iter=0,deriv,*mi,io,io0,no;
  SEXP JJp,kr,DH;
  char ntrans = 'N';
  p = length(BETA);p2 = p*p;
  n = nrows(x);deriv = asInteger(DERIV);
  M = PROTECT(coerceVector(M,INTSXP));
  MI = PROTECT(coerceVector(MI,INTSXP));
  IND = PROTECT(coerceVector(IND,INTSXP));
  K = PROTECT(coerceVector(K,INTSXP));
  mi = INTEGER(MI); m = INTEGER(M); k = INTEGER(K);ind = INTEGER(IND);
  l1 = REAL(L1);l2=REAL(L2);
  nm = length(M);nlp = length(JJ);nsp = length(dH);no=length(IND);
  eta = REAL(ETACV);H=REAL(h);Hi = REAL(hi);
  beta = REAL(BETA);

  if (deriv>0) {
    l3=REAL(L3);deta=REAL(DETA);detacv=REAL(DETACV);db = REAL(DB);
  }

  jj = (int **)CALLOC((size_t) nlp,sizeof(int *));
  plp = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) nlp,sizeof(int));
  for (l=0;l<nlp;l++) {
    JJp = VECTOR_ELT(JJ, l);JJp = PROTECT(coerceVector(JJp,INTSXP));
    plp[l] = length(JJp); jj[l] = INTEGER(JJp);
    jjl=jj[l];for (i=0;i<plp[l];i++) jjl[i]--;
  }
  X = REAL(x);
  g = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) 3*p,sizeof(double));
  d = g+p;
  d1 = d + p;
  cgwork = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p*5,sizeof(double));
  Hp = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p2,sizeof(double));
  for (io=ii=0,i=0;i<nm;i++) {
    i0=ii;io0=io;

    for (l=0;l<p;l++) g[l] = 0.0;
    for (l=0;l<p2;l++) Hp[l] = H[l];
    for (;ii<m[i];ii++) {
      ki=k[ii];
      for (l2i=l=0;l<nlp;l++) {
        jjl = jj[l];ln=l*n;
        for (j=0;j<plp[l];j++) g[jjl[j]] += X[n*jjl[j]+ki] * l1[ln+ki];
	for (q=l;q<nlp;q++,l2i++) {
	  jjq = jj[q];ln = l2i*n;
	  for (r=0;r<plp[q];r++) {
	    for (j=0;j<plp[l];j++) {

	      xx = X[jjl[j]*n+ki]*X[jjq[r]*n+ki]*l2[ln+ki];
              Hp[jjl[j]+jjq[r]*p] += xx;
	      if (q>l) Hp[jjl[j]*p+jjq[r]] += xx;
	    }
	  }
	}
      }
    }
    xx = 0.0;z=1.0;
    F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,&p,&p,&z,Hi,&p,g,&one,&xx,d,&one FCONE);
    kk=CG(Hp,Hi,g,d,p,1e-13,cgwork);
    if (kk<0) {
      Rprintf("npd! ");kk = -kk;
    }

    if (iter < kk) iter=kk;

    for (;io<mi[i];io++)
    for (l=0;l<nlp;l++) {
      ln = no*l;jjl = jj[l];
      for (xx=0.0,j=0;j<plp[l];j++) {
	  q = jjl[j];
	  xx += X[n*q+ind[io]] * (beta[q]-d[q]);
      }
      eta[ln+io] = xx;
    }
    if (deriv>0) {
      for (l=0;l<nsp;l++) {
	ln = l*nlp;
	DH = VECTOR_ELT(dH, l);dh = REAL(DH);
	xx=0.0;z=1.0;
        F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,&p,&p,&z,dh,&p,d,&one,&xx,g,&one FCONE);

        for (i1=i0;i1<m[i];i1++) {
	  ki=k[i1];
	  for (j=0;j<nlp;j++) for (q=0;q<nlp;q++) {
	    v = l3[i3f(j,q,0,nlp)*n+ki]*deta[ln*n+ki];
	    for (r=1;r<nlp;r++) v += l3[i3f(j,q,r,nlp)*n+ki]*deta[(ln+r)*n+ki];
	    jjl=jj[j];jjq=jj[q];
	    for (xx=0.0,kk=0;kk<plp[q];kk++) xx += X[ki+n*jjq[kk]]*d[jjq[kk]];
            xx *= v;
	    for (kk=0;kk<plp[j];kk++) g[jjl[kk]] -= X[ki+n*jjl[kk]]*xx;

	    for (xx=0.0,kk=0;kk<plp[q];kk++) xx += X[ki+n*jjq[kk]]*db[jjq[kk]+p*l];
	    xx *= l2[ki+i2f(j,q,nlp)*n];
	    for (kk=0;kk<plp[j];kk++) g[jjl[kk]] += X[ki+n*jjl[kk]]*xx;
	  }
	}

	xx=0.0;z=1.0;
	F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,&p,&p,&z,Hi,&p,g,&one,&xx,d1,&one FCONE);
        kk=CG(Hp,Hi,g,d1,p,1e-13,cgwork);

	if (kk<0) {
          Rprintf("npdg! ");kk = -kk;
	}

	if (iter1 < kk) iter1 = kk;
	for (io=io0;io<mi[i];io++)
        for (q=0;q<nlp;q++) {
	  jjq=jj[q];dbp = db + p*l;
	  for (xx=0.0,j=0;j<plp[q];j++) {
	      kk = jjq[j];
	      xx += X[ind[io]+kk*n] * (dbp[kk]-d1[kk]);
	  }
          detacv[io + q*no +l*(no*nlp)] = xx;
        }
      }
    } else if (deriv<0) {
      for (j=0;j<p;j++) detacv[i+nm*j] = d[j];
    }
  }
  for (l=0;l<nlp;l++) {

    jjl=jj[l];for (i=0;i<plp[l];i++) jjl[i]++;
  }
  FREE(jj);FREE(plp);FREE(g);FREE(Hp);FREE(cgwork);
  PROTECT(kr=allocVector(INTSXP,2));
  INTEGER(kr)[0] = iter;
  INTEGER(kr)[1] = iter1;
  UNPROTECT(5+nlp);
  return(kr);
}

SEXP Rncvls(SEXP x,SEXP JJ,SEXP R1,SEXP dH,SEXP L1, SEXP L2,SEXP L3,SEXP IND, SEXP MI, SEXP M, SEXP K,SEXP BETA,
	    SEXP ETACV,SEXP DETACV,SEXP DETA,SEXP DB,SEXP DERIV,SEXP EPS,SEXP NT) {

  double *X,*R,*l1,*l2,*l3,*beta,*g,*R0,xx,z,*d,*d1,*eta,*deta,v,*db,*dbp,*detacv,*dh,*b,alpha,alpha0,eps,*Rb,*ddbuf,*p0,*p3,*work;
  int **jj,*jjl,*jjq,*ind,*m,*k,n,p,nm,nlp,*plp,ii,i,j,i0,i1,l,ln,ki,p2,q,r,l2i,one=1,kk,nsp,*error,deriv,nddbuf,
    *mi,io,io0,no,pdef=1,maxn,buffer_size=0,nwork=0,nt=1,tid=0,*iwork=NULL,use_minres=1,niwork=0;
  SEXP JJp,kr,DH;
  char trans = 'T',ntrans = 'N',uplo='U',diag='N';
  p = length(BETA);p2 = p*p;
  n = nrows(x);deriv = asInteger(DERIV);
  eps = asReal(EPS);
  M = PROTECT(coerceVector(M,INTSXP));
  MI = PROTECT(coerceVector(MI,INTSXP));
  IND = PROTECT(coerceVector(IND,INTSXP));
  K = PROTECT(coerceVector(K,INTSXP));
  mi = INTEGER(MI); m = INTEGER(M); k = INTEGER(K);ind = INTEGER(IND);
  l1 = REAL(L1);l2=REAL(L2);
  nm = length(M);nlp = length(JJ);nsp = length(dH);no=length(IND);
  eta = REAL(ETACV);beta = REAL(BETA);R=REAL(R1);

  if (deriv>0) {
    l3=REAL(L3);deta=REAL(DETA);db = REAL(DB);
  } else l3=deta=db=NULL;
  if (deriv!=0) detacv=REAL(DETACV); else detacv = NULL;

  jj = (int **)CALLOC((size_t) nlp,sizeof(int *));
  plp = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) nlp,sizeof(int));
  for (l=0;l<nlp;l++) {
    JJp = VECTOR_ELT(JJ, l);JJp = PROTECT(coerceVector(JJp,INTSXP));
    plp[l] = length(JJp); jj[l] = INTEGER(JJp);
    jjl=jj[l];for (i=0;i<plp[l];i++) jjl[i]--;
  }

  maxn = ii = 0;
  for (j=0;j<nm;j++) {
    i = m[j]; if (i-ii>maxn) maxn = i-ii; ii = i;
  }
  #ifdef _OPENMP
  nt = asInteger(NT);
  #endif

  X = REAL(x);
  g = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) (3*p+nlp)*nt,sizeof(double));
  d = g + p*nt;
  d1 = d + p*nt;
  b = d1 + p*nt;
  R0 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p2*nt,sizeof(double));
  Rb = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) p2*nt,sizeof(double));
  niwork = maxn*nlp*(nlp+1)/2;
  if (use_minres) {
    nwork =  p*(niwork+7)+niwork;
  } else {
    iwork = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)niwork*nt,sizeof(int));

    nwork = -1;z = 1.0;i=1;
    F77_CALL(dsysv)(&uplo,&niwork,&i,d,&niwork,iwork,d,&niwork,&xx,&nwork,&j FCONE);
    nwork = (int) ceil(xx);
    nwork += niwork*(p+1+niwork);
  }
  work = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) nwork*nt,sizeof(double));

  buffer_size = p*niwork;
  ddbuf = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) buffer_size*nt,sizeof(double));
  error = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)nt,sizeof(int));
  #ifdef _OPENMP

#pragma omp parallel for schedule(guided) private(i,ii,i0,io,io0,tid,l,pdef,nddbuf,ki,j,l2i,jjl,ln,alpha0,jjq,alpha,xx,p0,p3,q,i1,DH,dh,z,v,r,kk,dbp) num_threads(nt)
  #endif
  for (i=0;i<nm;i++) {
    if (!i) io=ii=0;else {io=mi[i-1];ii=m[i-1];}
    #ifdef _OPENMP
    tid = omp_get_thread_num();
    #endif
    i0=ii;io0=io;

    for (p0=g+p*tid,l=0;l<p;l++) p0[l] = 0.0;
    for (p0=R0+p2*tid,l=0;l<p2;l++) p0[l] = R[l];
    pdef=1;
    nddbuf = 0;
    for (;ii<m[i];ii++) {
      ki=k[ii];
      for (p0=b+nlp*tid,j=0;j<nlp;j++) p0[j] = 0.0;
      for (kk=0;kk<2;kk++)
      for (l2i=l=0;l<nlp;l++) {
        jjl = jj[l];ln=l*n;
        if (!kk) for (p0=g+p*tid,j=0;j<plp[l];j++) p0[jjl[j]] += X[n*jjl[j]+ki] * l1[ln+ki];
        alpha0 = l2[l2i*n+ki];l2i++;
	for (q=l+1;q<nlp;q++,l2i++) {

          jjq = jj[q];alpha = l2[l2i*n+ki];

	  if (!kk) { *(b+l+nlp*tid) += alpha;*(b+q+nlp*tid) += alpha;}
          if ((!kk&&alpha>0)||(kk&&alpha<0)) {
	    xx = sqrt(fabs(alpha));
	    for (p0=d+p*tid,j=0;j<p;j++) p0[j] = 0.0;
	    for (j=0;j<plp[l];j++) p0[jjl[j]] =  X[jjl[j]*n+ki]*xx;
	    for (j=0;j<plp[q];j++) p0[jjq[j]] +=  X[jjq[j]*n+ki]*xx;
            if (alpha>0) j=1; else {
	      for (p0=Rb+p2*tid,p3=R0+tid*p2,j=0;j<p;j++,p0+=p,p3+=p) for (q=0;q<=j;q++) p0[q] = p3[q];
              j=0;
	    }
	    chol_up(R0+p2*tid,d+tid*p,&p,&j,&eps);
	    if (!j && *(R0+1+p2*tid) < -0.5) {
              pdef=0;*(R0+1+p2*tid) = 0.0;
	      for (p0=Rb+tid*p2,p3=R0+tid*p2,j=0;j<p;j++,p0+=p,p3+=p) for (q=0;q<=j;q++) p3[q] = p0[q];
	      for (p0=ddbuf+p*nddbuf+tid*buffer_size,p3=d+tid*p,j=0;j<p;j++) p0[j] = p3[j];
              nddbuf++;
	    }
	  }
	}

	alpha = alpha0 - *(b+l+tid*nlp);
	if ((!kk&&alpha>0)||(kk&&alpha<0)) {
	  xx = sqrt(fabs(alpha));for (p0=d+tid*p,j=0;j<p;j++) p0[j] = 0.0;
	  for (j=0;j<plp[l];j++) p0[jjl[j]] =  xx*X[jjl[j]*n+ki];

	  if (alpha>0) {
	    j=1;
	  } else {
            for (p0=Rb+p2*tid,p3=R0+tid*p2,j=0;j<p;j++,p0+=p,p3+=p) for (q=0;q<=j;q++) p0[q] = p3[q];
            j=0;
	  }
	  chol_up(R0+tid*p2,d+tid*p,&p,&j,&eps);
	  if (!j && *(R0+1+p2*tid) < -0.5) {
	    pdef=0;*(R0+1+p2*tid) = 0.0;

	    for (p0=Rb+tid*p2,p3=R0+tid*p2,j=0;j<p;j++,p0+=p,p3+=p) for (q=0;q<=j;q++) p3[q] = p0[q];
	    for (p0=ddbuf+p*nddbuf+tid*buffer_size,p3=d+tid*p,j=0;j<p;j++) p0[j] = p3[j];
            nddbuf++;
	  }
        }
      }

    }

    if (pdef) {
      for (p0=d+tid*p,p3=g+tid*p,j=0;j<p;j++) p0[j] = p3[j];
      F77_CALL(dtrsv)(&uplo,&trans,&diag,&p,R0+tid*p2,&p,d+tid*p,&one FCONE FCONE FCONE);
      F77_CALL(dtrsv)(&uplo,&ntrans,&diag,&p,R0+tid*p2,&p,d+tid*p,&one FCONE FCONE FCONE);
    } else {
      error[tid]++;

      j = nddbuf;
      if (use_minres) minres(R0+tid*p2,ddbuf+buffer_size*tid,g+tid*p,d+tid*p,&p,&j,work+nwork*tid); else {
        *(iwork+niwork*tid) = nwork;
	woodbury(R0+tid*p2,ddbuf+buffer_size*tid,g+tid*p,d+tid*p,&p,&j,work+nwork*tid,iwork+niwork*tid);
      }

    }

    for (;io<mi[i];io++)
    for (l=0;l<nlp;l++) {
      ln = no*l;jjl = jj[l];i1=ind[io];
      for (p0=d+tid*p,xx=0.0,j=0;j<plp[l];j++) {
	  q = jjl[j];
	  xx += X[n*q+i1] * (beta[q]-p0[q]);
      }
      eta[ln+io] = xx;
    }
    if (deriv>0) {
      for (l=0;l<nsp;l++) {
	ln = l*nlp;
	DH = VECTOR_ELT(dH, l);dh = REAL(DH);
	xx=0.0;z=1.0;
        F77_CALL(dgemv)(&ntrans,&p,&p,&z,dh,&p,d+tid*p,&one,&xx,g+tid*p,&one FCONE);
        for (i1=i0;i1<m[i];i1++) {
	  ki=k[i1];
	  for (j=0;j<nlp;j++) for (q=0;q<nlp;q++) {
	    v = l3[i3f(j,q,0,nlp)*n+ki]*deta[ln*n+ki];
	    for (r=1;r<nlp;r++) v += l3[i3f(j,q,r,nlp)*n+ki]*deta[(ln+r)*n+ki];
	    jjl=jj[j];jjq=jj[q];
	    for (p0=d+tid*p,xx=0.0,kk=0;kk<plp[q];kk++) xx += X[ki+n*jjq[kk]]*p0[jjq[kk]];
            xx *= v;
	    for (p0=g+tid*p,kk=0;kk<plp[j];kk++) p0[jjl[kk]] -= X[ki+n*jjl[kk]]*xx;

	    for (xx=0.0,kk=0;kk<plp[q];kk++) xx += X[ki+n*jjq[kk]]*db[jjq[kk]+p*l];
	    xx *= l2[ki+i2f(j,q,nlp)*n];
	    for (kk=0;kk<plp[j];kk++) p0[jjl[kk]] += X[ki+n*jjl[kk]]*xx;
	  }
	}

        if (pdef) {
	  for (p0=d1+tid*p,p3=g+tid*p,j=0;j<p;j++) p0[j] = p3[j];
          F77_CALL(dtrsv)(&uplo,&trans,&diag,&p,R0+tid*p2,&p,d1+tid*p,&one FCONE FCONE FCONE);
          F77_CALL(dtrsv)(&uplo,&ntrans,&diag,&p,R0+tid*p2,&p,d1+tid*p,&one FCONE FCONE FCONE);
        } else {
          j = nddbuf;
	  if (use_minres) minres(R0+tid*p2,ddbuf+tid*buffer_size,g+tid*p,d1+tid*p,&p,&j,work+nwork*tid); else {
            *(iwork+niwork*tid) = nwork;
	    woodbury(R0+tid*p2,ddbuf+tid*buffer_size,g+tid*p,d1+tid*p,&p,&j,work+nwork*tid,iwork+niwork*tid);
	  }

        }

	for (io=io0;io<mi[i];io++)
        for (q=0;q<nlp;q++) {
	  jjq=jj[q];dbp = db + p*l;
	  for (p0=d1+tid*p,xx=0.0,j=0;j<plp[q];j++) {
	      kk = jjq[j];
	      xx += X[ind[io]+kk*n] * (dbp[kk]-p0[kk]);
	  }
          detacv[io + q*no +l*(no*nlp)] = xx;
        }
      }
    } else if (deriv<0) {
      for (p0=d+tid*p,j=0;j<p;j++) detacv[i+nm*j] = p0[j];
    }
  }
  for (l=0;l<nlp;l++) {

    jjl=jj[l];for (i=0;i<plp[l];i++) jjl[i]++;
  }
  FREE(jj);FREE(plp);FREE(g);FREE(R0);
  FREE(Rb);FREE(ddbuf);FREE(work);if (!use_minres) FREE(iwork);
  for (j=i=0;i<nt;i++) j+=error[i];
  FREE(error);
  PROTECT(kr=allocVector(INTSXP,1));
  INTEGER(kr)[0] = j;
  UNPROTECT(5+nlp);
  return(kr);
}

# --- End of mgcv/src/ncv.c ---

# --- Start of mgcv/src/qp.c ---

#include <stdlib.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <string.h>
#include "matrix.h"
#include "qp.h"
#include "general.h"
#include "mgcv.h"

#define DELMAX 35

#define max(a,b)    (((a) > (b)) ? (a) : (b))
#define min(a,b)    (((a) < (b)) ? (a) : (b))

#define round(a) ((a)-floor(a) <0.5 ? (int)floor(a):(int) floor(a)+1)

matrix addconQT(matrix *Q,matrix T,matrix a,matrix *u)

{ int q,i,j;
  double la,ra=0.0,*cV,*bV,*T1V,**QM;
  matrix b,c;
  c=initmat(Q->r,1);b=initmat(Q->r,1);(*u)=initmat(Q->r,1);
  QM = Q->M;
  for (i=0;i<c.r;i++) for (j=0;j<a.c;j++) c.V[i]+=a.V[j]*QM[j][i];
  la=dot(c,c);
  cV=c.V;bV=b.V;
  q=T.c-T.r-1;
  if (q!=0)
  { for (i=q+1;i<a.c;i++) { ra+=cV[i]*cV[i];bV[i]=cV[i];}
    if ((la-ra)<0.0)
    { error(_("ERROR in addconQT."));}
    else
    bV[q]=sqrt(la-ra);
    if (cV[q]>0.0) bV[q]= -bV[q];
    householder(u,c,b,q);
    Hmult((*Q),(*u));
  } else
  for (i=0;i<a.c;i++) bV[i]=cV[i];
  T1V=T.M[T.r];T.r++;
  for (j=0;j<T.c;j++) T1V[j]=bV[j];
  freemat(b);
  freemat(c);
  return(T);
}

void GivensAddconQT(matrix *Q,matrix *T,matrix *a,matrix *s,matrix *c)

{ int q,i,j;
  double Qi,r,cc,ss,*bV,*sV,*cV,*aV,**QM,*QV,bb,bb1;
  matrix b;
  b.V=T->M[T->r]; b.r=Q->r;b.c=1;QM=Q->M; bV=b.V;aV=a->V;
  for (i=0;i<T->c;i++) bV[i]=0.0;
  for (i=0;i<b.r;i++) for (j=0;j<Q->r;j++) bV[i]+=QM[j][i]*aV[j];

  cV=c->V;sV=s->V;
  q=T->c-T->r-1;
  for (i=0;i<q;i++)
  {
    bb=bV[i];bb1=bV[i+1];
    r=bb*bb+bb1*bb1;r=sqrt(r);
    if (r==0.0) { ss=sV[i]=0.0;cc=cV[i]=1.0;} else
    { ss=sV[i]=bb/r;cc=cV[i]= -bb1/r;

      bV[i+1]=r;
    }

    for (j=0;j<Q->r;j++)
    { QV=QM[j];
      Qi=QV[i];
      QV[i]=cc*Qi + ss*QV[i+1];
      QV[i+1]=ss*Qi - cc*QV[i+1];
    }
  }
  T->r++;
}

void LSQPaddcon(matrix *Ain,matrix *Q,matrix *T,matrix *Rf,matrix *Py,matrix *PX,
                matrix *s,matrix *c,int sth)

{ matrix a;
  double RfMji,*RfV,*RfV1,ss,cc,r,x1,x2;
  int i,j,k;
  a.V=Ain->M[sth];a.r=Ain->c;a.c=1;
  s->r=T->c-T->r-1;

  GivensAddconQT(Q,T,&a,s,c);

  for (i=0;i<s->r;i++)
  { cc=c->V[i];ss=s->V[i];
    k=i+2;if (k>Rf->r) k--;
    for (j=0;j<k;j++)
    { RfV=Rf->M[j];
      RfMji=RfV[i];
      RfV[i]=cc*RfMji+ss*RfV[i+1];
      RfV[i+1]=ss*RfMji - cc*RfV[i+1];
    }
  }

  for (i=0;i<s->r;i++)
  {
    RfV=Rf->M[i];RfV1=Rf->M[i+1];
    x1=RfV[i];x2=RfV1[i];
    r=sqrt(x1*x1+x2*x2);ss=x2/r;cc=x1/r;
    Rf->M[i][i]=r;Rf->M[i+1][i]=0.0;
    for (j=i+1;j<Rf->c;j++)
    { x1=RfV[j];x2=RfV1[j];
      RfV[j]=cc*x1+ss*x2;
      RfV1[j]=ss*x1-cc*x2;
    }

    x1=Py->V[i];x2=Py->V[i+1];
    Py->V[i]=cc*x1+ss*x2;
    Py->V[i+1]=ss*x1-cc*x2;

    for (j=0;j<PX->c;j++)
    { x1=PX->M[i][j];x2=PX->M[i+1][j];
      PX->M[i][j]=cc*x1+ss*x2;
      PX->M[i+1][j]=ss*x1-cc*x2;
    }
  }
}

int LSQPstep(int *ignore,matrix *Ain,matrix *b,matrix *p1,matrix *p,matrix *pk)

{ double Ap1,ap,apk,alpha,alphamin,*AV,*pV,*p1V,*pkV;
  int imin,i,j;
  alphamin=1.0;imin= -1;
  p1V=p1->V;pV=p->V;pkV=pk->V;
  for (i=0;i<p->r;i++) p1V[i]=pV[i]+pkV[i];
  for (i=0;i<Ain->r;i++)
  { AV=Ain->M[i];
    if (!ignore[i])
    { Ap1=0.0;
      for (j=0;j<Ain->c;j++) Ap1+=AV[j]*p1V[j];
      if ((b->V[i]-Ap1)>0)
      { ap=0.0;apk=0.0;
	for (j=0;j<Ain->c;j++)
	{ ap+=AV[j]*pV[j];
	  apk+=AV[j]*pkV[j];
	}
	if (fabs(apk)>0.0)
	{ alpha=(b->V[i]-ap)/apk;
          if (alpha<alphamin)
	  { alphamin=max(0.0,alpha);imin=i;
            for (j=0;j<p->r;j++) p1V[j]=pV[j]+alphamin*pkV[j];
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return(imin);

}

void LSQPdelcon(matrix *Q,matrix *T,matrix *Rf,matrix *Py,matrix *PX,int sth)

{ int i,j,colj,coli,k,Tr,Tc,Qr,T1r,T1c;
  double r,s,c,xi,xj,**TM,**QM,*TV,*QV,*T1V,*RfV,*RfV1;
  Tr=T->r;TM=T->M;QM=Q->M;Tc=T->c;Qr=Q->r;
  for (i=sth+1;i<Tr;i++)
  { coli=Tc-i-1;colj=Tc-i;
    xi=TM[i][coli];xj=TM[i][colj];
    r=xi*xi+xj*xj;
    r=sqrt(r);
    s=xi/r;c=xj/r;
    for (j=i;j<Tr;j++)
    { TV=TM[j];
      xi=TV[coli];
      TV[coli]= -c*xi+s*TV[colj];
      TV[colj]=s*xi+c*TV[colj];
    }
    for (j=0;j<Qr;j++)
    { QV=QM[j];
      xi=QV[coli];
      QV[coli]= -c*xi+s*QV[colj];
      QV[colj]=s*xi+c*QV[colj];
    }

    for (j=0;j<=colj;j++)
    { RfV=Rf->M[j];
      xi=RfV[coli];
      RfV[coli]= -c*xi+s*RfV[colj];
      RfV[colj]=s*xi+c*RfV[colj];
    }

    xi=Rf->M[coli][coli];xj=Rf->M[colj][coli];
    r=sqrt(xi*xi+xj*xj);
    s=xj/r;c=xi/r;
    Rf->M[coli][coli]=r;Rf->M[colj][coli]=0.0;

    RfV=Rf->M[coli];RfV1=Rf->M[colj];
    for (j=colj;j<Rf->c;j++)
    { xi=RfV[j];xj=RfV1[j];
      RfV[j]=c*xi+s*xj;
      RfV1[j]=s*xi-c*xj;
    }

    xi=Py->V[coli];xj=Py->V[colj];
    Py->V[coli]=c*xi+s*xj;
    Py->V[colj]=s*xi-c*xj;

    for (j=0;j<PX->c;j++)
    { xi=PX->M[coli][j];xj=PX->M[colj][j];
      PX->M[coli][j]=c*xi+s*xj;
      PX->M[colj][j]=s*xi-c*xj;
    }
  }

  T->r--;T1r=T->r;T1c=T->c;
  for (k=0;k<T1r;k++)
  { T1V=TM[k];TV=TM[k];
    for (j=0;j<T1c-k-1;j++) T1V[j]=0.0;
    for (j=T1c-k-1;j<T1c;j++)
    if (k<sth) T1V[j]=TV[j];
    else T1V[j]=TM[k+1][j];
  }
}

int LSQPlagrange(matrix *X,matrix *Q,matrix *T,matrix *p,matrix *Xy,matrix *p1,
                 matrix *y1,int *fixed, int fixed_cons)

{ int i,j,tk;
  double x;
  tk=T->r;
  vmult(X,p,y1,0);
  vmult(X,y1,p1,1);
  for (i=0;i<p1->r;i++) p1->V[i]+= -Xy->V[i];

  for (i=0;i<tk;i++)
  { y1->V[i]=0.0;
    for (j=0;j<Q->r;j++) y1->V[i]+=p1->V[j]*Q->M[j][Q->c-tk+i];
  }

  for (i=tk-1;i>=fixed_cons;i--)
  { x=0.0;for (j=i+1;j<tk;j++) x+=p1->V[j]*T->M[j][T->c-i-1];
    if (T->M[i][T->c-i-1]!=0.0) p1->V[i]=(y1->V[tk-i-1]-x)/T->M[i][T->c-i-1];else p1->V[i]=0.0;
  }

  x=0.0;j=-1;
  for (i=fixed_cons;i<tk;i++)
  if ((!fixed[i-fixed_cons])&&(p1->V[i]<x)) { j=i;x=p1->V[i];}

  if (j!=-1) j -= fixed_cons;
  return(j);
}

void QPCLS(matrix *Z,matrix *X, matrix *p, matrix *y,matrix *Ain,matrix *b,matrix *Af,int *active)

{ matrix Q,T,Rf,PX,Py,a,P,p1,s,c,Xy,y1,u,Pd,pz,pk;
  int k,i,j,tk,*I,*ignore,*fixed,*delog,maxdel=100;
  double x;
  I=(int *)CALLOC((size_t) p->r,sizeof(int));
  fixed=(int *)CALLOC((size_t) p->r,sizeof(int));
  ignore=(int *)CALLOC((size_t) Ain->r,sizeof(int));
  delog=(int *)CALLOC((size_t) Ain->r,sizeof(int));
  p1=initmat(p->r,1);
  y1=initmat(y->r,1);
  s=initmat(p->r,1);c=initmat(p->r,1);
  Xy=initmat(p->r,1);
  vmult(X,y,&Xy,1);
  Rf=initmat(X->r,X->c);
  mcopy(X,&Rf);
  T=initmat(p->r,p->r);
  Q=initmat(p->r,p->r);

  for (i=0;i<p->r;i++) for (j=0;j<p->r;j++) Q.M[i][j]=0.0;
  for (i=0;i<p->r;i++) Q.M[i][i]=1.0;
  T.r=0;a.r=1;a.c=Af->c;
  for (i=0;i<Af->r;i++)
  { a.V=Af->M[i];
    T=addconQT(&Q,T,a,&u);
    Hmult(Rf,u);
    freemat(u);
  }

  P=initmat(Rf.c,Rf.r);
  QR(&P,&Rf);
  Py=initmat(y->r,1);mcopy(y,&Py);
  OrthoMult(&P,&Py,0,(int)P.r,0,1,1);
  PX=initmat(X->r,X->c);mcopy(X,&PX);
  OrthoMult(&P,&PX,0,(int)P.r,0,1,1);
  freemat(P);
  P=initmat(b->r,1);
  Pd=initmat(y->r,1);pz=initmat(p->r,1);pk=initmat(p->r,1);
  tk=0;
  for (i=0;i<active[0];i++) {
    k = active[i+1];ignore[k]=1;I[tk] = k;
    LSQPaddcon(Ain,&Q,&T,&Rf,&Py,&PX,&s,&c,k);tk++;
  }

  while(1) {
    vmult(&PX,p,&Pd,0);
    for (i=0;i<Pd.r;i++) Pd.V[i] = Py.V[i]-Pd.V[i];
    Rf.c=Rf.r=p->r-tk-Af->r;
    for (i=0;i<Rf.c;i++) if (Rf.M[i][i]==0.0) error(_("QPCLS - Rank deficiency in model"));
    Rsolv(&Rf,&pz,&Pd,0);
    Rf.r=X->r;Rf.c=X->c;
    pz.r=p->r-tk-Af->r;

    for (i=0;i<pk.r;i++)
    { pk.V[i]=0.0; for (j=0;j<pz.r;j++) pk.V[i]+=Q.M[i][j]*pz.V[j];}

    k=LSQPstep(ignore,Ain,b,&p1,p,&pk);
    mcopy(&p1,p);
    if (k>-1)
    { I[tk]=k;ignore[k]=1;
      LSQPaddcon(Ain,&Q,&T,&Rf,&Py,&PX,&s,&c,k);tk++;
      if (delog[k]>maxdel)
      fixed[tk-1]=1;

    } else
    { k=LSQPlagrange(X,&Q,&T,p,&Xy,&p1,&y1,fixed,(int)Af->r);
      if (k>-1)
      { LSQPdelcon(&Q,&T,&Rf,&Py,&PX,k+(int)Af->r);

        { for (i=k;i<tk-1;i++)
          fixed[i]=fixed[i+1];
        }
        tk--;
        if (k>-1)
        { ignore[I[k]]=0;
          delog[I[k]]++;
          for (i=k;i<tk;i++) I[i]=I[i+1];
        }
      } else
      {

        *Z=Q; Z->c -= tk;

        active[0]=tk;
        for (i=0;i<tk;i++) active[i+1]=I[i];

        freemat(T);freemat(Rf);freemat(PX);freemat(Py);freemat(p1);freemat(y1);
        freemat(s);freemat(c);freemat(Xy);freemat(Pd);freemat(pz);freemat(pk);
        FREE(I);FREE(ignore);freemat(P);FREE(fixed);FREE(delog);

        return;
      }
    }
  }
}

void PCLS(matrix *X,matrix *p,matrix *y,matrix *w,matrix *Ain,matrix *b,
          matrix *Af,matrix *S,int *off,double *theta,int m,int *active)

{ int i,j,k,n;
  matrix z,F,W,Z,B;
  double *p1,*C;

  if (m>0) z=initmat(y->r+p->r,1);else z=initmat(y->r,1);
  W=initmat(w->r,1);
  for (i=0;i<y->r;i++) { W.V[i]=sqrt(w->V[i]);z.V[i]=W.V[i]*y->V[i];}

  F=initmat(z.r,p->r);

  for (i=0;i<X->r;i++) for (j=0;j<X->c;j++) F.M[i][j]=W.V[i]*X->M[i][j];

  if (m>0) {
    n = p->r;
    C = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(n*n),sizeof(double));
    for (k=0;k<m;k++) for (i=0;i<S[k].r;i++) for (j=0;j<S[k].c;j++)
					       C[i+off[k]+n*(j+off[k])] += theta[k]*S[k].M[i][j];

    k = -1;
    mroot(C,&k,&n);

    for (p1=C,i=0;i<n;i++) for (j=0;j<k;j++,p1++) F.M[j+X->r][i] = *p1;
    FREE(C);
  }

  QPCLS(&Z,&F,p,&z,Ain,b,Af,active);

  B=initmat(z.r,1);matmult(B,F,*p,0,0);

  freemat(B);

  freemat(F);freemat(z);freemat(W);freemat(Z);
}

void RUnpackSarray(int m,matrix *S,double *RS)

{ int start,i,j,k;
  start=0;
  for (k=0;k<m;k++)
  { for (i=0;i<S[k].r;i++) for (j=0;j<S[k].c;j++) S[k].M[i][j]=RS[start+i+S[k].r*j];
    start += S[k].r*S[k].c;
  }
}

void  RPCLS(double *Xd,double *pd,double *yd, double *wd,double *Aind,double *bd,
            double *Afd,double *Sd,
            int *off,int *dim,double *theta, int *m,int *nar,int *active)

{ matrix y,X,p,w,Ain,Af,b,*S;
  int n,np,i;

  np=nar[1];n=nar[0];

  X=Rmatrix(Xd,(long)n,(long)np);
  p=Rmatrix(pd,(long)np,1L);
  y=Rmatrix(yd,(long)n,1L);
  w=Rmatrix(wd,(long)n,1L);
  if (nar[2]>0) Ain=Rmatrix(Aind,(long)nar[2],(long)np); else Ain.r=0L;
  if (nar[3]>0) Af=Rmatrix(Afd,(long)nar[3],(long)np); else Af.r=0L;
  if (nar[2]>0) b=Rmatrix(bd,(long)nar[2],1L);else b.r=0L;

  if (*m) S=(matrix *)CALLOC((size_t) *m,sizeof(matrix));
  else S=NULL;
  for (i=0;i< *m;i++) S[i]=initmat((long)dim[i],(long)dim[i]);
  RUnpackSarray(*m,S,Sd);

  PCLS(&X,&p,&y,&w,&Ain,&b,&Af,S,off,theta,*m,active);

  for (i=0;i<p.r;i++) pd[i]=p.V[i];

  for (i=0;i< *m;i++) freemat(S[i]);
  if (*m) FREE(S);

  freemat(X);freemat(p);freemat(y);freemat(w);

  if (Ain.r) freemat(Ain);
  if (Af.r) freemat(Af);
  if (b.r) freemat(b);

}

void GivensAddconQT(matrix *Q,matrix *T,matrix *a,matrix *s,matrix *c);

void PCLS(matrix *X,matrix *p,matrix *y,matrix *w,matrix *Ain,matrix *b,
          matrix *Af,matrix *S,int *off,double *theta,int m, int *active);

typedef struct
{ long constraints;
  double obj, obj_change;
  int converged;
} qpoutdatatype;

#include <math.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <R.h>
#include "mgcv.h"

void boundary(int *G, double *d, double *dto, double *x0, double *y0, double *dx, double *dy,
              int *nx, int *ny, double *x, double *y,double *break_code, int *n, int *nb)

{ int segi,j,j0,j1,k,kk,i,reversed,*inb,*ip,*ip1,*ip2,bnd_count,ii,out_lim;
  double x1,y1,x2,y2,xb0,yb0,xl,yl,xc,yc,dist,dist_to,grad=0.0,b,len2,*p1,*p2;

  p1 = d;p2 = dto;
  for (x1 = *x0,i=0;i<*nx;i++,x1 += *dx) {
    for (y1 = *y0,j=0;j<*ny;j++,y1 += *dy,p1++,p2++) {
      *p1 = x1;*p2 = y1;
    }
  }
  k = *nx * *ny;
  out_lim = -k;
  inb = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)k,sizeof(int));
  in_out(x,y,break_code,d,dto,inb,n,&k);
  j = -(k + 10);
  for (ip = inb,ip1 = G,p2 = dto,ip2=G+k;ip1<ip2;ip1++,p2++,ip++) {
    if (!*ip) *ip1 = j; else *ip1 = 1;
    *p2 = -1.0;
  }
  FREE(inb);

  xb0 = *x0 - *dx/2;yb0 = *y0 - *dx/2;

  dist = 0.0;
  bnd_count = 0;
  nb[0] = 0;
  ii = 0;
  for (segi=1;segi<*n;segi++) {

    if (x[segi-1]<x[segi]) {
      x1=x[segi-1];x2=x[segi];
      y1=y[segi-1];y2=y[segi];
      reversed = 0;
    } else {
      x1=x[segi];x2=x[segi-1];
      y1=y[segi];y2=y[segi-1];
      reversed = 1;
    }

    j0 = (int) ceil( (x1 - xb0) / *dx);
    j1 = (int) floor( (x2 - xb0) / *dx);

    if (x2-x1>0) grad = (y2-y1)/(x2-x1); else j1=j0-1;

    for (j=j0;j<=j1;j++) {
      xl = xb0 + j * *dx;
      yl = y1 + (xl - x1)*grad;
      k = (int) floor(( yl - yb0)/ *dy);

      kk = (j-1) * *ny + k;
      if (G[kk]>0||G[kk]< out_lim) {
        G[kk] = -ii;
        ii++;
        nb[bnd_count]++;
      }
      kk += *ny;
      if (G[kk]>0||G[kk]< out_lim) {
        G[kk] = -ii;
        ii++;
        nb[bnd_count]++;
      }

      for (i=0;i<2;i++) {
        xl = x2-x1;yl=y2-y1;
        xc = (j-i) * *dx + *x0;
        yc = k * *dy + *y0;
        xc -= x1;yc -= y1;
        len2 = yl*yl + xl*xl;
        b = (xc*xl + yc*yl)/len2;
        xl = xl*b+x1;yl = yl * b + y1;

        if (xl < x1) {xl = x1;yl = y1;}
        if (xl > x2) {xl = x2;yl = y2;}

        dist_to = sqrt((xl-xc)*(xl-xc) + (yl-yc)*(yl-yc));
        kk = (j-i) * *ny + k;

        if (dist_to < dto[kk] || dto[kk]<0) {
          dto[kk] = dist_to;
          xl -= x1; yl -= y1;
          if (reversed)
	    d[-G[kk]] = dist + sqrt(len2) - sqrt(xl*xl + yl*yl);
          else
            d[-G[kk]] = dist + sqrt(xl*xl + yl*yl);
	}

      }
    }

     if (y[segi-1]<y[segi]) {
      x1=x[segi-1];x2=x[segi];
      y1=y[segi-1];y2=y[segi];
      reversed=0;
    } else {
      x1=x[segi];x2=x[segi-1];
      y1=y[segi];y2=y[segi-1];
      reversed=1;
    }

    j0 = (int) ceil( (y1 - yb0) / *dy);
    j1 = (int) floor( (y2 - yb0) / *dy);

    if (y2-y1>0) grad = (x2-x1)/(y2-y1); else j1=j0-1;

    for (j=j0;j<=j1;j++) {
      yl = yb0 + j * *dy;
      xl = x1 + (yl - y1)*grad;
      k = (int) floor(( xl - xb0)/ *dx);

      kk = k * *ny + j - 1;
      if (G[kk]>0||G[kk]< out_lim) {G[kk] = -ii;ii++;nb[bnd_count]++;}
      kk ++;
      if (G[kk]>0||G[kk]< out_lim) {G[kk] = -ii;ii++;nb[bnd_count]++;}

      for (i=0;i<2;i++) {
        xl = x2-x1;yl=y2-y1;
        yc = (j-i) * *dy + *y0;
        xc = k * *dx + *x0;
        xc -= x1;yc -= y1;
        len2 = yl*yl + xl*xl;
        b = (xc*xl + yc*yl)/len2;
        xl = xl*b+x1;yl = yl * b + y1;

        if (yl < y1) {xl = x1;yl = y1;}
        if (yl > y2) {xl = x2;yl = y2;}

        dist_to = sqrt((xl-xc)*(xl-xc) + (yl-yc)*(yl-yc));
        kk = k * *ny + j-i;

        if (dist_to < dto[kk] || dto[kk]<0) {
          dto[kk] = dist_to;
          xl -= x1; yl -= y1;
          if (reversed)
	    d[-G[kk]] = dist + sqrt(len2) - sqrt(xl*xl + yl*yl);
          else
            d[-G[kk]] = dist + sqrt(xl*xl + yl*yl);
	}

      }
    }

    x2 = x2-x1;y2=y2-y1;
    dist += sqrt(x2*x2+y2*y2);

    if (segi < *n - 1 && x[segi+1] <= *break_code) {
      dist = 0.0;
      segi++;segi++;
      bnd_count++;
      if (segi < *n) nb[bnd_count] = 0;
    }
  }

  k = *nx * *ny;for (i=ii;i<k;i++) d[i] = -1;

  for (ip1 = G,ip2 = G + k;ip1<ip2;ip1++) if (*ip1 > 0) {*ip1 = ii;ii++;}

}

void pde_coeffs(int *G,double *x,int *ii,int *jj,int *n,int *nx,int *ny,double *dx,double *dy) {

  int i,j,*ip,outside,Gk0,Gk1,k0,k1;
  double xc,dx2,dy2,thresh=0.0;
  thresh = dx2= 1.0/(*dx * *dx);dy2 = 1.0/(*dy * *dy);
  if (dy2 < thresh) thresh = dy2;
  thresh *= .5;
  outside = - *nx * *ny - 1;
  *n=0;
  for (ip=G,i=0;i<*nx;i++) for (j=0;j<*ny;j++,ip++) if (*ip > outside){
    if (*ip <= 0) {
      *x=1.0;*jj = *ii= - *ip;
      x++;ii++;jj++;*n += 1;
    } else {
      xc=0.0;
      if (i>0&&i< *nx-1) {
         k0 = (i-1) * *ny + j;
         k1 = k0 + 2 * *ny;
         Gk0 = G[k0];Gk1 = G[k1];
         if (Gk0 > outside && Gk1 > outside) {
           xc += 2*dx2;
           if (Gk0<0) Gk0 = -Gk0;
           *x = -dx2;*ii = *ip;*jj = Gk0;
           x++;ii++;jj++;*n += 1;
           if (Gk1<0) Gk1 = -Gk1;
           *x = -dx2;*ii = *ip;*jj = Gk1;
           x++;ii++;jj++;*n += 1;
         }
      }
      if (j>0&&j< *ny-1) {
         k0 = i * *ny + j - 1;
         k1 = k0 + 2;
         Gk0 = G[k0];Gk1 = G[k1];
         if (Gk0 > outside && Gk1 > outside) {
           xc += 2*dy2;
           if (Gk0<0) Gk0 = -Gk0;
           *x = -dy2;*ii = *ip;*jj = Gk0;
           x++;ii++;jj++;*n += 1;
           if (Gk1<0) Gk1 = -Gk1;
           *x = -dy2;*ii = *ip;*jj = Gk1;
           x++;ii++;jj++;*n += 1;
         }
         if (xc > thresh) {
           *x = xc;*ii = *jj = *ip;
           x++;ii++;jj++;*n += 1;
         }
      }
    }
  }
}

void gridder(double *z,double *x,double *y,int *n,double *g, int *G,int *nx, int *ny,double *x0,
             double *y0,double *dx,double *dy,double NA_code) {

  int i,ix,iy,ok,Gthresh,Gk,k,ok00,ok01,ok10,ok11;
  double xx,yy,xx0,yy0,dmax,xa,ya,g00=0.0,g01=0.0,g10=0.0,g11=0.0,b0,b1,b2,b3,dist,d1;
  dmax = (*dx * *dx + *dy * *dy)*2;
  xx0 = *x0;yy0 = *y0;
  Gthresh = - *nx * *ny;
  for (i=0;i < *n;i++) {
    xx = x[i];yy = y[i];
    ix = (int) floor((xx - xx0) / *dx);
    iy = (int) floor((yy - yy0) / *dy);
    k = ix * *ny + iy;
    ok = 0;

    if (ix<0||ix>=*nx||iy<0||iy>=*ny) ok00 = 0; else {
      Gk=G[k];
      if (Gk < Gthresh) ok00 = 0; else {
	ok00 = 1; ok++;
	if (Gk < 0) Gk = -Gk;
        g00 = g[Gk];
      }
    }
    iy++;k++;
    if (ix<0||ix>=*nx||iy<0||iy>=*ny) ok01 = 0; else {
      Gk=G[k];
      if (Gk < Gthresh) ok01 = 0; else {
	ok01 = 1; ok++;
	if (Gk < 0) Gk = -Gk;
        g01 = g[Gk];
      }
    }
    ix++; k += *ny;
    if (ix<0||ix>=*nx||iy<0||iy>=*ny) ok11 = 0; else {
      Gk=G[k];
      if (Gk < Gthresh) ok11 = 0; else {
	ok11 = 1; ok++;
	if (Gk < 0) Gk = -Gk;
        g11 = g[Gk];
      }
    }
    iy--;k--;
    if (ix<0||ix>=*nx||iy<0||iy>=*ny) ok10 = 0; else {
      Gk=G[k];
      if (Gk < Gthresh) ok10 = 0; else {
	ok10 = 1; ok++;
	if (Gk < 0) Gk = -Gk;
        g10 = g[Gk];
      }
    }
    ix--;

    if (ok==4) {
      b0 = g00;
      b1 = (g10-g00) / *dx;
      b2 = (g01-g00) / *dy;
      b3 = (g11-g10-g01+g00)/( *dx * *dy);
      xx = xx - xx0 - ix * *dx;
      yy = yy - yy0 - iy * *dy;

      z[i] = b0 + b1 * xx + b2 * yy + b3 * xx * yy;

    } else if (!ok) {
      z[i] = NA_code;
    } else {
      xa = xx - xx0 - ix * *dx;
      ya = yy - yy0 - iy * *dy;
      dist = dmax;
      if (ok00) {
        dist = xa*xa + ya*ya;
        z[i] = g00;
      }
      if (ok01) {
        ya = *dy - ya;
	d1 = xa*xa + ya*ya;
        if (d1 < dist) {
          dist=d1;
          z[i] = g01;
        }
      }
      if (ok11) {
        xa = *dx - xa;
	d1 = xa*xa + ya*ya;
        if (d1 < dist) {
          dist=d1;
          z[i] = g11;
        }
      }
      if (ok10) {
        ya = *dy - ya;
	d1 = xa*xa + ya*ya;
        if (d1 < dist) {
          z[i] = g10;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

# --- Start of mgcv/src/sparse-smooth.c ---

#include <R.h>
#include <Rdefines.h>
#include <Rinternals.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include "mgcv.h"
#include "general.h"

void kd_sizes(kdtree_type kd,int *ni,int *nd) {

  *nd = 1 + kd.d * kd.n_box * 2;
  *ni = 3 +
    2 * kd.n +
    5 * kd.n_box;
}

void kd_dump(kdtree_type kd,int *idat,double *ddat) {

  int *p,*p0,*p1,i,nb,d,*pc1,*pc2,*pp,n;
  double *pd,*pd1;
  nb = idat[0] = kd.n_box;
  d = idat[1] = kd.d;
  n = idat[2] = kd.n;
  *ddat = kd.huge;ddat++;

  for (p=idat+3,p0=kd.ind,p1=p0+n;p0<p1;p++,p0++) *p = *p0;

  for (p0=kd.rind,p1=p0+n;p0<p1;p++,p0++) *p = *p0;

  pp = idat + 3 + 2 * n;
  pc1 = pp + nb;
  pc2 = pc1 + nb;
  p0 = pc2 + nb;
  p1 = p0 + nb;
  for (i=0;i<nb;i++) {

    for (pd=kd.box[i].lo,pd1=pd+d;pd<pd1;pd++,ddat++) *ddat = *pd;

    for (pd=kd.box[i].hi,pd1=pd+d;pd<pd1;pd++,ddat++) *ddat = *pd;
    *pp = kd.box[i].parent;pp++;
    *pc1 = kd.box[i].child1;pc1++;
    *pc2 = kd.box[i].child2;pc2++;
    *p0 = kd.box[i].p0;p0++;
    *p1 = kd.box[i].p1;p1++;
  }
}

void kd_read(kdtree_type *kd,int *idat,double *ddat,int new_mem) {

  int nb,d,*pp,*pc1,*pc2,*p0,*p1,i,n;
  box_type *box;
  double *ddat_in,*pd,*pd1;
  nb = kd->n_box = idat[0];
  d = kd->d = idat[1];
  n = kd->n = idat[2];
  kd->huge = *ddat;ddat++;
  if (new_mem) {
    kd->ind = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)n,sizeof(int));
    for (pp=kd->ind,p0=pp+n,p1=idat+3;pp<p0;pp++,p1++) *pp = *p1;
    kd->rind = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)n,sizeof(int));
    for (pp=kd->rind,p0=pp+n;pp<p0;pp++,p1++) *pp = *p1;
    ddat_in = ddat;
    ddat = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) (d * nb * 2),sizeof(double));
    for (pd=ddat,pd1=pd+d*nb*2;pd<pd1;pd++,ddat_in++) *pd = *ddat_in;
  } else {
    kd->ind = idat + 3;
    kd->rind = idat + 3 + n;
  }

  kd->box = (box_type *)CALLOC((size_t)nb,sizeof(box_type));

  pp = idat + 3 + 2*n;
  pc1 = pp + nb;
  pc2 = pc1 + nb;
  p0 = pc2 + nb;
  p1 = p0 + nb;
  box = kd->box;
  for (i=0;i<nb;i++,box++) {
    box->lo = ddat;ddat += d;
    box->hi = ddat;ddat += d;
    box->parent = *pp;pp++;
    box->child1 = *pc1;pc1++;
    box->child2 = *pc2;pc2++;
    box->p0 = *p0;p0++;
    box->p1 = *p1;p1++;
  }
}

void kd_sanity(kdtree_type kd) {
  int ok=1,i,*count,n=0;
  for (i=0;i<kd.n_box;i++) if (kd.box[i].p1>n) n = kd.box[i].p1;
  count = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)n,sizeof(int));
  for (i=0;i<kd.n_box;i++) if (!kd.box[i].child1) {
    if (kd.box[i].p1-kd.box[i].p0>1) { Rprintf("More than 2 points in a box!!\n");ok=0;}
    count[kd.box[i].p0]++;
    if (kd.box[i].p1!=kd.box[i].p0) count[kd.box[i].p1]++;
  }
  for (i=0;i<n;i++) {
    if (count[i]!=1) { Rprintf("point %d in %d boxes!\n",i,count[i]);ok=0;}
  }
  if (ok) Rprintf("kd tree sanity checks\n");
  FREE(count);
}

void k_order(int *k,int *ind,double *x,int *n) {

  int l,r,m,ip,ri,li,dum;
  double xp;
  l = 0;
  r = *n-1;
  while (1) {
    if (r > l+1) {
      m = (l+r) / 2;

      dum = ind[l+1];ind[l+1] = ind[m];ind[m] = dum;

      if (x[ind[l]] > x[ind[r]]) {
        dum = ind[r];ind[r] = ind[l];ind[l] = dum;
      }
      if (x[ind[l]] > x[ind[l+1]]) {
        dum = ind[l];ind[l] = ind[l+1];ind[l+1] = dum;
      }
      else if (x[ind[l+1]] > x[ind[r]]) {
        dum = ind[l+1];ind[l+1] = ind[r];ind[r] = dum;
      }
      ip = ind[l+1];
      xp = x[ip];

      ri = r;

      li = l+1;
      while (1) {

        li++;ri--;
        while(x[ind[li]] < xp) li++;
        while(x[ind[ri]] > xp) ri--;
        if (ri < 0) Rprintf("ri<0!!\n");
        if (li >= *n) Rprintf("li >= n!!\n");
        if (ri<li) break;
        dum = ind[ri];ind[ri] = ind[li];ind[li] = dum;
      }

      ind[l+1] = ind[ri];
      ind[ri] = ip;

      if (ri >= *k ) r = ri - 1;
      if (ri <= *k ) l = li;
   } else {
      if (r == l+1 && x[ind[r]] < x[ind[l]]) {
         dum = ind[r];ind[r] = ind[l];ind[l] = dum;
      }
      return;
    }
  }
}

void free_kdtree(kdtree_type kd) {

  FREE(kd.ind);FREE(kd.rind);
  FREE(kd.box[0].lo);
  FREE(kd.box);
}

void kd_tree(double *X,int *n, int *d,kdtree_type *kd) {

  int *ind,*rind,*p,i,m,todo[50],todo_d[50],item,bi,nb,np,k,dim,b,p0,p1;
  box_type *box;
  double huge=1e100,*pd,*x,*dum1,*dum2,*dum3;

  ind = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(int));
  for (i=0,p=ind;i < *n;i++,p++) *p = i;

  m=2;while (m < *n) m *= 2;
  nb = *n * 2 - m / 2 - 1;
  if (nb > m-1) nb = m - 1;

  box = (box_type *)CALLOC((size_t)nb,sizeof(box_type));

  pd = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)nb * (2 * *d),sizeof(double));
  for (i=0;i<nb;i++) {
    box[i].lo = pd;pd += *d;
    box[i].hi = pd;pd += *d;
  }

  for (i=0;i< *d;i++) {
    box[0].lo[i] = -huge;box[0].hi[i] = huge;
  }
  box[0].p1 = *n-1;
  todo[0]=0;
  todo_d[0]=0;
  item=0;

  bi=0;
  while (item >= 0) {
    b = todo[item];
    dim = todo_d[item];
    p0 = box[b].p0;p1=box[b].p1;
    np = p1-p0+1;
    x = X + dim * *n;
    k = (np-1)/2;

    k_order(&k,ind+p0,x,&np);

    item--;

    bi++;

    if (bi>nb-1) Rprintf("too many boxes!!");
    box[b].child1=bi;

    for (dum1=box[bi].lo,dum2=dum1 + *d,dum3=box[b].lo;dum1<dum2;dum1++,dum3++) *dum1 = *dum3;
    for (dum1=box[bi].hi,dum2=dum1 + *d,dum3=box[b].hi;dum1<dum2;dum1++,dum3++) *dum1 = *dum3;
    box[bi].hi[dim] = x[ind[p0+k]];
    box[bi].parent=b;
    box[bi].p0=box[b].p0;
    box[bi].p1=box[b].p0+k;
    if (k>1) {
      item++;
      todo[item] = bi;
      todo_d[item] = dim+1;
      if (todo_d[item] == *d) todo_d[item] = 0;
    }
    bi++;
    if (bi>nb-1) Rprintf("too many boxes!!");
    box[b].child2=bi;

    for (dum1=box[bi].lo,dum2=dum1 + *d,dum3=box[b].lo;dum1<dum2;dum1++,dum3++) *dum1 = *dum3;
    for (dum1=box[bi].hi,dum2=dum1 + *d,dum3=box[b].hi;dum1<dum2;dum1++,dum3++) *dum1 = *dum3;
    box[bi].lo[dim] = x[ind[p0+k]];
    box[bi].parent=b;
    box[bi].p1=box[b].p1;
    box[bi].p0=box[b].p0+k+1;
    if (np-k>3) {
      item++;
      todo[item] = bi;
      todo_d[item] = dim+1;
      if (todo_d[item] == *d) todo_d[item] = 0;
    }
  }
  if (bi!=nb-1) Rprintf("bi not equal to nb-1 %d %d\n",bi,nb-1);
  rind = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(int));

  for (i=0;i<*n;i++) rind[ind[i]]=i;

  kd->box = box;kd->ind = ind;kd->rind = rind;kd->n_box = nb;kd->huge = huge;
  kd->d = *d;kd->n = *n;
}

void Rkdtree0(double *X,int *n, int *d,int *idat,double *ddat) {

  kdtree_type kd;
  kd_tree(X,n,d,&kd);
  kd_dump(kd,idat,ddat);
  free_kdtree(kd);
}

static void kdFinalizer(SEXP ptr) {
  kdtree_type *kd;
  kd = (kdtree_type *) R_ExternalPtrAddr(ptr);

  free_kdtree(*kd);

  FREE(kd);
}

SEXP Rkdtree(SEXP x) {

  kdtree_type *kd;
  double *X,*ddat;
  int *dim,n,d,ni,nd,*idat;
  SEXP DIM, ans, ptr, ansi;
  static SEXP kd_symb = NULL,kd_symbi;
  if (!kd_symb) kd_symb = install("kd_ptr");
  if (!kd_symbi) kd_symbi = install("kd_ind");
  X = REAL(x);
  DIM = getAttrib(x, install("dim"));
  dim=INTEGER(DIM);
  n=dim[0];d=dim[1];
  kd = (kdtree_type *) CALLOC((size_t)1,sizeof(kdtree_type));
  kd_tree(X,&n,&d,kd);
  kd_sizes(*kd,&ni,&nd);

  ans = PROTECT(allocVector(REALSXP, nd));
  ansi = PROTECT(allocVector(INTSXP, ni));
  idat = INTEGER(ansi);
  ddat = REAL(ans);
  kd_dump(*kd,idat,ddat);

  ptr = R_MakeExternalPtr(kd,R_NilValue, R_NilValue);
  PROTECT(ptr);

  R_RegisterCFinalizerEx(ptr, kdFinalizer, TRUE);

  setAttrib(ans, kd_symb, ptr);
  setAttrib(ans, kd_symbi, ansi);
  UNPROTECT(3);
  return ans;
}

void update_heap(double *h,int *ind,int n) {

  double h0;
  int i,i0,ind0;
  h0 = h[0];
  ind0 = ind[0];
  i0 = 0;
  i = 1;
  while (i < n) {
    if (i < n-1&&h[i]<h[i+1]) i++;
    if (h0 > h[i]) break;

    h[i0] = h[i];
