    ind[i0] = ind[i];
    i0 = i;
    i = 2*i+1;
  }
  h[i0] = h0;
  ind[i0] = ind0;
}

double box_dist(box_type *box,double *x,int d) {

  double d2 = 0.0,z,*bl,*bh,*xd;
  for (xd=x+d,bl=box->lo,bh=box->hi; x < xd;x++,bl++,bh++) {
    if (*x < *bl) { z = *x - *bl;d2 += z*z;}
    if (*x > *bh) { z = *x - *bh;d2 += z*z;}
  }
  return(sqrt(d2));
}

int which_box(kdtree_type *kd,int j) {

  int i,bi,b1;
  i = kd->rind[j];
  bi=0;

  while (kd->box[bi].child1) {

    b1 = kd->box[bi].child1;
    if (kd->box[b1].p1>=i) bi = b1;
    else bi = kd->box[bi].child2;
  }
  return(bi);
}

int xbox(kdtree_type *kd,double *x) {

  int bi,d,b1;
  box_type *box;
  bi=0;
  box = kd->box;
  d=0;
  while (box[bi].child1) {
    b1 = box[bi].child1;
    if (box[b1].hi[d]!=box[box[bi].child2].lo[d]) Rprintf("child boundary problem\n");

    if (x[d] <= box[b1].hi[d]) bi = b1; else
    bi = box[bi].child2;
    d++; if (d == kd->d) d=0;
  }
  return(bi);
}

double ijdist(int i, int j, double *X,int n,int d) {

  double *pi,*pj,*pil,dist=0.0,x;
  for (pi=X+i,pil=pi+n*d,pj=X+j;pi<pil;pi+=n,pj+=n) {x = *pi - *pj;dist += x*x;}
  return(sqrt(dist));
}

double xidist(double *x,double *X,int i,int d, int n) {

  double dist=0.0,z;
  int j;
  for (j=0;j<d;j++) {
    z = x[j] - X[j*n+i];
    dist += z*z;
  }
  return(sqrt(dist));
}

int closest(kdtree_type *kd, double *X,double *x,int n,int *ex,int nex) {

  int bx,ni,i,j,k,d,todo[100],bi,*ind,item,ok=0;
  double nd,d1,dix;
  box_type *box;
  if (nex<0) nex=0;
  nd = kd->huge;
  bx = xbox(kd,x);

  d = kd->d;
  box = kd->box;
  ind = kd->ind;
  ni = -1;
  while (ni<0) {
    for (j=box[bx].p0;j<box[bx].p1;j++) {
      k = ind[j];

      ok = 1;for (i=0;i<nex;i++) if (k == ex[i]) {ok = 0;break;}
      if (ok) {
        d1 = xidist(x,X,k,d,n);
        if (d1<nd) { nd=d1;ni=k;}
      }
    }
    if (ni < 0&& bx!=0) bx = box[bx].parent;
  }

  todo[0] = 0;
  item = 0;

  while (item>=0) {
    if (todo[item]==bx) {
       item--;
    } else {
      bi = todo[item];
      item--;
      if (box_dist(box+bi,x,d)<nd) {

        if (box[bi].child1) {
           item++;
           todo[item] = box[bi].child1;
           item++;
           todo[item] = box[bi].child2;
        } else {
          for (j=box[bi].p0;j<=box[bi].p1;j++) {
            k = ind[j];

            ok = 1;for (i=0;i<nex;i++) if (k == ex[i]) {ok = 0;break;}
            if (ok) {
              dix = xidist(x,X,k,d,n);
              if (dix<nd) {
                nd = dix;
                ni = k;
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return(ni);
}

void p_area(double *a,double *X,kdtree_type kd,int n,int d) {

  double *wa,*lo,*hi,*x0,*x1,min_w,x;
  int np,bi,i,j,k,ok=1,*count,check;

  wa = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)d,sizeof(double));
  lo = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)d,sizeof(double));
  hi = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)d,sizeof(double));
  x0 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)d,sizeof(double));
  x1 = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)d,sizeof(double));
  count = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)d,sizeof(int));

  for (bi=0;bi<kd.n_box;bi++) {
    for (j=0;j<d;j++) {
      if (kd.box[bi].lo[j]!= - kd.huge && kd.box[bi].hi[j]!= kd.huge) {
        count[j]++; wa[j] += kd.box[bi].hi[j] - kd.box[bi].lo[j];
      }
    }
  }
  for (j=0;j<d;j++) wa[j] /= count[j];

  for (i=0;i<n;i++) {
    bi = which_box(&kd,i);
    for (j=0;j<d;j++) {
      lo[j] = kd.box[bi].lo[j];
      if (lo[j]==-kd.huge) ok = 0;
      hi[j] = kd.box[bi].hi[j];
      if (hi[j]==kd.huge) ok = 0;
    }
    np = kd.box[bi].p1-kd.box[bi].p0+1;
    if (!ok) {
      check = 0;
      k = kd.ind[kd.box[bi].p0];
      if (k==i) check=1;
      for (j=0;j<d;j++) x0[j] = X[k + j * n];
      if (np>1) {
        k = kd.ind[kd.box[bi].p1];
        if (k==i) check=1;
        for (j=0;j<d;j++) x1[j] = X[k + j * n];
      }
      if (!check) Rprintf("indexing error in p_area!\n");

      ok=1; min_w = -1.0;
      for (j=0;j<d;j++) {
        if (lo[j] == -kd.huge) {
           x = x0[j]; if (np>1 && x1[j]<x) x = x1[j];
           if (x < hi[j]) lo[j] = x;
           else ok=0;
        }
        if (hi[j] == kd.huge) {
           x = x0[j]; if (np>1 && x1[j]>x) x = x1[j];
           if (x > lo[j]) hi[j] = x;
           else ok=0;
        }
        if (lo[j] != -kd.huge && hi[j] != kd.huge) {
           x = hi[j]-lo[j];
           if (min_w < 0 || x < min_w) min_w = x;
        }
      }
      if (!ok) {
        for (j=0;j<d;j++) {
          if (lo[j] == -kd.huge) {
            x = x0[j]; if (np>1 && x1[j]<x) x = x1[j];
            if (min_w>0) x -= min_w; else x -= wa[j];
            lo[j] = x;
          }
          if (hi[j] == kd.huge) {
            x = x0[j]; if (np>1 && x1[j]>x) x = x1[j];
            if (min_w>0) x += min_w; else x += wa[j];
            hi[j] = x;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    for (x=1.0,j=0;j<d;j++) x*= hi[j]-lo[j];
    x /= np;
    a[i] = x;
  }
  FREE(count);
  FREE(x0);FREE(x1);FREE(lo);FREE(hi);FREE(wa);
}

void k_radius(double r, kdtree_type kd, double *X,double *x,int *list,int *nlist) {

  int todo[100],item,bi,bi_old,dim,d,c1,c2,*ind,n,i;
  box_type *box;
  box = kd.box;
  d = kd.d;
  n = kd.n;
  ind = kd.ind;
  *nlist = 0;
  bi = 0;
  dim = 0;

  while (box[bi].child1) {
    bi_old = bi;
    c1 = box[bi].child1;c2 = box[bi].child2;

    if (x[dim]+r <= box[c1].hi[dim]) bi = c1;
    else if (x[dim]-2 >= box[c2].lo[dim]) bi = c2;
    dim++; if (dim==d) dim = 0;
    if (bi==bi_old) break;
  }

  item=0;
  todo[0] = bi;
  while (item>=0) {
    bi = todo[item];item--;
    if (box_dist(box+bi,x,d) < r) {
      if (box[bi].child1) {
        item++;todo[item] = box[bi].child1;
        item++;todo[item] = box[bi].child2;
      } else {
        for (i=box[bi].p0;i<=box[bi].p1;i++) {
          if (xidist(x,X,ind[i],d,n) < r) {
            list[*nlist] = ind[i]; (*nlist)++;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

SEXP Rkradius(SEXP kdr,SEXP Xr,SEXP xr,SEXP rr,SEXP offr) {

  double *X,*x,*r,*xx,*ddat;
  kdtree_type *kd;
  int *dim,m,d,*off,*nei,*list,nn,i,j,n_buff=0,nlist,*ni,nprot=1,*idat;
  SEXP DIM,ptr,neir,IDAT;
  static SEXP kd_symb = NULL, dim_sym = NULL,kd_symbi=NULL;
  if (!dim_sym) dim_sym = install("dim");
  if (!kd_symbi) kd_symbi = install("kd_ind");
  if (!kd_symb) kd_symb = install("kd_ptr");
  DIM = getAttrib(xr, dim_sym);
  dim = INTEGER(DIM); m = dim[1];

  X = REAL(Xr);x = REAL(xr);
  r = REAL(rr);
  ptr = getAttrib(kdr, kd_symb);
  kd = (kdtree_type *) R_ExternalPtrAddr(ptr);
  if (!kd) {

    IDAT = getAttrib(kdr, kd_symbi);
    idat = INTEGER(IDAT);
    ddat = REAL(kdr);
    kd = (kdtree_type *) CALLOC((size_t)1,sizeof(kdtree_type));
    kd_read(kd,idat,ddat,1);

    ptr = R_MakeExternalPtr(kd,R_NilValue, R_NilValue);
    PROTECT(ptr);

    R_RegisterCFinalizerEx(ptr, kdFinalizer, TRUE);

    setAttrib(kdr, kd_symb, ptr);
    nprot++;

  }
  d = kd->d;
  off = INTEGER(offr);

  list = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)kd->n,sizeof(int));
  n_buff = kd->n*10;
  nei = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)n_buff,sizeof(int));
  xx=x;nn=0;off[0]=0;
  for (i=0;i<m;i++) {
    k_radius(*r, *kd, X,xx,list,&nlist);
    if (nn+nlist>n_buff) {
      n_buff *= 2;
      nei = (int *)R_chk_realloc(nei,(size_t)n_buff*sizeof(int));
    }
    for (j=nn;j<nn+nlist;j++) nei[j] = list[j-nn];
    nn += nlist;
    off[i+1] = nn;
    xx += d;
  }
  neir = PROTECT(allocVector(INTSXP, nn));
  ni = INTEGER(neir);
  for (dim=nei;dim<nei+nn;dim++,ni++) *ni = *dim;
  FREE(list);FREE(nei);
  UNPROTECT(nprot);
  return neir;
}

void Rkradius0(double *r,int *idat,double *ddat,double *X,double *x,int *m,int *off,int *ni,int *op) {

  static int *nei,nn;
  kdtree_type kd;
  double *xx;
  int d,i,j,n_buff=0,nlist,*list;
  if (*op) {
    for (i=0;i<nn;i++) ni[i]=nei[i];
    FREE(nei);nn=0;
    return;
  }
  kd_read(&kd,idat,ddat,0);
  d = kd.d;

  list = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)kd.n,sizeof(int));
  n_buff = kd.n*10;
  nei = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)n_buff,sizeof(int));
  xx=x;nn=0;off[0]=0;
  for (i=0;i<*m;i++) {
    k_radius(*r, kd, X,xx,list,&nlist);
    if (nn+nlist>n_buff) {
      n_buff *= 2;
      nei = (int *)R_chk_realloc(nei,(size_t)n_buff*sizeof(int));
    }
    for (j=nn;j<nn+nlist;j++) nei[j] = list[j-nn];
    nn += nlist;
    off[i+1] = nn;
    xx += d;
  }
  FREE(list);
  FREE(kd.box);
}

void k_newn_work(double *Xm,kdtree_type kd,double *X,double *dist,int *ni,int*m,int *n,int *d,int *k) {

  int i,j,bi,*ik,bii,todo[100],item,pcount,*ind;
  box_type *box;
  double *dk,huge,*p,*p1,*p2,dij,*x;

  huge = kd.huge;
  ind = kd.ind;
  box = kd.box;

  dk = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*k,sizeof(double));
  ik = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)*k,sizeof(int));
  x = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*d,sizeof(double));
  pcount=0;

  for (i=0;i < *m;i++) {
    for (p=Xm+i,p1=x,p2=p1 + *d;p1<p2;p1++, p+= *m) *p1 = *p;
    for (p=dk,p1=dk + *k;p<p1;p++) *p = huge;

    bi = xbox(&kd,x);

    while (box[bi].p1-box[bi].p0 < *k) bi = box[bi].parent;

    for (j=box[bi].p0;j<=box[bi].p1;j++) {
      pcount++;

      dij = xidist(x,X,ind[j],*d,*n);
      if (dij<dk[0]) {
        dk[0] = dij;
        ik[0] = ind[j];
        if (*k>1) update_heap(dk,ik,*k);
      }
    }

    todo[0] = 0;
    item=0;
    bii = bi;
    while (item>=0) {
      if (todo[item]==bii) {
        item--;
      } else {
        bi = todo[item];
        item--;
        if (box_dist(box+bi,x,*d)<dk[0]) {

          if (box[bi].child1) {
            item++;
            todo[item] = box[bi].child1;
            item++;
            todo[item] = box[bi].child2;
          } else {
            for (j=box[bi].p0;j<=box[bi].p1;j++) {
              pcount++;

              dij = xidist(x,X,ind[j],*d,*n);
              if (dij<dk[0]) {
                dk[0] = dij;
                ik[0] = ind[j];
                if (*k>1) update_heap(dk,ik,*k);
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    for (j=0;j<*k;j++) {
      dist[i + j * *m] = dk[j];
      ni[i + j * *m] = ik[j];
    }
  }

  FREE(dk);
  FREE(ik);
  FREE(x);
  *n = pcount;
}

void Rkdnearest0(double *X,int *idat,double *ddat,int *n,double *x, int *m, int *ni, double *dist,int *k) {

  kdtree_type kd;
  int d;
  kd_read(&kd,idat,ddat,0);
  d = kd.d;

  k_newn_work(x,kd,X,dist,ni,m,n,&d,k);
  FREE(kd.box);
}

SEXP Rkdnearest(SEXP kdr,SEXP Xr,SEXP xr,SEXP kr) {

  double *X,*x,*dis,*ddat;
  kdtree_type *kd;
  int *dim,n,m,d,*k,*nei,*idat,nprot=2;
  SEXP DIM,ptr,neir,dir,IDAT;
  static SEXP kd_symb = NULL, dim_sym = NULL,dist_sym = NULL,kd_symbi=NULL;
  if (!dim_sym) dim_sym = install("dim");
  if (!dist_sym) dist_sym = install("dist");
  if (!kd_symb) kd_symb = install("kd_ptr");
  if (!kd_symbi) kd_symbi = install("kd_ind");
  DIM = getAttrib(Xr, dim_sym);
  dim = INTEGER(DIM); n = dim[0];
  DIM = getAttrib(xr, dim_sym);
  dim = INTEGER(DIM); m = dim[0];
  X = REAL(Xr);x = REAL(xr);
  k = INTEGER(kr);
  ptr = getAttrib(kdr, kd_symb);
  kd = (kdtree_type *) R_ExternalPtrAddr(ptr);
  if (!kd) {

    IDAT = getAttrib(kdr, kd_symbi);
    idat = INTEGER(IDAT);
    ddat = REAL(kdr);
    kd = (kdtree_type *) CALLOC((size_t)1,sizeof(kdtree_type));
    kd_read(kd,idat,ddat,1);
    ptr = R_MakeExternalPtr(kd,R_NilValue, R_NilValue);
    PROTECT(ptr);

    R_RegisterCFinalizerEx(ptr, kdFinalizer, TRUE);

    setAttrib(kdr, kd_symb, ptr);
    nprot++;
  }
  d = kd->d;
  neir = PROTECT(allocMatrix(INTSXP,m,*k));
  nei = INTEGER(neir);
  dir = PROTECT(allocMatrix(REALSXP,m,*k));
  dis = REAL(dir);
  k_newn_work(x,*kd,X,dis,nei,&m,&n,&d,k);
  setAttrib(neir, dist_sym,dir);
  UNPROTECT(nprot);
  return neir;
}

void k_nn_work(kdtree_type kd,double *X,double *dist,int *ni,int *n,int *d,int *k) {

  int i,j,bi,*ik,bii,todo[100],item,pcount,*ind;
  box_type *box;
  double *dk,huge,*p,*p1,*p2,dij,*x;

  huge = kd.huge;
  ind = kd.ind;
  box = kd.box;

  dk = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*k,sizeof(double));
  ik = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)*k,sizeof(int));
  x = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*d,sizeof(double));
  pcount=0;

  for (i=0;i < *n;i++) {
    for (p=X+i,p1=x,p2=p1 + *d;p1<p2;p1++, p+= *n) *p1 = *p;
    for (p=dk,p1=dk + *k;p<p1;p++) *p = huge;

    bi = which_box(&kd,i);

    while (box[bi].p1-box[bi].p0 < *k) bi = box[bi].parent;

    for (j=box[bi].p0;j<=box[bi].p1;j++)
    if (ind[j]!=i) {
      pcount++;
      dij = ijdist(i,ind[j],X,*n,*d);
      if (dij<dk[0]) {
        dk[0] = dij;
        ik[0] = ind[j];
        if (*k>1) update_heap(dk,ik,*k);
      }
    }

    todo[0] = 0;
    item=0;
    bii = bi;
    while (item>=0) {
      if (todo[item]==bii) {
        item--;
      } else {
        bi = todo[item];
        item--;
        if (box_dist(box+bi,x,*d)<dk[0]) {

          if (box[bi].child1) {
            item++;
            todo[item] = box[bi].child1;
            item++;
            todo[item] = box[bi].child2;
          } else {
            for (j=box[bi].p0;j<=box[bi].p1;j++) {
              pcount++;
              dij = ijdist(i,ind[j],X,*n,*d);
              if (dij<dk[0]) {
                dk[0] = dij;
                ik[0] = ind[j];
                if (*k>1) update_heap(dk,ik,*k);
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    for (j=0;j<*k;j++) {
      dist[i + j * *n] = dk[j];
      ni[i + j * *n] = ik[j];
    }
  }

  FREE(dk);
  FREE(ik);
  FREE(x);
  *n = pcount;
}

void k_nn(double *X,double *dist,double *a,int *ni,int *n,int *d,int *k,int *get_a) {

  kdtree_type kd;
  kd_tree(X,n,d,&kd);
  if (*get_a) p_area(a,X,kd,*n,*d);
  k_nn_work(kd,X,dist,ni,n,d,k);
  free_kdtree(kd);
}

void tri2nei(int *t,int *nt,int *n,int *d,int *off) {

  int i,j,k,l,ii,jj,*p,*p1,*nn,k0,k1;

  for (p=off,p1=off + *n;p<p1;p++) *p = 0;
  for (p=t,p1=t + *nt * (*d+1);p<p1;p++) off[*p] += *d;

  for (i=1;i< *n ;i++) off[i] += off[i-1];

  nn = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)off[*n-1],sizeof(int));
  for (p=nn,p1 = nn + off[*n-1];p<p1;p++) *p = -1;

  for (i=0;i<*nt;i++) {
    for (j=0;j<*d+1;j++) {
      ii = t[i + j * *nt];
      if (ii==0) k0=0; else k0=off[ii-1];
      k1=off[ii];

      for (l=0;l<*d+1;l++) if (l!=j) {
        jj = t[i + l * *nt];
	for (k=k0;k<k1;k++) {
          if (nn[k]<0) { nn[k] = jj;break;}
          if (nn[k]==jj) break;
	}
      }
    }
  }

  j= k0 = 0;
  for (i=0;i<*n;i++) {
    k1=off[i];
    for (k=k0;k<k1;k++) {
      if (nn[k]<0) break;
      t[j] = nn[k];j++;
    }
    off[i]=j;
    k0 = k1;
  }

  FREE(nn);
}

void ni_dist_filter(double *X,int *n,int *d,int *ni,int *off,double *mult) {

  int i,j,k,i0,i1;
  double *dist,z,z2,md=0.0;
  dist = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) off[*n-1],sizeof(double));

  i0 = 0;
  for (j=0;j<*n;j++) {
    i1 = off[j];

    for (i=i0;i<i1;i++) {
      z2=0.0;
      for (k=0;k<*d;k++) {
        z = (X[j + *n * k] - X[ni[i] + *n * k]);
        z2 += z*z;
      }
      dist[i] = sqrt(z2);
      md += dist[i];
    }
    i0=i1;
  }
  md/=i0;

  k = i0 = 0;
  for (j=0;j<*n;j++) {
    i1=off[j];
    for (i=i0;i<i1;i++) {
      if (dist[i]< md * *mult) {
	ni[k] = ni[i];k++;
      }
    }
    off[j]=k;
    i0 = i1;
  }
  FREE(dist);
}

void nei_penalty(double *X,int *n,int *d,double *D,int *ni,int *ii,int *off,
                                  int *m,int *a_weight,double *kappa) {

  int i,j,k,True=1,kk,l,i0,i1,max_nn=0,jj,di,doff;
  double *M,*Mi,*Vt,*sv,
    x,z;

  z = 10.0;
  ni_dist_filter(X,n,d,ni,off,&z);

  i0 = 0;
  for (j=0;j<*n;j++) {
    i1 = off[j];
    if (i1-i0>max_nn) max_nn = i1-i0;
    i0=i1;
  }
  max_nn++;
  if (max_nn<6) max_nn=6;

  M = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) 6 * max_nn,sizeof(double));
  Mi = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) 6 * max_nn,sizeof(double));
  Vt = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) 6 * 6,sizeof(double));
  sv = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) 6,sizeof(double));

  di = i0 = 0;
  doff = off[*n-1] + *n;
  for (j=0;j<*n;j++) {
    i1 = off[j];
    k = kk = i1-i0 + 1;
    if (kk<6) {
      kk=6;
      for (i=0;i<6*kk;i++) M[i]=0.0;
    }
    l=0;

    M[0] = 1.0;for (i=1;i<6;i++) M[i*kk] = 0.0;
    for (i=i0;i<i1;i++) {
      ii[i] = j;
      l++;
      jj = ni[i];
      x = X[jj] - X[j];
      z = X[jj + *n] - X[j + *n];
      M[l] = 1.0;
      M[l + kk] = x;
      M[l + 2*kk] = z;
      M[l + 3*kk] = x*x/2;
      M[l + 4*kk] = z*z/2;
      M[l + 5*kk] = x*z;
    }

    i = 6;
    mgcv_svd_full(M,Vt,sv,&kk,&i);

    jj = k; if (jj>6) jj=6;
    kappa[i] = sv[0]/sv[jj-1];

    for (i=0;i<jj;i++) if (sv[i]>sv[0]*1e-10) sv[i] = 1/sv[i]; else sv[i]=0.0;

    if (k<kk) {
      jj=0;
      for (i=0;i<6;i++) for (l=0;l<kk;l++)  if (l < k) { M[jj] = M[l + kk * i];jj++;}
      for (i=k;i<kk;i++) sv[i] = 0.0;
    }

    for (i=0;i<6;i++) {
      x=sv[i];
      for (l=0;l<k;l++) M[l+i*k] *= x;
    }
    i=6;
    mgcv_mmult(Mi,Vt,M,&True,&True,&i,&k,&i);

     x = 1.0;

    for (l=0;l<3;l++)
      D[j + doff * l] = Mi[3 + l];

    for (i=1;i<k;i++) {
      for (l=0;l<3;l++) D[di + doff * l + *n] = Mi[3 + l + 6 * i];
      di++;
    }
    i0=i1;
  }

  FREE(M);
  FREE(Mi);
  FREE(Vt);
  FREE(sv);
}

void QTz(int i,int j,double c,double s, double *z) {

  double temp;
  temp=z[i]*c+z[j]*s;
  z[j]=z[j]*c-z[i]*s;
  z[i]=temp;
}

void givens(double a, double b,double *c,double *s) {

  double t;
  if (a==0.0) { *c=1.0;*s=0.0;} else
  if (fabs(a)<=fabs(b))
  { t=a/b;
    *s=1/sqrt(1+t*t);
    *c= (*s)*t;
  } else
  { t=b/a;
    *c=1/sqrt(1+t*t);
    *s=(*c)*t;
  }
}

void ss_setup(double *ub,double *lb,double *x,double *w, int *n) {

  double *h,*hh,*hh1,*lb1,*ub2,*ub1;
  int i;
  h = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(double));
  hh=(double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(double));
  hh1=(double *)CALLOC((size_t) *n,sizeof(double));
  for (i=0;i< *n-1;i++) h[i]=x[i+1]-x[i];
  for (i=0;i< *n-2;i++) hh[i]=2.0*(h[i]+h[i+1])/3.0;
  for (i=0;i< *n-3;i++) hh1[i]=h[i+1]/3.0;

  lb[0] = sqrt(hh[0]);
  lb1 = lb + *n;

  for (i=1;i< *n-3;i++)
  { lb[i]= sqrt(hh[i]-lb1[i-1]*lb1[i-1]);
    lb1[i]= hh1[i]/lb[i];
  }
  lb[*n-3] = sqrt(hh[*n-3]-lb1[*n-4]*lb1[*n-4]);
  ub1 = ub + *n;ub2 = ub1 + *n;
  for (i=0;i< *n-2;i++)
  { ub[i] = w[i]/h[i];
    ub1[i] = -w[i+1]*(1/h[i]+1/h[i+1]);
    ub2[i] = w[i+2]/h[i+1];
  }
  FREE(h);FREE(hh);FREE(hh1);
}

void sspl_construct(double *lambda,double *x,double *w,double *U,double *V,
             double *diagA,double *lb,int *n,double *tol) {

  double *ub,rho,*p,*p1,*ub1,*ub2,*lb1,c,s,*U0s,*U0c,*U1s,*U1c,
    *V0s,*V0c,*V1s,*V1c,upper,w2=0.0,
    L11=0.0,L12=0.0,L13,L21,L22,L23,L31,L32,L33,X1,X2,X3,Lt,temp;
  int i,k,ok;

  k=0;ok=1;
  for (i=1;i<*n;i++) if (x[k] + *tol < x[i]) {
     if (!ok) { w[k] = sqrt(w2);}
     k++;
     x[k] = x[i];
     w[k] = w[i];ok=1;
   } else {
     if (ok) { w2 = w[k]*w[k];}
     w2 += w[i]*w[i];
     ok=0;
  }
  if (!ok) { w[k] = sqrt(w2);}
  *n = k+1;

  for (i=0;i<*n;i++) w[i] = 1/w[i];

  ub = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)(*n * 3),sizeof(double));
  ss_setup(ub,lb,x,w,n);
  rho = sqrt(*lambda);

  for (p = ub,p1 = ub + *n * 3;p < p1;p++) *p *= rho;

  ub1 = ub + *n;ub2 = ub1 + *n;
  lb1 = lb + *n;
  U0s = U;U0c = U + *n;
  U1s = U + *n * 2;U1c = U + *n *3;
  V0s = V;V0c = V + *n;
  V1s = V + *n * 2;V1c = V + *n *3;
  for (i=0;i<*n-3;i++)
  { givens(ub[i+1],lb1[i],&c,&s);
    temp = c*lb[i]-s*ub1[i];
    ub[i+1]=s*lb1[i]+c*ub[i+1];
    ub1[i]=s*lb[i]+c*ub1[i];
    lb[i]=temp;
    U1s[i] = -s; U1c[i] = c;

    givens(ub[i],lb[i],&c,&s);
    ub[i]=c*ub[i]+s*lb[i];
    U0s[i] = -s; U0c[i] = c;

    givens(ub[i],ub1[i],&c,&s);
    ub[i]=c*ub[i]+s*ub1[i];
    upper=s*ub[i+1];
    ub[i+1]=c*ub[i+1];
    V0s[i] = -s;V0c[i] = c;

    givens(ub[i],ub2[i],&c,&s);
    ub1[i+1]=c*ub1[i+1]-s*upper;
    if (i!=(*n-4)) ub[i+2]=c*ub[i+2];
    V1s[i] = -s; V1c[i] = c;

  }
  i = *n-3;
  givens(ub[i],lb[i],&c,&s);
  ub[i]=c*ub[i]+s*lb[i];
  U0s[i] = -s; U0c[i] = c;

  givens(ub[i],ub1[i],&c,&s);
  ub[i]=c*ub[i]+s*ub1[i];
  V0s[i] = -s;V0c[i] = c;

  givens(ub[i],ub2[i],&c,&s);

  V1s[i] = -s; V1c[i] = c;

  V0c += *n-3;V0s += *n-3;
  V1c += *n-3;V1s += *n-3;
  U0c += *n-3;U0s += *n-3;
  U1c += *n-3;U1s += *n-3;

  L31 = *V1c;L33 = - *V1s;
  L32 = -L31 * *V0s;L31 = L31 * *V0c;

  X3 = -L31 * *U0s;
  L31 = L31 * *U0c;

  diagA[*n-1] = L33*L33;

  L33 = L32;L32=L31;

  V0c--;V0s--;V1c--;V1s--;U0c--;U0s--;U1c--;U1s--;

  L21 = *V1c;L23 = - *V1s;
  L31 = L33 * *V1s;
  L33 = L33 * *V1c;
  L22 = -L21 * *V0s;
  L21 = L21 * *V0c;
  Lt = L31 * *V0c + L32 * *V0s;
  L32 = L32 * *V0c - L31 * *V0s;
  L31=Lt;

  X2 = -L21 * *U0s;L21 = L21 * *U0c;
  X3 = -L31 * *U0s;L31 = L31 * *U0c;
  L22 = L22 * *U1c + X2 * *U1s;
  L32 = L32 * *U1c + X3 * *U1s;

  diagA[*n-2]=(L33*L33+L23*L23);

  givens(L21,L31,&c,&s);
  L21 = L21*c+L31*s;
  Lt=L22*c+L32*s;
  L32=L32*c-L22*s;L22=Lt;
  L33=L32;L23=L22;L22=L21;

  for (i = *n-5;i>=0;i--)
  { V0c--;V0s--;V1c--;V1s--;U0c--;U0s--;U1c--;U1s--;
    L13 = - *V1s;L11 = *V1c;
    L21 = L23 * *V1s;L23 *=  *V1c;
    L31 = L33 * *V1s;L33 *=  *V1c;
    givens(L11,L31,&c,&s);s = -s;

    L11 = L11*c - L31*s;
    L12 =- L11 * *V0s;
    L11 *= *V0c;
    Lt = L21 * *V0c + L22 * *V0s;
    L22 = L22 * *V0c - L21 * *V0s;
    L21=Lt;

    X1 = -L11 * *U0s;
    L11 *= *U0c;
    L12 = L12 * *U1c + X1 * *U1s;

    X2 = -L21 * *U0s;
    L21 *= *U0c;
    L22 = L22 * *U1c + X2 * *U1s;

    givens(L11,L21,&c,&s);

    L11 = L11*c+L21*s;
    Lt = L12*c+L22*s;
    L22 = L22*c-L12*s;L12=Lt;

    diagA[i+2]=L33*L33+L23*L23+L13*L13;
    if (i!=0)
    { L33=L22;L23=L12;L22=L11;
    }
  }
  diagA[1]=L22*L22+L12*L12;
  diagA[0]=L11*L11;
  for (i=0;i<*n;i++) diagA[i] = 1.0 - diagA[i];
  FREE(ub);
}

void sspl_apply(double *y,double *x,double *w,double *U,double *V,int *n,int *nf,double *tol) {

  int i,k,ok;
  double *Wy,*U0s,*U0c,*U1s,*U1c,
    *V0s,*V0c,*V1s,*V1c,*p,*p1,*p2,w2,*xx;
  if (*nf > *n) {
   xx = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*nf,sizeof(double));
   for (p=x,p1=x + *nf,p2=xx;p<p1;p++,p2++) *p2 = *p;
   k=0;ok=1;
   for (i=1;i<*nf;i++) if (xx[k] + *tol < xx[i]) {
     if (!ok) { w[k] = sqrt(w2);y[k] /= w2;}
     k++;
     xx[k] = xx[i];y[k] = y[i];
     w[k] = w[i];ok=1;
   } else {
     if (ok) { w2 = w[k]*w[k];y[k] *= w[k]*w[k];}
     w2 += w[i]*w[i];
     y[k] += y[i] * w[i] * w[i];
     ok=0;
   }
   if (!ok) { w[k] = sqrt(w2);y[k]/=w2;}
   FREE(xx);
  }

  for (i=0;i<*n;i++) w[i] = 1/w[i];

  Wy = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) 2 * *n,sizeof(double));
  for (i=0;i < *n;i++) Wy[i] = y[i]/w[i];

  U0s = U;U0c = U + *n;
  U1s = U + *n * 2;U1c = U + *n *3;
  V0s = V;V0c = V + *n;
  V1s = V + *n * 2;V1c = V + *n *3;
  for (i=0;i<*n-3;i++)
  { QTz(i+1,*n+i,U1c[i],-U1s[i],Wy);

    QTz(i,*n+i,U0c[i],-U0s[i],Wy);

    QTz(i,i+1,V0c[i],-V0s[i],Wy);

    QTz(i,i+2,V1c[i],-V1s[i],Wy);
  }
  i= *n-3;
  QTz(i,*n+i,U0c[i],-U0s[i],Wy);

  QTz(i,i+1,V0c[i],-V0s[i],Wy);

  QTz(i,i+2,V1c[i],-V1s[i],Wy);

  for (i = *n-2;i<2 * *n ;i++) Wy[i]=0.0;

  for (i= *n-3;i>=0;i--)
  { QTz(i,i+2,V1c[i],V1s[i],Wy);
    QTz(i,i+1,V0c[i],V0s[i],Wy);
    QTz(i,*n+i,U0c[i],U0s[i],Wy);
    if (i != *n-3) QTz(i+1,*n+i,U1c[i],U1s[i],Wy);
  }

  for (i=0;i<*n;i++) Wy[i] = y[i] - Wy[i]*w[i];

  if (*nf > *n) {
   k=0;ok=1;
   y[0] = Wy[0];
   for (i=1;i<*nf;i++) if (x[k] + *tol < x[i]) {
     k++;x[k] = x[i]; y[i] = Wy[k];
   } else {
     y[i] = Wy[k];
   }
  } else {
    for (i=0;i<*n;i++) y[i] = Wy[i];
  }
  FREE(Wy);
}

void sspl_mapply(double *y,double *x,double *w,double *U,double *V,int *n,int *nf,double *tol,int *m) {

  int i,xw_store=0;
  double *xx,*ww,*p,*p1,*p2;
  if (*m > 1 && *nf != *n) xw_store=1;
  if (xw_store) {
    xx = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*nf,sizeof(double));
    ww = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)*nf,sizeof(double));
    for (p=xx,p1=xx + *nf,p2=x;p<p1;p++,p2++) *p = *p2;
    for (p=ww,p1=ww + *nf,p2=w;p<p1;p++,p2++) *p = *p2;
  }
  for (i=0;i < *m;i++) {
    if (xw_store) {
      for (p=xx,p1=xx + *nf,p2=x;p<p1;p++,p2++) *p2 = *p;
      for (p=ww,p1=ww + *nf,p2=w;p<p1;p++,p2++) *p2 = *p;
    }
    sspl_apply(y,x,w,U,V,n,nf,tol);
    y += *nf;
  }
  if (xw_store) {FREE(xx);FREE(ww);}
}

void ss_coeffs(double *lb,double *a,double *b,double *c,double *d,double *x, int *n) {

  double *GTA,*z,*h,*lb1;
  int i;
  GTA=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
  z=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n,sizeof(double));
  h=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)*n-1,sizeof(double));
  for (i=0;i<*n-1;i++) h[i]=x[i+1]-x[i];
  for (i=0;i<*n-2;i++)
  GTA[i]=a[i]/h[i]-a[i+1]*(1/h[i]+1/h[i+1])+a[i+2]/h[i+1];
  lb1 = lb + *n;
  z[0]=GTA[0]/lb[0];
  for (i=1;i<*n-2;i++) z[i]=(GTA[i]-lb1[i-1]*z[i-1])/lb[i];
  c[*n-2]=z[*n-3]/lb[*n-3];c[*n-1]=0.0;c[0]=0.0;
  for (i=*n-4;i>=0;i--)
  c[i+1]=(z[i]-lb1[i]*c[i+2])/lb[i];
  b[*n-1]=d[*n-1]=0;
  for (i=0;i<*n-1;i++)
  { d[i]=(c[i+1]-c[i])/(3*h[i]);
    b[i]=(a[i+1]-a[i])/h[i]-c[i]*h[i]-d[i]*h[i]*h[i];
  }
  FREE(GTA);FREE(z);FREE(h);
}

# --- End of mgcv/src/sparse-smooth.c ---

# --- Start of mgcv/src/sparse.c ---

#include <stdlib.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <math.h>

#ifdef _OPENMP
#include <omp.h>
#endif

#include "mgcv.h"
#include <R.h>

typedef struct {
  int m,c,
    n,
    nk,
    *p,
    *i,
    *k,
    *r,
    *off,
    nzmax;
  double *x;
} spMat;

typedef struct {
  int rb,cb,r,c,r_start,c_start,r_size,c_size;
} XWXblock;

SEXP getListEl(SEXP list, const char *str) {

    SEXP elmt = R_NilValue, names = getAttrib(list, R_NamesSymbol);
    for (int i = 0; i < length(list); i++)
        if(strcmp(CHAR(STRING_ELT(names, i)), str) == 0) {
           elmt = VECTOR_ELT(list, i);
           break;
        }
    return elmt;
}

void tri_to_cs(int *Ti,int *Tj,double *Tx,int *Cp,int *Ci,double *Cx,int *w, int nz, int c) {

  int i,j;

  for (i=0;i<nz;i++) w[Tj[i]]++;

  for (j=0,i=0;i<c;i++) {
    Cp[i] = j;
    j+=w[i];
    w[i]=Cp[i];
  }
  Cp[c] = j;

  for (i=0;i<nz;i++) {
    j = w[Tj[i]]++;
    Ci[j] = Ti[i];
    Cx[j] = Tx[i];
  }
  for (i=0;i<c;i++) w[i] = 0;
}

void cs_trans(int *Ap,int *Ai,double *Ax,int *Cp,int *Ci, double *Cx,int *w,int r,int c) {

  int i,j,q;
  for (i=0;i<r;i++) w[i] = 0;
  for (i = 0 ; i<Ap[c];i++) w[Ai[i]]++;

  for (j=0,i=0;i<r;i++) {Cp[i] = j;j+=w[i];w[i]=Cp[i];} Cp[r] = j;

  for (j=0;j<c;j++) {
    for (i=Ap[j];i<Ap[j+1];i++) {
      q = w[Ai[i]]++;
      Ci[q] = j;
      Cx[q] = Ax[i];
    }
  }
}

void sprealloc(spMat *A, int nzmax) {

#pragma omp critical
  { A->i = (int *)REALLOC(A->i,sizeof(int) * nzmax);
    A->x = (double *)REALLOC(A->x,sizeof(double) * nzmax);
    A->nzmax = nzmax;
  }
}

void spalloc(spMat *A,int c,int nzmax) {
  A->p = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)c+1,sizeof(int));
  A->c = c;
  A->nzmax = nzmax;
  A->i = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)nzmax,sizeof(int));
  A->x = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)nzmax,sizeof(double));
}

void spfree(spMat *A,int m) {

  spMat *a;
  for (a=A;a<A+m;a++) {
    FREE(a->p);FREE(a->i);FREE(a->x);
  }
}

void cs_mult(spMat *A,spMat *B,spMat *C,int *w, double *x,int init) {

  int i,j,k,r,c,nz=0,*Cp,*Ci,*Bp,*Bi,*Ap,*Ai,ii,jj;
  double *Cx,*Bx,*Ax,b;
  C->c = c = B->c;
  C->m = r = A->m;
  Cp = C->p;Ci = C->i;Cx = C->x;
  Bp = B->p;Bi = B->i;Bx = B->x;
  Ap = A->p;Ai = A->i;Ax = A->x;

  for (i=0;i<r;i++) w[i] = -1;
  for (j=0;j<c;j++) {
    if (init && nz+r > C->nzmax) {
      sprealloc(C,C->nzmax*2+r);
      Ci = C->i;Cx = C->x;
    }
    Cp[j]=nz;
    for (i=Bp[j];i<Bp[j+1];i++) {
      b = Bx[i];
      jj = Bi[i];
      for (k=Ap[jj];k<Ap[jj+1];k++) {
        ii = Ai[k];
	if (w[ii]<j) {
	  w[ii] = j;
	  Ci[nz] = ii;nz++;
	  x[ii] = b * Ax[k];
	} else x[ii] += b * Ax[k];
      }
    }
    for (i=Cp[j];i<nz;i++) Cx[i] = x[Ci[i]];
  }
  Cp[c] = nz;

  if (init==1 && nz!=C->nzmax) { if (!nz) nz=1; sprealloc(C,nz); C->nzmax=nz;}
}

int sum_dup(int *Cp,int *Ci,double *Cx,int *w, int r, int c) {

  int i,j,k,nz=0,q,Cpj=0;

  for (i=0;i<r;i++) w[i] = -1;
  for (j=0;j<c;j++) {
    q=nz;
    for (k=Cpj;k<Cp[j+1];k++) {
      i = Ci[k];
      if (w[i]>=q) {
        Cx[w[i]] += Cx[k];
      }	else {
        w[i] = nz;
	Ci[nz] = i;
	Cx[nz] = Cx[k];
	nz++;
      }
    }
    Cpj = Cp[j+1];
    Cp[j+1] = nz;
  }
  for (i=0;i<r;i++) w[i] = 0;
  return(nz);
}

void cs_accumulate(spMat *A,spMat *B,int *iwork) {

  int nzmax,i,j,k,*Ap,*Bp,*Ai,*Bi;
  double *Bx,*Ax;
  Ap = A->p;Bp = B->p;
  Ax = A->x;Bx = B->x;
  Ai = A->i;Bi = B->i;
  nzmax = Ap[A->c] + Bp[B->c];
  if (A->nzmax < nzmax) sprealloc(A,nzmax);
  k = nzmax-1;
  for (j=A->c;j>0;j--) {
    for (i=Bp[j]-1;i >= Bp[j-1];i--) {
      Ax[k] = Bx[i];
      Ai[k] = Bi[i];
      k--;
    }
    for (i=Ap[j]-1;i >= Ap[j-1];i--) {
      Ax[k] = Ax[i];
      Ai[k] = Ai[i];
      k--;
    }
    Ap[j] = nzmax;
    nzmax = k + 1;
  }

  nzmax = sum_dup(Ap,Ai,Ax,iwork,A->m,A->c);
}

void spMtv(spMat *M,double *v,double *u,int add) {

  int i,j,Mc,*Mp,*Mi;double *Mx;
  Mc = M->c;Mi = M->i;Mp = M->p;Mx = M->x;
  if (!add) for (j=0;j<Mc;j++) u[j] = 0.0;
  for (j=0;j<Mc;j++,u++) {
    for (i=Mp[j];i<Mp[j+1];i++) *u += v[Mi[i]] * Mx[i];
  }
}

void spMtA(spMat *M,double *A,double *B,int c,int add) {

  int i,j,k,Mm,Mc,*Mi,*Mp;
  double *Mx;
  Mm = M->m;Mc = M->c;Mi = M->i;Mp = M->p;Mx = M->x;
  if (!add) for (j=0;j < Mm * c ;j++) B[j] = 0.0;
  for (j=0;j < Mc;j++) {
    for (i=Mp[j];i<Mp[j+1];i++) for (k=0;k<c;k++) B[j + k* Mc] += A[Mi[i]+Mm*k] * Mx[i];
  }
}

void spMv(spMat *M,double *v,double *u) {

  int i,j,Mm,Mc,*Mi,*Mp;
  double *Mx;
  Mm = M->m;Mc = M->c;Mi = M->i;Mp = M->p;Mx = M->x;
  for (j=0;j<Mm;j++) u[j] = 0.0;
  for (j=0;j<Mc;j++) {
    for (i=Mp[j];i<Mp[j+1];i++) u[Mi[i]] += v[j] * Mx[i];
  }
}

void spMA(spMat *M,double *A,double *B,int c) {

  int i,j,k,Mm,Mc,*Mi,*Mp;
  double *Mx;
  Mm = M->m;Mc = M->c;Mi = M->i;Mp = M->p;Mx = M->x;
  for (j=0;j < Mm * c ;j++) B[j] = 0.0;
  for (j=0;j < Mc;j++) {
    for (i=Mp[j];i<Mp[j+1];i++) for (k=0;k<c;k++) B[Mi[i] + k* Mm] += A[j+Mc*k] * Mx[i];
  }
}

void sp_to_dense_insitu(spMat *A,int r){

  int i,j,Ac,*Ap,*Ai;
  double x,*Ax;
  Ac = A->c;Ai=A->i;Ap=A->p;Ax=A->x;
  for (j = Ac-1;j >= 0;j--)
    for (i = Ap[j+1]-1;i>=Ap[j];i--) {

      x = Ax[i];
      Ax[i] = 0.0;
      Ax[Ai[i] + j * r] = x;
  }
  Ap[0] = -1;
}

void sp_to_dense(spMat *A,double *x,int r,int c,int nr) {

  int i,j,Ac,*Ap,*Ai,jc;double *Ax;
  Ac = A->c;Ai=A->i;Ap=A->p;Ax=A->x;
  for (j=0;j<Ac;j++) {
    jc = j+c;
    for (i=Ap[j];i<Ap[j+1];i++) x[r + Ai[i] + jc * nr] = Ax[i];
  }
}

void dense_to_sp(spMat *A) {

  int i,j,*Ai,Am;
  Am = A->m;
  A->i = REALLOC(A->i,sizeof(int)*Am * A->c);
  A->p = REALLOC(A->p,sizeof(int)*(A->c+1));
  Ai = A->i;
  for (j=0;j < A->c;j++) {
    A->p[j] = Am*j;
    for (i=0;i<Am;i++,Ai++) *Ai = i;
  }
  A->p[A->c] = Am * A->c;
}

void left_con_vec(double *x,double *v,double *y,int n,int rev) {

  double vx=0.0;
  int i;
  if (rev) rev=1;
  for (i=rev;i<n;i++) vx += v[i]*x[i-rev];
  if (rev) {
    y[0] = -v[0]*vx;
    for (i=1;i<n;i++) y[i] = x[i-1] - v[i]*vx;
  } else for (i=1;i<n;i++) y[i-1] = x[i] - v[i]*vx;
}

void left_con(spMat *A,double *v,double *work) {

  char trans='T';
  int one=1,Am,i,j,k;
  double done=1.0,dzero=0.0,*Ax,x,*Ax1;
  Ax = A->x;Am = A->m;

  F77_CALL(dgemv)(&trans, &(A->m), &(A->c),&done,Ax,&Am,v,&one,&dzero,work,&one FCONE);

  for (k=j=0;j<A->c;j++) for (x=work[j],i=0;i<Am;i++,k++) Ax[k] -= v[i] * x;

  for (Ax1=Ax,j=0;j<A->c;j++) {
    Ax++;
    for (i=1;i<Am;i++,Ax++,Ax1++) *Ax1 = *Ax;
  }
  A->m--;
}

void right_con(spMat *A,double *v,double *work) {

  char trans='N';
  int one=1,Am,i,j,k;
  double done=1.0,dzero=0.0,*Ax,x,*Ax1,*Ax2;
  Ax = A->x;Am = A->m;

  F77_CALL(dgemv)(&trans, &(A->m), &(A->c),&done,Ax,&Am,v,&one,&dzero,work,&one FCONE);

  for (k=j=0;j<A->c;j++) for (x=v[j],i=0;i<Am;i++,k++) Ax[k] -= work[i] * x;

  Ax1 = Ax; Ax += Am; Ax2 = Ax1 + (A->c-1)*A->m;
  for (;Ax1<Ax2;Ax1++,Ax++) *Ax1 = *Ax;
  A->c--;
}

void sXWXdij(double *w, double *d,spMat *Xs,spMat *Xt,int rb,int cb,int r,int c,int *dn,int *ts,
	     int *dt,spMat *W,spMat *V,spMat *XWX,int *init,int *iwork,double *xwork) {

  double x,*Wx,*Xsx;
  int q,qr,qc,qt,l,i,k,ri,ci,ii,jj,k1,j,j1,first_mat,
    first_col=0,first_s=0,*kr,*kc,n,Wnz,*Wi,*Wj,*Xsi,*Xsr,*Xsoff,s,t,r0,qr0,qc0,c0;

  n = Xs[0].n;
  ri = ts[rb];
  if (dt[rb]==1) qr=0; else
  for (qr=1,i = ri;i < ri+dt[rb]-1;i++) qr *= Xs[i].c;
  r0=r;qr0=qr;
  ci = ts[cb];
  if (dt[cb]==1) qc=0; else
  for (qc=1,i = ci;i < ci+dt[cb]-1;i++) qc *= Xs[i].c;
  c0=c;qc0=qc;
  for (s=0;s<Xs[ri].nk;s++) for (t=0;t<Xs[ci].nk;t++) {
    kr = Xs[ri+dt[rb]-1].k + n*s;
    kc = Xs[ci+dt[cb]-1].k + n*t;
    r=r0;qr=qr0;c=c0;qc=qc0;Wnz=0;first_mat = -1;
    qt = dt[cb]+dt[rb] - 2;
    q=0;

    Wi = W->i;Wx = W->x;Wj = W->p;
    for (i = ri;i < ri+dt[rb]-1;i++) {
      qr /= Xs[i].c;
      l = r/qr;
      r = r%qr;
      Xsi = Xs[i].i;Xsx = Xs[i].x;Xsr = Xs[i].r+n*s;Xsoff = Xs[i].off+Xs[i].m*s+s;
      if (first_mat<0) {first_mat=i;first_col=l;first_s=s;}
      k1 = Xs[i].p[l+1];
      for (k=Xs[i].p[l];k<k1;k++) {
        j = Xsi[k];
        x = Xsx[k];
        j1 = Xsoff[j+1];
        for (jj=Xsoff[j];jj<j1;jj++) {
          ii = Xsr[jj];
	  if (q == dn[ii]) {
	    if (q==0) d[ii] = x * w[ii]; else d[ii] *= x;
	    dn[ii]++;
	    if (q==qt-1) {
              Wi[Wnz] = kr[ii];Wj[Wnz] = kc[ii];Wx[Wnz]=d[ii];Wnz++;
	    }
	  }
        }
      }
      q++;
    }

    for (i = ci;i < ci+ dt[cb]-1;i++) {
      qc /= Xs[i].c;
      l = c/qc;
      c = c%qc;
      Xsi = Xs[i].i;Xsx = Xs[i].x;Xsr = Xs[i].r+n*t;Xsoff = Xs[i].off+Xs[i].m*t+t;
      if (first_mat<0) {first_mat=i;first_col=l;first_s=t;}
      k1 = Xs[i].p[l+1];
      for (k=Xs[i].p[l];k<k1;k++) {
        j = Xsi[k];
        x = Xsx[k];
        j1 = Xsoff[j+1];
        for (jj=Xsoff[j];jj<j1;jj++) {
          ii = Xsr[jj];
	  if (q == dn[ii]) {
	    if (q==0) d[ii] = x * w[ii]; else d[ii] *= x;
	    dn[ii]++;
	    if (q==qt-1) {
	      Wi[Wnz] = kr[ii];
	      Wj[Wnz] = kc[ii];
	      Wx[Wnz]=d[ii];
	      Wnz++;
	    }
	  }
        }
      }
      q++;
    }

    if (qt) {
      Xsi = Xs[first_mat].i;Xsr = Xs[first_mat].r+first_s*n;
      Xsoff = Xs[first_mat].off+first_s*(Xs[first_mat].m+1);
      k1 = Xs[first_mat].p[first_col+1];
      for (k=Xs[first_mat].p[first_col];k<k1;k++) {
        j = Xsi[k];
        j1 = Xsoff[j+1];
        for (jj=Xsoff[j];jj<j1;jj++) {
          ii = Xsr[jj];
	  d[ii] = 0.0;dn[ii] = 0;
        }
      }
    } else {
      for (ii=0;ii<n;ii++) {
        Wi[Wnz] = kr[ii];Wj[Wnz] = kc[ii];Wx[Wnz]=w[ii];Wnz++;
      }
    }

    c = Xs[ts[cb]+dt[cb]-1].m; r = Xs[ts[rb]+dt[rb]-1].m;
    tri_to_cs(Wi,Wj,Wx,V->p,V->i,V->x,dn,Wnz,c);
    Wnz = sum_dup(V->p,V->i,V->x,dn,r,c);
    V->m = r;V->c=c;

    W->m = r;W->c= Xs[ts[cb]+dt[cb]-1].c;

    j  = *init; if (j==1) j = 2;
    cs_mult(V,Xs+ts[cb]+dt[cb]-1,W,iwork, xwork,j);

    if (!s && !t) cs_mult(Xt+ts[rb]+dt[rb]-1,W,XWX,iwork, xwork,*init); else {
      V->m = Xs[ts[rb]+dt[rb]-1].c;V->c = W->c;
      cs_mult(Xt+ts[rb]+dt[rb]-1,W,V,iwork, xwork,j);
      cs_accumulate(XWX,V,iwork);
    }

    if (*init) { if (XWX->nzmax > W-> nzmax) *init = XWX->nzmax; else *init = W->nzmax;}
  }

  k = XWX->p[XWX->c];
  if (k>1) {
    if (V->nzmax<k) sprealloc(V,k);
    cs_trans(XWX->p,XWX->i,XWX->x,V->p,V->i,V->x,iwork,XWX->m,XWX->c);
    V->m = XWX->c;V->c = XWX->m;
    cs_trans(V->p,V->i,V->x,XWX->p,XWX->i,XWX->x,iwork,V->m,V->c);
  }

}

SEXP sXWXd(SEXP X,SEXP W,SEXP LT, SEXP RT,SEXP NT) {

  spMat *Xs,*Xt,*xwx,*V1,*W1,*Mp;
  int mx,i,j,k,b,i1,j1,*dim,n,*kd,*ks,*r,*off_start,*off,nt,*ts,*dt,*qc,*lt,*rt,nlt,nrt,nb,nr,nc,nrc,ncc,
    brs0,brs1,bcs0,bcs1=0,is,js,ic,jc,is0,ic0,js0,jc0,init=1,p,ii,jj,*str,*stc,symmetric,*sub_blocks,*block_size,
    rcum=0,ccum=0,is1,rcum0,ccum0,*dn,*iwork,*XWXi,*XWXp,nzmax=0,rb,cb,*ip,n_threads,tid,*B,nprot=0;
  SEXP Xd,M, i_sym,x_sym,dim_sym,p_sym,ul_sym,KD,R,KS,OFF,TS,DT,QC,V,XWX,OFFS;
  double **v,*w,*d,*xwork,*XWXx,*xp,*xp1,*cost;
  XWXblock *block,*blp;

  p_sym = install("p");
  dim_sym = install("Dim");
  i_sym = install("i");
  x_sym = install("x");
  ul_sym = install("uplo");
  Xd = getListEl(X,"Xd");
  KD = getListEl(X,"kd");
  n = nrows(KD);

  KD = PROTECT(coerceVector(KD,INTSXP));nprot++;
  kd = INTEGER(KD);
  R = getListEl(X,"r");
  R = PROTECT(coerceVector(R,INTSXP));nprot++;
  r = INTEGER(R);
  OFF =  getListEl(X,"off");
  OFF = PROTECT(coerceVector(OFF,INTSXP));nprot++;
  off = INTEGER(OFF);
  OFFS =  getListEl(X,"offstart");
  OFFS = PROTECT(coerceVector(OFFS,INTSXP));nprot++;
  off_start = INTEGER(OFFS);

  KS = getListEl(X,"ks");
  KS = PROTECT(coerceVector(KS,INTSXP));nprot++;
  ks = INTEGER(KS);

  mx = length(Xd);

  Xs = (spMat *) CALLOC((size_t)mx,sizeof(spMat));
  Xt = (spMat *) CALLOC((size_t)mx,sizeof(spMat));
  iwork = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)n,sizeof(int));
  for (i=0;i<mx;i++) {
    M = VECTOR_ELT(Xd, i);
    Xs[i].x = REAL(R_do_slot(M,x_sym));
    Xs[i].p = INTEGER(R_do_slot(M,p_sym));
    Xs[i].i = INTEGER(R_do_slot(M,i_sym));
    dim = INTEGER(R_do_slot(M,dim_sym));
    Xs[i].m = dim[0];Xs[i].c = dim[1];
    Xs[i].k = kd + n * ks[i];
    Xs[i].r = r + n * ks[i];
    Xs[i].n = n;
    Xs[i].nk = ks[mx+i] - ks[i];
    Xs[i].off = off + off_start[ks[i]];
    j = Xs[i].p[Xs[i].c];
    if (j>nzmax) nzmax=j;

    spalloc(Xt+i,Xs[i].m,j);
    cs_trans(Xs[i].p,Xs[i].i,Xs[i].x,Xt[i].p,Xt[i].i,Xt[i].x,iwork,Xs[i].m,Xs[i].c);
    Xt[i].m = Xs[i].c; Xt[i].c = Xs[i].m;
  }
  FREE(iwork);

  TS = getListEl(X,"ts");
  nt = length(TS);
  TS = PROTECT(coerceVector(TS,INTSXP));nprot++;
  ts = INTEGER(TS);
  DT = getListEl(X,"dt");
  DT = PROTECT(coerceVector(DT,INTSXP));nprot++;
  dt= INTEGER(DT);
  QC = getListEl(X,"qc");
  QC = PROTECT(coerceVector(QC,INTSXP));nprot++;
  qc= INTEGER(QC);
  V = getListEl(X,"v");
  v = (double **) CALLOC((size_t)nt,sizeof(double *));
  for (i=0;i<nt;i++) if (qc[i]) {
      v[i] = REAL(VECTOR_ELT(V, i));
  }
  nlt = length(LT);lt = INTEGER(LT);
  nrt = length(RT);rt = INTEGER(RT);
  n_threads = asInteger(NT);
  if (n_threads<1) n_threads = 1;
  w = REAL(W);

  if (nlt==nrt) { for (symmetric=1,i=0;i<nlt;i++) if (lt[i]!=rt[i]) { symmetric=0;break;}} else symmetric = 0;

  sub_blocks = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)nt,sizeof(int));
  block_size = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)nt,sizeof(int));

  for (p=0,i=0;i<nt;i++) {
    sub_blocks[i] = 1;
    if (dt[i]>1) for (j=ts[i];j<ts[i]+dt[i]-1;j++) sub_blocks[i] *= Xs[j].c;
    block_size[i] = Xs[ts[i]+dt[i]-1].c;
    p += block_size[i] * sub_blocks[i];
  }

  if (nzmax<n) nzmax = n;
  if (nzmax<p) nzmax=p;

  for (j=0,i=0;i<nlt;i++) j += sub_blocks[lt[i]];
  if (symmetric) { k=j;nb = j*(j+1)/2;} else {
    for (k=0,i=0;i<nrt;i++) k += sub_blocks[rt[i]];
    nb = j*k;
  }

  nr = j;nc=k;
  str = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)nr*nc,sizeof(int));

  block = (XWXblock *)CALLOC((size_t)nb,sizeof(XWXblock));

  brs1=rcum0=0;
  for (ii=0,blp = block,i=0;i<nlt;i++) {
    if (symmetric) i1 = i+1; else i1 = nrt;
    brs0=brs1;
    for (ccum0=0,bcs0=j=0;j<i1;j++) {
      for (rcum=rcum0,brs1=brs0,is=0;is<sub_blocks[lt[i]];is++,rcum++) {
        if (symmetric&&i==j) is1 = is+1; else is1 = sub_blocks[rt[j]];
	for (ccum=ccum0,bcs1=bcs0,js=0;js<is1;js++,blp++,ccum++) {
          blp->rb = lt[i];blp->cb = rt[j];
	  blp->r = is;blp->c = js;
	  blp->r_size = block_size[lt[i]];
	  blp->c_size = block_size[rt[j]];
	  blp->r_start = brs1;
	  blp->c_start = bcs1;
	  bcs1 += block_size[rt[j]];
	  str[rcum + nr * ccum] = ii++;
	}
	brs1 += block_size[lt[i]];
      }
      ccum0 = ccum;
      bcs0 = bcs1;
    }
    rcum0 = rcum;
  }

  blp--;
  rcum = blp->r_start+blp->r_size;
  ccum = blp->c_start+blp->c_size;

  xwx = (spMat *)CALLOC((size_t)nb,sizeof(spMat));
  for (b=0;b<nb;b++) spalloc(xwx+b,block[b].c_size,250);
  d = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)n*n_threads,sizeof(double));
  dn = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)n*n_threads,sizeof(int));
  if (n>p) j=n; else j=p;
  iwork = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)j*n_threads,sizeof(int));
  xwork = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)j*n_threads,sizeof(double));
  V1 = (spMat *)CALLOC((size_t)n_threads,sizeof(spMat));
  W1 = (spMat *)CALLOC((size_t)n_threads,sizeof(spMat));
  for (i=0;i<n_threads;i++) { spalloc(V1+i,n,nzmax);spalloc(W1+i,n,nzmax);}

  B = (int *) CALLOC((size_t)nb,sizeof(int));
  cost = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)nb,sizeof(double));
  for (b=0;b<nb;b++) {
    B[b] = b;
    cost[b] = block[b].r_size*block[b].c_size;
  }
  revsort(cost,B,nb);

  tid=0;
  #ifdef _OPENMP

  #pragma omp parallel for schedule(guided) private(tid,b,i) num_threads(n_threads)
  #endif
  for (i=0;i<nb;i++) {
    b=B[i];
    #ifdef _OPENMP
    tid = omp_get_thread_num();
    #endif
    sXWXdij(w, d+tid*n,Xs,Xt,block[b].rb,block[b].cb,block[b].r,block[b].c,
	    dn+tid*n,ts,dt,W1+tid,V1+tid,xwx+b,&init,iwork+tid*j,xwork+tid*j);

  }
  FREE(B);FREE(cost);

  for (nrc=b=i=0;i<nlt;i++) {
    rb=lt[i];k = 1;
    if (!qc[rb]) k *= sub_blocks[rb];
    nrc += k;
    for (j=0;j<nrt;j++) {
      cb=rt[j];
      if (!qc[cb]) b += k*sub_blocks[cb]; else b += k;
    }
  }
  ncc = b/nrc;

  if (nr*nc != b) {
    stc = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)b,sizeof(int));
    for (i=0;i<b;i++) stc[i] = -1;

    for (is0=ic0=i=0;i<nlt;i++) {
      if (symmetric) j1 = i+1; else j1 = nrt;
      rb = lt[i];
      for (js0=jc0=j=0;j<j1;j++) {

	cb = rt[j];
	if (!qc[rb]&&!qc[cb])
	  for (ic = ic0,is = is0; is < is0 + sub_blocks[rb];is++,ic++)
	    for (jc = jc0, js = js0; js < js0 + sub_blocks[cb];js++,jc++) stc[ic + nrc * jc] = str[is + nr * js];
	else if (!qc[cb]){
	  for (jc = jc0, js = js0; js < js0 + sub_blocks[cb];js++,jc++) {
             k = sub_blocks[rb]*block_size[rb]*block_size[cb];
	     b = str[is0+nr*js];
	     stc[ic0 + nrc * jc] = b;
	     block[b].r_size = sub_blocks[rb]*block_size[rb];
	     xwx[b].x = REALLOC(xwx[b].x,sizeof(double)*k);

	     for (xp=xwx[b].x+xwx[b].p[xwx[b].c],xp1=xwx[b].x+k;xp<xp1;xp++) *xp=0.0;
	     sp_to_dense_insitu(xwx+b,block[b].r_size);
             xwx[b].m =  block[b].r_size;
	     for (is = is0+1; is < is0 + sub_blocks[rb];is++) {
	       sp_to_dense(xwx + str[is + nr*js],xwx[b].x,(is-is0)*block_size[rb],0,xwx[b].m);
	     }
	     left_con(xwx + b,v[rb],xwork);
	     block[b].r_size--;
	     dense_to_sp(xwx+b);
	  }
	} else if (!qc[rb]) {
	  for (ic = ic0,is = is0; is < is0 + sub_blocks[rb];is++,ic++) {
             k = sub_blocks[cb]*block_size[rb]*block_size[cb];
	     b = str[is+nr*js0];
	     stc[ic + nrc * jc0] = b;
	     block[b].c_size = sub_blocks[cb]*block_size[cb];
	     xwx[b].x = REALLOC(xwx[b].x,sizeof(double)*k);

	     for (xp=xwx[b].x+xwx[b].p[xwx[b].c],xp1=xwx[b].x+k;xp<xp1;xp++) *xp=0.0;
	     sp_to_dense_insitu(xwx+b,block[b].r_size);
	     xwx[b].c =  block[b].c_size;
             for (js = js0+1; js < js0 + sub_blocks[cb];js++) {
               sp_to_dense(xwx + str[is + nr*js],xwx[b].x,0,(js-js0)*block_size[cb],xwx[b].m);
	     }
	     right_con(xwx + b,v[cb],xwork);
	     block[b].c_size--;
	     dense_to_sp(xwx+b);
	  }
        } else {

	  k = sub_blocks[rb]*block_size[rb]*sub_blocks[cb]*block_size[cb];
	  b = str[is0+nr*js0];
          stc[ic0 + nrc * jc0] = b;
	  block[b].r_size = sub_blocks[rb]*block_size[rb];
	  block[b].c_size = sub_blocks[cb]*block_size[cb];

	  xwx[b].x = REALLOC(xwx[b].x,sizeof(double)*k);

	  for (xp=xwx[b].x+xwx[b].p[xwx[b].c],xp1=xwx[b].x+k;xp<xp1;xp++) *xp=0.0;
	  sp_to_dense_insitu(xwx+b,block[b].r_size);
          xwx[b].m =  block[b].r_size;
	  xwx[b].c =  block[b].c_size;

	  for (is = is0; is < is0 + sub_blocks[rb];is++) {
	    for (js = js0; js < js0 + sub_blocks[cb];js++) {
	      if ((is==is0&&js==js0) ||(symmetric&&j==i&&js>is)) {
	      }	else {

                sp_to_dense(xwx + str[is + nr*js],xwx[b].x,(is-is0)*block_size[rb],(js-js0)*block_size[cb],xwx[b].m);
              }
	    }
	  }
	  if (symmetric && i==j) {
	    k = xwx[b].m;
            for (is=0;is<k;is++) for (js=0;js<is;js++) xwx[b].x[js + k * is] = xwx[b].x[is + k * js];
	  }

	  if (qc[rb]) {
	    left_con(xwx + b,v[rb],xwork);
	    block[b].r_size--;
	  }
	  if (qc[cb]) {
	    right_con(xwx + b,v[cb],xwork);
	    block[b].c_size--;
	  }
	  dense_to_sp(xwx+b);
	}
	js0 += sub_blocks[cb];
	if (qc[cb]) jc0++; else jc0 += sub_blocks[cb];
      }
      is0 += sub_blocks[rb];
      if (qc[rb]) ic0++; else ic0 += sub_blocks[rb];

    }

    k = stc[0];
    for (i=1;i<nrc;i++) {

      b = stc[i-1];k = stc[i];
      block[k].r_start = block[b].r_start + block[b].r_size;
      if (symmetric) j1=i; else j1=ncc-1;
      for (j=0;j<j1;j++) {
        b = stc[i + j * nrc];k = stc[i + (j+1) * nrc];
	block[k].c_start = block[b].c_start + block[b].c_size;
	block[k].r_start = block[b].r_start;
      }
    }

    rcum = block[k].r_start + block[k].r_size;
    ccum = block[k].c_start + block[k].c_size;
  } else {
    stc = str;
  }

  for (k=0,i=0;i<nrc;i++) {
    if (symmetric) j1 = i+1; else j1 = ncc;
    for (j=0;j<j1;j++) {
      b = stc[i + nrc * j];
      if (xwx[b].p[0]<0) k += xwx[b].m * xwx[b].c;
      else k += xwx[b].p[xwx[b].c];
    }
  }

  if (symmetric) {
    XWX = PROTECT(R_do_new_object(PROTECT(R_getClassDef("dsCMatrix"))));nprot++;nprot++;
    SET_STRING_ELT(PROTECT(R_do_slot(XWX,ul_sym)),0,PROTECT(mkChar("L")));nprot++;nprot++;
  } else { XWX = PROTECT(R_do_new_object(PROTECT(R_getClassDef("dgCMatrix"))));nprot++;nprot++;}
  dim = INTEGER(R_do_slot(XWX,dim_sym));
  dim[0] = rcum;dim[1] = ccum;
  if (symmetric) {
    XWXi = (int *) CALLOC((size_t)k,sizeof(int));
    XWXx = (double *) CALLOC((size_t)k,sizeof(double));
  } else {
    R_do_slot_assign(XWX,x_sym,allocVector(REALSXP,k));
    R_do_slot_assign(XWX,i_sym,allocVector(INTSXP,k));
    XWXi = INTEGER(R_do_slot(XWX,i_sym));
    XWXx = REAL(R_do_slot(XWX,x_sym));
  }
  R_do_slot_assign(XWX,p_sym,allocVector(INTSXP,dim[1]+1));
  XWXp = INTEGER(R_do_slot(XWX,p_sym));

  XWXp[0]= 0;
  for (ii=0,jj=0,j=0;j<ncc;j++) {
    for (js=0;js<xwx[stc[j*nrc+j*symmetric]].c;js++,jj++) {
      for (i=j*symmetric;i<nrc;i++) {
	k = stc[i+j*nrc];
	if (k<0) Rprintf("\n WARNING: incomplete constrained structure matrix\n ");
	Mp = xwx + k;
	blp = block + k;
	is = Mp->p[js];
	if (i==j&&symmetric) {
	  while (Mp->i[is]<js&&is<Mp->p[js+1]) is++;
	}

	while (is < Mp->p[js+1]) {
	  XWXi[ii] = Mp->i[is] + blp->r_start;
	  XWXx[ii] = Mp->x[is];ii++;is++;
        }
      }
      XWXp[jj+1] = ii;
    }
  }
  if (symmetric) {
    R_do_slot_assign(XWX,x_sym,allocVector(REALSXP,ii));
    R_do_slot_assign(XWX,i_sym,allocVector(INTSXP,ii));
    ip = INTEGER(R_do_slot(XWX,i_sym));
    xp = REAL(R_do_slot(XWX,x_sym));
    for (i=0;i<ii;i++) {xp[i]=XWXx[i];ip[i]=XWXi[i];}
    FREE(XWXi);FREE(XWXx);
  }
  if (ncc*nrc!=nr*nc) FREE(stc);
  FREE(block);
  for (i=0;i<n_threads;i++) {
    spfree(V1+i,1);spfree(W1+i,1);
  }
  FREE(W1);FREE(V1);
  spfree(Xt,mx);FREE(Xt);
  spfree(xwx,nb);FREE(xwx);
  FREE(iwork); FREE(xwork);FREE(dn);FREE(d); FREE(str);FREE(sub_blocks);FREE(block_size);
  FREE(Xs);
  FREE(v);
  UNPROTECT(nprot);
  return(XWX);
}

SEXP sXyd(SEXP X,SEXP Y,SEXP LT) {

  spMat *Xs,*Xj;
  int mx,i,j,k,q,out_start,n,bb,nzmax=0,*dim,*kd,*ks,*r,*off_start,*off,nt,
    *ts,*dt,*qc,*lt,nlt,*dn,*ki,*c,*p,P,s,l,ii,b,c0,os,qq,nc=0,no=0,*tps,cy,yj;
  SEXP Xd,M,XY,
    i_sym,x_sym,dim_sym,p_sym,
    KD,R,KS,OFF,TS,DT,QC,V,OFFS;
  double **v,*y,*Xy,*yb,*My,*Xyo,*yp;
  p_sym = install("p");
  dim_sym = install("Dim");
  i_sym = install("i");
  x_sym = install("x");
  Xd = getListEl(X,"Xd");
  KD = getListEl(X,"kd");
  n = nrows(KD);

  KD = PROTECT(coerceVector(KD,INTSXP));
  kd = INTEGER(KD);
  R = getListEl(X,"r");
  R = PROTECT(coerceVector(R,INTSXP));
  r = INTEGER(R);
  OFF =  getListEl(X,"off");
  OFF = PROTECT(coerceVector(OFF,INTSXP));
  off = INTEGER(OFF);
  OFFS =  getListEl(X,"offstart");
  OFFS = PROTECT(coerceVector(OFFS,INTSXP));
  off_start = INTEGER(OFFS);

  KS = getListEl(X,"ks");
  KS = PROTECT(coerceVector(KS,INTSXP));
  ks = INTEGER(KS);

  mx = length(Xd);

  Xs = (spMat *) CALLOC((size_t)mx,sizeof(spMat));
  for (i=0;i<mx;i++) {
    M = VECTOR_ELT(Xd, i);
    Xs[i].x = REAL(R_do_slot(M,x_sym));
    Xs[i].p = INTEGER(R_do_slot(M,p_sym));
    Xs[i].i = INTEGER(R_do_slot(M,i_sym));
    dim = INTEGER(R_do_slot(M,dim_sym));
    Xs[i].m = dim[0];Xs[i].c = dim[1];
    Xs[i].k = kd + n * ks[i];
    Xs[i].r = r + n * ks[i];
    Xs[i].n = n;
    Xs[i].nk = ks[mx+i] - ks[i];
    Xs[i].off = off + off_start[ks[i]];
    j = Xs[i].p[Xs[i].c];
    if (j>nzmax) nzmax=j;
  }

  TS = getListEl(X,"ts");
  nt = length(TS);
  TS = PROTECT(coerceVector(TS,INTSXP));
  ts = INTEGER(TS);
  DT = getListEl(X,"dt");
  DT = PROTECT(coerceVector(DT,INTSXP));
  dt= INTEGER(DT);
  QC = getListEl(X,"qc");
  QC = PROTECT(coerceVector(QC,INTSXP));
  qc= INTEGER(QC);
  V = getListEl(X,"v");
  v = (double **) CALLOC((size_t)nt,sizeof(double *));
  for (i=0;i<nt;i++) if (qc[i]) {
    v[i] = REAL(VECTOR_ELT(V, i));
  }
  nlt = length(LT);lt = INTEGER(LT);
  for (i=0;i<nlt;i++) if (qc[lt[i]]) nc++;
  y = REAL(Y);
  cy = length(Y)/n;
  out_start = 0;

  for (ii=bb=no=i=0;i<nlt;i++) {
    b = lt[i];
    for (q=1,j=0;j<dt[b];j++) q *= Xs[ts[b]+j].c;
    no += q;
    if (Xs[ts[b]+dt[b]-1].m > bb) bb = Xs[ts[b]+dt[b]-1].m;
    if (ii<dt[b]) ii=dt[b];
  }

  Xy = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) no*cy,sizeof(double));
  yb = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) bb,sizeof(double));
  c = (int *) CALLOC((size_t)ii,sizeof(int));
  p = (int *) CALLOC((size_t)ii,sizeof(int));
  My = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) n * ii,sizeof(double));
  dn = (int *) CALLOC((size_t) n,sizeof(int));
  tps = (int *) CALLOC((size_t) nlt+1,sizeof(int));
  for (bb=0;bb<nlt;bb++) {
    b = lt[bb];
    tps[bb]=out_start;
    if (dt[b] == 1) {
      for (yj=0;yj<cy;yj++) {
	yp = y + yj * n;
        for (i=0;i<Xs[ts[b]].m;i++) yb[i] = 0.0;
        for (s=0;s<Xs[ts[b]].nk;s++) {
          ki = Xs[ts[b]].k+s*n;
          for (i=0;i<n;i++) yb[ki[i]] += yp[i];
        }

        spMtv(Xs + ts[b],yb,Xy + yj*no + out_start,0);
      }
      out_start += Xs[ts[b]].c;
    } else {
      for (P=1,i=0;i<dt[b]-1;i++) {
	p[i] = Xs[ts[b]+i].c;
	P *= p[i];
        c[i] = 0;
      }
      p[i] = Xs[ts[b]+i].c;

      os = out_start;
      for (yp=y,yj=0;yj<cy;yj++,yp += n)
      for (s=0;s<Xs[ts[b]].nk;s++) {
        k = 0;out_start=os;
	for (i=0;i<dt[b];i++) c[i] = 0;
	if (s) for (i=0;i<n;i++) dn[i] = 0;
        ki = Xs[ts[b]+dt[b]-1].k + n *s;
        for (l=0;l<P;l++) {

	  for (i=0;i<Xs[ts[b]+dt[b]-1].m;i++) yb[i] = 0.0;
	  for (j=k;j<dt[b]-1;j++) {
	    Xj = Xs + ts[b] + j;
	    for (q=Xj->p[c[j]];q<Xj->p[c[j]+1];q++)
	      for (qq = Xj->i[q],ii=Xj->off[qq + (Xj->m+1)*s];ii<Xj->off[qq+1+ (Xj->m+1)*s];ii++) {
              i = Xj->r[ii + s*n];
	      if (dn[i]==j) {
	        dn[i]++;

	        if (j>0) My[i+n*j] = My[i+n*(j-1)]*Xj->x[q];
	        else My[i] = yp[i] * Xj->x[q];
	        if (j==dt[b]-2) yb[ki[i]] += My[i+j*n];
	      }
	    }
	  }

	  spMtv(Xs + ts[b]+dt[b]-1,yb,Xy + yj*no + out_start,s);
	  out_start += Xs[ts[b]+dt[b]-1].c;

	  k = dt[b]-2;c[k]++;
	  while (c[k]==p[k])  {
	    c[k]=0; if (k) { k--;c[k]++;}
	  }

	  for (j=dt[b]-2;j>=k;j--) {
	    if (c[j]>0) c0 = c[j] - 1; else c0 = p[j] - 1;
	    Xj = Xs + ts[b] + j;
	    for (q=Xj->p[c0];q<Xj->p[c0+1];q++)
	    for (qq = Xj->i[q],ii=Xj->off[qq];ii<Xj->off[qq+1];ii++) {
              i = Xj->r[ii + s*n];
	      if (dn[i]==j+1) dn[i]--;
	    }
	  }
	}
      }
    }
  }
  tps[bb] = out_start;

  PROTECT(XY=allocVector(REALSXP,cy*(no-nc)));
  Xyo=REAL(XY);
  for (yp=Xy,yj=0;yj<cy;yj++,Xyo += no-nc,yp += no)
  for (i=bb=0;bb<nlt;bb++) {
    b = lt[bb];
    if (qc[b]) {
      k = tps[bb+1]-tps[bb];
      left_con_vec(yp+tps[bb],v[b],Xyo+i,k,0);
      i += k-1;
    } else {
      for (j=tps[bb];j<tps[bb+1];j++,i++) Xyo[i] = yp[j];
    }
  }

  FREE(Xy);FREE(Xs);FREE(v);FREE(yb);FREE(c);FREE(p);FREE(My);FREE(tps);FREE(dn);
  UNPROTECT(9);
  return(XY);
}

int spac(int *i,int i0,int j0,int j1,int r,int c,int *ii,int *q) {

  int i1,ik,j00,k;
  i1 = i0 + r*c;
  while (j0>0&&i[j0]>i0) j0--;
  while (i[j0]<i0&&j0<j1) j0++;
  j00 = j0;
  k = 0;
  q[0] = 0;ik = 0;
  while (i[j0]<i1&&j0<j1) {
    while (i[j0]-i0 < r+k*r && j0<j1) ii[ik++] = i[j0++] - i0 - k*r;
    if (j0<j1) while (k<c && i[j0]-i0 >= r+k*r) {k++;q[k]=ik;}
  }
  while (k<c) {k++;q[k]=ik;}
  return(j00);
}

void sXbsdwork(double *Xb,double *a,spMat beta0,int bp,spMat *Xs,double **v,int *qc,int nt,
	       int *ts,int *dt,int *lt,int nlt,int n,double *work,int *worki,int unit_a) {

  spMat *Xj,beta,bsub,C;
  int nc,i,j,k,k0,q,bb,*dim,maxd,maxdim,/
 j0,j1,*ri;
  double *d;
  for (nc=i=0;i<nt;i++) if (qc[i]) nc++;
  beta.x = work;work += bp+nc;
  beta.i = worki;worki += bp + nc;
  beta.p = worki;worki += 2;
  dim = worki;worki += nt;
  tps = worki;worki += nt;
  dn = worki;worki += n;

  for (maxdim=maxd=k=0,bb=0;bb<nlt;bb++) {
    i = lt[bb];
    b = ts[i];
    for (dim[i]=1,j=0;j<dt[i];j++) dim[i] *= Xs[b+j].c;
    tps[i] = k; k += dim[i];
    if (dt[i] > maxd) maxd = dt[i];
    if (dim[i] > maxdim) maxdim = dim[i];
  }

  for (j0=j1=q=k=bb=0;bb<nlt;bb++) {
    i = lt[bb];
    if (qc[i]) {

      for (j=0;j<dim[i];j++) work[j] = 0.0;
      while (j0<beta0.p[1] && beta0.i[j0]<q+dim[i]-1) {
	work[beta0.i[j0]-q] = beta0.x[j0];j0++;
      }
      left_con_vec(work,v[i],beta.x+j1,dim[i],1);
      for (j=k+dim[i];k<j;k++,j1++) { beta.i[j1] = k;}
      q += dim[i]-1;
    } else {
      while (j0<beta0.p[1] && beta0.i[j0]<q+dim[i]) {
        beta.i[j1] = beta0.i[j0] + k - q;
	beta.x[j1] = beta0.x[j0];
	j1++;j0++;
      }
      q += dim[i];k += dim[i];
    }
  }

  beta.p[1] = j1;beta.p[0]=0;

  d = work; work += n*maxd;

  p = worki;worki += maxd;
  c = worki;worki += maxd;
  c0 = worki;worki += maxd;
  bsub.i = worki; worki += bp + nc;
  bsub.x = work; work += bp + nc;
  bsub.p = worki;worki += maxdim + 1;
  spalloc(&C,maxdim,1000);
  j0 = 0;
  for (bb=0;bb<nlt;bb++) {
    b = lt[bb];
    if (dt[b]==1) {

      Xj = Xs + ts[b];
      j0 = spac(beta.i,tps[b],j0,beta.p[1],Xj->c,1,bsub.i,bsub.p);
      for (i=0;i<bsub.p[1];i++) bsub.x[i] = beta.x[i+j0];
      j0 += bsub.p[1];bsub.m = Xj->c;bsub.c=1;
      cs_mult(Xj,&bsub,&C,worki,work,1);

      for (s=0;s<Xj->nk;s++) {
        ri = Xj->r + s * n;
	for (q=0;q<C.p[1];q++)
	  for (j=Xj->off[q+s*Xj->m];j<Xj->off[q+1+s*Xj->m];j++)
	    if (unit_a) Xb[ri[j]] += C.x[q]; else
	      Xb[ri[j]] += a[ri[j]] * C.x[q];
      }
    } else {
      for (j=0;j<dt[b];j++) p[j]=Xs[ts[b]+j].c;
      for (P=1,j=0;j<dt[b]-1;j++) P *= p[j];

      Xj = Xs + ts[b] + dt[b] - 1;
      j0 = spac(beta.i,tps[b],j0,beta.p[1],Xj->c,P,bsub.i,bsub.p);
      for (i=0;i<bsub.p[P];i++) bsub.x[i] = beta.x[i+j0];
      j0 += bsub.p[P]; bsub.m = Xj->c;bsub.c = P;
      cs_mult(Xj,&bsub,&C,worki,work,1);
      for (s=0;s<Xs[ts[b]].nk;s++) {
	for (i=0;i<dt[b];i++) c0[i]=c[i] = 0;
	k0=0;
        for (l=0;l<P;l++) {
	  if (C.p[l]<C.p[l+1]) {
            k = k0;
	    k0 = dt[b]-2;
	    for (m=k;m<dt[b]-1;m++) {
	      c0[m] = c[m];
	      Xj = Xs + ts[b] + m;
	      for (q = Xj->p[c[m]];q<Xj->p[c[m]+1];q++) {
                for (qq = Xj->i[q],ii=Xj->off[qq + (Xj->m+1)*s];ii<Xj->off[qq+1+ (Xj->m+1)*s];ii++) {
                  i = Xj->r[ii + s*n];
	          if (dn[i]==m) {
	            dn[i]++;
	            if (m==0) d[i] = Xj->x[q]; else d[i+m*n] = d[i+m*n-n] * Xj->x[q];
		  }
	        }
	      }
	    }

	    m = dt[b] - 2;
	    Xj = Xs + ts[b] + m + 1;
	    for (q = C.p[l];q<C.p[l+1];q++) {
	      for (qq = C.i[q],ii=Xj->off[qq + (Xj->m+1)*s];ii<Xj->off[qq+1+(Xj->m+1)*s];ii++) {
                i = Xj->r[ii + s*n];
	        if (dn[i]==dt[b]-1) {
                  if (unit_a) Xb[i] += d[i+m*n] * C.x[q]; else Xb[i] += a[i] * d[i+m*n] * C.x[q];
	        }
	      }
	    }
	  }

	  k = dt[b]-2;c[k]++;
	  while (c[k]==p[k]) {
            c[k]=0;
	    if (k>0) {
              k--;c[k]++;
	    }
	  }
	  if (k<k0) k0=k;
	  if (l==P-1 || C.p[l+1]<C.p[l+2]) {
	    for (m=dt[b]-2;m>=k0;m--) {
	      Xj = Xs + ts[b] + m;
	      for (q = Xj->p[c0[m]];q<Xj->p[c0[m]+1];q++)
	      for (qq = Xj->i[q],ii=Xj->off[qq + (Xj->m+1)*s];ii<Xj->off[qq+1+ (Xj->m+1)*s];ii++) {
                i = Xj->r[ii + s*n];
	        if (dn[i]==m+1) dn[i]--;
	      }
	    }
	  }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  spfree(&C,1);
}

void sXbdwork(double *Xb,double *a,double *beta0,int bp,spMat *Xs,double **v,int *qc,int nt,
	      int *ts,int *dt,int *lt,int nlt,int n,double *work,int *worki,int unit_a) {

  spMat *Xj;
  int nc,i,j,k,q,bb,*dim,maxd,ok,*tps,
    *dn,*ki,*c,*p,P,s,l,ii,b,qq,m,mx;
  double *beta,*C,*d;
  for (nc=i=0;i<nt;i++) if (qc[i]) nc++;
  beta = work;work += bp+nc;
  dim = worki;worki += nt;
  tps = worki;worki += nt;
  dn = worki;worki += n;

  for (k=maxd=0,bb=0;bb<nlt;bb++) {
    i = lt[bb];
    b = ts[i];
    for (dim[i]=1,j=0;j<dt[i];j++) dim[i] *= Xs[b+j].c;
    tps[i] = k; k += dim[i];
    if (dt[i] > maxd) maxd = dt[i];
  }
  for (q=k=bb=0;bb<nlt;bb++) {
    i = lt[bb];
    if (qc[i]) {
      left_con_vec(beta0+q,v[i],beta+k,dim[i],1);
      q += dim[i]-1;k += dim[i];
    } else {
      for (j=0;j<dim[i];j++,k++,q++) beta[k] = beta0[q];
    }
  }
  d = work;

  p = worki;worki += maxd;
  c = worki;

  for (bb=0;bb<nlt;bb++) {
    b = lt[bb];
    if (dt[b]==1) {
      spMv(Xs+ts[b],beta+tps[b],d);
     for (s=0;s<Xs[ts[b]].nk;s++) {
        ki = Xs[ts[b]].k + s * n;
	if (unit_a) for (j=0;j<n;j++) Xb[j] += d[ki[j]];
	else for (j=0;j<n;j++) Xb[j] += a[j] * d[ki[j]];
      }
    } else {
      for (j=0;j<dt[b];j++) p[j]=Xs[ts[b]+j].c;
      for (P=1,j=0;j<dt[b]-1;j++) P *= p[j];

      mx = Xs[ts[b]+dt[b]-1].m;
      C = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)P * mx,sizeof(double));
      spMA(Xs+ts[b]+dt[b]-1,beta+tps[b],C,P);
      for (s=0;s<Xs[ts[b]].nk;s++) {
        ki = Xs[ts[b]+dt[b]-1].k + s * n;
	for (i=0;i<dt[b];i++) c[i] = 0;
	k=0;
        for (l=0;l<P;l++) {
          for (m=k;m<dt[b]-1;m++) {
	    Xj = Xs + ts[b] + m;
	    for (q = Xj->p[c[m]];q<Xj->p[c[m]+1];q++) {
              for (qq = Xj->i[q],ii=Xj->off[qq + (Xj->m+1)*s];ii<Xj->off[qq+1+ (Xj->m+1)*s];ii++) {
                i = Xj->r[ii + s*n];
	        if (dn[i]==m) {
	          dn[i]++;
	          if (m==0) d[i] = Xj->x[q]; else d[i+m*n] = d[i+m*n-n] * Xj->x[q];
	          if (m==dt[b]-2) {
		    if (unit_a) Xb[i] += d[i+m*n] * C[ki[i] + l*mx];
		    else Xb[i] += a[i] * d[i+m*n] * C[ki[i] + l*mx];
		  }
	        }
	      }
	    }
          }

	  k = dt[b]-2;ok = 0;
	  while (!ok) {
	    Xj = Xs + ts[b] + k;
	    for (q = Xj->p[c[k]];q<Xj->p[c[k]+1];q++)
	    for (qq = Xj->i[q],ii=Xj->off[qq + (Xj->m+1)*s];ii<Xj->off[qq+1+ (Xj->m+1)*s];ii++) {
              i = Xj->r[ii + s*n];
	      if (dn[i]==k+1) dn[i]--;
	    }
	    c[k]++;ok=1;
	    if (c[k]==p[k]) {
	      c[k]=0; if (k>0) {k--;ok=0;}
	    }
	  }
        }
      }
      FREE(C);
    }
  }
}

SEXP sXbd(SEXP X,SEXP BETA,SEXP LT) {

  spMat *Xs;
  int bp,mx,i,j,k,n,nzmax=0,*dim,*kd,*ks,*r,*off_start,*off,nt,
    *ts,*dt,*qc,*lt,nlt,maxd,nc,*worki,maxm=0,bc;
  SEXP Xd,M,
    i_sym,x_sym,dim_sym,p_sym,
    KD,R,KS,OFF,TS,DT,QC,V,XB,OFFS;
  double **v,*beta0,*Xb,*work,a=1;
  p_sym = install("p");
  dim_sym = install("Dim");
  i_sym = install("i");
  x_sym = install("x");
  Xd = getListEl(X,"Xd");
  KD = getListEl(X,"kd");
  n = nrows(KD);

  KD = PROTECT(coerceVector(KD,INTSXP));
  kd = INTEGER(KD);
  R = getListEl(X,"r");
  R = PROTECT(coerceVector(R,INTSXP));
  r = INTEGER(R);
  OFF =  getListEl(X,"off");
  OFF = PROTECT(coerceVector(OFF,INTSXP));
  off = INTEGER(OFF);
  OFFS =  getListEl(X,"offstart");
  OFFS = PROTECT(coerceVector(OFFS,INTSXP));
  off_start = INTEGER(OFFS);

  KS = getListEl(X,"ks");
  KS = PROTECT(coerceVector(KS,INTSXP));
  ks = INTEGER(KS);

  mx = length(Xd);

  Xs = (spMat *) CALLOC((size_t)mx,sizeof(spMat));

  for (i=0;i<mx;i++) {
    M = VECTOR_ELT(Xd, i);
    Xs[i].x = REAL(R_do_slot(M,x_sym));
    Xs[i].p = INTEGER(R_do_slot(M,p_sym));
    Xs[i].i = INTEGER(R_do_slot(M,i_sym));
    dim = INTEGER(R_do_slot(M,dim_sym));
    Xs[i].m = dim[0];Xs[i].c = dim[1];
    if (maxm<dim[0]) maxm=dim[0];
    Xs[i].k = kd + n * ks[i];
    Xs[i].r = r + n * ks[i];
    Xs[i].n = n;
    Xs[i].nk = ks[mx+i] - ks[i];
    Xs[i].off = off + off_start[ks[i]];
    j = Xs[i].p[Xs[i].c];
    if (j>nzmax) nzmax=j;
  }

  TS = getListEl(X,"ts");
  nt = length(TS);
  TS = PROTECT(coerceVector(TS,INTSXP));
  ts = INTEGER(TS);
  DT = getListEl(X,"dt");
  DT = PROTECT(coerceVector(DT,INTSXP));
  dt= INTEGER(DT);
  QC = getListEl(X,"qc");
  QC = PROTECT(coerceVector(QC,INTSXP));
  qc= INTEGER(QC);
  V = getListEl(X,"v");
  v = (double **) CALLOC((size_t)nt,sizeof(double *));
  for (nc=i=0;i<nt;i++) if (qc[i]) {
    v[i] = REAL(VECTOR_ELT(V, i));
    nc++;
  }
  for (maxd=i=0;i<nt;i++) if (maxd<dt[i]) maxd=dt[i];
  nlt = length(LT);lt = INTEGER(LT);
  beta0 = REAL(BETA);

  bp = nrows(BETA);bc = ncols(BETA);
  PROTECT(XB=allocVector(REALSXP,n*bc));
  Xb=REAL(XB);
  for (i=0;i<n*bc;i++) Xb[i] = 0.0;

  k = n*maxd+bp+nc;
  work = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) k,sizeof(double));

  k = n+2*(nt+maxd);
  worki = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) k,sizeof(int));

  for (j=0;j<bc;j++,beta0 += bp,Xb += n)
    sXbdwork(Xb,&a,beta0,bp,Xs,v,qc,nt,ts,dt,lt,nlt,n,work,worki,1);

  FREE(worki);FREE(work);FREE(v);FREE(Xs);
  UNPROTECT(9);
  return(XB);
}

SEXP sdiagXVXt(SEXP X, SEXP V, SEXP LT, SEXP RT) {

  spMat Vs,Ii,Vi,*Xs;
  int i,j,k,p,n,mx,*r,*kd,*off,*ks,*off_start,nt,*ts,*dt,*qc,nc,maxd,maxm=0,nlt,*lt,nrt,*rt,*dim,*worki;
  SEXP Xd,i_sym,x_sym,dim_sym,p_sym,KD,R,OFF,OFFS,KS,M,TS,DT,QC,Vc,DXVX;
  double **v,*dxvx,*a,*work;
  p_sym = install("p");
  dim_sym = install("Dim");
  i_sym = install("i");
  x_sym = install("x");
  Xd = getListEl(X,"Xd");
  KD = getListEl(X,"kd");
  n = nrows(KD);
  KD = PROTECT(coerceVector(KD,INTSXP));
  kd = INTEGER(KD);
  R = getListEl(X,"r");
  R = PROTECT(coerceVector(R,INTSXP));
  r = INTEGER(R);
  OFF =  getListEl(X,"off");
  OFF = PROTECT(coerceVector(OFF,INTSXP));
  off = INTEGER(OFF);
  OFFS =  getListEl(X,"offstart");
  OFFS = PROTECT(coerceVector(OFFS,INTSXP));
  off_start = INTEGER(OFFS);

  KS = getListEl(X,"ks");
  KS = PROTECT(coerceVector(KS,INTSXP));
  ks = INTEGER(KS);

  mx = length(Xd);

  Xs = (spMat *) CALLOC((size_t)mx,sizeof(spMat));
  for (i=0;i<mx;i++) {
    M = VECTOR_ELT(Xd, i);
    Xs[i].x = REAL(R_do_slot(M,x_sym));
    Xs[i].p = INTEGER(R_do_slot(M,p_sym));
    Xs[i].i = INTEGER(R_do_slot(M,i_sym));
    dim = INTEGER(R_do_slot(M,dim_sym));
    Xs[i].m = dim[0];Xs[i].c = dim[1];
    if (maxm<dim[0]) maxm=dim[0];
    Xs[i].k = kd + n * ks[i];
    Xs[i].r = r + n * ks[i];
    Xs[i].n = n;
    Xs[i].nk = ks[mx+i] - ks[i];
    Xs[i].off = off + off_start[ks[i]];

  }

  Vs.x =  REAL(R_do_slot(V,x_sym));Vs.p = INTEGER(R_do_slot(V,p_sym));
  Vs.i = INTEGER(R_do_slot(V,i_sym));
  dim = INTEGER(R_do_slot(V,dim_sym));
  p = Vs.m = dim[0];Vs.c = dim[1];

  TS = getListEl(X,"ts");
  nt = length(TS);
  TS = PROTECT(coerceVector(TS,INTSXP));
  ts = INTEGER(TS);
  DT = getListEl(X,"dt");
  DT = PROTECT(coerceVector(DT,INTSXP));
  dt= INTEGER(DT);
  QC = getListEl(X,"qc");
  QC = PROTECT(coerceVector(QC,INTSXP));
  qc= INTEGER(QC);
  Vc = getListEl(X,"v");
  v = (double **) CALLOC((size_t)nt,sizeof(double *));
  for (nc=i=0;i<nt;i++) if (qc[i]) {
    v[i] = REAL(VECTOR_ELT(Vc, i));
    nc++;
  }
  for (maxd=i=0;i<nt;i++) if (maxd<dt[i]) maxd=dt[i];
  nlt = length(LT);lt = INTEGER(LT);
  nrt = length(RT);rt = INTEGER(RT);
  PROTECT(DXVX=allocVector(REALSXP,n));
  dxvx=REAL(DXVX);
  a = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)n,sizeof(double));
  for (i=0;i<n;i++) a[i] = dxvx[i] = 0.0;

  Vi.p = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)2,sizeof(int));
  Ii.m = Vi.m = p; Ii.c = Vi.c = 1;
  spalloc(&Ii,1,1);Ii.x[0]=1.0;Ii.p[0]=0;Ii.p[1]=1;
  k = n*maxd+2*(p+nc) + maxm;
  work = (double *)CALLOC((size_t) k,sizeof(double));
  k = 3*maxd+p+1+2*(p+nc+nt+1) + maxm + n +1;
  worki = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) k,sizeof(int));
  for (i=0;i<p;i++) {

    Vi.i = Vs.i + Vs.p[i]; Vi.x = Vs.x + Vs.p[i];
    Vi.p[1] = Vs.p[i+1] - Vs.p[i];
    for (j=0;j<n;j++) a[j]=0.0;
    sXbsdwork(a,a,Vi,p,Xs,v,qc,nt,ts,dt,lt,nlt,n,work,worki,1);

    Ii.i[0] = i;
    sXbsdwork(dxvx,a,Ii,p,Xs,v,qc,nt,ts,dt,rt,nrt,n,work,worki,0);

  }
  spfree(&Ii,1);FREE(Vi.p);FREE(work);FREE(worki);FREE(a);FREE(v);FREE(Xs);
  UNPROTECT(9);
  return(DXVX);
}

SEXP stmm(SEXP X) {

  spMat *Xs;
  int mx,i,ii,j,k,l,*c,n,p,*Rp,*Ri=NULL,rj,*dn,*dim,op,*ip;
  SEXP M,R,i_sym,x_sym,dim_sym,p_sym;
  double *Rx=NULL, *pp,*pp0,*pp1;

  p_sym = install("p");
  dim_sym = install("Dim");
  i_sym = install("i");
  x_sym = install("x");
  mx = length(X);
  if (mx==1) return(VECTOR_ELT(X, 0));

  Xs = (spMat *) CALLOC((size_t)mx,sizeof(spMat));

  for (p=1,i=0;i<mx;i++) {
    M = VECTOR_ELT(X, i);
    Xs[i].x = REAL(R_do_slot(M,x_sym));
    Xs[i].p = INTEGER(R_do_slot(M,p_sym));
    Xs[i].i = INTEGER(R_do_slot(M,i_sym));
    dim = INTEGER(R_do_slot(M,dim_sym));
    Xs[i].m = dim[0];Xs[i].c = dim[1];
    p *= dim[1];
  }
  n = Xs[mx-1].m;
  c = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)mx,sizeof(int));
  pp = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)mx*n,sizeof(double));
  dn = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)n,sizeof(int));
  R =  PROTECT(R_do_new_object(PROTECT(R_getClassDef("dgCMatrix"))));
  dim = INTEGER(R_do_slot(R,dim_sym));dim[0] = n;dim[1] = p;
  R_do_slot_assign(R,p_sym,allocVector(INTSXP,p+1));
  Rp = INTEGER(R_do_slot(R,p_sym));

  for (op=0;op<2;op++) {
    k = 0;
    for (l=0;l<mx;l++) c[l] = 0;
    if (op) {
      R_do_slot_assign(R,x_sym,allocVector(REALSXP,rj));
      R_do_slot_assign(R,i_sym,allocVector(INTSXP,rj));
      Ri = INTEGER(R_do_slot(R,i_sym));
      Rx = REAL(R_do_slot(R,x_sym));
    }
    rj = 0;
    for (l=0;l<p;l++) {
      Rp[l] = rj;
      for (i=k;i<mx;i++) {
	ip = Xs[i].p + c[i] + 1;
	pp1 = pp + i * n;
	if (i) pp0 = pp + (i-1)*n;
        for (j = *(ip-1);j<*ip;j++) {
	  ii = Xs[i].i[j];
          if (dn[ii]==i) {
            dn[ii]++;
            if (op) {
	      if (!i) pp[ii] = Xs[i].x[j]; else if (i<mx-1) pp1[ii] = pp0[ii]*Xs[i].x[j]; else {
                Rx[rj] = pp0[ii]*Xs[i].x[j];
		Ri[rj] = ii;
		rj++;
              }
	    } else if (i==mx-1) rj++;
	  }
        }
      }

      k = mx-1;

      ip = Xs[k].p + c[k] + 1;
      for (j= *(ip-1);j<*ip;j++) { ii = Xs[k].i[j]; if (dn[ii]==k+1) dn[ii]--;}
      c[k]++;
      while (c[k]==Xs[k].c) {
        c[k]=0;
        if (k>0) {
	  k--;
	  ip = Xs[k].p + c[k] + 1;
	  for (j= *(ip-1);j<*ip;j++) { ii = Xs[k].i[j]; if (dn[ii]==k+1) dn[ii]--;}
	  c[k]++;
        }
      }

    }
    Rp[l] = rj;
  }
  FREE(Xs);FREE(pp);FREE(dn);FREE(c);
  UNPROTECT(2);
  return(R);
}

SEXP AddBVB(SEXP A,SEXP bt, SEXP vbt) {

  SEXP i_sym,x_sym,dim_sym,p_sym;
  int *dim,*Ai,*Ap,n,p,i,j,k;
  double *Ax,*B,*VB,x,*b,*b1,*vb;
  p_sym = install("p");
  dim_sym = install("Dim");
  i_sym = install("i");
  x_sym = install("x");
  dim = INTEGER(R_do_slot(A,dim_sym));
  n = dim[0];
  Ap = INTEGER(R_do_slot(A,p_sym));
  Ai = INTEGER(R_do_slot(A,i_sym));
  Ax = REAL(R_do_slot(A,x_sym));
  B = REAL(bt);
  p = nrows(bt);
  VB = REAL(vbt);
  for (j=0;j<n;j++) {
    for (k=Ap[j];k<Ap[j+1];k++) {
      i = Ai[k];
      x = 0.0;
      for (b=B+i*p,vb=VB+j*p,b1=b+p;b<b1;b++,vb++) x += *b * *vb;
      Ax[k] += x;
    }
  }
  return(R_NilValue);
}

static inline int kij(int *Ap,int *Ai,int i, int j) {

  int k0,k1,kt;
  k0 = Ap[j];k1 = Ap[j+1]-1;
  if (Ai[k0]==i) return(k0);
  if (Ai[k1]==i) return(k1);
  while(1) {

    kt = (k0+k1)/2;
    if (Ai[kt] == i) return(kt);
    if (Ai[kt] < i) k0 = kt; else k1 = kt;
  }
}

SEXP isa1p(SEXP L,SEXP S,SEXP NT) {

  SEXP i_sym,x_sym,dim_sym,p_sym,kr;
  int *Lp,*Li,*Sp,*Si,i,j,k,q,*dim,s,k0,k1,l0,l1,n,mm,
    *li0,*li1,s0,s1,s2,m,*ul,*ll,*ul0,*ll0,kk,*ulq,*llq,*llq1,*liq,nt,tid;

  double *Lx,*Sx,x=0.0,Lii,*lxp;

  p_sym = install("p");
  dim_sym = install("Dim");
  i_sym = install("i");
  x_sym = install("x");
  nt = asInteger(NT);

  Lp = INTEGER(R_do_slot(L,p_sym));
  dim = INTEGER(R_do_slot(L,dim_sym));
  n=dim[1];
  Li = INTEGER(R_do_slot(L,i_sym));
  Lx = REAL(R_do_slot(L,x_sym));

  Sp = INTEGER(R_do_slot(S,p_sym));
  Si = INTEGER(R_do_slot(S,i_sym));
  Sx = REAL(R_do_slot(S,x_sym));

  for (mm=0,i=0;i<n;i++) {
    j = Lp[i+1] - Lp[i]; if (j>mm) mm=j;
  }

  ll0 = ll = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)mm*nt,sizeof(int));
  ul0 = ul = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)mm*nt,sizeof(int));

  for (i=n-1;i>=0;i--) {
    Lii = Lx[Lp[i]];
    l0 = Lp[i]+1;l1 = Lp[i+1];
    li0 = Li + l0; li1 = Li + l1;
    k1 = Sp[i+1]-1;
    k0 = kij(Sp,Si,i,i);

    tid = 0;
    #ifdef _OPENMP
#pragma omp parallel private(k,tid,ul,ll,j,m,q,s,s1,kk,ulq,llq,liq,llq1,s2,s0,x,lxp) num_threads(nt)
    #endif
    {
    #ifdef _OPENMP
    #pragma omp for
    #endif
    for (k=k1;k>k0;k--) {
      #ifdef _OPENMP
      tid = omp_get_thread_num();
      #endif
      ul = ul0 + tid*mm;
      ll = ll0 + tid*mm;
      j = Si[k];

      m = l1-l0;
      if (m>0) {
	s = kij(Sp,Si,*li0,j);
        s1 = kij(Sp,Si,li1[-1],j);
      }	else s1=s=Sp[j];
      for (q=0;q<m;q++) {
        ul[q] = s1;ll[q] = s;
      }

      kk = 0;

      while (kk<m-1) {
        s = (ll[kk] + ul[kk])/2;
	s1 = Si[s];

	for (q=kk;q<m;q++) {
          if (s1 > li0[q]) {
            if (s<ul[q]) ul[q] = s;
          } else {
            if (s>ll[q]) ll[q] = s; else break;
          }
	}

	if (ul[kk] <= ll[kk+1]||ul[kk] == ll[kk]+1) kk++;
      }

      ulq=ul;llq=ll;liq=li0;llq1=ll+m;
      for (x=0.0,lxp=Lx+l0;llq<llq1;llq++,ulq++,liq++,lxp++) {
        s = *llq;s2 = *ulq;kk = *liq;
	while (Si[s] != kk) {
          s0 = (s+s2+1)/2;
	  if (Si[s0] > kk) s2=s0; else s=s0;
	}
	x -=  *lxp * Sx[s];
      }

      x /= Lii;
      Sx[k] = x;
      s = kij(Sp,Si,i,j);
      Sx[s] = x;
    }
    }

    ul=ul0;ll=ll0;

    m = l1-l0;
    if (m>0) {
      s = kij(Sp,Si,*li0,i);
        s1 = kij(Sp,Si,li1[-1],i);
    }  else s1=s=Sp[i];
    for (q=0;q<m;q++) {
      ul[q] = s1;ll[q] = s;
    }

    kk = 0;

    while (kk<m-1) {
      s = (ll[kk] + ul[kk])/2;
      s1 = Si[s];

      for (q=kk;q<m;q++) {
        if (s1 > li0[q]) {
          if (s<ul[q]) ul[q] = s;
        } else {
          if (s>ll[q]) ll[q] = s; else break;
        }
      }

      if (ul[kk] <= ll[kk+1]||ul[kk] == ll[kk]+1) kk++;
    }

    ulq=ul;llq=ll;liq=li0;llq1=ll+m;
    for (x=0.0,lxp=Lx+l0;llq<llq1;llq++,ulq++,liq++,lxp++) {
      s = *llq;s2 = *ulq;kk = *liq;
      while (Si[s] != kk) {
        s0 = (s+s2+1)/2;
	if (Si[s0] > kk) s2=s0; else s=s0;
      }
      x -=  *lxp * Sx[s];
    }
    x += 1/Lii;
    x /= Lii;
    Sx[k0] = x;

  }
  FREE(ul0);FREE(ll0);
  PROTECT(kr=allocVector(REALSXP,1));
  REAL(kr)[0] = 0.0;
  UNPROTECT(1);
  return(kr);
}

# --- End of mgcv/src/sparse.c ---

# --- Start of mgcv/src/tprs.c ---

#include <stdio.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <float.h>
#include "mgcv.h"
#include "matrix.h"
#include "general.h"
#include "tprs.h"

#define ROUND(a) ((a)-(int)floor(a)>0.5) ? ((int)floor(a)+1):((int)floor(a))

double eta_const(int m,int d) {

  double pi,Ghalf;
  double f;
  int i,k,d2,m2;
  pi=asin(1.0)*2;
  Ghalf = sqrt(pi);
  d2 = d/2;m2 = 2*m;
  if (m2 <= d) error(_("You must have 2m>d for a thin plate spline."));
  if (d%2==0)
  { if ((m+1+d2)%2) f= -1.0; else f=1.0;
    for (i=0;i<m2-1;i++) f/=2;
    for (i=0;i<d2;i++) f/=pi;
    for (i=2;i<m;i++) f/=i;
    for (i=2;i<=m-d2;i++) f/=i;
  } else
  { f=Ghalf;
    k=m-(d-1)/2;
    for (i=0;i<k;i++) f/= -0.5-i;
    for (i=0;i<m;i++) f/= 4;
    for (i=0;i<d2;i++) f/=pi;
    f /= Ghalf;
    for (i=2;i<m;i++) f/=i;
  }
  return(f);
}

static inline double fast_eta(int m,int d,double r,double f) {
  int d2,i;

  d2 = d/2;
  if (r<=0.0) return(0.0);
  if (d%2==0) {
    f *= log(r) * .5;
    for (i=0;i<m-d2;i++) f *= r;
  } else {
    for (i=0;i<m-d2-1 ;i++) f *= r;
    f *= sqrt(r);
  }
  return(f);
}

void tpsE(matrix *E,matrix *X,int m,int d)

{ int i,j,k,Xr,Xc;
  double r,x,eta0,**EM,**XMi,**XMj,*xi,*xj;
  (*E)=initmat(X->r,X->r);
  EM = E->M;
  eta0 = eta_const(m,d);
  XMi = X->M;Xr = X->r;Xc = X->c;
  for (i=0;i<Xr;i++,XMi++) for (XMj = X->M,j=0;j<i;j++,XMj++)
  { r=0.0;
    for (xi= *XMi,xj= *XMj,k=0;k<Xc;k++,xi++,xj++) {

      x = *xi - *xj;
      r+=x*x;
    }

    EM[i][j]=EM[j][i]=fast_eta(m,d,r,eta0);
  }
}

void gen_tps_poly_powers(int *pi ,int *M,int *m, int *d)

{ int *index,i,j,sum;
  index=(int *)CALLOC((size_t) *d,sizeof(int));
  for (i=0;i < *M;i++)
  {

    for (j=0;j< *d;j++) pi[i + *M * j]=index[j];

    sum=0;for (j=0;j< *d;j++) sum += index[j];
    if (sum< *m-1)
    index[0]++;
    else
    { sum -= index[0];
      index[0]=0;
      for (j=1;j< *d;j++)
      { index[j]++;sum++;
        if (sum== *m) { sum-=index[j];index[j]=0;}
        else break;
      }
    }
  }
  FREE(index);
}

void tpsT(matrix *T,matrix *X,int m,int d)

{ int M,i,j,k,*pin,z;
  double x;
  M=1;
  for (i=0;i<d;i++) M*=d+m-1-i;
  for (i=2;i<=d;i++) M/=i;

  pin = (int *)CALLOC((size_t) (M * d),sizeof(int));
  gen_tps_poly_powers(pin, &M, &m, &d);

  (*T)=initmat(X->r,M);
  for (i=0;i<T->r;i++)
  for (j=0;j<M;j++)
  { x=1.0;
    for (k=0;k<d;k++) for (z=0;z<pin[j + M * k];z++) x *= X->M[i][k];
    T->M[i][j]=x;
  }

  FREE(pin);
}

int null_space_dimension(int d, int m)

{ int M,i;
  if (2*m<=d) {m=1;while (2*m<d+2) m++;}
  M=1;
  for (i=0;i<d;i++) M*=d+m-1-i;
  for (i=2;i<=d;i++) M/=i;
  return(M);
}

double tps_g(matrix *X,matrix *p,double *x,int d,int m,double *b,int constant)

{ static int sd=0,sm=0,*pin,M;
  static double eta0=1.0;
  double r,g,z,**XM,*dum,*XMi,*pb;
  int i,j,k,off,n;
  if (sd==0&&d==0) return(0.0);
  if (2*m<=d&&d>0) { m=0;while (2*m<d+2) m++;}
  if (sd!=d||sm!=m)
  { if (sd>0&&sm>0)
    {  FREE(pin);}
    sd=d;sm=m;
    if (d>0)
    { M=1;
      for (i=0;i<d;i++) M*=d+m-1-i;
      for (i=2;i<=d;i++) M/=i;

      pin=(int *)CALLOC((size_t)(M*d),sizeof(int));
      gen_tps_poly_powers(pin, &M, &m, &d);
      eta0 = eta_const(m,d);
    } else return(0.0);
  }
  g=0.0;XM=X->M;n = X->r;
  for (pb=b,i=0;i<n;i++,pb++)
  { r=0.0;XMi=XM[i];
    for (dum=x;dum<x+d;dum++) { z= *XMi - *dum;XMi++;r+=z*z;}

    *pb = fast_eta(m,d,r,eta0);
    if (p->r) g += *pb *p->V[i];
  }
  off=1-constant;
  for (i=off;i<M;i++,pb++)
  { r=1.0;

    for (j=0;j<d;j++) for (k=0;k<pin[i+M*j];k++)  r*=x[j];
    *pb = r;
    if (p->r) g+=p->V[i+n-off]*r;
  }
  return(g);
}

int Xd_row_comp(double *a,double *b,int k)

{ int i;
  for (i=0;i<k;i++) if (a[i]!=b[i]) return(0);
  return(1);
}

int *Xd_strip(matrix *Xd)

{ int *yxindex,start,stop,ok,i;
  double xi,**dum;
  yxindex = (int *)CALLOC((size_t)Xd->r,sizeof(int));
  dum = (double **)CALLOC((size_t)Xd->r,sizeof(double *));
  msort(*Xd);
  start=stop=0;ok=1;
  while(ok)
  {
    while(start<Xd->r-1&&!Xd_row_comp(Xd->M[start],Xd->M[start+1],Xd->c-1))
    {
      xi=Xd->M[start][Xd->c-1];
      yxindex[ROUND(xi)]=start;
      start++;
    }
    if (start==Xd->r-1)
    { ok=0;
      xi=Xd->M[start][Xd->c-1];
      yxindex[ROUND(xi)]=start;
    }
    if (ok)
    { stop=start+1;
      while(stop<Xd->r-1&&Xd_row_comp(Xd->M[stop],Xd->M[stop+1],Xd->c-1)) stop++;
      for (i=start;i<=stop;i++)
      { xi=Xd->M[i][Xd->c-1];
        yxindex[ROUND(xi)]=start;
        dum[i-start]=Xd->M[i];
      }
      for (i=stop+1;i<Xd->r;i++)
      { Xd->M[i-stop+start]=Xd->M[i];}
      Xd->r -= stop-start;
      for (i=1;i<=stop-start;i++)
      { Xd->M[Xd->r-1+i]=dum[i];}
    }
  }
  FREE(dum);
  return(yxindex);
}

void tprs_setup(double **x,double **knt,int m,int d,int n,int k,int constant,matrix *X,matrix *S,
                matrix *UZ,matrix *Xu,int n_knots)

{ matrix X1,E,U,v,TU,T,Z,p;
  const char trans='T';
  int l,i,j,M,*yxindex,pure_knot=0,nk,minus=-1,kk,one=1;
  double w,*xc,*XMi,*Ea,*Ua,tol=DBL_EPSILON,*b,*a,*uz,alpha=1.0,beta=0.0,*p0,*p1;
  tol = pow(tol,.7);

  if (n_knots<k)
  { *Xu=initmat(n,d+1);
    for (i=0;i<n;i++) { for (j=0;j<d;j++) Xu->M[i][j]=x[j][i];Xu->M[i][d]=(double)i;}
  } else
  { *Xu=initmat(n_knots,d+1);
    for (i=0;i<n_knots;i++) { for (j=0;j<d;j++) Xu->M[i][j]=knt[j][i];Xu->M[i][d]=(double)i;}
  }

  yxindex=Xd_strip(Xu);

  Xu->c--;
  if (Xu->r<k)
  error(_("A term has fewer unique covariate combinations than specified maximum degrees of freedom"));
  if (2*m<=d) { m=0;while (2*m<d+2) m++;}
  tpsE(&E,Xu,m,d);
  tpsT(&T,Xu,m,d);
  M=(int)T.c;

  if (k<M+1)
  {  k=M+1;
     if (Xu->r<k) error(_("A term has fewer unique covariate combinations than specified maximum degrees of freedom"));
  }
  if (Xu->r==k) pure_knot=1;

  if (pure_knot)
  { *UZ=initmat(T.r+M-1+constant,T.r);
    UZ->r=T.r;
    TU=initmat(T.c,T.r);
    for (i=0;i<T.r;i++) for (j=0;j<T.c;j++) TU.M[j][i]=T.M[i][j];
    QT(*UZ,TU,1);
    for (i=0;i<T.r;i++) for (j=0;j<T.c;j++) TU.M[j][i]=T.M[i][j];
    Z=initmat(M,T.r);
    QT(Z,TU,0);
  } else
  { v=initmat(k,1);

      nk = E.r;
      Ea = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) (nk*nk),sizeof(double));
      Ua = (double *) CALLOC((size_t) (nk*k),sizeof(double));
      RArrayFromMatrix(Ea,nk,&E);
      minus = -1;kk=k;

      Rlanczos(Ea,Ua,v.M[0],&nk, &kk, &minus,&tol,&one);

      U = Rmatrix(Ua,E.r,k);FREE(Ea);FREE(Ua);

    TU=initmat(M,k);
    matmult(TU,T,U,1,0);

    Z=initmat(M,TU.c);
    QT(Z,TU,0);
    *UZ=initmat(U.r+M-1+constant,U.c);UZ->r=U.r;
    mcopy(&U,UZ);
    HQmult(*UZ,Z,0,0);UZ->c -= M;
    UZ->c += M-1+constant;
  }
  UZ->r +=M-1+constant;

  for (i=0;i<E.r;i++) for (j=k-M;j<UZ->c;j++) UZ->M[i][j]=0.0;
  for (i=0;i<M-1+constant;i++) UZ->M[UZ->r-i-1][UZ->c-i-1]=1.0;

  if (n_knots<k&&!pure_knot)
  { X1=initmat(U.r,k);
    mcopy(&U,&X1);
    for (i=0;i<X1.r;i++) for (j=0;j<X1.c;j++) X1.M[i][j]*=v.V[j];
    HQmult(X1,Z,0,0);
    for (i=0;i<X1.r;i++) for (j=X1.c-M;j<X1.c;j++) X1.M[i][j]=0.0;

    if (constant)
    for (i=0;i<X1.r;i++) for (j=0;j<T.c;j++) X1.M[i][X1.c-M+j]=T.M[i][j];
    else
    { for (i=0;i<X1.r;i++) for (j=1;j<T.c;j++) X1.M[i][X1.c-M+j-1]=T.M[i][j];X1.c--;}

    *X=initmat(n,X1.c);
    for (i=0;i<n;i++)
    { l=yxindex[i];
      for (j=0;j<X1.c;j++) X->M[i][j]=X1.M[l][j];
    }
    freemat(X1);
  } else
  { p.r=0;
    xc=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)d,sizeof(double));
    kk = (int) UZ->r;
    b=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)kk,sizeof(double));
    *X=initmat(n,k);
    a = (double *)CALLOC((size_t)k,sizeof(double));

    uz = (double *) CALLOC((size_t)(kk*k),sizeof(double));
    RArrayFromMatrix(uz,kk,UZ);
    for (i=0;i<n;i++) {
      for (j=0;j<d;j++) xc[j]=x[j][i];
      tps_g(Xu,&p,xc,d,m,b,constant);

      F77_CALL(dgemv)(&trans,&kk,&k,&alpha,uz,&kk, b, &one,&beta, a, &one FCONE);
      XMi = X->M[i];
      for (p0=a,p1=a+k;p0<p1;p0++,XMi++) *XMi = *p0;

    }
    tps_g(Xu,&p,xc,0,0,b,constant);
    FREE(xc);FREE(b);FREE(a);FREE(uz);
  }

  *S=initmat(k,k);
  if (pure_knot) mcopy(&E,S);
  else for (i=0;i<v.r;i++) S->M[i][i]=v.V[i];
  HQmult(*S,Z,0,0);HQmult(*S,Z,1,1);
  for (i=0;i<S->r;i++) for (j=S->r-M;j<S->r;j++) S->M[i][j]=S->M[j][i]=0.0;
  if (!constant) {S->r--;S->c--;}

  for (i=0;i<X->c;i++)
  { w=0; for (j=0;j<X->r;j++) w+=X->M[j][i]*X->M[j][i]; w=sqrt(w/X->r);
    for (j=0;j<X->r;j++) X->M[j][i]/=w;
    for (j=0;j<UZ->r;j++) UZ->M[j][i]/=w;
    for (j=0;j<S->r;j++) S->M[i][j]/=w;
    for (j=0;j<S->r;j++) S->M[j][i]/=w;
  }
  FREE(yxindex);freemat(Z);freemat(TU);freemat(E);freemat(T);
  if (!pure_knot) {freemat(U);freemat(v);}
}

void construct_tprs(double *x,int *d,int *n,double *knt,int *nk,int *m,int *k,double *X,double *S,
                    double *UZ,double *Xu,int *nXu,double *C)

{ double **xx,**kk=NULL,*dum,**XM;
  matrix Xm,Sm,UZm,Xum;
  int i,j,Xr;
  xx=(double **)CALLOC((size_t)(*d),sizeof(double*));
  for (i=0;i<*d;i++) xx[i]=x + i * *n;
  if (*nk)
  { kk=(double **)CALLOC((size_t)(*d),sizeof(double*));
    for (i=0;i<*d;i++) kk[i]=knt + i * *nk;
  }
  tprs_setup(xx,kk,*m,*d,*n,*k,1,&Xm,&Sm,&UZm,&Xum,*nk);
  RArrayFromMatrix(X,Xm.r,&Xm);
  RArrayFromMatrix(S,Sm.r,&Sm);
  RArrayFromMatrix(UZ,UZm.r,&UZm);
  RArrayFromMatrix(Xu,Xum.r,&Xum);
  *nXu=Xum.r;

  dum=C;XM=Xm.M;Xr=Xm.r;
  for (i=0;i< *k;i++)
  { *dum = 0.0;
    for (j=0;j<Xr;j++) *dum += XM[j][i];
    dum++;
  }
  freemat(Xm);
  freemat(Sm);
  freemat(UZm);
  freemat(Xum);
  FREE(xx);if(*nk) FREE(kk);
}

void predict_tprs(double *x, int *d,int *n,int *m,int *k,int *M,double *Xu,int *nXu,
                  double *UZ,double *by,int *by_exists,double *X)

{ double *b,by_mult,*xx,*a,*xp,*xxp,*xxp1,*xp1,*Xp,alpha=1.0,beta=0.0,*Xup,*Xup1,r,z,*pb,
         eta0;
  int i,j,l,kk,one=1,nobsM,*pin;
  const char trans='T';

  if (2 * *m <= *d && *d > 0) { *m = 0;while ( 2 * *m < *d+2) (*m)++;}

  pin=(int *)CALLOC((size_t) (*M * *d),sizeof(int));
  gen_tps_poly_powers(pin, M, m, d);
  eta0 = eta_const(*m,*d);

  nobsM = *nXu + *M;

  b=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)nobsM,sizeof(double));
  a=(double *)CALLOC((size_t)*k,sizeof(double));

  xx=(double*)CALLOC((size_t) *d,sizeof(double));
  for (Xp=X,xp=x,i=0;i< *n;i++,xp++,Xp++)
  { if (*by_exists) by_mult=by[i]; else by_mult=1.0;
    if (by_mult==0.0) {

      for (xxp=Xp,j=0;j < *k;j++,xxp+= *n) *xxp = 0.0;
    } else {
      for (xxp=xx,xxp1=xx + *d,xp1=xp;xxp < xxp1;xxp++,xp1 += *n) *xxp = *xp1;

      for (Xup=Xu,Xup1=Xu+*nXu,pb=b;Xup<Xup1;Xup++,pb++) {
        r=0.0;
        for (xxp=xx,xxp1=xx + *d,xp1=Xup;xxp<xxp1;xxp++,xp1+= *nXu) { z = *xp1 - *xxp;r += z*z;}

        *pb = fast_eta(*m,*d,r,eta0);
      }

      for (l=0;l< *M;l++,pb++) {
        r=1.0;
        for (j=0;j<*d;j++) for (kk=0;kk<pin[l + *M * j];kk++)  r *= xx[j];
        *pb = r;
      }

      F77_CALL(dgemv)(&trans,&nobsM,k,&alpha,UZ,&nobsM, b, &one,&beta, a, &one FCONE);
      if (*by_exists)
      for (xp1=Xp,xxp=a,xxp1=a + *k;xxp<xxp1;xxp++,xp1+= *n) *xp1 = *xxp * by_mult;
      else
      for (xp1=Xp,xxp=a,xxp1=a + *k;xxp<xxp1;xxp++,xp1+= *n) *xp1 = *xxp;

    }
  }

  FREE(b);FREE(a);
  FREE(xx);FREE(pin);
}

# --- End of mgcv/src/tprs.c ---

# --- Start of mgcv/src/tprs.h ---

#ifndef MATRIX_HEADER_IN
#include "matrix.h"
#endif

void tpsE(matrix *E,matrix *X,int m,int d);

void tpsT(matrix *T,matrix *X,int m,int d);
double tps_g(matrix *X,matrix *p,double *x,int d,int m,double *b,int constant);
void tprs_setup(double **x,double **knt,int m,int d,int n,int k,int constant,matrix *X,matrix *S,
                matrix *UZ,matrix *Xu,int n_knots);
int null_space_dimension(int d,int m);
int *Xd_strip(matrix *Xd);
double eta_const(int m,int d);
int Xd_row_comp(double *a,double *b,int k);
int *Xd_strip(matrix *Xd);
void tprs_setup(double **x,double **knt,int m,int d,int n,int k,int constant,matrix *X,matrix *S,
                matrix *UZ,matrix *Xu,int n_knots);
